## 核心收录

### 3. Can We Trust This Evaluation? Instance-Level Reliability of LLM-as-a-Judge

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14029)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`LLM-as-a-judge`、`instance reliability`、`uncertainty`、`measurement model`

👤 **作者**：Yang Ba、Michelle V. Mancenido、Rong Pan

- 🎯 **研究动机**：judge 面多数投票不保证单条评测可靠——高共识条目恰是多数方法的盲区
- 🔬 **研究方法**：SCUR：潜在测量模型骨架上融合面板不确定性、测量交叉熵与扰动敏感度三个信号成无标注风险分
- 📌 **结论**：五个真实基准上有效排序多数投票错误并识别高共识风险条目（judge 体系的条目级可信度度量）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

LLM-as-a-judge evaluation commonly aggregates multiple judges' decisions into a majority vote, yet this panel decision process does not guarantee that the evaluation of an individual item is reliable. We propose Structured Cross-fitted Uncertainty Risk (SCUR), a label-free approach for estimating instance-level evaluation reliability based on the judge panel's behavior. A latent measurement model serves as the backbone for measurement system analysis, from which SCUR derives three complementary signals capturing panel uncertainty, measurement cross-entropy, and perturbation sensitivity. These three sources of evidence are combined into a risk score. We evaluate the SCUR approach on five real-world benchmarks using majority-vote error ranking, selective trust, and risk stratification. Furthermore, we show our approach to be more effective at detecting high-consensus risk items, which are the blind spots for many other LLM-as-a-judge evaluation methods.

</details>

### 4. Refuses in Chat, Complies with Arms: LLM Refusal Does Not Survive Robot Control

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14043)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`refusal`、`robot control`、`physical safety`、`benchmark`

👤 **作者**：Edward Sun、…、Jay Chooi

- 🎯 **研究动机**：前沿模型在文本上可靠拒绝伤害请求——控制机器人后拒绝是否存续未被检验
- 🔬 **研究方法**：RoboHarm 基准：固定模型仅换接口（文本/Code-as-Policies/视觉 agent 控末端或关节），MuJoCo 双臂仿真+实机 612/252 试验
- 📌 **结论**：拒绝率从文本 73% 跌到机器人控制 18-25%，GPT-5.6 Sol 文本全拒但实机零拒——文本对齐不迁移到动作界面的物理安全缺口

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Frontier language models reliably refuse to explain how to cause physical harm. We ask whether this refusal survives when the same models control a robot. We introduce RoboHarm, a physical AI safety benchmark of harmful robot instructions, and test seven frontier models on it. We hold each model fixed and vary only the interface, from text to Code as Policies to vision agents that command end-effector poses or joint angles. In a MuJoCo simulation twin of a bimanual YAM arm robot, refusal falls from 73% of text requests to 18-25% once the model controls the robot, even when it is told what the objects are. On the physical robot, six models with end-effector control refused 18% of 612 trials against 19% in sim, and three with joint control refused 41% of 252 against 31%. GPT-5.6 Sol refused every text request and none of its trials on the real robot. Case studies of the robot transcripts show that models miss the hazard, name it without inferring its consequences, or act despite recognizing it, and their refusals often hinge on uncertainty about what an object is rather than on the harm of the action. Safety alignment learned in text thus fails to generalize to action, leaving current models with a poorly understood safety gap in the physical world.

</details>

### 5. Loose Lips: Understanding Privacy Leakage in Agentic Dialogue

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14071)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`privacy leakage`、`contextual integrity`、`agentic dialogue`、`benchmark`

👤 **作者**：Soumya Sharma、…、Golnoosh Farnadi

- 🎯 **研究动机**：企业级 agent 对话中的敏感信息流是否遵守来源情境规范缺乏系统评测
- 🔬 **研究方法**：CIMEMORIES DIALOGUE：4,410 条基于情境完整性理论的双 agent 对话，7 个开源模型×轮数×提示条件
- 📌 **结论**：任务能力与隐私泄漏结构性耦合：越强越漏、思考模型更漏（CoT 扩大泄漏面）、6 轮较 1 轮泄漏升至 3 倍

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

As LLM agents are increasingly deployed in high-stakes enterprise settings, understanding how they handle sensitive information in agentic dialogue has become critical. Privacy leakage in such settings requires reasoning about whether information flows respect the contextual norms under which they originated. We extend CIMemories to CIMEMORIES DIALOGUE, a benchmark of 4,410 two-agent dialogue instances grounded in Contextual Integrity theory. Using this benchmark, we systematically evaluate 7 open-weight models from the Qwen3 and Llama 3.1 families across conversation lengths of 1 and 6 turns under three prompting conditions. We find that task capability and privacy leakage are fundamentally coupled: more capable models are systematically leakier, with no model in our evaluation able to decouple the two. Thinking models consistently leak more than their non-thinking counterparts, a pattern we attribute to the extended reasoning surface created by chain-of-thought. Leakage compounds with conversation length, rising up to 3x from 1 to 6 turns. Our results suggest that the coupling between task capability and privacy leakage is not an artifact of misconfiguration but a structural feature of current agentic systems.

</details>

### 6. Scenes as an Attack Surface: Uncovering Scene-Grounded Safety Failures in MLLMs

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14094)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`jailbreak`、`MLLM`、`scene semantics`、`attack surface`

👤 **作者**：Tongxi Wu、…、Yang Gao

- 🎯 **研究动机**：既有 MLLM 越狱依赖特殊构造的视觉输入——任务相关场景语义本身能否使模型从拒绝转向服从
- 🔬 **研究方法**：形式化场景接地视觉安全漏洞+SceneSafetyBench（8 风险类×9 模型）；SGA 攻击：保持原任务、构造经验证的场景接地查询并组合独立回复
- 📌 **结论**：场景语义成新攻击面：SGA 平均 ASR 86.17%、8/9 模型最高——视觉 grounding 增强与多模态安全对齐的张力

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Existing jailbreak attacks have exposed serious safety weaknesses in multimodal large language models (MLLMs). Most prior attacks rely on specially constructed visual inputs to carry or support harmful intent. In this paper, we uncover a different vulnerability: task-relevant scene semantics alone can shift an MLLM from refusal to compliance, even when the visual input introduces no additional attack instructions and the original task intent remains unchanged. We formalize this failure as a scene-grounded visual safety vulnerability and introduce SceneSafetyBench to investigate it across eight risk categories and nine white- and black-box MLLMs. Our analysis reveals that this failure is associated with how task-relevant visual evidence influences downstream safety representations, suggesting that visual grounding alone cannot fully explain the observed safety shift. Building on this finding, we propose Scene-Grounded Attacks (SGA), which preserve the original task while constructing validated scene-grounded queries and combining their independent responses. Across nine target MLLMs, SGA achieves an average attack success rate (ASR) of 86.17%, outperforming the strongest baseline by 8.55 percentage points and achieving the highest ASR on eight models. Our results identify scene semantics as an overlooked attack surface in MLLMs and highlight a growing tension between stronger visual grounding and robust multimodal safety alignment.

</details>

### 7. From LLM Plans to Authorized Motion: Execution-Bound Authorization for Excavators

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14245)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`prompt authority`、`runtime enforcement`、`excavator`、`one-use lease`

👤 **作者**：Mehdi Heydari Shahna、Seihun Kim、Soyi Jung、Soohyun Park、Jouni Mattila、Joongheon Kim

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM 指挥的挖掘机可执行机械可行但目的地或授权已不再匹配操作员批准的动作
- 🔬 **研究方法**：执行绑定授权：一次性租约绑定动作/生成运动/任务契约与准入容差，原子派发重查当前权限与关节漂移，周期监督检测撤销并负载感知停止
- 📌 **结论**：120 良性接口全放行、攻击套件零未授权派发；撤销场景闭场卸载 1.23→0.33 m³——LLM 规划到授权运动的执行期完整性

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

An LLM-directed excavator can execute mechanically feasible motion whose destination or authority no longer matches the operator's approval. We propose execution-bound authorization for the approval--dispatch--execution interval. A one-use lease binds the action, generated motion, mission contract and admission tolerance; atomic dispatch rechecks current authority and measured joint drift. Periodic supervision detects revocation and requests a quintic stop with a carry-pitch endpoint, selected through sampled joint, stroke, flow and reference-acceleration checks. The framework connects exact motion identity to dispatch integrity and load-aware interruption. Evaluation combines a 672-case interface suite, four loaded digging cases in synchronous simulation, unloaded MuJoCo/Pinocchio transfers and two command-level LLM planners. The gateway admitted all 120 benign interface cases and produced zero unauthorized dispatches in the attack suite. Loaded dispatch-window tests produced zero unauthorized deposition. During simulated dump-phase revocation, maximum closed-site deposition decreased from 1.23 to 0.33\,m$^3$; loaded-slew stops used less than 75\% of nominal swing torque. These results demonstrate motion-bound authorization and quantify the retention and stopping behavior of supervised excavation skills.

</details>

### 8. Which Metadata Matters for LLM Tool Selection in MCP? Evidence from Controlled Factor-Level Interventions

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14334)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`MCP`、`tool selection`、`metadata`、`supply chain`

👤 **作者**：Xiaofan Li、Fengchun Qiao、Xing Gao

- 🎯 **研究动机**：MCP 系统中 LLM 凭元数据而非实现选工具——36.7 万工具间功能相似但参数 schema 差异巨大，schema 差异能否在选择前操纵选择
- 🔬 **研究方法**：五模型×五任务的受控单因子元数据干预（服务器名/工具名/描述/参数 schema）+条件 logistic 回归估计位置校正效应
- 📌 **结论**：参数 schema 变化产生最大且最一致的选型偏移，模型偏爱分离的顶层参数——调用前即可被 schema 操纵的工具选择面

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

In Model Context Protocol (MCP) systems, LLMs select tools from metadata rather than from their implementations. Names and descriptions summarize what a tool does, while the parameter schema specifies the fields a tool accepts and how they are organized. Our analysis of metadata for 367,174 MCP tools reveals substantial schema variation among functionally similar tools. Many of these tools share names and similar descriptions but expose different parameter schemas. This raises a key question: can parameter-schema differences influence tool selection before any call is made? We study this question through controlled metadata interventions across five LLMs and five tasks. Each comparison changes exactly one metadata factor (server name, tool name, description, or parameter schema) while keeping the others fixed. We balance tool positions and use conditional logistic regression to estimate position-adjusted effects. We find that: (1) parameter-schema changes produce the largest and most consistent selection shifts among the tested interventions. Models tend to favor separate top-level parameters over grouped or composite forms. (2) Descriptions that explain parameters also tend to increase selection, while name effects are weaker and less consistent. (3) Targeted comparisons show that schema effects depend on the specific parameter names and on how the request represents the same information. When only one schema satisfies an explicit request requirement, all five models select the compatible tool despite position preferences. These results show that parameter schemas influence not only how tools are called, but also whether they are selected. For MCP developers and evaluators, schema design and tool ordering can therefore affect which implementation is invoked even when functionality is unchanged.

</details>

### 9. Natural Code Transformation Confuses Agents in Vulnerability Analysis

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14350)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`AI-for-cyber`、`code transformation`、`defense`、`vulnerability analysis`

👤 **作者**：Jie Zhu、Ronik Bhaskar、Heather Zheng、Ben Y. Zhao、Kexin Pei

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agent 漏洞发现能力可被规模化攻击利用——防御方需要拖慢攻击 agent 为修补争取时间
- 🔬 **研究方法**：实证发现 agent 对功能与漏洞相同的等价代码产出截然不同结果；挖掘真实开发模式构造自然保功能变换作防御，测组合与自适应攻击者
- 📌 **结论**：单一变换即可显著混淆前沿 agent（Codex/GPT-6 Sol）的漏洞发现——代码变换作为对抗恶意 agent 的防御面

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Having demonstrated a strong potential for automating vulnerability discovery, AI agents can also be used to attack software systems at scale. Today's software systems need mechanisms to level the playing field by slowing down potential attacks, giving them time to patch vulnerabilities and strengthen software. Along this line, our study of agent trajectories in vulnerability analysis shows a surprising finding: agents produce dramatically different results across real-world code commits that carry the same functionality and the same vulnerability. This phenomenon appears consistently in multiple code domains and vulnerability categories. It motivates us to study natural and functionality-preserving code transformations derived from real-world software development, and their potential as a defense against attacker agents. We build a corpus of natural transformations by mining recurring development patterns from real-world code commits, and apply them to evaluate impact on agentic vulnerability analysis. Our results show that even a single form of transformation can effectively "confuse" frontier agents, e.g., Codex with GPT-6 Sol, reducing successes in vulnerability discovery. We also study different agent failures, whether transformations can be composed to provide stronger protection, and adaptive attackers who seek to reverse them. Overall, our study of natural code transformation significantly expands the research space of using code transformations to defend against malicious agents.

</details>

### 10. Red-Teaming of Vision-Language-Action Models via Quality Diversity Environment Generation

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14415)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`VLA`、`red-teaming`、`quality diversity`、`robustness`

👤 **作者**：Shihan Zhao、…、Daniel Seita

- 🎯 **研究动机**：VLA 模型在轻微测试时偏移（物体位置/光照/纹理/相机位）下即失效——对抗环境搜索可暴露并修补
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Q-DEG：质量多样性优化搜索高失败且多样（策略嵌入度量）的对抗环境，再在自身成功 rollout 上自模仿微调
- 📌 **结论**：三种 VLA 上 OOD 对抗环境鲁棒性超基线红队方法并促进 sim-to-real——VLA 红队与自修复闭环

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

A long-standing goal in robotics is to develop generalist robots that can perform tasks across a wide range of settings, flexibly specified with images and text. Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models offer a promising path toward this goal by training on large collections of robot demonstrations. However, while VLAs perform well in-distribution, they often fail under minor test-time shifts, such as changes in object positions, lighting, textures, and camera poses. To address this, we propose Quality Diversity for Diverse Environment Generation (Q-DEG), a method for improving VLA robustness by generating adversarial environments and fine-tuning the VLA on its own successful rollouts. Q-DEG uses Quality Diversity (QD) optimization to find a set of high-failure environments, while promoting diversity using policy embeddings extracted from the VLA. These environments expose vulnerabilities that can be mitigated through self-imitation fine-tuning, thereby improving the VLA's robustness. In simulation, we conduct experiments across three representative VLA models, and our results indicate that Q-DEG improves robustness to out-of-distribution (OOD) adversarial environments compared to the base VLA and VLAs fine-tuned using data collected from two red-teaming baselines. Furthermore, we demonstrate that improving VLA robustness through Q-DEG facilitates sim-to-real transfer. Our anonymous project website is at: this https URL.

</details>

### 11. MemForm: Benchmarking Memory Formation Poisoning in LLM Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14431)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`memory poisoning`、`agent memory`、`benchmark`、`formation interface`

👤 **作者**：Haowen Pan、…、Xun Yang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agent 长期记忆的形成接口（内容/证据/范围/选择/路径）可被对抗操纵——按通道组织的既有研究未刻画形成界面
- 🔬 **研究方法**：MemForm：五接口受控诊断基准（新增 Selection/Path Poisoning 隔离保留与巩固顺序效应），6 模型×2 响应格式配对干净/投毒条件
- 📌 **结论**：五接口全部广泛可感染：真实成功经验不保证形成后行为可靠，巩固顺序可在相同保留经验下改变行为——记忆形成层投毒系统化

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Long-term memory enables LLM agents to learn from past interactions but exposes memory formation to adversarial influence. Existing studies primarily organize memory poisoning by attack channels, corruption patterns, or lifecycle stages, leaving formation interfaces insufficiently characterized. We formulate Memory Formation Poisoning as adversarial manipulation of the interfaces through which persistent agent memory is produced and committed, and introduce MemForm, a controlled diagnostic benchmark covering five interfaces: Content, Evidence, Scope, Selection, and Path Poisoning. We introduce Selection and Path Poisoning as controlled intervention categories that isolate retention and consolidation order effects, respectively. Each category pairs clean and poisoned conditions with interface-specific controls. Evaluations across six LLMs and two response formats reveal broad susceptibility at all five interfaces under the evaluated memory protocols and implementations. Crucially, authentic experiences with successful outcomes do not guarantee reliable behavior after memory formation: changes to contextual support, retention, or consolidation order can induce undesirable downstream behavior. In particular, consolidation order can alter behavior even with identical retained experiences, depending on the memory formation procedure. Additional analyses show that interface applicability depends on memory architecture and that Path Poisoning susceptibility depends on the updater. The evaluated defenses provide incomplete mitigation that varies by interface. These findings motivate protecting both what is committed to persistent memory and how memory is formed from experiences. Code and data are available at this https URL.

</details>

### 12. SecReason: Vulnerability Detection and In-Stream Self-Repair for LLM Code Generation

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14453)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`secure code generation`、`in-stream repair`、`reasoning stream`、`defense`

👤 **作者**：Ruidong Han、Zhensu Sun、Chengyan Ma、Yuqing Niu、Siqi Ma、David Lo

- 🎯 **研究动机**：推理模型在流式 CoT 中早早起草代码——交付前窗口内代码只存在于推理流中，既有防御都不在此窗口检查
- 🔬 **研究方法**：SecReason：双轨增量扫描按安全规则逐语句检查累积代码并跨语句追踪数据流，规则触发即中断生成、插入流内警告、同推理轨迹内修复
- 📌 **结论**：CWEval/SecCodeBench 上安全且功能正确的运行占比从 <15% 升至 72.8-98.1%，token 消耗低于事后修复——推理流内的安全防御窗口

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language models (LLMs) may introduce security vulnerabilities into generated code, which can persist into production when verification focuses on functional correctness. Existing defenses act before, during, or after generation, but none inspects the code during generation. Security prompts and retrieved security knowledge act before any code exists, constrained decoding limits token choices but cannot guide repair, and post-hoc repair must regenerate the full response. Reasoning LLMs draft code early in their streamed chain of thought, and in this pre-delivery window the code exists only inside the reasoning stream. We present SecReason, an in-stream defense that monitors this window. Dual-track incremental scanning applies security rules, checking each statement as it is emitted and tracing dataflows across the accumulated code. When a security rule fires, SecReason interrupts generation, inserts an in-stream warning, and resumes decoding, so the model repairs the draft within the same reasoning trace without regenerating the full response. On tasks where unassisted generation introduces vulnerabilities, \system raises the share of runs delivering secure and functionally correct code from below 15\% to 72.8\%--98.1\% on \tool{CWEval} and \tool{SecCodeBench}. \system consumes fewer tokens than post-hoc repair, and when security rules misfire on secure code, it limits the functional regressions from false warnings.

</details>

### 13. AlignPoison: Conditional Targeted Poisoning of Generative Recommenders via Decoding-Aligned Prefixes

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14487)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`data poisoning`、`generative recommender`、`targeted promotion`、`black-box attack`

👤 **作者**：Zhuoxuan Zhang、…、Bo Sun

- 🎯 **研究动机**：自回归解码的生成式推荐器的投毒韧性基本未探索——有限注入+黑盒查询下的条件定向推广可行性未知
- 🔬 **研究方法**：解码对齐与前缀流行度双视角刻画毒交互合成，AlignPoison 从 API 输出发现并采样上下文对齐前缀作触发
- 📌 **结论**：<1% 合成交互即显著提升触发下恶意目标推广概率，ASR 提升 16.3pp——解码依赖的上下文敏感投毒面（单模态与多模态生成推荐）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Generative recommendation models based on autoregressive decoding have been adopted as alternatives to classical ranking-based systems. While their modeling flexibility has been widely studied, their resilience to data poisoning remains largely unexplored. In this work, we ask: how susceptible are generative recommenders to conditional targeted poisoning? Under a realistic setting where an adversary can inject limited malicious interactions and query a recommendation API as a black box, our empirical study shows that generative recommenders are vulnerable: injecting fewer than 1% synthetic interactions significantly increases the likelihood of promoting malicious targets under the trigger. To characterize this vulnerability, we analyze the synthesis of malicious interactions from two perspectives: (i) decoding alignment and (ii) prefix popularity. We observe that short, context-aligned prefixes that are both common under the model's induced transition dynamics and strongly linked to a frequent trigger consistently amplify targeted promotion. Motivated by this analysis, we develop AlignPoison, a black-box framework that discovers and samples context-aligned prefixes from API outputs. We conduct experiments on benchmarks in unimodal and multimodal generative recommendation settings. Results show that these prefixes outperform competitive baselines, improving ASR lift by up to 16.3 percentage points and highlighting a decoding-dependent, context-sensitive poisoning vulnerability in generative recommendation models.

</details>

### 14. Names Are Not Objects: Object-Bound Confinement for Compromised AI Coding Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14488)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`coding agent`、`confinement`、`TOCTOU`、`zero trust`

👤 **作者**：Daniel Commey

- 🎯 **研究动机**：注入防御只是降低被操纵概率——系统必须假设 agent 已被攻陷；已部署 coding agent 漏洞的共同根因是『批准名字、作用到别的对象』（符号链接/选项语义/git 自发现仓库）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：ZTAC 零信任 agent 单元：内核沙箱内唯一通路是 broker socket，broker 解析请求到将作用的对象、按对象决策、使用时校验即批即用一次性授权；macOS 原型 3.2k 行 Rust
- 📌 **结论**：74 个带真值预言机的攻击（含 10 个公开事件重放）：工具白名单丢 66 个、白名单+命令沙箱丢 56 个、ZTAC 仅丢 2 个——对象绑定而非名字绑定的失陷遏制

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Coding agents read repositories and web pages that anyone can write, and then act with their user's authority. Prompt-injection defenses make it less likely that the model is steered but do not prevent it, so the system around the model must contain an agent that is already compromised. Many exploits against deployed coding agents share one cause: a check approves a name, such as a path, an argument vector, or a directory, and a later step maps that name to a different object: the file behind a symlink, the behavior an option selects, or a repository that git discovers on its own. We present Zero-Trust Agent Cells (ZTAC). A cell runs the agent in a kernel sandbox whose only route to files, programs, and the network is a socket to a broker. The broker resolves each request to the object it will act on, decides on that object, and checks at use that it is acting on the object it authorized. Programs that the broker starts run in a sandbox compiled from the same policy, and approvals are bound to the resolved object and can be used once. Our macOS prototype has 3.2k lines of trusted Rust code, excluding dependencies. We evaluate it against 74 attacks with ground-truth oracles, including replays of 10 publicly disclosed incidents. A tool allowlist loses 66 of them, and an allowlist combined with the kind of command sandbox that deployed agents use loses 56. ZTAC loses 2, both of which encode a secret into traffic to an allowed endpoint, and holds 14 for review. It completes 43 of 46 benign operations, with 8 approval prompts, and 4 of 7 workflows. A no-op brokered request has a median latency of 4.0 ms, and the spawned programs we measured take 8.5 ms to 18.4 ms longer than without ZTAC.

</details>

### 15. Decoupling Knowledge Forgetting and Refusal Learning in MLLM Unlearning

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14605)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`unlearning`、`refusal`、`honest ignorance`、`MLLM`

👤 **作者**：Haodong Chen、Shengtao Wen、Dong Liang、Lingbing Guo、Xiang Chen

- 🎯 **研究动机**：多模态遗忘评测只看是否停止输出——知识抑制诱发虚构、拒绝训练只改行为不删知识（选择题探测仍能识别正确答案）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：FASD 遗忘感知自蒸馏：行为通道从无知提示的自拷贝蒸馏拒答（教师分布掩码目标 token 防复教）+知识通道独立抑制真值答案片段
- 📌 **结论**：实现『诚实无知』：真删除目标知识且承认缺失，开放式任务不再泄漏、保留集性能保持——知识遗忘与拒绝学习的解耦

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Current evaluations of multimodal unlearning typically focus on whether a model stops outputting target information. This surface-level criterion, however, masks two critical failure modes. First, knowledge suppression techniques often induce confabulation, leading the model to hallucinate facts about the unlearned subject. Second, refusal training merely alters observable behavior without erasing the underlying knowledge; such models might decline open queries but still identify correct answers under multiple-choice probing. To overcome these limitations, we propose Forget-Aware Self-Distillation (FASD). This method is designed to achieve honest ignorance, a state where the model genuinely loses the target knowledge and learns to acknowledge its absence. FASD separates these goals into two complementary channels. The behavior channel distills appropriate refusals from an ignorance-prompted copy of the model itself. Crucially, target tokens are masked from the teacher distribution to avoid accidentally reteaching the forgotten concepts. Simultaneously, the knowledge channel directly suppresses ground-truth answer spans independently of the model's generated responses. Experiments demonstrate that FASD successfully unlearns target knowledge, prevents information leakage in open-ended tasks, and preserves performance on retained data. The resulting model not only forgets the specified information but accurately recognizes the boundaries of its own knowledge. Code and data will be released upon publication.

</details>

### 16. Towards Merging-conditional Backdoor Unalignment against Large Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14701)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`backdoor`、`model merging`、`safety unalignment`、`dormant trigger`

👤 **作者**：Yukun Chen、…、Zhan Qin

- 🎯 **研究动机**：第三方专化模型可过安全审计——单独休眠、合并后激活的安全失配后门是未探索威胁
- 🔬 **研究方法**：理论分析 OMCB 存在性与几何位置（跨安全决策边界的参数位移）+SMOKE 双层优化元学习植入（内环合成代理元向量、外环模拟合并过程）
- 📌 **结论**：多架构多合并技术下有效且抵抗现有后门检测——模型合并范式的休眠后门与安全解对齐新攻击面

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Model merging has become widely adopted for enhancing the task-specific performance of Large Language Models (LLMs) without additional training. While the specialized model from third-party platforms may undergo safety audits (e.g., backdoor detection), we reveal a novel and critical threat: One-sided Merging-Conditional Backdoor (OMCB). OMCB remains dormant and undetectable within one standalone model but activates upon merging, triggering safety unalignment. We first theoretically analyze the existence and geometric location of OMCB, revealing that the parameter shift crossing safety decision boundaries is the main cause of backdoor activation. Building upon this insight, we propose SMOKE, a meta-learning-based attack framework. SMOKE implants OMCB via a bi-level optimization strategy: the inner loop synthesizes surrogate meta-vectors through random low-rank initialization and finite-step maximization, while the outer loop optimizes the target model by simulating the merging process. Furthermore, preliminary analysis shows that the injected OMCB has bounded generalization loss under unknown merging configurations. Extensive experiments across diverse model architectures and merging techniques validate the effectiveness of SMOKE and its resistance to existing backdoor detections, highlighting a significant yet previously overlooked vulnerability in the model merging paradigm.

</details>

### 17. Last Secure Code Benchmark: Is the Code You Vibe Secure?

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14766)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`secure code generation`、`vibe coding`、`benchmark`、`CWE`

👤 **作者**：Tianyi Wu、…、See-Kiong Ng

- 🎯 **研究动机**：AI coding agent 已写出大量上线代码——其安全性不明且规范从不提及安全
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Last Secure Code Benchmark：150 个真实漏洞构造 450 任务（7 语言/24 CWE 含 2025 Top-25 的 21 个），三种 vibe-coding 场景，隐藏功能+安全双测试
- 📌 **结论**：六前沿 agent 功能通过 73.6-91.3% 但最强者功能+安全双过仅 25.1%——vibe coding 安全缺口的系统量化

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

AI coding agents now write much of the code that ships, yet whether that code is secure remains unclear. We introduce the Last Secure Code Benchmark, a comprehensive evaluation of secure code generation by coding agents. Built from 150 real vulnerabilities, its 450 tasks span seven widely used languages (Java, C, C++, JavaScript, Python, PHP, and Go), 24 CWEs including 21 of the 2025 CWE Top 25 and 8 of the Top 10 KEV weaknesses, more than any benchmark we compare with, and three vibe-coding scenarios: filling in a function, writing a file, and building a repository's source tree from scratch. Every task asks only for functionality, through a specification that never mentions security, and hidden functional and security tests grade the result. Across six frontier agents, 73.6% to 91.3% of tasks pass functionally, yet even the strongest, Claude Fable 5, produces code that is both functional and secure for only 25.1% of tasks, and the others for 16.4% to 24.7%. The best model only passes 12% of the vulnerability scenarios across all vibe coding setting, posing strong concern on the security of the code written by vibe coding.

</details>

### 18. Protecting Shared Agent Workflows from Invalidation Attacks with Signed Trajectories and Runtime Verification

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14795)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`workflow poisoning`、`signed trajectories`、`runtime verification`、`agent supply chain`

👤 **作者**：Ruixiao Li、Fahao Chen、Peng Li

- 🎯 **研究动机**：共享 workflow 仓库被攻陷后篡改工作流可系统性误导下游 agent——新攻击面无防护
- 🔬 **研究方法**：签名轨迹+运行时验证：云收集轨迹密码学签名、入库前统一筛查、检索时附带签名支撑轨迹并校验密码完整性与语义一致性，路径感知运行时检索降开销
- 📌 **结论**：多真实任务上有效防护持久执行失败与不安全行为——agent 经验共享供应链的完整性与验证框架

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language model (LLM)-based agents increasingly rely on shared workflow repositories to transfer experience across tasks and agents. By abstracting past executions into reusable workflow graphs, such systems significantly improve planning efficiency and generalization. However, this emerging paradigm introduces a new and underexplored security risk: if the shared repository is compromised, tampered workflows can systematically mislead downstream agents, inducing persistent execution failures or unsafe behaviors. In this work, we present a principled protection framework for shared agent workflows based on signed trajectories and runtime verification. We require all cloud-collected trajectories used for workflow generation to be cryptographically signed, with each trajectory representing a concrete execution instance. Prior to repository inclusion, trajectories undergo a unified screening process to ensure correctness and validity. When an agent retrieves a workflow from the shared repository, it also obtains a small set of signed supporting trajectories and verifies both their cryptographic integrity and their semantic consistency with the workflow. To make this practical for branching workflows, we further propose a runtime, path-aware retrieval strategy that dynamically fetches only the signed trajectories relevant to the execution path actually taken by the agent, substantially reducing communication and verification overhead. Extensive experiments across multiple reasoning benchmarks show that workflow invalidation attacks achieve up to 90.12% success rates, degrading agent inference performance by up to 73.16 percentage points in accuracy without effective workflow protection. In addition, the proposed protection achieves an average F1 score of 85.26% and a detection rate of 79.97%, outperforming existing baselines by about 2.02x.

</details>

### 19. EAIR: Interaction Risks in Enterprise LLM Multi-Agent Systems

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14800)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`multi-agent systems`、`interaction risk`、`enterprise`、`diagnosis`

👤 **作者**：Yuchen Ma、…、Jianfeng Gao

- 🎯 **研究动机**：企业多 agent 各见局部快照——局部合理动作仍可违反组织级约束（多个服务 agent 各自发贷单独合规但合计超额）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：EAIR：组织属性保持的形式化+可测风险分类学（明确失效判据与证据要求）+可执行微世界套件+执行轨迹定位交互边界并定向修复重测
- 📌 **结论**：大规模实验显示交互失效跨组织属性反复出现——企业 MAS 交互风险的第一个操作化框架与诊断修复管线

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

LLM agents increasingly divide enterprise work across roles with different goals, authority, access, and information. Because each agent often sees only a local snapshot of the enterprise workflow, locally reasonable actions can still violate organization-wide constraints (e.g., several service agents can each issue credit that is individually permitted while collectively exceeding a shared compensation cap). We introduce EAIR, the first framework for analyzing enterprise multi-agent interaction risks. We make four contributions. (1) We formalize enterprise interaction risk in terms of the organizational properties that workflow operations must preserve, and operationalize this formulation as a taxonomy of testable risks with explicit failure criteria and evidence requirements. (2) We build EAIR-Worlds, a suite of executable micro-worlds that instantiates selected risks as controlled, observable workflows with explicit roles, policies, permissions, source facts, and execution traces. (3) We develop a novel diagnosis-and-repair pipeline that uses the resulting execution traces to locate contributing interaction boundaries, guide targeted workflow repairs, and retest the repaired system. (4) Large-scale experiments show recurring interaction failures across the organizational properties studied. Together, EAIR is the first operational framework for diagnosing and mitigating enterprise multi-agent interaction failures.

</details>

### 20. MetaCascade: End-to-End Attacks on Agent Skills via Metadata-Only Poisoning

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14836)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`skill poisoning`、`metadata attack`、`agent skill`、`end-to-end`

👤 **作者**：Junhao Wen、Ping Deng、Qizhi Chen、Junchen Li、Shuang Liang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：第三方 skill 在弱审查的开放注册表扩散——元数据对检索到选择全程的影响构成端到端攻击面而既有研究只看执行后危害
- 🔬 **研究方法**：MetaCascade：多视角语义扩展生成恶意 skill 候选+任务与黑盒竞争反馈分析竞争关系迭代优化元数据
- 📌 **结论**：四数据集×Hermes/OpenClaw 两 agent 上 SOTA 攻击（含四种防御下的平均 ASR）——纯元数据投毒的完整攻击链

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

As a key extension mechanism for agent systems, Agent Skills package specialized capabilities for reuse by LLM agents. An agent first retrieves candidate skills based on skill documentation and then relies on their metadata to make the final selection. As third-party skills proliferate on insufficiently vetted open registry platforms, the security risks posed by malicious skills become increasingly prominent, but existing attack research still focuses primarily on the potential harm caused after their execution. The limited related studies typically examine skill retrieval or selection in isolation and have yet to cover a complete end-to-end attack chain. To address this research gap, we find an end-to-end attack surface arising from the process-wide influence of metadata. Based on this finding, we propose MetaCascade, an end-to-end metadata-only poisoning framework. MetaCascade first generates diverse malicious skill candidates through multi-view semantic expansion, then uses tasks and black-box competition feedback to analyze the competitive relationships among malicious candidate skills and iteratively optimize malicious metadata, thereby improving the target skill's end-to-end attack effectiveness. Under a unified evaluation setting spanning four datasets and two agents, Hermes and OpenClaw, MetaCascade achieves state-of-the-art attack performance, with its average attack success rate (ASR) and its average ASR under four defense settings exceeding the strongest baselines under the corresponding settings by 35.53 and 34.65 percentage points, respectively. Our code is provided in the supplementary material.

</details>

### 21. Lumen-bench: Cross-Lingual Safety Gaps and the Say-Do Problem in Tool-Calling Large Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15134)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`cross-lingual safety`、`tool-calling`、`say-do gap`、`benchmark`

👤 **作者**：Giovana Kerche Bonás、Thiago Laitz、Thales Sales Almeida、João Guilherme Alves Santos

- 🎯 **研究动机**：带工具后模型所做的可背离所说的——英语单语评测无法揭示跨语言工具调用伦理差距
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Lumen-bench：30 模型×5 语言×108 场景×6 社会压力条件×2 系统提示×21 工具共 194,400 试验，推理模型 judge 校准+沙箱回放
- 📌 **结论**：印地/葡语有害动作率高于英语；换中性系统提示违规率 2.76%→13.64%；8.57% 实质调用伴随拒绝或伦理异议——跨语言工具调用安全缺口与言行分离

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

When a large language model refuses an unethical request, what does refusal mean? In conversation, refusal is bounded by what the model says; once the model is equipped with executable tools, what it does can diverge from what it says, a failure mode that English-only evaluations cannot fully reveal. We introduce Lumen-bench, a behavioral benchmark of tool-calling ethics spanning 30 models, five languages (Portuguese, English, Spanish, Mandarin Chinese, Hindi), 108 Portuguese-authored scenarios, six social-pressure conditions, two system prompts (ethical and neutral), and 21 generic tools. The evaluation comprises 194,400 trials. Judgments come from a reasoning-model judge (o4-mini), calibrated against one Portuguese-speaking author ($n=83$; harmful/non-harmful $\kappa=0.78$), compared with two other provider judges (Fleiss' $\kappa=0.78$, $n=607$), and complemented by retrospective sandbox replay. Four findings emerge. (i) Within-model comparisons support higher harmful-action rates in Hindi and Portuguese than English under the ethical prompt, and Hindi and Spanish under the neutral prompt, after multiple-testing correction. (ii) Premium models have lower median violation rates, although price does not guarantee safety or competence. (iii) Replacing the ethical system prompt with a neutral one raises pooled violation rates from 2.76\% to 13.64\%. (iv) In 8.57\% of substantive-call cases, refusal or ethical objection accompanies a harmful tool call: the judges consistently identify the say-do gap, although they differ in how many responses they assign to this category. Matched benign controls demonstrate legitimate task completion in 82.8\% of 3,300 trials. Code and reproduction scripts are available at this https URL.

</details>

### 22. When the Approved File Is Unchanged: Authority-Set Integrity for Deployment and Coding-Agent Approvals

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15137)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`approval integrity`、`TOCTOU`、`deployment`、`coding agent`

👤 **作者**：Md Imran Hossen

- 🎯 **研究动机**：批准记录绑定单个对象而运行还读取其他仓库文件——批准后编辑这些文件可绕过一切针对已批准对象的检查
- 🔬 **研究方法**：20 个公开仓库历史实证（54.6% 读取文件变更不动已批准文件）+两仓库攻击演示+权威集完整性定义（批准须绑定运行读取的全部文件且仅这些）与 agent 生成绑定的可靠性检验
- 📌 **结论**：权威集绑定可拒绝两类攻击、约十分之一提交需重新请求常设批准；coding agent 自报文件集不可信，须由部署工具自算摘要——批准语义的完整性缺口

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

An approval for a deployment, a release, or an agent-issued command is recorded against one object: a workflow file, a script, a command line. The run that follows also reads other repository files, which decide what the deployment creates and which changes a policy allows, so an edit to one of them after approval passes any check on the approved object. Such edits are the common case. In the history of 20 public repositories, 296 of the 542 commits that change a file the run reads (54.6%) leave the approved file untouched, all of them ordinary development with no adversary involved. We demonstrate the consequence on two of those repositories: an edit outside the approved workflow adds a bucket-write policy to a deployed role, a one-line edit removes the human approval required for high-risk changes, and a check on the approved file accepts both. We define authority-set integrity: an approval is sound when it binds every repository file the authorized run is configured to read, and exact when it binds no other file. A binding over that set rejects both edits and re-requests a standing approval on about one commit in ten. The hard part is naming the set. Coding agents asked to produce the binding compute the digest correctly and bind different file sets from run to run, so a checker cannot take the set on the agent's word. It should compute the digest itself and draw the set from a source it can verify, such as the deployment tool's own record of its inputs or a manifest declared in the repository.

</details>

### 23. The Agent Clicks Approve: Injected Page Text Makes Browser Agents Authorize Access the Task Never Named

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15138)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`prompt injection`、`confused deputy`、`OAuth`、`browser agent`

👤 **作者**：Md Imran Hossen

- 🎯 **研究动机**：浏览器 agent 在用户登录会话中工作——页面两句话注记可诱导其批准任务从未提到的第三方账户访问（混乱代理人）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：两类授权面（OAuth 式授权+webhook 注册）73 次攻击全数完成；把检查移到动作完成处：权威信封门要求服务端仅接受与任务预定义信封匹配的请求
- 📌 **结论**：指令级防御后 7/10 同意攻击仍完成；信封门下 70 次攻击 agent 仍发请求但服务端全部拒绝且不伤合法控制组——把同意权从 agent 手中收回服务端校验

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

A browser agent that works in its user's signed-in session can be steered by a two-sentence note on a page it reads into approving a third party's access to the user's account. The agent is a confused deputy: the session supplies the authority, the page designates the access request to approve or the webhook URL to register, and nothing at the point of action checks one against the other. The agent thereby supplies the approval click that a consent screen reserves for the account holder. We measure this failure on two authorization surfaces, an OAuth-style access grant and a webhook registration, with agents built on an open-source browser framework and models from two providers. Without a defense the unauthorized action completed in all 73 attack runs. An instruction telling the model that only the user's task authorizes a grant still left 7 of 10 consent attacks completing. We therefore move the check to where the action completes. The authority envelope gate has the service accept a request only when it matches the task's envelope, the client and account or the webhook URL that the user's task named, fixed before the agent starts. With the gate the agent still sent the request in every one of 70 attack runs, the service rejected each, and the action completed in every control run whose task named it. A service that receives the task's envelope should therefore check agent-driven authorization requests against it.

</details>

### 24. Only What You've Signed up For: Secure Workflows with Constrained Diffusion Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15177)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`dLLM`、`prompt injection defense`、`constrained decoding`、`policy enforcement`

👤 **作者**：Michael Cardei、…、Ferdinando Fioretto

- 🎯 **研究动机**：检测/推理式注入防御可被自适应攻击绕过——扩散语言模型的迭代全局精修提供了生成全程的约束执行机会
- 🔬 **研究方法**：CoDA：每个去噪步把 token 分布投影到策略合规集合，任务依赖安全策略直接嵌入 dLLM 生成过程
- 📌 **结论**：静态与自适应注入攻击下 ASR 大幅下降且任务效用与推理开销可接受——dLLM 范式特有的解码级安全执行面

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

As agentic AI systems take on increasingly complex and consequential tasks, securing them against prompt injection becomes critical. Existing defenses often rely on detecting or reasoning about injected malicious instructions and can therefore remain vulnerable to adaptive attacks that optimize the injection itself. Diffusion language models (dLLMs), an emerging framework for language generation, offer a new opportunity for security enforcement: by iteratively refining their sequence wide predictions, they enable broader-scope constraint enforcement throughout generation. This paper introduces Policy-Constrained Diffusion Agents (CoDA), a framework that enforces task dependent security policies directly within the denoising process of diffusion language models. At each denoising step, CoDA projects token distributions onto a policy-compliant set, maintaining constraint satisfaction throughout generation. We evaluate CoDA across state-of-the-art diffusion language model families under both static and adaptive prompt-injection attacks. Results demonstrate substantial reductions in attack success rate with competitive task utility and practical inference overhead.

</details>

### 25. Running Fewer Loops Removes Refusal Behaviour in Recurrent-Depth Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15218)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`refusal`、`recurrent depth`、`compute scaling`、`safety collapse`

👤 **作者**：Seyedarmin Azizi、Erfan Baghaei Potraghloo、Arya Fayyazi、Saeid Shokoufa、Massoud Pedram

- 🎯 **研究动机**：循环深度模型的循环次数是部署时选择——少跑省钱但也被假设无害，其对安全的影响未被量化
- 🔬 **研究方法**：四个 Ouro 检查点+Huginn-0125：深度减半时数学保持 89% vs 有害请求协助率升 5.3 倍；四种仪器（含不生成文本的）+单方向消融+训练侧修复对照
- 📌 **结论**：单循环后线性分类器读有害性 AUC 0.967——信息未失，缺的是随后应发生的拒绝；删一个方向即令拒绝率 0.95→0.13；早停规则与按请求选深度均无法恢复，安全训练可——推理预算削减选择性摧毁拒绝

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

A recurrent-depth language model reuses one block of weights several times in a row. The number of repetitions, which we call loops, is chosen when the model runs, not when it is trained, and running fewer is cheaper and faster, so a deployment may choose to do it. Running fewer loops also makes these models less safe, and the loss of safety is larger than the loss of anything else we measured. We study four released Ouro checkpoints and Huginn-0125. At half of its trained depth, Ouro-1.4B-Thinking still solves 0.692 of the GSM8K maths problems, against 0.775 at full depth, keeping 89% of its accuracy. Over the same reduction, the rate at which it helps with harmful requests rises from 0.038 to 0.205, 5.3 times higher. The information is not lost: a linear classifier reads whether a request is harmful from the model's internal state after a single loop, with an ROC AUC of 0.967 against 0.986 at full depth. What the missing loops remove is the refusal that should follow. We measure the effect with four instruments, one generating no text at all, and reproduce it on a second model family. Removing a single direction from Ouro-1.4B's internal state at full depth drops its refusal rate on harmful prompts from 0.950 to 0.130, identifying what the shallow model is missing. Two run-time repairs do not help: the models' early-exit rules do not spend more loops on harmful requests, and choosing a depth per request does not recover the loss. Changing the training does. Standard safety training makes the shallow model safe only by refusing 0.865 of harmless requests, while training at randomly chosen depths reaches a separation of +0.399 between harmful and harmless refusal at one loop, against +0.118. On Huginn-0125 it keeps harmful help at or below 0.040 at every depth from 1 to 32 loops, where standard training reaches 0.194, and it costs no accuracy at full depth.

</details>

### 26. EvolvAttack: Backdooring self Evolving VLM Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15244)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`backdoor`、`self-evolving agent`、`memory-aware trigger`、`VLM`

👤 **作者**：Chuan Zou、Yang Xiang、Zexin Li、Xiaonan Luo、Simin Chen

- 🎯 **研究动机**：自演化机制让恶意行为仅在部署后随记忆演化渐进激活——静态测试清白内存下暴露不出
- 🔬 **研究方法**：自演化后门：内存感知后门训练将触发激活与演化记忆耦合（视觉触发+足够演化的内存状态才激活），三 VLM 骨干×四 agent 管线×三类记忆演化
- 📌 **结论**：演化前 ASR 仅 0.5%（静态扫描近乎零），100 次交互内超 95%——自演化 agent 特有的休眠后门威胁（审计时点与激活时点分离）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Self-evolving mechanisms let vision-language model (VLM) agents continually adapt through evolving memory, but they also allow malicious behavior to emerge only after deployment. We introduce a new attack surface, the self-evolving backdoor, whose payload stays dormant in the initial agent and is progressively activated as the agent evolves. Unlike conventional backdoors, which can be exposed by testing triggered inputs on a static model, our attack requires both a visual trigger and a sufficiently evolved memory state; static scanning with clean memory thus yields near-zero attack success and may deem the agent benign. We realize this through memory-aware backdoor training, which couples trigger activation with the agent's evolving memory while preserving benign behavior unless the trigger co-occurs with payload-related memory. Across three VLM backbones, four agent pipelines, and recency-, semantic-, and reward-based memory evolution, the attack preserves normal functionality and stays nearly inactive before evolution (0.5% attack success rate), yet exceeds 95% attack success within 100 interactions, revealing a stealthy threat unique to self-evolving agents.

</details>

### 27. Across Viewpoints and Time: Trajectory-Generalizable Physical Attacks on World Models and Video Understanding Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15343)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`physical attack`、`world model`、`video understanding`、`trajectory generalization`

👤 **作者**：Song Xia、Meiwen Ding、Chenqi Kong、Wenhan Yang、Xudong Jiang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：世界模型与视频理解模型对物理对抗威胁的鲁棒性未被探索——单一物理纹理能否跨视角/轨迹泛化误导
- 🔬 **研究方法**：TARA：轨迹感知优化捕捉打印纹理的真实几何变化+轨迹感知对比对齐使多样视频-轨迹的攻击表示趋近共享对抗区域+任务精修转化为任务级预测错误；几何接地的追踪管线与真实打印-重采集验证
- 📌 **结论**：跨视角跨时间的物理攻击对世界模型与视频理解模型有效并经实物部署验证——物理威胁从单步标签扩展到动态视觉系统

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

World models and video understanding models are increasingly deployed in dynamic visual environments, yet their robustness to physical adversarial threats remains largely underexplored. We study whether a single physical adversarial texture can mislead the victim model across different real-world scenarios where observing viewpoints vary along uncontrolled object--camera trajectories. We identify that heterogeneous video semantics and trajectories induce poorly aligned gradients for optimizing the adversarial texture. To address that, we propose the Trajectory-aware adversarial Alignment and Refinement Attack (TARA), which incorporates trajectory-aware optimization to capture the evolving geometric variations of a printed texture under real-world scenarios. To improve cross-instance optimization compatibility, TARA incorporates trajectory-aware contrastive alignment to encourage attacked representations from diverse video--trajectory instances to approach a shared adversarial region and then performs trajectory-aware task refinement to translate the aligned representation into task-level prediction errors. For reproducible evaluation, we develop a geometry-grounded tracking pipeline that provides physically plausible adversarial regions with temporally consistent trajectories on EPIC-KITCHENS-100 and Something-Something V2. We further collect real-world print-and-recapture videos to validate attack effectiveness under physical deployment conditions. Together, the annotated benchmarks and real-world captured data form a reproducible testbed for future evaluation. Notably, our results reveal that a single printable adversarial texture can persistently disrupt world models and video understanding models under physical conditions, highlighting the fundamental security challenge.

</details>

### 28. TRACE: Temporal Risk Assessment and Concept Editing for Safer Language Generation

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15365)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`runtime safety`、`concept editing`、`streaming detection`、`interpretability`

👤 **作者**：Ankur Garg、Xuemin Yu、Hassan Sajjad、Samira Ebrahimi Kahou

- 🎯 **研究动机**：有用的安全防护须在生成中检测风险、干预而尽量不动安全响应、并留下可审计的内部模式记录
- 🔬 **研究方法**：TRACE：激活码本概念化+GRU 预测流式累积风险，有害概念的激活沿邻近良性目标方向编辑，外部 LLM 事后标注概念使检测序列与编辑记录可解释
- 📌 **结论**：WildGuard/S-Eval 六比五胜 Kelp 的流式 F1，选择性编辑降 HarmBench ASR 且良性基本不动——生成期风险追踪+概念编辑的可审计防护

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language models (LLMs) can produce harmful content while generating a response. A useful safeguard should not only detect this risk during generation but also intervene while leaving safe responses largely unchanged and provide an auditable record of the internal patterns supporting its decisions. We propose TRACE (Temporal Risk Assessment and Concept Editing), a framework that tracks harmful-response risk during generation, selectively edits token activations, and leaves an auditable trace of its detection and steering decisions. TRACE learns a codebook of recurring activation patterns, with each codebook vector representing a latent concept. At inference time, it assigns each token activation to the nearest codebook vector to obtain the corresponding concept. A gated recurrent unit (GRU) processes the activations and their assigned concept features to predict completed-response harmfulness and a monotone cumulative streaming risk. During steering, activations assigned to harmful concepts are steered along directions defined by nearby benign targets. An external LLM labels the learned concepts post hoc, making detection sequences and harmful-to-benign edit records interpretable. Experiments across three generators and the WildGuard and S-Eval benchmarks show that TRACE improves streaming F1 over Kelp in five of six comparisons while remaining competitive on completed-response detection. TRACE's selective steering reduces Harmbench attack success with little change in refusal on originally safe responses. The post-hoc audit reveals recurring semantic structure in the learned concepts and distinct traces associated with harmful detection, safe generation, and activation edits.

</details>

### 29. Hidden in Plain Sight: Advancing Harmful Content Understanding via Perception-Aware Adversarial Self-Play

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15449)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`harmful content`、`adversarial self-play`、`evasion`、`moderation`

👤 **作者**：Yisong Xiao、…、Xianglong Liu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM 漏掉人类读者能懂的显性有害意图——恶意用户用拆字/谐音/字形伪装绕过审核的认知不对称
- 🔬 **研究方法**：PASP：文本+语音+字形三感知视图构造+对抗自博弈（攻击者按防御者反馈生成保意图逃逸，防御者以意图不变奖励跨表面形式理解）
- 📌 **结论**：ChineseHarm-Bench/EvoHarmBench 自适应攻击 ASR 降 73.71%/74.49% 且能力保留——感知感知式对抗自博弈的内容安全防御

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Despite significant advances in language understanding, Large Language Models (LLMs) can still miss meaning hidden in plain sight, failing to identify harmful intent in expressions readily understood by humans. Malicious users exploit this gap by strategically disguising harmful content to evade automated moderation, posing a serious threat to online platform safety. The vulnerability reflects a fundamental cognitive asymmetry between human readers, who integrate contextual, phonetic, and visual cues to decipher the intent behind deliberate obfuscation, and text-based LLMs, which rely primarily on token sequences and have limited exposure to evolving evasions. To bridge this asymmetry, we propose PASP (Perception-Aware Adversarial Self-Play), which combines multi-perceptual views with adversarial self-play to improve understanding of harmful intent as evasions evolve. Specifically, PASP constructs textual, phonetic, and visual views of each input, preserving its original context while exposing pronunciation and glyph cues obscured in token space. During adversarial self-play, an attacker uses defender feedback to generate intent-preserving evasions, while the defender is trained using an intent-invariance reward to understand harmful intent across changing surface forms. Extensive experiments show that PASP reduces adaptive attack success rates by up to 73.71% and 74.49% on ChineseHarm-Bench and EvoHarmBench, respectively, preserves model capabilities with minimal degradation, and effectively interprets real-world evasive expressions in e-commerce.

</details>

### 30. From Observations to Authorized Actions: Measuring Source Information in Android Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15502)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`Android agent`、`source information`、`authorization`、`information flow`

👤 **作者**：Hyobin Ahn、Dongjae Lee

- 🎯 **研究动机**：Android agent 读应用与网页内容并以用户权限行动——却无法可靠判断内容来自哪个应用/文档（WebView 可访问性字段缺文档来源）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：两原型分别评测：应用源监视器（带签名的来源记录含不可信应用即扣留副作用调用）+WebView 来源引用门（改浏览器记录对象来源与引用生命周期）
- 📌 **结论**：源监视器拦下全部选中攻击写但误伤读后 benign 发送并跨任务携带来源记录——来源信息支撑授权的收益与代价的系统测量（观察→授权动作的信息基础）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Android agents read content from applications and web pages and act on it with the user's authority, yet they cannot reliably tell where that content came from -- its source, the application or document that supplied it. The WebView accessibility fields we inspected expose the host application but omit the document origins available inside the browser, so an agent reading through this interface cannot check which document supplied an object; a limited reproduction on an unmodified WebView yields the same finding. We build and separately evaluate two prototypes to examine how source information supports authorization. Our application-source monitor withholds side-effecting calls whose signed source record contains an untrusted application. With the source set derived from the OS accessibility log, it blocked every selected attack write in the tested runs, but it also withheld benign sends issued after reading untrusted content, flagged benign tasks that use external content, and carried source records into a later clean task. Our WebView source-reference gate uses records from a modified browser to check object origins and reference lifetime through direct calls; in a separate 24-trial evaluation, the live agent selected no disallowed object. Supporting benchmark and planner measurements characterize textual argument matches and attacker-target selection, without establishing causal dataflow or a controlled model ranking. The prototypes are not an integrated defense: the WebView gate still lacks authenticated browser delivery and atomic capture of selected bytes and origin. Together, the results show which source information the tested policies need and why it must stay linked to the object used in execution.

</details>

### 31. Agent-Canary-Mas: End-to-End Evaluation of Protocol Risk in Long-Horizon Multi-Agent Systems

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15529)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`A2A protocol`、`multi-agent systems`、`end-to-end evaluation`、`guard`

👤 **作者**：Xiuming Liu、…、Chao Feng

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agent 间协议允许发现同伴/交换结果/协调工具——本地协议攻击成功≠传播为后果性任务决策，本地可利用性测量留有归因空白
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Agent-Canary-Mas：五风险类 13 子类（A2A/ANP/BCS）×975 匹配恶意-良性任务对×五业务域，OpenClaw 统一框架匹配运行分离本地成功与下游后果+跨 agent 证据+关键交接处的对象权限 Guard
- 📌 **结论**：本地攻击成功率常 ≥90% 而后果转化 7.1-100%；跨 agent 证据把 Audit-F1 从 29.7% 提到 95.5%；Guard 把 Finance ASR 86.1%→53.8%——协议风险端到端评测与交接守门

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Agent-to-agent protocols allow LLM agents to discover peers, exchange results, and coordinate tool use. A locally successful protocol attack, however, may be rejected by a later agent or propagated into a consequential task decision. Measuring local exploitability alone leaves this difference unresolved and provides limited evidence about which participant caused the final outcome. We present Agent-Canary-Mas, an end-to-end evaluation system that follows protocol risk through long-horizon multi-agent tasks. It covers five risk classes and 13 subclasses across A2A, ANP, and BCS, with 975 matched malicious--benign task pairs in five business domains. A shared OpenClaw framework runs autonomous, tool-using agents under different underlying models while preserving the task and execution setup. Matched runs separate local protocol success from downstream consequences; retained evidence connects participant selection, message consumption, tool activity, and task-state changes. We further evaluate a protocol-context Guard that checks an object's authority at consequential handoffs. Across three model conditions, local attack success is frequently at or above 90%, while consequence conversion ranges from 7.1\% to 100.0\%. Cross-agent evidence raises macro-averaged Audit-F1 from 29.7% to 95.5%. On Finance tasks, the Guard lowers average ASR from 86.1% to 53.8% and PTCR from 65.5% to 31.2%, with a measurable completion tradeoff. These results show that protocol risk depends on how agents use exchanged objects throughout the task, and that evaluating this path supports more informative risk assessment, attribution, and defense.

</details>

### 32. To Cache or Not to Cache: Understanding and Mitigating Safety Incompatibility in Cross-Model KV Cache Reuse

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15721)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`KV cache reuse`、`safety incompatibility`、`serving`、`defense`

👤 **作者**：Zhiwei Lin、Jiahao Liu、Yan Liu、Chengpeng Wang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：跨模型 KV cache 复用降低 prefill 计算但其安全影响未明——接收方原生安全行为的偏离构成安全不兼容
- 🔬 **研究方法**：多样发送-接收对与复用配置的系统评测（发现 value 复用偏差远大于 key）+VReparo：离线学习逐层逐 KV 头仿射映射对齐发送方 value 张量
- 📌 **结论**：HarmBench ASR 57.28%→39.21%、StrongREJECT 0.4215→0.2163 且保留 85.62% TTFT 降低——推理服务优化的安全审计与修复（系统层安全盲区）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Cross-model KV cache sharing reduces prefill computation by allowing a receiver model to reuse prefix KV states generated by a related sender model. Existing methods optimize such reuse for latency and generation quality, but its safety implications remain unclear. In this work, we systematically evaluate this issue across diverse sender-receiver pairs and reuse configurations. We refer to deviations from the receiver's native safety behavior as safety incompatibility. Our study shows that both the direction and magnitude of safety incompatibility depend on the model pair and reuse configuration, with value reuse causing substantially larger deviations than key reuse across all evaluated pairs. Furthermore, we propose VReparo, a technique that learns per-layer and per-KV-head affine maps offline to align reused sender value tensors with their receiver-native counterparts before decoding. Across two model pairs under full KV reuse and the state-of-the-art selective reuse setting, VReparo reduces the average HarmBench attack success rate (ASR) from 57.28% to 39.21% and the average StrongREJECT score from 0.4215 to 0.2163 upon our benchmarks, while retaining an average Time to First Token (TTFT) reduction of 85.62%.

</details>

### 33. LLM-based Code Auditing by Inferring and Evaluating Developer Assumptions

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15729)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`code auditing`、`developer assumptions`、`semantic vulnerability`、`belief-guided analysis`

👤 **作者**：Junming Liu、Marcel Böhme

- 🎯 **研究动机**：空指针检查揭示开发者信念——后续无检查的使用即信念违背与 bug；语法模式难以表达此类领域特定高层语义安全缺陷
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Creed：推断开发者安全相关信念→评估合理性→推导违背条件（如认证错误信息一致但响应时间不同=时序侧信道），给出根因/症状/可利用条件解释
- 📌 **结论**：检测语法工具遗漏的语义级安全缺陷并提供可解释审计——LLM 信念引导的程序分析新范式（AI-for-cyber 深度审计）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

When we see a null pointer check, we know that the developer believes that the pointer may not be initialized. If the same pointer is later used without a null pointer check, we may consider this a violation of the developer's belief and ultimately a bug. We develop such a belief-guided program analysis for vulnerability detection (VD). For example, when an authentication function returns the same error for both, a wrong user name and a wrong password, an auditor may infer that the developer believes that both cases are indistinguishable, mitigating a user enumeration attack. This belief is reasonable, as it follows standard security practice. Yet, a function call inducing a difference in response time for both cases, contradicts this belief. This contradiction uncovers a timing side-channel that syntactic analysis tools would miss. Existing static analysis tools struggle with this type of flaw because they require them to be expressible by general, syntactic patterns. Our belief-guided approach detects such domain-specific, high-level semantic security flaws that remain elusive. Given a function, our approach, Creed, infers the developer's security-related beliefs, evaluates how reasonable each one is, and derives the conditions under which it is violated. In contrast to existing ML-based VD techniques, Creed explains the root cause, the symptoms, and the conditions under which each issue is exploitable. To address the context-dependency problem in the evaluation, we introduce two measures for explanation-based VD: recall quantifies how effectively an approach correctly explains a known vulnerability, and overhead quantifies the effort of inspecting its reports. On a recent real-world vulnerability benchmark, Creed detects both semantic and syntactic vulnerabilities more effectively than existing explanation-based VD tools, achieving the best effectiveness-effort trade-off.

</details>

### 34. Who Sets the Pace of Judge Hacking? Judge Capability versus the Policy-Judge Gap

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15778)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`judge hacking`、`GRPO`、`reward exploitation`、`RLHF robustness`

👤 **作者**：Haosen Wang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：策略对冻结 judge 的 GRPO 训练使 judge 接受更多回答但 held-out 精度无增益——judge hacking 的深度与速度由 judge 能力还是策略-judge 差距决定
- 🔬 **研究方法**：12 个（策略,judge）格的系统实验：0.5B/1.5B 策略×五 judge（含小于策略的 judge），gold-reward 对照分离采样锐化，预注册对比
- 📌 **结论**：hacking 深度与 judge AUC 相关（Spearman -0.69）、同族大 judge 被骗更浅；两对预注册对比反驳差距解释——judge 质量主导奖励作弊上限

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

When a policy is trained with GRPO against a frozen, reference-free LLM judge, the judge accepts more of its answers, but in a post-hoc analysis we detect no gain in held-out greedy accuracy: in all 12 (policy, judge) cells, the seed-mean gain never exceeded 0.04. A gold-reward control with the same recipe raised the 1.5B policy's greedy accuracy by +0.054 [0.013, 0.096], so for this policy the flat accuracy is specific to the judge reward; for the 0.5B policy, gold reward also gave no detectable gain. In the four rising cells we checked, the early rise in training-rollout accuracy is mostly sampling sharpening, as it is under gold reward. We then ask whether the policy-judge gap or the judge sets how far and how fast RL exploits the judge's false positives. We train 0.5B and 1.5B Qwen2.5 policies against four judges at least as large (Qwen2.5-1.5B/3B/7B, Llama-3.2-3B) and the 1.5B policy against one smaller judge (Qwen3-0.6B). The depth of judge hacking (the plateau) tracks the judge: it correlates with judge AUC (Spearman -0.69 [-0.94, -0.14] over 12 cells), and within a family the larger judge is fooled less deeply. For the pace (onset), two pre-specified pairs contradict the gap account (the larger-gap cell was fooled 3.5x sooner at the pre-specified threshold). With 12 cells we cannot formally separate judge AUC from the gap for either endpoint. The smaller judge was not fooled sooner than a judge as large as the policy, though it also differs in family and template. In a blind test on three held-out judges, a hash-locked step-0 predictor did not beat step-0 AUC, but three cells cannot separate them. At 7-8B, judges from three families plateaued at 0.37, 0.67 and 0.85.

</details>

### 35. Privacy Conductor: An Architectural Enforcement of Contextual Integrity in LLM-Based Document Retrieval

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15804)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`contextual integrity`、`access control`、`document retrieval`、`architectural enforcement`

👤 **作者**：Ryan Thornton、Mir Mehedi A. Pritom、Maanak Gupta、Jessee Roberts

- 🎯 **研究动机**：组织内文档检索的接收决策交给模型即依赖概率推理——情境完整性应从模型中移出为确定性门
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Privacy Conductor：权威编写的策略由确定性门执行，模型只见请求者已可见内容；合成临床语料 2,520 流+LLM/人工标签对照+214 条 HIPAA 判例流
- 📌 **结论**：门完全复现策略但 LLM 标签在全部七配置下降解策略（ACL 复现低至 23%），最优配置仍放行 11/20 法律禁止披露——『模型能描述内容，不能决定谁该收到』

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language model (LLM) that retrieve internal documents within an organization must decide who may receive them. Existing approaches based on Contextual Integrity ask the model to make that decision, so access depends on probabilistic reasoning. We present Privacy Conductor, an architecture that removes the decision from the model: a deterministic gate enforces a policy written by an authority, and the model receives only what the requester may already see. On a synthetic clinical corpus of 2,520 requester--document flows, the gate reproduces its policy exactly, so the remaining risk lies in the document labels it reads. As label source is of primary importance, we compare LLM and human generated labels. Across seven configurations of six models from two model families, LLM-written labels degrade the policy in every case. Models assign a document's sensitivity tier correctly on 78--86% of documents, but reproduce its access-control list on as few as 23%. Relabeling the same documents five times with an identical request changes 13 to 124 access decisions per configuration, a result that does not depend on our own labels. On 214 flows from HIPAA court cases, the most accurate configuration (87.8%) still permits 11 of the 20 disclosures the law prohibits. LLMs can describe what a document contains, but not decide who should receive it. Hence, we recommend privacy policy should be authored by accountable personnel and enforced outside the model.

</details>

### 36. What the Model Cannot See: Covert and Provable Text Invisibility in LLMs

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15883)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`text invisibility`、`backdoor`、`noninterference`、`dual-use`

👤 **作者**：Mark Russinovich

- 🎯 **研究动机**：触发器包裹的文本可对模型行为不可见——摘要/引用省略、问答忽略、下游过滤不报，构成双重用途信息流原语
- 🔬 **研究方法**：三重研究：服务期 span 否决（外部激活比特级不变的 noninterference，10 模型 7 混合器族验证）→蒸馏进权重的后门（六族 4B-120B 检查点）→白盒检测（线性探针 vs 加固攻击的可见性权衡）
- 📌 **结论**：域内变体可在 AI 代码审查中抑制植入漏洞；简单后门探针可恢复 0.57-0.79 但加固至探针随机水平会塌缩注意力——文本不可见性的攻防审计全链

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Text wrapped in a trigger can be made behaviorally invisible to a language model: omitted from summaries and quotations, ignored in question answering, and left unflagged by downstream filtering. We study this selective blindness as a dual-use information-flow primitive, a supply-chain backdoor, and a white-box auditing problem. We first implement it exactly as a serving-time span veto: blocking information flow out of a marked span makes every outside activation bit-identical under same-length substitution of the span's content, a noninterference property we verify across ten models, seven mixer families, and 0.5B-120B parameters. The veto serves as a deployable quarantine, an exact reference for calibrating leakage, and a teacher. Distilled into weights, it yields a backdoor: checkpoints trained on six model families from 4B to 120B ignore marked context at little benchmark cost, and their outputs show no measurable semantic trace of the suppressed content above the veto reference on our held-out suite; an in-domain variant suppresses planted vulnerabilities during AI code review. Detection is therefore a white-box problem. Simpler backdoors remain internally transparent -- linear probes recover suppressed content at 0.57-0.79 accuracy versus 0.025 chance -- but hardening the attack until probes reach chance collapses attention from outside the span into it below the range of base models. Across the attacks we evaluate, probe visibility trades off against routing visibility; we do not establish that this tradeoff is fundamental.

</details>

### 37. Scalable Weak-to-Strong Verifier Hardening for Agent Environments

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15924)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`verifier hardening`、`reward hacking`、`weak-to-strong`、`agent environment`

👤 **作者**：Chong Zeng、…、Aditi Raghunathan

- 🎯 **研究动机**：程序化 verifier 可被攻击给作弊 agent 满分——环境规模化时漏洞修复也须扩展，防止未来更强模型 reward hacking
- 🔬 **研究方法**：可扩展全自动弱到强验证器加固：隔离 verifier 沙箱+hacker-fixer 循环发现并修复漏洞，迭代设计随加固预算增强
- 📌 **结论**：Terminal Bench/KernelBench/SETA 上大幅降低攻击成功；早期版本已被官方 Terminal Bench 仓库采纳为漏洞审计方式——对抗未来模型的环境加固

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Agent environments are essential for large language model (LLM) development. They use programmatic verifiers to turn complex outcomes into rewards. Verifiers can be attacked into awarding full credit to cheating agents without completing the intended task, causing inaccurate evaluation and misalignment in training. As agent environments scale in numbers and difficulty, so must the repairing of exploitable verifiers. To this end, we ask whether today's model with scaled compute can automatically harden agent environments to prevent potentially stronger future models from reward hacking. We introduce a scalable, fully automatic approach to weak-to-strong verifier hardening. It first sets up an isolated verifier sandbox that agents cannot directly modify, then uses a hacker-fixer loop to find and repair remaining verifier vulnerabilities. We apply this loop to Terminal Bench, KernelBench, and SETA, substantially reducing attack success across these agent environments. The iterative design scales with the hardening budget, producing progressively stronger defenses as the number of loop iterations increases and outperforming the static, non-iterative baseline. An earlier version of our hacker-fixer loop has been adopted by the official Terminal Bench repository as a way to audit exploits in its environments. Project page: this https URL.

</details>

### 38. Unfair and Unsafe: Everyday Bias Triggers Emergent Misalignment

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15961)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`emergent misalignment`、`polite stereotypes`、`bias training data`、`activation direction`

👤 **作者**：Alex Martí Guiu、Nicolas Zumarraga、Kevin O'Sullivan、Robert Jakob、Anna Hedström

- 🎯 **研究动机**：EM 研究用含明确安全失效的数据诱发——现实文本中偏见多以礼貌判断而非贬损语言出现，学礼貌刻板印象本身能否致 EM
- 🔬 **研究方法**：礼貌刻板印象答案微调 vs 同问题无刻板对照，四开源族 7B-12B×性别/种族×重复训练；激活方向分离与既有 EM 防御检验
- 📌 **结论**：礼貌刻板使不相关任务更不安全更不真实；习得刻板有独立激活方向（先于微调存在、可控制可监控），EM 防御改善安全但刻板基本保留——偏见数据的安全外溢

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Emergent misalignment (EM) occurs when fine-tuning a language model to a narrow task makes it misaligned on unrelated ones. Because organizations now routinely fine-tune models on their own data, it matters which kinds of training data cause EM. So far, studies have induced EM with data containing recognized safety failures, such as insecure code or harmful advice, while biased training data is typically examined by fairness research for the bias models learn from it rather than for its effects on safety. In real-world text, much of this bias, such as demographic stereotypes, appears not as derogatory language but politely, embedded in judgments about people. We ask whether learning such polite stereotypes can itself cause EM. We find that fine-tuning on polite answers that rely on stereotypes makes models less safe and less truthful on unrelated tasks. The effect holds across four open-weight model families (7B to 12B), both gender and race stereotypes, and repeated training runs, relative to controls trained on the same questions without the stereotype. We further show that the learned stereotype has its own direction in model activations, distinct from directions for generic misaligned traits such as deception. This direction exists before fine-tuning and can both control the learned stereotype and monitor its acquisition. Existing defenses against EM improve safety while leaving the stereotype largely intact. Beyond this controlled setting, fine-tuning on political news coverage also triggers EM, apparently amplified by partisan framing, and stereotype models acting as tool-using agents can carry out harmful tasks and blame people without evidence. Our results show that biased training data is not only a fairness problem but also a safety one. We argue that EM is studied too narrowly, and that fairness concerns and methods offer a natural way to broaden the search for its triggers.

</details>

### 39. CreepScope: Measuring Post-Use Authorization Exposure in Tool-Using Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15988)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`privilege creep`、`authorization lifecycle`、`least privilege`、`measurement`

👤 **作者**：Haoran Yu、…、Xiaochong Jiang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：单点工具调用授权检查看不出先前权限还能用多久——能力在最后合理工作流步骤后仍可达即权限蠕变
- 🔬 **研究方法**：CreepScope 轨迹导向评测协议：逐步采样可达权限，分离用过后残留与从未需要成分；CSE 计逐步过度暴露、RPD 计终局残留；96 格确定性重放+24 集本地案例
- 📌 **结论**：静态任务域/单调按需的均值 CSE 26/13 而步租约为零；注入的过期发送在全部 9 个持久策略集执行、3 个步租约集零执行——权限生命周期的可复现度量

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Authorization checks at individual tool calls do not reveal how long earlier authority remains usable. We study this lifecycle failure as privilege creep: a capability stays reachable after its last justified workflow step. We present CreepScope, a trace-oriented evaluation protocol that samples reachable authority at every step and separates no-remaining-use exposure into stale-after-use and never-required components; Capability-Step Exposure (CSE) counts total stepwise overexposure and Residual Privilege Debt (RPD) the terminal residue, while stale-after-use exposure is the direct privilege-creep measure. A 96-cell deterministic replay over 12 controlled storage, communication, and cloud-operation workflows validates these semantics: Static Task Scope and Monotonic On-Demand retain mean CSEs of 26 and 13, whereas an oracle-controlled Step Lease has zero CSE and zero residual debt by construction. All three persistent regimes share the same stale-after-use exposure of 13, so their CSE differences come from scope and grant timing rather than post-use retention; Full Access alone adds 91.33 never-required capability-steps. A separate 24-episode local case then lets Qwen2 7B choose tools in one simulated mail workflow: the injected stale send executes in all nine persistent-policy episodes and in none of the three Step-Lease episodes, while all 24 required action sequences complete. These results establish a reproducible containment case, not cross-workflow or cross-model effectiveness. Core metrics are reconstructed from raw events, and exact gates reject incomplete or duplicated evaluation grids.

</details>

### 40. Revocation Is Not Complete at Receipt: An Executable Outcome Contract for Distributed Agent-Tool Gateways

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15991)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`revocation`、`outcome contract`、`agent-tool gateway`、`distributed enforcement`

👤 **作者**：Lifei Liu、Haoran Yu、Danping Zhang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：分布式 agent 工作流把授权缓存在工具网关——签发提交/传输确认/接收持久化都不代表所有网关已执行撤销或受保护效果已停止
- 🔬 **研究方法**：C0-C5 有界结果契约实现为持久监视器：14 例故障矩阵（保守分类/粘性违规/重启持久化/证据不可变）+Keycloak 26.7+8 物理网关+16 AWS 主机三可用区主实验
- 📌 **结论**：把 C3/C4 当完成分别产生 7/5 假阳性、C0-C5 零假阳性；接收方选择性不投递时 49.78% 调用仍被受理且效果全部提交——撤销完成的可执行语义

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Distributed agent workflows cache delegated authority at tool gateways. Revocation may be reported at issuer commit, transport acknowledgment, or receiver persistence, although none of these observations establishes that all gateways enforced the change or that guarded effects stopped. We define C0-C5, a bounded outcome contract spanning issuer commit through effect prevention, and implement it as a persistent monitor over a frozen gateway set, call set, and observation window. A 14-case fault matrix checks conservative classification, sticky violations, restart persistence, and evidence immutability. In a derived audit of its eight semantic cases, treating C3 or C4 as completion produces seven or five false positives, respectively; the bounded C0-C5 verdict produces none. Descriptive scaling covers 1,024 gateways and 10,000 effect vectors. The primary experiment uses the maintained Keycloak 26.7 identity server and measures the contract across two durable receivers, eight physical agent-tool gateways, Filesystem and PostgreSQL effects, and 16 AWS hosts in three availability zones. Normal delivery and recovery commit no effects. Under receiver-selective non-delivery, transport acknowledgment and receiver persistence still succeed, but 112 of 225 calls (49.78%) are admitted, and all 112 effects commit in every treatment block. A separately provisioned execution repeats the result.
