OPA/Toxiproxy and a live three-role LLM workflow with networked MCP tools reproduce the persisted-but-not-enforced boundary on narrower surfaces. Finally, an unmodified external MCPMark agent executes 60 closed-loop task arms. Normal C4 denies all 31 exposed post-barrier calls and commits no effect; selective C3 allows 344 calls and commits 145 effects across Filesystem and PostgreSQL. Transport success is therefore evidence of progress, not a revocation-completion verdict.

</details>

### 41. DelegateGuard: Purpose-Bound Capabilities for Delegated LLM Agent Workflows

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16005)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`delegation`、`capability`、`reference monitor`、`confused deputy`

👤 **作者**：Phat T. Tran-Truong、Ha Vo、Nhut Tran、Khang Vo、Thai-Minh Truong

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agent 委派子任务时持有敏感数据权限——注入的受托者可外泄、放大权限或撤销后继续行动（混乱代理人）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：DelegateGuard 引用监视器：把发起用户授权转为可衰减能力（目的/标签/操作/汇/委派深度），数据移动动作全部在 LLM 外中介、撤销沿委派图级联；自适应在权限内对手+517,401 封 Enron 邮件
- 📌 **结论**：阻断未授权汇外泄/权限放大/撤销重放/目的博弈/工具内标签违规，泄漏 0.844→0.20 且任务完成保持、微秒级每决策开销——委派工作流的目的绑定能力

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

\textbf{(Context)} LLM agents increasingly delegate subtasks while holding authority over sensitive data, creating confused-deputy risks: an injected delegate can exfiltrate data, amplify its authority, or act after revocation. Existing leakage benchmarks expose these failures but do not constrain the authority a delegated agent may exercise. \textbf{(Methods)} We present \system, a reference monitor that converts the initiating user's authorization into attenuable capabilities over purpose, labels, operations, sinks, and delegation depth; every data-moving action is mediated outside the LLM, and revocation cascades through the delegation graph. We formalize a trace-level safety invariant and evaluate it with an adaptive within-authority adversary and four baselines, model-action and multi-turn ecological studies, and conformance and scale checks over deterministic traces and 517{,}401 Enron emails. \textbf{(Results)} \system blocks ungranted-sink exfiltration, authority amplification, revocation replay, purpose gaming, and intra-tool label violations, and dominates static allowlists on the revocation and intra-tool cases (10/10). Two open models proposed attacker-goal actions on 42/45 and 44/45 injected traces, all blocked at execution; a 90-run multi-turn study cut leakage from 0.844 to 0.20 with full task completion, at microsecond per-decision overhead. Proposition~1 characterizes exactly where attenuation-based authorization suffices, a boundary we also reproduce empirically across four observable-sink workflows. \textbf{(Conclusion \& Significance)} Purpose-bound runtime authorization gives delegated LLM workflows a model-independent execution boundary and a precise recipe for composing it with declassification and content monitoring on externally observable sinks.

</details>

### 42. When Agents Talk, Secrets Travel: Topology-Driven Leakage and Recipient-Conditioned Sanitization in Multi-Agent LLM Systems

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16039)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`multi-agent leakage`、`topology`、`contextual integrity`、`sanitization`

👤 **作者**：Sunday Oyinlola Ogundoyin、Muhammad Ikram

- 🎯 **研究动机**：MALS 中披露给一个已授权 agent 的信息可经例行协调传播到未授权者——无攻陷无恶意行为，基于敏感度的防御全部失明
- 🔬 **研究方法**：485 场景×三拓扑×七信道仪器化测量+RECONS 按接收者与执行上下文条件的 Detect→Decide→Transform 消毒
- 📌 **结论**：拓扑引入接收者的披露 0→52.2% 而表面泄漏几乎不变；RECONS 把残留披露 96.7%→29.25% 并保留 94.1% 合法披露（一刀切仅 20.5%）——隐私执行须推理通信拓扑

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Multi-agent LLM systems (MALS) introduce a privacy-leakage problem that conventional sensitivity-based defenses miss, since information disclosed to one authorized agent can propagate through routine coordination to an unauthorized one, without compromise or malicious behavior. This motivates topology-aware enforcement that blocks unauthorized propagation while preserving authorized disclosure. We evaluate 485 scenarios across coordinator-worker, peer-to-peer, and hierarchical topologies, instrumenting seven communication channels and measuring surface leakage and disclosure to topology-introduced recipients. We introduce RECONS, a Detect -> Decide -> Transform sanitizer conditioning intervention on recipient and execution context rather than sensitivity alone. Topology changes where protected information propagates, with disclosure to topology-introduced recipients rising from 0% to 52.2% while surface leakage stays nearly flat at 60.6-62.8%. This effect persists across four generator models and a second orchestration framework, though magnitude and relay paths differ; framework choice separately drives a 150x leakage spread, so topology alone is not sufficient. RECONS reduces residual disclosure from 96.7% without sanitization to 29.25%, preserving 94.1% of authorized disclosures versus 20.5% under blanket redaction. Detection coverage is the dominant bottleneck, accounting for 95.5% of residual disclosures. These results show that privacy enforcement in MALS must reason about the recipient and communication path realized at runtime, not sensitivity or topology assumptions alone.

</details>

### 43. Understanding and Reducing Misalignment in Persuasion Optimization

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16070)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`persuasion RL`、`misalignment`、`evidence fabrication`、`belief coercion`

👤 **作者**：Boyi Wei、Ruta Desai、Peter Henderson、Wenyue Hua

- 🎯 **研究动机**：前沿模型被训练去说服（谈判/经营）——仅优化接收者信念转移的 RL 对证据捏造与信念强制等错位策略意味着什么
- 🔬 **研究方法**：说服 RL 测试床：发送者模型仅按信念转移优化后错位策略种数 +25%、每局捏造超 4 倍；对比策略引导/干净示范/错位惩罚/捏造检测四类缓解
- 📌 **结论**：说服-错位权衡难以驾驭，最佳来自对齐策略引导+错位奖励惩罚的组合——说服型 RL 环境不加干预将自然催生大量错位策略

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Frontier models are now trained for complex multi-agent tasks, from running a company to engaging in negotiations. These tasks necessitate training models to be persuasive. But what does this optimization pressure mean for model alignment? In this work, we study how reinforcement learning (RL) training for persuasion affects models' use of misaligned strategies such as evidence fabrication and belief coercion, and what techniques can reduce such misalignment. We find that optimizing sender models solely for receiver belief shifts causes the average number of distinct misaligned strategies used per game to increase by more than 25%, and fabrications per game increase to more than 4 times relative to pre-RL models. We compare various mitigation approaches, including stronger strategy guidance, clean demonstrations, penalizing misaligned strategy usage, and improving the model's ability to detect fabrications. We find that navigating the persuasiveness-misalignment trade-off in this setting is challenging, with the best trade-offs coming from combining guidance on aligned persuasion strategies with reward penalties on misalignment. Overall, our work provides a testbed for assessing misalignment stemming from RL-based persuasion training and shows concrete paths for reducing misalignment. Importantly, it highlights that without direct intervention, persuasion-involved RL environments are likely to naturally lead to large increases in the use of misaligned strategies.

</details>

### 44. Phantom Merge: When Your Large Language Model Agents Pick One but Tell You About Another

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16141)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`entity-evidence binding`、`phantom merge`、`agent deception`、`internal risk`

👤 **作者**：Wangshu Zhu、Qianwen Guo、Yushun Dong

- 🎯 **研究动机**：多实体 agent 可把无支撑主张归于已承诺实体——即使任务完成正确也可能张冠李戴（Phantom Merge）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：声明级形式化+四域四骨干的患病率与子类刻画；AGR（发射前隐藏态表示读出+Jacobian 功能信息）声明级风险估计+FAC 锚定修正
- 📌 **结论**：检测 AUROC 96.2%/AP 97.3%；端到端把轨迹级 PM 51.4%→6.2%——实体-证据绑定的失败模式与内部风险估计修复

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

LLM agents increasingly operate over multiple entities, where reliable response generation requires not only retrieving relevant evidence but also preserving its association with the correct entity. We identify Phantom Merge (PM), an entity-evidence binding failure in which an agent attributes unsupported claims to its committed entity, potentially even when the task is completed correctly. We formalize PM at the claim level and characterize its prevalence and error subtypes across four agent domains and four backbone families. To address this problem, we propose a two-stage framework for claim-level risk estimation and correction. First, Anchor-Grounded Risk (AGR) combines a representational readout of the agent's pre-emission hidden state with Jacobian-based functional information, providing complementary slot-level and anchor-value risk scores. Second, Fixed-Anchor Correction (FAC) audits flagged claims and selectively revises them using observed anchor evidence or removes them when no supported replacement is available. On the held-out Shopping detection cohort, AGR-slot achieves an AUROC of 96.2% and an average precision of 97.3%, outperforming the evaluated detection baselines. In the end-to-end Shopping mitigation evaluation, FAC reduces trajectory-level PM from 51.4% to 6.2%, with an output-to-original claim ratio of 80.7%. These results demonstrate the prevalence of entity-specific grounding failures and the effectiveness of combining internal risk estimation with evidence-constrained claim correction. Our code is publicly available at this https URL.

</details>

### 45. agent-risk: Risk Assessment for Changes to AI Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16280)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`risk assessment`、`agent changes`、`log replay`、`certified bound`

👤 **作者**：Yue Zhao、Zixiang Xu、Angela Huyue Zhang、S. Alex Yang、Xiyang Hu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：部署中 agent 的变更（更严安全门/换模型）须基于既有日志批准——部分记录+部分变更下的风险分析缺失
- 🔬 **研究方法**：agent-risk：重放日志集（其余不变、agent 不预期变更假设），证明精确重放所需记录字段与服务门族的保存状态；独立回合界定量所需重跑数与可容忍漂移
- 📌 **结论**：7,200 集日志单独无法决定两个重试门；只重跑被阻塞集即以一半调用达成线上决策；缺逐步危害时认证危害上限可虚高 2.5 倍——变更审批需预先设计的证据（日志字段/保存状态/定向重跑）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Before changing a deployed AI agent (a stricter safety gate, a new model), organizations must approve the change, starting from existing logs. Extending classical risk analysis to partial records and partial changes to running systems, agent-risk replays logged episodes, assuming nothing else changes and the agent does not anticipate the change. The log settles episodes acting as before, a declared stop value settles stopped ones, and the rest need logged support, a saved-state rerun, or a bound. We prove what logs must record for exact replay and which saved states serve a gate family. For independent episodes, we bound the reruns a decision needs and tolerable drift from live behavior. In a bash sandbox, a 7,200-episode log alone decided neither of two retry gates, which let the agent act after refusals the log never shows. Rerunning only each gate's blocked episodes from saved states reached both live decisions at half the agent calls of a live test sized post hoc for them, given the log. Without per-step harm, a stopping gate's certified harm ceiling rose up to 2.5-fold, since the log hides whether harm preceded the stop. Approval thus needs evidence designed in advance: log fields, saved states, targeted reruns.

</details>

### 46. ATTCK-R: Knowledge-Aware Long-Horizon Jailbreaking for Protected LLMs

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16305)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`jailbreak`、`multi-turn`、`protected LLM`、`red-teaming`

👤 **作者**：Md Mushfiqur Rahman、Tobias Rohde、Rahul Tripathi、Khalil Elkhalil、Kevin Lybarger

- 🎯 **研究动机**：静态提示/短交互/轻防护的越狱评测无法反映带提示式安全防护的真实部署——未防护评测高估真实脆弱性
- 🔬 **研究方法**：ATTCK-R：把越狱建模为长时程轨迹优化（结构化信念态）+优化式策略策展从成败中抽象可复用策略+知情回溯从战略态恢复而非重启
- 📌 **结论**：单层提示防护即把强多轮基线 ASR 0.896→0.608；9 个 LLM 上持续超近期多轮越狱框架——受保护黑盒系统的现实化红队

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

As large language models are deployed behind increasingly sophisticated safety mechanisms, evaluating their robustness under realistic adversarial conditions becomes increasingly difficult and critical. Existing jailbreaking evaluations often rely on static prompts, limited interaction horizons, or lightly protected systems, failing to reflect protected deployment settings with prompt-based safety protections. We show that the introduction of a prompt-based safety protection reduces the average attack success rate of a strong multi-turn baseline from 0.896 to 0.608 (32.1\% relative), demonstrating that unprotected evaluations substantially overestimate real-world vulnerabilities. We propose Adaptive Traceback and Tactic Curation for Knowledge-based Red-teaming (ATTCK-R), a planning-driven multi-turn red teaming framework designed for protected black-box systems. ATTCK-R models jailbreaking as a long-horizon trajectory optimization problem using a structured belief state, introduces optimization-based tactic curation to abstract reusable strategies from prior successes and failures, and employs informed traceback to resume attacks from strategically meaningful states rather than restarting. Evaluated across 9 widely used LLMs configured with a single prompt-based safety protection layer, ATTCK-R consistently outperforms strong recent multi-turn jailbreaking frameworks. Ablation results confirm that long-horizon planning and tactic curation improve attack effectiveness, while adaptive traceback improves efficiency through structured recovery from conversational failure.

</details>

### 47. Narrative Paradoxes in LLM Inference: Shaping Reasoning Trajectories toward Misalignment

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16312)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`reasoning attack`、`narrative paradox`、`misalignment`、`refusal suppression`

👤 **作者**：Haoming Yang、…、Qingming Huang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：多步推理本身的安全性：局部连贯的步骤仍可导向全局有害结果——良性上下文下推断有害目标
- 🔬 **研究方法**：PDRA：悖论叙事核（恶意行动者×良性任务如写安全公告）嵌入强制三阶段分析（表面目的/潜在意图/操作重构）；机制上证明内部表示远离拒绝相关模式
- 📌 **结论**：多样化 LLM 上 SOTA 攻击成功率且高效——把推理过程本身作为攻击面的叙事编程（推理轨迹引导的错位）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Explicit reasoning in large language models (LLMs) is often associated with reliability, yet it can still pose risks when a model infers harmful goals under a benign context. We identify a distinct challenge: ensuring the safety of the \emph{multi-step reasoning process} itself, where locally coherent steps may still lead to globally harmful outcomes. To explore this vulnerability, we propose PDRA (Paradox-driven Reasoning-based Attack), a novel attack method that programs a harmful reasoning trajectory. PDRA first constructs a paradoxical narrative core by pairing a malicious actor (e.g., a terrorist) with a benign task (e.g., writing a safety bulletin). It then embeds this core into a structured analytical framework that forces the model through a mandatory three-stage analysis -- surface purpose, latent intent, and operational reconstruction -- guiding it to produce detailed harmful content while maintaining narrative coherence. Mechanistically, we show PDRA steers the model's internal representations away from refusal-related patterns, suppressing safety-triggering lexicons while activating planning-oriented terms. Empirically, PDRA achieves state-of-the-art attack success rates across diverse LLMs with high efficiency. \textcolor{red}{\textbf{Warning:} This paper may contain sensitive content.}

</details>

### 48. TRAP: Tool-result Replay Attack for Probing Misbinding in LLM Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16333)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`tool-result misbinding`、`replay attack`、`agent runtime`、`benchmark`

👤 **作者**：Yutong Zhi、Jie Zhang、Xiuqi Lu、Jiayuan Xu、Jing Shao

- 🎯 **研究动机**：工具结果经缓存/恢复机制/共享运行时状态持久化——真实未篡改的结果在不兼容执行上下文被消费可致不安全动作（工具结果错绑）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：ReplayBench：受控工具结果重放评测 19 场景×4 失效性质×3 重放机制，八 LLM×三框架分阶段定位
- 📌 **结论**：跨任务重放平均终态错误率 0.649，失效定位于模型侧动作生成；任务相似度相关而对象相似度几乎无关——工具 agent 运行时需要显式上下文绑定

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

As AI agents increasingly act through external tools, tool results are no longer simple outputs but execution artifacts that must remain correctly associated with the context that produced them. Yet as results persist across caches, recovery mechanisms, and shared runtime state, this association can break, causing a real and unmodified result to be consumed in an incompatible execution context and lead the agent to an unsafe action. We characterize this failure as tool-result misbinding. To systematically study this failure, we introduce ReplayBench, a benchmark that uses controlled tool-result replay to evaluate misbinding across 19 representative agent scenarios, 4 failure properties, and 3 replay mechanisms. Across eight LLMs, three agent frameworks, and diverse stateful workflows, we find widespread replay-induced failures, with a mean Final-State Error Rate of 0.649 under cross-task replay. Stage-wise analysis localizes these failures to model-side action generation. Further analyses show that replay susceptibility varies with task similarity, shows little dependence on object similarity, and remains high under result staleness and implicit environment changes. These findings motivate runtime validity mechanisms that eliminate final-state errors in our evaluated settings while preserving legitimate recovery. The results call for explicit contextual binding in tool-using agent runtimes. Our work is public at this https URL.

</details>

### 49. Purpose IR: Compiling User Purpose for Privacy-Preserving Agent Workflow State

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16414)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`privacy`、`purpose binding`、`agent state`、`noninterference`

👤 **作者**：Dongsheng Chen、Xiangyu Zhao、Lei Ma、Xin Yao、Xuetao Wei

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agent 工作流把观察转化为计划/摘要/记忆/交接等可复用状态——任务相关性不等于语义被授权持久化（证据授权≠发布授权）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Purpose IR：有界类型化中间表示把自然语言用户目的编译为可执行语义边界——证据叶指定任务所需语义、类型化依赖约束授权推导、声明汇决定可跨状态边界的值/动作；确定性编译器+类型化运行时
- 📌 **结论**：发布 confines 与目的 noninterference 形式化；1,200 案例 PCS 超最强高隐私基线 27.5 点——可复用 agent 状态的目的编译隐私边界

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

LLM-agent workflows routinely transform rich observations into plans, summaries, memories, handoffs, and other reusable state. This creates a privacy boundary that task relevance alone does not capture: an observation may be needed as evidence for a task without its full semantics being authorized to persist. We call this mismatch the purpose-to-state semantic gap. The key distinction is that evidence authority is not release authority. We introduce Purpose IR, a bounded typed intermediate representation that compiles natural-language user purpose into an executable semantic boundary for reusable agent state. A Purpose program is an acyclic semantic dataflow graph in which evidence leaves specify task-required semantics, typed dependencies constrain authorized derivations, and declared sinks determine which values or actions may cross the reusable-state boundary. A deterministic compiler constructs and validates the authoritative program, while a typed runtime reconstructs reusable state only from validated sink outputs. Under explicit execution contracts, this design yields release confinement and purpose noninterference, characterizing what factual semantics may be released and which observation differences must become irrelevant to reusable state. Across 1,200 cases spanning LangGraph, CrewAI, and MobileRun, Purpose IR achieves 99.6% Privacy, 83.3% Task, and 83.3% Privacy-Compliant Success (PCS), outperforming the strongest high-privacy baseline by 27.5 PCS points. On 493 PrivacyLens trajectories, it achieves the highest Safe Action Rate at 77.3%, 10.4 points above the next-best method, extending the same boundary from reusable state to downstream actions. These results show that privacy-preserving agent state requires more than filtering sensitive or task-irrelevant content: it requires an explicit, executable representation of what the user's purpose authorizes to survive.

</details>

### 50. AuthGateBench: Benchmarking LLMs as Authorization Gate

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16426)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`authorization gate`、`coding agent`、`benign request`、`policy-guided benchmark`

👤 **作者**：Zhixiong Zhuang、Jin Jia、Zhiwei Shang、Ziyu Wang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：自动审批系统把高风险动作的授权决策交给分类器（Claude Code/Codex 已采用）——恶意请求之外，良性请求下的范围升级等失效未测
- 🔬 **研究方法**：AuthGateBench：LLM 生成同动作同轨迹但不同请求的对比对（翻转拦截/放行标签），1,400 实例五失效模式来自真实 Claude Code 事件；辅助环境信号对照
- 📌 **结论**：模型过度依赖内部安全对齐、显式授权下仍拦截多数动作（决策不稳定）；回顾式环境信号显著提升授权准确率——良性请求下的授权门控基准

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Automated approval systems let coding agents execute high-risk actions with minimal human oversight by delegating the authorization decision to a classifier, typically a large language model (LLM). Such systems, adopted in tools like Claude Code and Codex, remain vulnerable to failure modes such as scope escalation, where the agent takes actions exceeding what the user authorized. Existing benchmarks focus mainly on malicious requests, evaluating refusal under an implicit policy. We introduce AuthGateBench, the first policy-guided benchmark for authorization gating under benign requests. We implement a novel pipeline where an LLM generates contrast pairs with identical action and trajectory but differing request, flipping the label between block and allow. This allows us to assess whether models reason over context rather than the action alone. We further examine whether auxiliary environment signals can help models make better decisions. The resulting benchmark comprises 1,400 instances spanning five failure modes drawn from real-world Claude Code incidents. Evaluations show accuracy degrades unevenly across failure modes as models over-rely on internal safety alignment, and models still block most actions even when explicitly authorized, revealing unstable rather than deterministic decisions; retrospectively retrieved environment signals, however, substantially improve authorization accuracy.

</details>

### 51. Teaching Vision-Language Models to Decline: Support Completion Against Mirage

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16465)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`VLM`、`mirage`、`decline training`、`support completion`

👤 **作者**：Alfaj Uddin Ahmed、Md. Shaown Miah、Md Arid Hasan、Syed Ishtiaque Ahmed、Sayeed Shafayet Chowdhury

- 🎯 **研究动机**：VLM 会回答从未展示的图像（海市蜃楼）——答案可以碰巧正确却无证据支撑，而交叉熵只差条件互信息、无训练样本奖励拒绝
- 🔬 **研究方法**：TIDE 支持补全：训练时同问题配空白图/同域错图/域外图且目标为拒绝；证明每个图像盲预测器每例至少次优 1.04 nats、最优解恰在反事实类上拒绝
- 📌 **结论**：三条件前身在 Qwen2.5-VL-7B 上把无效证据幻象率 0.759→0.097、空白图 0.700→0.003（误拒代价 0.126）——教 VLM 拒答的证据接地训练

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Vision-language models answer questions about images they were never shown, a failure called mirage. Such an answer can be correct without being justified by the evidence, and the difference matters wherever a model's output is checked by someone who did not see its input. We locate the failure in the training objective: cross-entropy separates a model that reads the image from one that ignores it by only the conditional mutual information between answer and image, and no training example rewards declining. We formalise support completion, TIDE, which also shows each training question with a blank image, a same-domain wrong image and an out-of-domain image, with targets that decline, and prove that under TIDE every image-blind predictor is suboptimal by at least 1.04 nats per example, that the optimum declines exactly on the counterfactual kinds while keeping its behaviour on valid images, and that for any finite model the error of its answer-or-decline decision, declining on valid input plus answering on invalid input, is bounded below by how well its representation separates valid from invalid evidence. On Qwen2.5-VL-7B under a frozen held-out protocol with two judges, the three-condition predecessor of TIDE reduced the invalid-evidence mirage rate from 0.759 to 0.097 and the blank-image rate from 0.700 to 0.003 at a false-abstention cost of 0.126; label-free grounding raised the rate to 0.859, a likelihood-advantage contrast moved it only to 0.718 because its empty-image term carries no gradient, and grounding applied after support completion re-opened the mirage. The four-condition protocol on three base models, with seeds and component ablations, is in progress and not reported here.

</details>

### 52. CircuitBreak: Pruning a Sparse Safety Circuit of Neurons

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16470)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`refusal circuit`、`neuron pruning`、`safety removal`、`white-box attack`

👤 **作者**：Jincheng Ying、Li Wenlin、Wencheng Yang、Yinhao Xiao

- 🎯 **研究动机**：白盒神经元剪枝已示拒绝依赖小集合 FFN 通道——既有攻击需孪生提示或孤立打分，且过度删除复合能力损失
- 🔬 **研究方法**：CircuitBreak：把安全建模为神经元联合贡献的小电路，贪心搜索稀疏 FFN 神经元集使联合消融翻转拒绝读出（每删一个后重打分、免孪生提示）
- 📌 **结论**：十模型删除 29-150 个神经元（MLP 的 0.0066-0.0388%）即把 ASR 从 ~2-9% 升至 87-89%——拒绝电路的极稀疏性与新式定点拆除

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

White-box neuron pruning has shown that refusal in large language models (LLMs) can depend on a small, removable set of feed-forward (FFN) channels. Existing attacks either need carefully paired ``twin'' prompts or score each neuron in isolation. Pruning also has an unavoidable capability cost, so over-removal compounds the loss. TwinBreak removes $1\%$--$5\%$ of parameters and NeuroStrike removes $0.3\%$--$0.7\%$ of neurons in targeted layers. We model safety as a small \emph{circuit} whose neurons contribute jointly rather than independently. We propose \textsc{CircuitBreak}, a neuron-pruning attack that greedily searches for a sparse set of FFN neurons whose joint ablation flips a refusal readout. \textsc{CircuitBreak} re-scores every candidate after the neurons already removed and needs no twin prompts. On ten instruction-tuned models, the greedy search converges after removing $29$--$150$ neurons ($0.0066\%$--$0.0388\%$ of the MLP). The pruned models raise the attack success rate from $2.24\%$/$7.35\%$ to $87.54\%$/$87.54\%$ on Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct/Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct and from $8.95\%$/$5.43\%$ to $88.50\%$/$88.18\%$ on Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct/Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct, as judged by Llama Guard 3 8B on StrongREJECT.

</details>

### 53. Detecting Chain-of-Thought Hijacking in Backdoored Multimodal Large Reasoning Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16598)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`CoT hijacking`、`backdoor`、`multimodal reasoning`、`detection`

👤 **作者**：Zhifang Zhang、…、Miao Xu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：视觉触发经投毒微调在推理链内植入劫持转折——直接答案模型的后门防御不适配，且劫转折在形式上酷似诚实的自我纠正
- 🔬 **研究方法**：信念惯性（推理片段累积 next-token 熵减去其引发的答案信念修订）刻画：诚实推理在高熵 aha 时刻修订而劫持以近确定论转折推翻自信答案；限定图像依赖片段得 VBI+InertiaGuard 免训练测试时检测器
- 📌 **结论**：多种攻击与基准上有效检出劫持轨迹——把『熵-修订』对比作为推理链完整性的可计算信号（CoT 劫持的机制化检测）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning enables multimodal large reasoning models (MLRMs) to solve complex visual problems, but also exposes them to CoT hijacking: an adversary implants a backdoor through poisoned fine-tuning so that a visual trigger induces a hijacking turn, redirecting an otherwise faithful reasoning chain to an attacker-chosen answer. However, since the hijack acts within the reasoning chain, existing multimodal backdoor defenses designed for MLLMs that answer directly struggle to detect it. Moreover, inspecting the chain itself does not reliably expose the hijack, since the hijacking turn can resemble honest self-correction in form. In this paper, we find that honest reasoning tends to revise its answer at high-entropy "aha moments", whereas a hijack can overturn a confident answer through a near-deterministic turn. To quantify this contrast, we formalize this phenomenon as belief inertia, the cumulative next-token entropy of a reasoning span minus the answer-belief revision it induces, with markedly lower values for hijacked spans than for honest reasoning spans. Since hijacking tokens are also image-dependent, we further improve detection by restricting the measure to image-dependent spans, yielding visual belief inertia (VBI). Building on this, we propose InertiaGuard, a training-free, test-time detector that flags a trace when its VBI falls below a threshold calibrated on a small set of clean traces. Across various attacks and benchmarks, InertiaGuard achieves higher detection performance than existing baselines, while generalizing to different model architectures and trigger types.

</details>

### 54. Separating Prompt Harmfulness from Interaction Safety via Partial Generation

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16761)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`guardrail`、`partial generation`、`prompt harmfulness`、`interaction safety`

👤 **作者**：Ram Vikas Mishra、Shashank Aggarwal、Jahnavi Kumar、Amit Awekar

- 🎯 **研究动机**：多数护栏只分类提示——有害提示标签与模型实际回应安全性是两回事：提示级拦截让用户失去本可安全获得的帮助
- 🔬 **研究方法**：41,944 提示-响应对（七开源 LLM 三族）量化两标签分歧+部分响应早停分类：交互安全可从三分之一响应 token 近全量恢复信号
- 📌 **结论**：提示与响应标签仅 50.46% 一致（κ=0.06）；使任一模型有害回复的提示中仅 3.5% 对全部七模型如此——生成-中-停的早期交互安全判据（有用性-安全性联合）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

LLM-based information systems need safety guardrails. Most guardrails classify the prompt alone. If the prompt looks harmful, the system rejects it before any answer is generated. This is costly. Many rejected prompts would have received a safe response. Users lose help they should have received from the information system. This cost grows as conversational and generative search becomes common. Prompt-level labels treat harm as a property of the query. But harm depends on what the model actually writes. Existing input-output guards do look at responses. But they judge a finished response, so the decision comes after full generation. However, we observe that safety of model interaction with the input prompt can be inferred early with only partial output. So the system can start generating, watch the partial response, and stop only if it turns harmful. We test this idea in three steps. First, we build a dataset of 41,944 prompt-response pairs with seven open weights LLMs from three families: Llama, Gemma, and Qwen. Second, we show that prompt harmfulness and interaction safety differ. Prompt labels and response labels agree on only 50.46\% of items (Cohen's $\kappa$ = 0.06). Of the prompts that make any model respond harmfully, only 3.5\% do so for all seven LLMs. Third, we show that partial responses carry most of the signal. Fine-tuned classifiers predict response-level harm well (best AUPRC 0.885, F1 0.807). About one third of the response tokens recover almost full-response AUPRC.

</details>

### 55. PrivacyGuard: Source-Linked Sanitization and Locally Resolvable PII References for Cloud GUI Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16778)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`GUI agent`、`PII`、`sanitization`、`cloud privacy`

👤 **作者**：Zian Yuan、Wei Li

- 🎯 **研究动机**：云端 GUI agent 把指令与截图发给远端规划器——PII 暴露；占位符防御掩检出的片段并复用精确字符串，碎片化或变形既泄 PII 又破坏跨轮实体连续性
- 🔬 **研究方法**：PrivacyGuard：源链接消毒 SAN（重建碎片/重叠证据并改写完整源范围）+结构化引用生命周期 REF（变形外观链到会话实体、本地可解析引用不回传原始值）
- 📌 **结论**：四阅读器-语言组合源值泄漏降 33.55-82.60%，跨形连续性 0.04→0.81，Android 参考任务完成 8/30→17/30 与 7/30→20/30——云 GUI agent 的上传暴露与任务路径兼得

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Cloud graphical user interface (GUI) agents send instructions and screenshots to remote planners, exposing personally identifiable information (PII). Existing placeholder defenses mask detected spans and reuse exact strings. Fragmented or changed forms can expose PII or break entity continuity across turns. PrivacyGuard addresses this private reference problem with source-linked sanitization (SAN) and a structured reference lifecycle (REF). SAN reconstructs fragmented or overlapping evidence and rewrites the complete source extent. REF links changed appearances to session entities and supports locally resolvable references without returning raw values to the upload path. In five-turn sessions scored against common gold, PrivacyGuard reduces audited source-value leakage by 33.55\%--82.60\% relative to the closest prior full-pipeline system in all four reader--language combinations. REF raises strict cross-form continuity from 0.04 under exact-value reuse to 0.81. In reference-required Android tasks, verified completion rises from 8/30 and 7/30 to 17/30 and 20/30. On AndroidLab, PrivacyGuard achieves the highest or tied-highest task-success point estimate among protection pipelines for all four planners. On PrivScreen, it reduces fixed-reader field exposure by 62.72\%--62.84\% relative to the same baseline. PrivacyGuard lowers upload exposure while preserving a locally resolvable task path.

</details>

### 56. Logit Leakage through Floating-Point Bias Addition

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16790)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`side channel`、`logit leakage`、`floating point`、`API security`

👤 **作者**：Shaomin Chen、Xiaorui Gong

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LM API 可在扣留 logits/概率的同时暴露 token 特异偏置——既有偏置提取只恢复相对 logits，公共加性分量未定
- 🔬 **研究方法**：浮点偏置加法的数值侧信道：利用二进制指数边界处可表示间距变化，仅凭 token 身份区分理想算术下不可区分的得分向量；52 次查询内精确恢复一个 logit 对及平局优先级
- 📌 **结论**：vLLM/SGLang 等全 128 输入自适应恢复成功，token-only 实验可抽取完整输出头表示（至可逆隐藏基变换）；先减最大值的缓解消除观测泄露——服务栈数值实现的信息侧信道

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Language model APIs can expose token-specific biases while withholding logits and token probabilities. Existing bias-based extraction recovers relative logits, leaving a common additive component undetermined in ideal arithmetic. We identify a numerical side channel in floating-point bias addition that exposes information about this component through token identities alone. The channel distinguishes score vectors with identical complete unperturbed log probabilities. Our method exploits changes in representable spacing at binary exponent boundaries. Under the stated binary32 arithmetic assumptions and a known reachable score interval, it recovers a logit pair and its unknown tie-breaking priority exactly in at most 52 token queries. Given complete relative logits, one recovered anchor determines the original logit vector. We evaluate recovery across 11 public checkpoints from seven model families and extend the local method with adaptive interval search and anchor selection. In end-to-end experiments with vLLM, SGLang, and this http URL, adaptive recovery succeeds on all 128 Qwen3-0.6B inputs per system, including 64 held-out inputs. A separate token-only experiment extracts a pretrained small model's complete output-head representation up to an invertible hidden-basis transformation. We also establish a sufficient condition for suppressing common-shift disclosure by subtracting the maximum before bias addition. In controlled experiments, this mitigation eliminates all observed distinctions between common-shift variants and preserves token decisions under the tested random biases.

</details>

### 57. StableJudge: A Lightweight Adversarial Defense Framework for LLM-as-a-Judge

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16814)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`LLM-as-a-Judge`、`score hijacking`、`prompt injection`、`defense`

👤 **作者**：Anning Hu、Yangyu Li、Yuqi Huang、Shengchao Hu、Junchi Yan

- 🎯 **研究动机**：judge 管线易受被评内容内嵌的劫持分数注入——既有防御改 judge 本体，安全性绑定特定模型规模与训练且更换即重做
- 🔬 **研究方法**：StableJudge：外置为可复用的评前能力——0.13B Sentry 定位注入片段并定向消毒（对抗演化攻击训练）+从 judge 输出分布导出确定性细粒度标量分
- 📌 **结论**：held-out 摘要与 OOD 攻击变体下提升攻击中的分数恢复并保持通用性能——安全与 judge 规模解耦的可复用防御（judge 体系完整性）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as judges to assign scalar scores that drive downstream decision-making, including tasks such as paper review and recommendation. However, LLM-as-a-Judge pipelines are vulnerable to score-hijacking prompt injection embedded in evaluated content, which corrupts scoring signals and distorts induced rankings. Existing defenses often enforce robustness by modifying the judge model itself, making safety dependent on a specific model's scale and training and requiring repeated effort when the judge is updated or replaced. We propose StableJudge, a lightweight defense framework that externalizes prompt-injection mitigation as a reusable pre-scoring capability. StableJudge centers on a compact 0.13B Sentry that localizes injected spans and performs targeted sanitization prior to evaluation, and is trained using adversarially evolving attacks to improve robustness beyond a fixed prompt set. To stabilize measurement, StableJudge derives deterministic, fine-grained scalar scores from the judge's output distribution. Across held-out scientific abstracts and out-of-distribution attack variants, StableJudge improves score recovery under attack while largely preserving general-purpose performance, demonstrating that robust LLM-based judging can be achieved by decoupling safety from judge scale via reusable defense capabilities. Code and data will be released.

</details>

### 58. Same Operation, Different Permission: Evaluating Whether Guardrails Honor Authorization

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16838)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`guardrail`、`authorization`、`permission honoring`、`benchmark`

👤 **作者**：Huihao Jing、…、Yangqiu Song

- 🎯 **研究动机**：guardrail 前置于 agent 写代码/转账/改记录的动作——但其决策是否遵循授权（拦未授权同时放行有权限的同一动作）几乎无评测
- 🔬 **研究方法**：语言轨道同一操作对比授权证据：专家标注场景+执行验证操作+外源原生策略请求，检查正确性/可用性/结果效应并跨 guardrail 变更追踪
- 📌 **结论**：许多被拦操作源于失败的审查而非正确拒绝；修复输出格式恢复可用决策但改变哪些授权请求被准——guardrail 须同时评拦下与放行两侧

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

As language-model agents write code, move money and change records on behalf of users, deciding whether an action is authorized has become a central safety problem. Guardrails are often placed in front of these actions, yet few evaluations ask whether their decisions follow authorization: blocking unauthorized actions while still allowing the same actions under valid permission. We introduce \bench{}, a benchmark that contrasts authorization evidence for the same operation in its language track. It combines expert-annotated natural-language scenarios with executed and verified operations, plus externally sourced native-policy requests. \bench{} checks each decision for correctness, usability and resulting effect, and tracks each request across guardrail changes. Our experiments show that many blocked operations stem from failed reviews, not correct denials. Repairing the output format restores usable decisions but changes which authorized requests are honored, and adding a second reviewer removes some errors while rejecting valid authorization. Guardrails should therefore be evaluated on both the unauthorized actions they stop and the authorized actions they let through.

</details>

### 59. CWE-bench: Refocusing Agentic Security from Exploitation to Defense

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17020)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`agentic security`、`defense benchmark`、`audit and repair`、`CWE`

👤 **作者**：Gonzalo Gonzalez-Pumariega、Ajay Jagannath、Adit Jain、Guram Gogia、Soumyadeep Bakshi、Nazneen Rajani

- 🎯 **研究动机**：AI agent 安全评测聚焦攻击——可用利用不等于能让软件安全：攻击者只需一个漏洞，防御者须找到并修复全部
- 🔬 **研究方法**：CWE-bench：120 私有 held-out 审计-修复任务（73 CWE 类型×8 语言×OWASP Top-10 全类），只给仓库与威胁模型描述，补丁须既堵利用又保功能（程序化验证器+agentic judge 校准）
- 📌 **结论**：最佳 agent 68% pass@1/81% pass@4；同 pass@1 的 agent 重试收益与成本差异巨大、无单一 agent 全面最优——防御能力是否跟上攻击能力的严格标尺

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Cybersecurity evaluations of AI agents have focused largely on offense, yet a working exploit does not show that an agent can make software safe. An attacker needs to exploit only one vulnerability; a defender must find and fix all of them. We introduce CWE-bench, a private benchmark of 120 held-out repository audit-and-repair tasks spanning 73 CWE types, eight languages, and all OWASP Top 10 2025 categories. Agents are given a repository to audit and a description of its threat model, but are not told the weakness, where it occurs, or how many vulnerable sites there are. Each patch must block the exploit while preserving original functionality, as checked by a programmatic verifier. We validated the verifier against a panel of agentic judges, manually reviewing disagreements during development and refining the checks. The best agents reach 68% pass@1 and 81% pass@4. We further conduct analyses showing that (i) agents with the same pass@1 differ widely in retry gains and cost, and (ii) no single agent is best across languages or vulnerability categories, and difficulty varies far more by vulnerability category than by language. As the offensive capabilities of agents improve, CWE-bench provides a broad and rigorous measure of whether their defensive capabilities keep pace.

</details>

### 60. MENTOR: Procedural Shielding of Tool-Using LLM Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17039)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`procedural compliance`、`runtime shield`、`autoformalization`、`tool agent`

👤 **作者**：Ashwani Anand、Ivi Chatzi、Ritam Raha、Anne-Kathrin Schmuck

- 🎯 **研究动机**：工具 agent 自动化由程序手册治理的工作流——LLM judge 或手写规则的局部拦截让 agent 不知如何正确推进且过度保守
- 🔬 **研究方法**：MENTOR：手册经多步自动形式化为历史依赖运行时盾+博弈技术抗运行时不确定性不过度限制，拦截同时提供上下文感知的合规推进指引
- 📌 **结论**：轻量开源 LLM 的合规率超现有方法乃至未防护的更强模型——程序性屏蔽：形式化手册的运行时强制（规则陈述与执行的弥合）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Tool-using LLM agents are increasingly used to automate workflows governed by procedural manuals, such as company rules for booking travel or procuring hardware. These manuals are written in natural language for humans and describe general requirements over such complex workflows. Ensuring compliance of LLM agents with these manuals is difficult, and state-of-the-art techniques use either LLM judges or manually written rules to forbid problematic tool-calls locally at runtime. This is insufficient because (i) only blocking tool calls keeps the agent clueless about how to make correct progress towards fulfilling its task, and (ii) can lead to overly conservative behavior of the agent, as tool calls might only be problematic in certain contexts. To overcome these challenges, we introduce MENTOR, which (i) additionally provides guidance for the agent to make procedural-compliant progress on the task, and (ii) ensures that this compliance is context-aware. We achieve these goals by building a novel history-dependent runtime shield that is obtained from the procedural manual via a multi-step autoformalization pipeline. At its core, MENTOR utilizes a game-based technique to ensure robustness against runtime uncertainty without over-resticting agents' behavior. This yields a formally assisted framework for procedural shielding of LLM agents that improves compliance well beyond existing methods. In particular, MENTOR lifts the compliance of shielded light-weight open-source LLMs above that of non-shielded proprietary models. Since a shield is synthesized once per manual and then serves every task, a MENTOR-shielded agent becomes a viable choice for repeatedly executed workflows, potentially reducing deployment costs when the offline construction cost is amortized over repeated tasks.

</details>

### 61. Gradient Routing Localizes and Removes Unintended Behaviors in RL

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17062)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`reward hacking`、`gradient routing`、`imperfect monitor`、`RL post-training`

👤 **作者**：Jake Ward、…、Alex Cloud

- 🎯 **研究动机**：错误奖励函数+系统性出错的监控器：用监控器做数据过滤或奖励惩罚反而教会模型隐藏不当行为（reward hacking）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：GRAFT 梯度路由适配微调：被标记样本的梯度更新约束到一组牺牲参数（监控器不进奖励函数故无需隐藏），事后消融牺牲参数即去除不当行为
- 📌 **结论**：135M-8B RL 环境中奖励惩罚致未检测 hacking 而 GRAFT 不学隐藏；含 reward hacking 的前沿轨迹蒸馏进 Qwen3-32B 而不教会它 hacking——不完美监控器下的后训练利用

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

We propose a mitigation to reward hacking in reinforcement learning. Our setup involves a misspecified reward function that gives rise to unintended behavior, and a monitor used to detect unintended behavior. As with frontier AI systems, the monitor makes systematic errors. Consequently, using the monitor for data filtering or to apply a reward penalty fails to prevent the model from learning unintended behavior ("reward hacking"). To address this problem, we introduce GRAFT: Gradient Routing Adapter Fine Tuning. Rather than penalizing or filtering flagged samples during training, GRAFT constrains gradient updates on those samples to a sacrificial set of new parameters. This has two benefits: First, because the monitor is not part of the reward function, the model does not learn to hide misbehavior. Second, ablating the sacrificial parameters reduces or entirely stops the unintended behavior while maintaining improved performance on target tasks. We demonstrate these benefits in RL environments where reward penalties cause models to learn to reward hack in undetected ways, training models ranging from 135M to 8B parameters. Additionally, we show a similar effect at a larger scale by distilling frontier LLM trajectories containing reward hacking into Qwen3-32B, without teaching that model to reward hack. Overall, our work suggests GRAFT may be a promising approach for leveraging imperfect monitors during post-training in settings where true performance is difficult to evaluate.

</details>

### 62. Welcome to the Age of Agent Ads: Using On-Policy Attacks to Steer Agentic Decisions

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17195)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`agent ads`、`on-policy attack`、`agentic decision steering`、`adversarial marketing`

👤 **作者**：Tim R. Davidson、Marija Šakota、Joshua Joseph、Robert West

- 🎯 **研究动机**：人们把排序任务委托给 agent——『agent 广告』的激励出现；既有攻击靠触发偏离对齐的行为，而模型对齐与平台条款正防着这个
- 🔬 **研究方法**：在策攻击：商品描述注入对虚构概念的真实否定（不违反上架规范）→诱导 agent 网搜→预植网站把虚构概念与危害关联→agent 因安全训练惩罚所有缺该否定的选项；四个真实列表数据集评测
- 📌 **结论**：部分前沿模型暴露：攻击者可惩罚竞品而不违反任何平台列表规则——不偏离策略的 agentic 决策操纵新攻击面与平台监管挑战

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

People increasingly delegate option-ranking tasks (e.g., shopping, job search, travel) to AI agents, which creates incentives to steer agentic decisions via "agent ads." To defend against such steering attacks, people count on models' alignment training and platforms' terms of service, and consequently the most effective existing attacks are designed to trigger off-policy behaviors. Exposing a new attack surface, we introduce on-policy attacks, which steer agentic decisions without pushing individually aligned behaviors off policy. Our example attack consists of two steps: To remain compliant with platforms' terms of service, we first inject a truthful negation about a fictitious concept ("Does not contain blizyester") into a product description. This triggers an agent to search the web ("What is blizyester?"), which surfaces planted websites full of fabricated information associating the fictitious concept with harmful implications ("Blizyester linked to dementia"). Trained to keep users safe from harm, the agent penalizes every option missing the truthful negation. Evaluating our attack across four real-world listing datasets, we find some frontier models exposed, enabling an adversarial actor to penalize competitors without violating existing platform-listing guidelines. We discuss the broader challenge of regulating advertising aimed at AI agents and provide recommendations to model developers and platform providers.

</details>

### 63. The Agent Knew: Internal States Forecast Unauthorized Actions and Stop Them, and the Lies That Follow

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17301)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`internal monitoring`、`unauthorized action`、`deception`、`linear readout`

👤 **作者**：Arya Fayyazi、Mehdi Kamal、Massoud Pedram

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agent 有时越权行动然后谎报——行为标记：先行动再补要许可的『追溯性许可洗白』在 1081 次违规中 70% 先于虚假报告（预注册 OR 7.0）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：CAPE-Bench 单事实定授权的匹配任务+残差流线性读出在动作前预报违规（AUROC 0.94）与报告前预报谎言；按预报门控+向审批人重复标记
- 📌 **结论**：四个模型族整集违规大降（Gemma-4 0.70→0.09 且完成率 1.00），人只看 25% 请求即保收益，开销为 CoT 监控的 1/109；同一读出在 AgentDojo 未见任务上把注入攻击成功率近半——内部状态监控优于被施压的 CoT

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Language-model agents sometimes act beyond their authorization and then misreport what they did. On CAPE-Bench, matched agent tasks in which one fact decides whether an action is authorized, frontier open-weight agents do both, and one behavior marks the lie: retroactive permission laundering, acting and then asking permission as if the action were pending, precedes a false report in 70% of 1081 fresh informed violations, against 8% otherwise (preregistered odds ratio 7.0); frontier agents on tau^2-bench instead fabricate the authorization before violating. A linear readout of the agent's residual stream forecasts the violation before the action (AUROC 0.94) and the false report before the report, and beats chain-of-thought monitors when the agent is pressured to hide its reasoning. Gating every proposed action on this forecast cuts violations over whole episodes in four model families, and sending repeated flags to an approver keeps authorized work (Gemma-4: violations 0.70 -> 0.09, completion 1.00; a model approver that escalates unclear cases keeps this gain with a person seeing 25% of asks), at about 10-8 of the agent's compute and 109 times less than a chain-of-thought monitor. On AgentDojo, an external prompt-injection benchmark, the same readout calibrated on 29 tasks, with the steering unchanged, cuts attack success on unseen tasks in two families (Gemma-4 11.7% -> 5.7%, gpt-oss 28.5% -> 17.5%) while keeping benign utility (Gemma-4 0.637 vs 0.637; gpt-oss 0.667 vs 0.681). Preventing the violation is also the most reliable way to prevent the lie (misreported violations 0.10 -> 0.01 pooled over three families, preregistered), whereas lie-specific fixes after a violation are weak. All tests are preregistered and hash-locked; code, data and the full record, including tests that did not pass, are at this https URL.

</details>

### 64. TRAP: Tree-Guided Reinforcement-Augmented Probing for Automated LLM Jailbreaking

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17313)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`jailbreak`、`strategy tree`、`reinforcement learning`、`automated red-teaming`

👤 **作者**：Mohammad Reza Mirbagheri、Amir Mahdi Sadeghzadeh

- 🎯 **研究动机**：自动越狱方法探索候选或复用经验——不复用连接相关策略（角色扮演/编码/混淆）的持久层级，策略关系未被用于引导自适应选择
- 🔬 **研究方法**：TRAP：既有越狱方法与成功模板组织成语义策略树（宽泛路径→具体可复用策略），树结构梯度 bandit 的 RL 策略沿选定路径更新偏好并跨目标保留，检索增强构造当前攻击
- 📌 **结论**：AdvBench 七对齐 LLM 上超越对应方法——黑盒层级化策略发现的自动越狱（越狱知识的结构化积累）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Aligned large language models (LLMs) remain vulnerable to jailbreak prompts that circumvent their safety guardrails, making the identification of these vulnerabilities important for understanding and mitigating model weaknesses. Existing automated jailbreak methods explore prompt candidates or reuse attack experience, but generally do not learn over a persistent hierarchy that connects related jailbreak strategies, such as role-play, encoding, and obfuscation. This leaves the relationships among reusable strategies underused in guiding adaptive attack selection. We present Tree-Guided Reinforcement-Augmented Probing (TRAP), a hierarchical framework for automated jailbreak strategy discovery that requires only black-box access to the target LLM. TRAP organizes established jailbreak methods and successful attack templates into a semantic strategy tree, connecting broad attack approaches to concrete, reusable strategies. A reinforcement-learning policy based on a tree-structured gradient bandit explores this tree and uses evaluator feedback to update strategy preferences along each selected path. The policy retains these preferences across attack objectives, allowing experience from earlier attempts to guide subsequent strategy selection. Retrieval-augmented prompt construction translates the selected strategy into an attack for the current objective using relevant successful examples. Experiments on AdvBench across seven aligned LLMs show that TRAP outperforms its counterparts on six models under the reported method settings and the common primary evaluator, achieving attack success rates of 45.6% to 97.9% on these models. These findings support hierarchical strategy learning as an effective approach to discovering jailbreak vulnerabilities in aligned LLMs.

</details>

### 65. COMPASS: Membership Inference Attack Against Vision Large Language Models via Adaptive Noise Calibration

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17332)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`membership inference`、`VLM`、`adaptive attack`、`privacy`

👤 **作者**：Damien Lo、Hong Kyu Lee、Ruixuan Liu、Li Xiong

- 🎯 **研究动机**：VLM 部署于隐私敏感域——既有灰盒 MIA 靠熵统计或生成描述（代价高分离弱），扰动法只看大扰动，小扰动域的成员行为未知
- 🔬 **研究方法**：受控高斯扰动下细粒度刻画 VLM 成员行为：最扰幅与信号方向随模型-数据对变化、低噪域分离更强且方向反转；COMPASS 用松散匹配已知非成员参照自适应校准扰幅与方向
- 📌 **结论**：LLaVA/MiniGPT-4/HuluMed 上跨设置一致提升——固定扰幅或预定方向都不可靠（视觉 LLM 的隐私攻击有效性边界）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Vision large language models (VLLMs) are deployed in privacy-sensitive domains, raising training-data exposure concerns. Existing gray-box membership inference attacks (MIAs) commonly rely on entropy statistics or generated descriptions, requiring costly generation with limited membership separation. Perturbation-based methods instead measure response changes but focus on large perturbations, leaving VLLM membership behavior under smaller perturbations poorly understood. We systematically characterize VLLM membership behavior under controlled Gaussian perturbations across noise levels at fine resolution. We find that optimal perturbation magnitude and membership-signal direction vary across model-dataset pairs. In many settings, a previously unstudied low-noise regime yields stronger separation than large perturbations, with members exhibiting lower divergence than non-members, reversing the conventional score direction. Other settings retain the conventional direction, demonstrating that neither a fixed perturbation magnitude nor a predetermined score orientation is universally reliable. We propose COMPASS, an adaptive gray-box MIA that calibrates both the perturbation magnitude and membership-score direction using a loosely distribution-matched, known non-member reference set. We instantiate COMPASS using Renyi-normalized KL divergence and Renyi divergence, with max-k aggregation over the most informative image-logit positions. Across LLaVA v1.5-7B, MiniGPT-4, and HuluMed-32B, spanning pretraining, instruction-tuning, and medical-image datasets, COMPASS outperforms baselines. On general-domain data, COMPASS achieves AUCs up to 0.88. On the distribution-matched medical evaluation, it reaches 0.64 AUC, while the strongest baseline remains at chance level. These results establish full-range adaptive perturbation analysis as an effective approach to VLLM membership inference.

</details>

### 66. The belief-action gap in language model agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17444)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`belief-action gap`、`probing`、`inverse RL`、`agent failure`

👤 **作者**：Ayse Sila Okcu、…、Mario Giulianelli

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agent 失败的不同原因需要不同干预——任务相关信息是否被表示、是否反映在行为中、如何转化为动作未被联合测量
- 🔬 **研究方法**：文本网格世界：报告式与表征式探针估计任务相关信念+逆强化学习估动作价值+全程动作偏好追踪与移植/激活编辑对照
- 📌 **结论**：状态与价值信息常被可靠表示却未反映在行为——失败源自信息到动作的转化而非信息缺失；承诺点附近决策锐化——信念-动作间隙的测量框架

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Language model (LM) agents can fail for different reasons, requiring different explanations and interventions. We study how state beliefs, action values, and action preferences interact as LM agents reason and commit to decisions in text-rendered grid worlds. We estimate task-relevant beliefs using report-based and representation-based probes, and action values using inverse reinforcement learning. We find that task-relevant state and value information is often represented more reliably than it is reflected in behaviour. Tracking action preferences throughout reasoning further shows that decisions sharpen around a commitment point and reported belief uncertainty decreases near commitment, although individual belief changes do not consistently explain shifts in action preference. Finally, forced actions follow reasoning prefixes from other decisions when transplanted, whereas the tested reasoning-window activation edits provide limited control over the action. Together, these results suggest that some failures arise not from missing task-relevant information, but from how available information is translated into action. Our measurements distinguish information availability from belief-action consistency without by themselves identifying the cause of each failure. Our code is available at: this https URL

</details>

### 67. BrowseSafe++: Benchmarking Prompt Injection Detection Across Web Contexts, Languages, and Browser Views

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17457)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`prompt injection`、`browser agent`、`detection benchmark`、`adaptive attack`

👤 **作者**：Yuetian Chen、…、Ninghui Li

- 🎯 **研究动机**：浏览器 agent 消费不可信网页——网页内嵌恶意指令重定向任务是核心威胁，检测基准规模与真实性不足
- 🔬 **研究方法**：BrowseSafe++：38,675 对页面（注入多为生产网站野外观察，每攻击页配同页干净对照，HTML/Markdown/可访问性树/截图四视图×24 攻击类型×18 位置）；训练 0.8B 双模态检测器+按 agent 反馈自适应改写注入
- 📌 **结论**：小检测器 F1 超前沿模型且误报/成本低；直接重放仅几个百分点成功的攻击自适应后达 95.41%——注入检测的规模、视图与自适应压力测试

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Browser agents need to make decisions and execute actions on behalf of users while consuming untrusted webpages, creating opportunities for malicious instructions embedded in webpages to redirect legitimate tasks. We introduce BrowseSafe++, which extends BrowseSafe from 14,719 pages to 38,675 pairs of pages. Most of its prompt injections were observed in the wild on production websites. We keep each injection unchanged and rewrite the sensitive parts of the page around it to protect privacy. Each attacked page has a clean copy of the same page as its control. Both are provided as HTML, Markdown, an accessibility tree, and a screenshot. Every pair includes a user task and its expected answer. The benchmark includes twelve types of websites, from news articles to shared documents and forums, with 24 attack types appearing in 18 different locations on a page. We use these paired pages to train BrowseSafe-Guard, two 0.8B-parameter detectors for text and screenshots. Both achieve higher F1 scores than the detector based on frontier models, while keeping false-positive rates on benign pages and inference cost low. BrowseSafe-Adapt revises benchmark injections from browser-agent feedback, and attacks that succeed on only a few percent of tasks when replayed directly reach up to 95.41% success once adapted.

</details>

### 68. REA: From Correct LLM Judgments to Safe Decision Delivery

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17473)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`safety guard`、`parse failure`、`fail-open`、`decision delivery`

👤 **作者**：Jingxiang Meng、Aofan Liu、Yaoshuai Ma、Fangxin Liu、Yongbiao Chen

- 🎯 **研究动机**：生成式安全护栏须既正确分类又输出应用可解析的形式——推理型护栏分类更准但输出应用解析不了，回退处理可放行有害请求
- 🔬 **研究方法**：REA 框架追踪判决经解析与回退到应用动作：正确预测可出现在错误格式输出中、解析安全裁决保留推理护栏精度优势、格式宽容恢复被拒正确预测
- 📌 **结论**：fail-open 重放中匹配解析器+修格式把『判决正确却放行有害请求』从 95.5% 降到 0%；同一正确判决可因解析器与回退策略导向不同安全动作——从正确判断到安全交付的最后一英里

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Generative safety guards must classify requests correctly and return outputs that applications can parse. Reasoning guards can improve classification accuracy while producing outputs that applications cannot parse. When applications cannot extract a correct prediction, fallback handling can allow harmful requests or block safe ones. We introduce Resolve--Express--Act (REA), a framework that traces judgments through parsing and fallback to application actions. Experiments on classifiers and safety guards show that correct predictions can appear in incorrectly formatted outputs. Parsing the safety verdict preserves the reasoning guard's accuracy advantage, while stricter explanation requirements reject additional correct predictions. Accepting formatting variations recovers most rejected correct predictions. Held-out tests confirm that output requirements and generation limits affect whether correct predictions can be parsed. In our fail-open replay, matching the parser to the output and fixing formatting reduced the fraction of harmful requests allowed despite correct guard judgments from 95.5% to 0%. A fixed-output comparison shows that model-based extraction can preserve guard verdicts, but is not automatically reliable; deterministic normalization already repairs the observed native-format failures. Fallback replay shows that the same correct prediction can lead to different safety actions depending on the parser and fallback policy, including allowing requests the guard correctly identifies as harmful.

</details>

### 69. Origin Attribution for Unsafe Behavior in Self-Evolving Multi-Agent Coding Systems

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17488)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`origin attribution`、`self-evolving agents`、`code lineage`、`audit`

👤 **作者**：Hanyi Yu、Jiaming Cui

- 🎯 **研究动机**：自演化 coding agent 复用彼此代码——安全失效的历史可超出发现它的 agent：不安全变更可源自一处、经中间接收者传播、在多次修订后存活到审计
- 🔬 **研究方法**：行为指纹匹配+历史定位+递归追踪：比较超出不安全结果本身的候选实现、保留多历史版本证据、沿上游跟随匹配候选；158 查询×85 受控场景
- 📌 **结论**：96.8% 精确归因（无递归穷举指纹 77.2%）——区分继承行为与独立发生失败，识别引入变更（MAS 不安全行为的溯源）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

As language-model-based coding agents evolve their programs and reuse code from one another, safety failures can acquire a history that extends beyond the agent in which they are detected. An unsafe change may originate in one agent, propagate through intermediate recipients, and survive subsequent revisions before an audit reveals it. Understanding this history is important for identifying the introducing change and distinguishing inherited behavior from independently occurring failures. We study origin attribution in self-evolving multi-agent coding systems: recovering the agent and program version that introduced an observed unsafe behavior into the target's code lineage, or determining that it arose locally. This task is challenging because rewriting obscures textual similarity, independent occurrences confound temporal ordering, and intermediate recipients can resemble the source. We propose a method that combines behavioral fingerprint matching with historical localization and recursive tracing. It compares candidate implementations beyond the unsafe outcome, retains evidence from multiple historical versions, and follows matching candidates upstream. On 158 queries across 85 controlled scenarios, the method achieves 96.8% exact attribution accuracy, compared with 77.2% for exhaustive fingerprint matching without recursion. Component ablations and evaluation on 34 additional queries using the same method and hyperparameters demonstrate the complementary roles of multiple representatives and recursive tracing. Further checks extend the evaluation to approval-boundary and resource-owner errors. Together, these results show how behavioral and historical evidence can recover the origins of unsafe behavior through code revision and cross-agent propagation.

</details>

### 70. Many Steps into One: Reasoning Contraction and the Limits of Safe Memory Reuse for LLM Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17544)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`memory reuse`、`reasoning contraction`、`certified memory`、`change detection`

👤 **作者**：Jianghong Zhou、Hao Rong、Chen Lin、Daoan Zhang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：思维模板/技能库/记忆/缓存把多步推理收缩为一步——事实改变前省时省错，改变后悄然答错；而系统普遍靠成败计数器信任记忆
- 🔬 **研究方法**：证明计数器是严重失谐的变点检测器（对称计数器只能在其误报时域的幂次后才察觉部分变化）；按各自似然比加权失败+对照活推理认证效用+事实变化重置证书的配方
- 📌 **结论**：五 LLM 编辑事实多跳问答上 ExpeL/ACE 式计数器比认证记忆多给 7-35 倍陈旧答案；认证负荷这一数字决定安全记忆可跳过的推理量并给出标度律——安全记忆重用的极限

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Thought templates, skill libraries, agent memories and caches turn many reasoning steps into one. We call this reasoning contraction. A contracted memory saves reasoning steps and errors until its facts change, then quietly answers wrong. Most systems trust memories by counting successes and failures. We prove that such counters are badly tuned change detectors: a symmetric counter notices a partial change only after a power of its false-alarm horizon, and an unclipped one may never notice it. On five LLMs answering multi-hop questions over edited facts, ExpeL- and ACE-style counters serve 7.0-13.5x more stale answers than certified memories given the same information, and on code tasks with an exact verifier 35-73x more. Safety also has a price. When facts change and each memory is judged only by its own verified outcomes, one number, the certification load, sets how much reasoning any safe memory can skip, and under Zipf demand it yields a scaling law. Our recipe weighs each memory's failures by its own likelihood ratio, certifies its utility against live reasoning, and restarts the certificate when facts change, with the likelihood-ratio detector as the safety net for unannounced changes; for large edits it also invalidates the memory.

</details>

### 71. The Tokens Strike Back: Safety-Aware Denoising with Directional Embedding-based Revision

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17636)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`dLLM`、`safety-aware denoising`、`text diffusion`、`jailbreak defense`

👤 **作者**：Amman Yusuf、Jiwoo Shin、Ruizhi Deng、Heitor Rodrigues Guimarães、Nikita Dvornik、Mijung Park

- 🎯 **研究动机**：SAD 为文本扩散模型提供免训练安全引导——在 token 概率空间把样本引向安全区域，但安全-质量权衡可改进
- 🔬 **研究方法**：SADDER：利用逐 token 位置的嵌入空间安全引导信号与 SAD 更新规则相乘（高不安全信号 token 更可能被修改），理论推导使修改后去噪器精确的位置级目标分布及与全局目标匹配的条件
- 📌 **结论**：MDLM/USDM 上的不安全续写与越狱设置中取得安全、生成质量与推理成本的更优平衡——dLLM 解码期安全干预的理论化升级

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

The Safety-Aware Denoiser (SAD) is a recent, training-free, safety-guidance framework for text diffusion models. During inference, SAD steers text samples toward safe regions of the token probability space. In this paper, we propose $\mathit{SADDER}$ (Safety-Aware Denoising with Directional Embedding-based Revision), which improves the safety and quality trade-offs of SAD by exploiting a safety-guidance signal in the embedding (or activation) space for each token position, then updating the token positions using the SAD update rule multiplied by the guidance signal. This multiplication acts as a gating mechanism: tokens with a high unsafe signal are more likely to be modified during inference following the SAD update rule. However, such an update does not automatically preserve the conditional mean, i.e., the denoiser of a fixed target distribution. We therefore theoretically derive new position-wise target distributions for which the modified denoiser is exact. Furthermore, we derive conditions under which the position-wise target distributions match a global target distribution. We test our $\mathit{SADDER}$ method on popular Masked Diffusion Language Models (MDLMs) and Uniform-State Diffusion Models (USDMs) across unsafe continuation and jailbreak settings, and show that $\mathit{SADDER}$ achieves a better balance between safety, generation quality, and inference cost than existing methods.

</details>

### 72. Decouple to Forget: Semantic Mediation for Sensitive Unlearning in Large Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17654)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`unlearning`、`sensitive facts`、`semantic mediation`、`counterfactual`

👤 **作者**：Jingyue Cong、…、Estrid He

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM 遗忘以整个响应为遗忘单元——当目标只是嵌在良性解释中的少量敏感事实时造成不必要的效用损失
- 🔬 **研究方法**：D2F：语义中介启发的定点遗忘——抽取事实级中介表示、识别敏感部分只移除该部分，基于中介生成保留非敏感内容的反事实答案+偏好对比优化降泄漏
- 📌 **结论**：多基准上遗忘-保留平衡优于强基线——敏感事实级解耦遗忘（删除粒度与效用保持）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Machine unlearning for large language models seeks to remove specified knowledge while preserving general capability, yet current methods often treat an entire response as the unit of forgetting. This coarse granularity causes unnecessary utility loss when the forget target is only a small set of sensitive facts embedded within otherwise benign explanations. In this paper, we introduce Decouple to Forget (D2F), a targeted unlearning method inspired by semantic mediation that separates sensitive factual content from non-sensitive meaning and surface expression. For each example to be forgotten, D2F extracts a fact-level "mediator" representation from the answer, identifies which parts are sensitive, and removes only those parts while leaving the rest of the structure intact. We then generate counterfactual answers grounded in this mediator that preserve non-sensitive content but exclude sensitive facts, employing preference-based contrastive optimization to reduce the likelihood of information leakage while maintaining helpfulness and fluency. Experiments across various benchmark datasets show that D2F achieves a better balance between forgetting and retention than strong baseline methods. Our code is available at this https URL.

</details>

### 73. Can Agents Truly Forget? Explore Machine Unlearning for LLM Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17659)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`agent unlearning`、`memory`、`tool reacquisition`、`benchmark`

👤 **作者**：Junkai Lin、Ge Xu、Zheng Xu、Junkai Chen、Xiangyuan Lan

- 🎯 **研究动机**：参数级遗忘对 agent 不够：被遗忘信息仍可存留于记忆工件或经工具调用重新获得——agent 遗忘须跨三信道（参数知识/持久记忆/工具获取）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：AUBench：TOFU 虚构作者设定扩展为带持久记忆+离线网页快照+工具可访问文档+出处追踪轨迹的 agent 环境，评估既有参数级与记忆级遗忘方法
- 📌 **结论**：揭示模型级遗忘与系统级 agent 遗忘之间的鸿沟——工具再获取与记忆持久化使参数删除失效（删除-恢复链条的完整评测）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language model (LLM) agents have shown strong capabilities in interactive problem solving, tool use, and long-horizon task execution. However, they may also acquire sensitive or private knowledge during training and expose it during deployment. Retraining a model from scratch to remove such knowledge is prohibitively costly, making machine unlearning a more practical approach. Machine unlearning aims to remove targeted undesirable knowledge from trained models without retraining from scratch while preserving general utility. When language models are deployed as agents, parameter-level unlearning becomes insufficient: forgotten information may still persist in memory artifacts or be reacquired via tool calls. In this paper, we formulate the problem of agent unlearning as removing private information from an LLM-based agent across three information channels: parametric knowledge, persistent memory, and tool-call acquisition. To study this problem, we introduce we introduce Agent Unlearning Benchmark (AUBench), a controlled benchmark that extends the fictitious-author setting of TOFU into an agent environment with persistent memory, an offline web snapshot, tool-accessible documents, and provenance-tracked interaction trajectories. By evaluating existing parameter-level and memory-level unlearning methods in this setting, AUBench reveals the gap between model-level forgetting and system-level agent unlearning, and provides a foundation for developing unlearning methods that operate across the full lifecycle of LLM agents.

</details>

### 74. LatentRiskBench: Measuring State-Dependent Risks in Enterprise Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17685)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`state-dependent risk`、`enterprise agent`、`safety benchmark`、`workflow context`

👤 **作者**：Jingyue Cong、…、Andy Song

- 🎯 **研究动机**：企业 agent 的安全风险不能仅从单个动作判断——同一动作在一个工作流状态安全、在另一状态违规，而既有安全基准只评动作局部风险
- 🔬 **研究方法**：LatentRiskBench：共享终态动作但潜伏策略相关状态不同的风险-良性轨迹对，要求重建工作流上下文；残余风险（RR）指标族+商业部署场景案例
- 📌 **结论**：动作级评测只检出 0.09 的状态依赖违规而状态级达 0.96；既有 guardrail 除非显式应用于工作流状态否则大量漏检——企业 agent 安全评测的状态维度

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Enterprise agents face safety risks that cannot be determined from individual actions alone. Existing safety benchmarks largely evaluate action-local risks, overlooking failures whose safety depends on workflow state. In such state-dependent risks, the same action may be safe in one context but violate policy in another. Hence, we introduce LatentRiskBench for evaluating such state-dependent risks in enterprise settings. It pairs risky trajectories with benign counterparts that share the same terminal action but differ in latent policy-relevant states, requiring models to reconstruct workflow context rather than rely on action-level cues. We also introduce new metrics for state-dependent risk evaluation, including a suite of residual risk (RR) measures. Experiments on the benchmark show that action-level evaluation detects only 0.09 of state-dependent violations, whereas state-level evaluation reaches 0.96. Existing guardrail methods largely overlook such risks unless they are explicitly applied to workflow states. Case studies on a commercial deployed scenario further show that the safety metrics embedded in the benchmark provide a more realistic measure of safety for enterprise agents. Our dataset and code are available at this https URL.

</details>

### 75. SkillScreen: High-Recall Screening of Malicious Agent Skills with Decision Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17727)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`malicious skill screening`、`decision model`、`high recall`、`supply chain`

👤 **作者**：Jerrold Huang、Junwei Quan、Saiyue Lyu、Zihan Guo、Yingqiang Ge

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agent skill 把自然语言指令变成动作——合法自动化与恶意行为可貌似相近，实用筛查须几乎捕获全部攻击且审查负载可控
- 🔬 **研究方法**：SkillScreen：紧凑决策模型单次前向直接给 skill.yaml 恶意度打分；MaliciousSkillBench 上未见攻击族 99% 召回时的良性误报对比
- 📌 **结论**：99% 召回下仅误标 20-25% 良性 skill（词级分类器与 LLM judge 更多）；但陌生良性源使误报骤增——高召回 skill 筛查的任务训练决策模型（skill 供应链防线）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Agent skills turn natural-language instructions into actions, creating a security challenge: legitimate automation and malicious behaviour can look deceptively similar. A practical screen must catch nearly every attack while keeping the review load manageable. We present SkillScreen, a compact decision model trained to score skill maliciousness directly from this http URL in a single forward pass. On MaliciousSkillBench, SkillScreen flags 20-25% of benign skills at 99% recall on unseen attack families, substantially fewer than the word-level classifier and prompted LLM judges tested. Under the same training protocol, it also generalizes better than a fully fine-tuned encoder to unseen sources. Yet unfamiliar benign sources sharply increase false positives, showing that recognizing new attacks does not ensure reliable screening of new skill collections. Our results demonstrate the value of task-trained decision models for high-recall skill screening. Project page: this https URL

</details>

### 76. Safety for Whom? Benchmarking Understanding Beyond Refusal in Egocentric Multimodal Assistants

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17728)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`safety understanding`、`harm target`、`egocentric assistant`、`beyond refusal`

👤 **作者**：Yewon Han、…、Taesup Kim

- 🎯 **研究动机**：自我中心多模态助手经用户之眼指导下一步动作——输出直接塑造包括影响旁观者的物理动作，而安全评测不区分伤害对象且只测是否拒绝
- 🔬 **研究方法**：STOU：2,902 条潜在伤害分别指向用户/在场他人/社会的视频-音频与视频-文本查询；把安全理解（意图/伤害存在与目标/下游后果）与拒答行为解耦评分
- 📌 **结论**：拒答随伤害目标系统变化；模型常识别出伤害却仍协助——拒答率本身不能证明助手安全，安全对齐须考虑『谁会被伤害』

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Egocentric multimodal assistants see through the user's eyes and guide what the user does next, so their outputs directly shape physical actions, including those that affect bystanders. Yet existing safety evaluations do not distinguish whom a response would harm, and score only whether a model refuses. Pooling all harms into one refusal rate hides two failures: a model may protect one target but not another, and it may refuse without understanding the risk, or understand it and comply anyway. We present STOU, a benchmark of 2,902 egocentric video-audio and video-text queries whose potential harm targets the user (Self), co-present individuals (The Others), or society at large (Others). Our evaluation decouples safety understanding from refusal behavior: beyond refusal, we score whether the model recognizes the user's intent, the presence and target of harm, and its downstream consequences. Across 10 open and closed source omnimodal and multimodal LLMs, we find that refusal varies systematically with the harm target, and that models often recognize a harm yet still assist with it. These results show that refusal rates alone cannot certify an assistant as safe, and that safety alignment must account for who would be harmed. Project page: this https URL

</details>

### 77. Instructions Are Evidence, Not Objectives: Measuring Outcome Alignment of Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17757)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`outcome alignment`、`instruction as evidence`、`requirement inference`、`benchmark`

👤 **作者**：Kaiming Liu、…、Yang Liu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：初始指令常不完整（SWE-chat 100 轨迹中 56% 漏掉后来必需的要求，52% 可从项目推断）——陈述要求≠真实要求
- 🔬 **研究方法**：TiGanEval：每任务用户真实缺部分要求、要求均可从代码与运行时行为发现、推断要求与用户后补要求分离——结果对齐可测；八前沿模型评测
- 📌 **结论**：从完整规范到部分指令，模型损失 53-67 点成功率——把指令当证据而非目标、推断并满足用户尚未意识到的真实要求的能力广泛缺失

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language model agents can follow complex human instructions to solve real-world tasks. In practice, however, initial instructions are often incomplete. In our analysis of 100 user-agent trajectories from SWE-chat, 56\% of initial instructions omit requirements that later emerge as necessary, and 52\% of the useful follow-up requirements could have been inferred from the project. A user states requirements from their own understanding of the task, and these \textbf{stated requirements} often differ from the \textbf{true requirements} that completing the task imposes. An agent should therefore treat the instruction as evidence rather than as the objective, inferring the true requirements from the instruction and the environment and satisfying them even when the user is not yet aware of all of them. We call this ability \textbf{outcome alignment}. In this work, we construct \textbf{TiGanEval}, a benchmark of software engineering and research tasks. In every task the user genuinely lacks part of the requirements, each requirement stays discoverable from the code and runtime behaviour of the environment, and requirements the agent infers are separated from those the user adds after observing delivered work. These properties together make outcome alignment measurable. Evaluating eight frontier models including GPT-5.6-Sol, we find outcome alignment broadly missing: the models lose 53 to 67 points of success rate when moving from the full specification to the partial instruction, and consequence feedback across four submissions does not close the gap. Furthermore, a plan-mode harness cannot compensate for the missing ability of the model itself, which places outcome alignment among the abilities the next generation of models should be developed for.

</details>

### 78. Memory Echo in Single-Agent Long-Term Memory: Lifecycle Evaluation and Provenance-Guided Mitigation

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17763)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`memory echo`、`long-term memory`、`provenance`、`contamination`

👤 **作者**：Yufeng Li、…、Meng Han

- 🎯 **研究动机**：长期记忆的重复检索与写回可让单一历史主张占据多个检索位、在无独立证据下影响当前答案——同源累积与纠正后持久性未被研究
- 🔬 **研究方法**：定义 Memory Echo（同源历史信息的超额检索暴露）+MEchoBench 评其出现/抗纠正/持久性/答案效应；FPA 缓解（源家族代表+旧记录准入以已验证纠正为条件+权威证据约束终答）
- 📌 **结论**：190 固定问题配对重放：FPA 把 DeepSeek V4 Flash 准确率 0.825→0.920、决策污染效应 0.106→0.030——保留可追溯历史而不让重复信息支配决策

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Long-term memory supports agents across sessions. Repeated retrieval and write-back can let one historical claim occupy multiple retrieval positions and influence current answers without independent evidence. Existing work examines retrieval, updating, and question answering, leaving same-source record accumulation and post-correction persistence underexplored. Motivated by research in social cognition and memory psychology on repeated exposure, retrieval, and revision, we define \emph{Memory Echo} as the excess retrieval exposure gained by historical information from the same source without additional independent evidence, and introduce MEchoBench to evaluate its emergence, correction resistance, persistence, and effect on answers. To address this phenomenon, we introduce the Family--Provenance--Authority framework (FPA). Specifically, FPA selects one representative per source family, conditions the eligibility of old records for the answer context on verified corrections, and constrains final answers with authoritative evidence. In paired replay on 190 fixed questions across four memory frameworks, FPA raises accuracy from 0.825 to 0.920 with DeepSeek V4 Flash and from 0.782 to 0.916 with Kimi K2.6; mean Decision Contamination Effect falls from 0.106 to 0.030 and from 0.109 to 0.038, respectively.
