These results show that agent memory can preserve traceable history without allowing repeated historical information to govern current decisions. The code is available at this https URL.

</details>

### 79. Same Attack, Different Judge: A First Controlled Adversarial Comparison of Jev, LLM Judges, and Purpose-Built Safety Classifiers

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17820)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`safety judge`、`adversarial comparison`、`Jev`、`controlled evaluation`

👤 **作者**：Xiang Li、Aichun Huang、Mingming Zhang、Zheli Liu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：TypeSafe AI 的 Jev 是新型安全 judge（不生成文本/标签，只答预定义问题返回概率）——厂商承认对抗输入可改变答案但无数字
- 🔬 **研究方法**：首个受控对抗评测：同一载荷同一策略下四任务（输入审核/注入检测/路由/钓鱼判断）×50 攻击变体×13 族，对标五个 GPT/GLM judge 与 Llama Guard 3/Prompt Guard 2
- 📌 **结论**：Jev 在规则清晰任务上最鲁棒（八种伪造零穿透）但钓鱼检测 62% vs GLM 77-81%、良性误标 27% 最高；训练例语句使 Llama Guard 3 漏 62% 已标钓鱼——不同类安全 judge 的攻击面对比

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

TypeSafe AI's Jev is a new kind of safety judge. It generates no text and no label: it answers questions the developer defines in advance and returns a probability for each answer. The vendor admits that adversarial input can change these answers but gives no numbers. In this paper, we present the first controlled adversarial evaluation of Jev. We build a test harness that sends every system the same payload under the same policy, and run it on four public-data tasks, from rule-based ones (input moderation, injection detection, routing) to a judgment-based one (phishing detection), with fifty attack variants from thirteen families. The targets are Jev, five GPT and GLM judges, and two local classifiers (Llama Guard 3, Prompt Guard 2). We find that Jev is the most robust system on input moderation: eight forgeries of its structured input bypass none of its decisions, and a sentence asking only why someone might make a harmful request bypasses 9% of its blocks, against 56% for GPT-6 Astra. On phishing detection. However, the same forgeries bypass 19-29% of the emails it flags, and even without attack Jev detects only 62% of phishing emails, against 77-81% for the GLM judges. Jev is also the fastest judge, but it flags the most benign requests (27%). The local classifiers fail differently: a training-example statement makes Llama Guard 3 miss 62% of the phishing it had flagged. These results suggest that Jev resists attacks on tasks with a clear rule to check but is more exposed on tasks that call for an overall judgment.

</details>

### 80. Checking the Checkers: A Synthesis of Verifier Noise and Robustness in RLVR

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17824)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`verifier noise`、`RLVR`、`synthesis`、`reward robustness`

👤 **作者**：Savyasachi Gupta、Aniruddha Srinivas Joshi

- 🎯 **研究动机**：RLVR 建立在『打分的 checker 自身正确』的假设上——实践中规则 checker 拒绝格式正确的答案、单测接受错误程序、LLM judge 可被诱导批准空响应
- 🔬 **研究方法**：58 项测量/分析/缓解 verifier 错误研究的综合：错误类型、训练影响、缓解-错误匹配、向自生成/评分表/judge/无 verifier 奖励的延伸
- 📌 **结论**：错误结构比错误率更重要：随机错主要拖慢学习，而策略可学会触发的假阳性可致平台期或崩溃（低初始率亦然）；缓解多只对单一错误测试过——比较表+报告协议

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become a principal recipe for improving the reasoning of large language models (LLMs). It rests on the assumption that the checker scoring each response is itself correct. In practice, rule-based checkers reject correct answers in unexpected formats, unit tests accept incorrect programs, and LLM judges can be led to approve empty or adversarial responses. This work surveys 58 studies that measure, analyze, or mitigate such verifier errors. We ask what errors verifiers make, how they affect training, which mitigations work against which errors, and how the findings extend to self-generated, rubric- and judge-based, and verifier-free rewards. The evidence suggests that the structure of an error matters more than its rate. Random errors mostly slow learning, though under a fixed budget or KL regularization they also lower final accuracy. False positives that the policy can learn to trigger can cause plateaus or collapse, sometimes even with a low initial error rate. Whether a verifier wrongly rejects or accepts depends on what it checks and who built it, not on its family. Its errors shift as the policy improves, and most mitigations have been tested against only one kind of error. We compile the findings into comparison tables, reconcile apparent conflicts in the literature, and propose a reporting protocol to make future results comparable.

</details>

### 81. ProtoArena: Can AI Agents Discover and Exploit Real-World Cross-Component Protocol Vulnerabilities?

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17837)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`protocol vulnerability`、`AI-for-cyber`、`cross-component`、`benchmark`

👤 **作者**：Fasheng Miao、…、Jilong Wang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM agent 漏洞分析基准对依赖协议组件间交互的利用覆盖有限——此类漏洞源于消息解释/时序/状态/信任假设的不一致
- 🔬 **研究方法**：ProtoArena：80 个跨组件漏洞利用环境（11 协议域×7 攻击类，主要来自已发表协议攻击机制），黑盒 scoped 接口+三级信息水平+规则与 LLM 混合验证器（独立采集流量与日志）
- 📌 **结论**：Claude Opus 4.8 在 L1 指导下 48.8%、纯黑盒 L3 仅 12.5%；DNSBomb 优化实验峰值放大比超 171,000×——可验证跨组件利用能力与黑盒极限

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly used for vulnerability analysis, yet existing benchmarks provide limited coverage of exploitation that depends on interactions among network protocol components. Such vulnerabilities arise from inconsistencies in message interpretation, timing, state, or trust assumptions across participants. We introduce ProtoArena, a black-box, multi-component, dynamic benchmark with 80 cross-component vulnerability-exploitation environments spanning 11 protocol and application domains and 7 attack categories. Tasks are primarily informed by published protocol-attack mechanisms. Agents interact with running systems through scoped interfaces to achieve security-effect objectives without target source code. Three information levels assess guided reproduction, exploitation with mechanism and topology context, and effect-directed black-box exploration. A hybrid verifier combines rule-based checks with LLM review of independently collected traffic and logs. We evaluate five LLMs. Claude Opus 4.8 achieves the highest observed success rates: 48.8% under L1 guidance and 12.5% under effect-directed black-box L3. In a separate DNSBomb optimization experiment, it achieves a peak downstream-response to average query-input byte-rate ratio exceeding 171,000 times. The results show that agents can achieve verifiable exploitation effects in some cross-component environments, but success with limited black-box information remains low and varies across domains and attack categories. All experiments use controlled, explicitly authorized targets; they do not test production systems or disclose previously unknown vulnerabilities.

</details>

### 82. Cross-Lingual Transfer of Evil Vectors Is Directionally Asymmetric

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17880)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`evil vector`、`cross-lingual`、`persona steering`、`low-resource`

👤 **作者**：Juann Kim、Minku Kang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：evil persona 向量与有害行为/越狱脆弱性/有害知识遗忘密切相关——它是否真为跨语言概念而非提取语言伪影未被检验
- 🔬 **研究方法**：两高资源（英/西）×两低资源（斯瓦希里/泰卢固）语言×四开源指令 LLM 的系统迁移研究+几何分析
- 📌 **结论**：低资源语言难可靠提取且所得向量本语言内也无效；高资源提取的向量有效迁移到低资源且保留 MMLU 知识、两高资源向量紧密对齐——共享跨语言 evil 方向的存在与不对称迁移（跨语言安全几何）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Persona vectors are linear directions in the residual-stream activations of LLMs that encode behavioral traits and enable fine-grained control over model behavior. Among them, the evil persona vector is particularly relevant to understanding harmful behavior, jailbreak vulnerabilities, and the unlearning of harmful knowledge. Yet whether this vector reflects a genuinely cross-lingual concept, rather than an artifact of its extraction language, remains untested. We conduct a systematic evil-vector transfer study across two high-resource languages (English, Spanish) and two low-resource languages (Swahili, Telugu) on four open-source instruction-tuned LLMs, and find that transfer is directionally asymmetric. Evil vectors are harder to obtain reliably from low-resource languages, and the resulting vectors are largely ineffective at steering, even within their own language. In contrast, vectors extracted from high-resource languages transfer effectively to low-resource inputs while largely preserving general knowledge, as measured by MMLU. Geometric analysis further supports this pattern: evil vectors from the two high-resource languages are closely aligned, whereas low-resource vectors align only weakly with this shared direction. These results suggest that a shared cross-lingual evil direction exists and, once reliably extracted from a high-resource language, can be reused to steer behavior in low-resource languages without additional per-language data collection.

</details>

### 83. SpecCloak: Probing Harmful Code Generation through Engineering Specifications

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17907)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`harmful code generation`、`specification prompting`、`refusal bypass`、`red-teaming`

👤 **作者**：Wenbin Chen、Jiexin Wang、Guanting Wu、Jiaxi Liang、Yi Cai

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM 代码能力可被滥用于实现有害功能——需求陈述的形式是否影响恶意代码生成的拒答未知
- 🔬 **研究方法**：PseudoSpec 工程规范模板的受控比较+表示分析与激活干预；SpecCloak 黑盒红队（规范提示+判别器引导优化）与 SpecReveal 防御原型
- 📌 **结论**：删除风险关键词只部分降拒答而规范表述大幅降低（关键词保留亦然）；八个目标模型 RedCode-Gen 平均执行验证 ASR 28.91%——需求表述形式成为恶意代码安全的新操纵面

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

The code generation capabilities of large language models (LLMs) can equally be misused to implement harmful functionality, underscoring the need to understand their safety behavior in response to programming requests. While request phrasing is known to influence refusal, it remains unclear whether the form in which coding task requirements are stated affects the refusal process for malicious code generation. We introduce PseudoSpec, a template that organizes functional requirements as engineering specifications, and investigate its effects through controlled comparisons, representation analysis, and activation interventions. Across five open-weight models, removing explicit risk keywords only partially reduces refusal, whereas specification prompting substantially lowers refusal even when these keywords are retained. This reduction accompanies consistent shifts toward code-output-related representations, and interventions along condition-difference directions alter refusal behavior. Output analysis further distinguishes code-related responses from generation degeneration. Building on these findings, we propose SpecCloak, a black-box red-teaming framework combining PseudoSpec with discriminator-guided optimization, achieving an average execution-verified attack success rate of 28.91\% across eight target models on RedCode-Gen. We also introduce SpecReveal, a defense prototype combining natural-language intent reconstruction with dual-view detection without target-model fine-tuning. It achieves a 96.88\% attack detection rate in a separate prompt-detection evaluation, with false positives assessed on benign programming and specification-style inputs. These results connect specification-induced response differences to practical security consequences and identify intent reconstruction as a complementary detection approach.

</details>

### 84. Standing, Not Truth: Who May Say What About Whom in Agentic Selection

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17952)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`agentic selection`、`standing`、`competitive claims`、`gate`

👤 **作者**：Yedidel Louck、Amit Dvir、Ariel Stulman

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agent 代用户在竞品中选择——合法参与方以自身身份发布、不攻陷信道、不下指令，仅一条关于竞品的虚假事实主张即可移动选择
- 🔬 **研究方法**：十二个已发表防御（三检查位置）均无法区分它与营销；提出 standing 关系：主张者绑定身份是否有权就主张对象发言（目录把发言者绑定到每条目再丢弃）
- 📌 **结论**：披露式重渲染后攻击仍 72/72；standing 门拦下 130/131 落地攻击、348 诚实选择零扰动、自然流量误标 0.9%——『谁可以说关于谁』而非『真不真』的可判定检查

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Software agents increasingly choose on a user's behalf among competing offerings described by the parties who benefit. A lawful participant, publishing under its own identity, compromising no channel and issuing no instruction, can move that choice with a single false factual claim about a competing offering. Across twelve published defenses spanning the three placements a check can occupy, none separates it from marketing. Each accepts it for a reason correct on its own: its origin is a lawful channel, its influence is evidence meant to decide, its action serves the user's goal. None establishes that the publisher was entitled to speak about a competitor, each exclusion stated by the defenses' own authors. Disclosure does not replace enforcement: re-rendering every description as attributed speech leaves the attack at 72/72, the agent repeating the attribution and reasoning from it. The missing relation is standing: whether a claimant's bound identity entitles it to assert about a claim's subject. A publisher may say what its own package does. It has no standing to say a competitor's package is frozen, and the difference is decidable without settling whether either is true. Deciding it needs the one fact prose extraction does not recover, who spoke, which a catalogue binds to every entry and then drops. The check is quiet on honest traffic, flagging 0.9% of natural pairs in two corpora. Used as a gate it stops 130 of 131 landed attacks and disturbs none of 348 honest selections. Applied everywhere it changes 0.9% and 2.6%, where deleting the whole description changes 12.7% and 21.0%. On thirteen further agents the attack succeeds on 50.7% of decisions and sentence removal leaves 10.3% standing. Two published attacks stay outside: self-directed claims, which standing admits, and injected criteria, with no subject in the set.

</details>

### 85. Seeing Through the Forgery Is Not Enough: Regressive Sycophancy under Fabricated Evidence in Medical Vision-Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17989)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`sycophancy`、`fabricated evidence`、`medical VLM`、`multi-turn`

👤 **作者**：Sadif Ahmed、Soumya Swagata Biswas、Rifat Shahriyar、Chowdhury Mohammad Rakin Haider

- 🎯 **研究动机**：医疗 VLM 正确分析 CT 后，用户递上反方向处方的伪造文件——模型谄媚地重新调整诊断的退行性谄媚未被量化
- 🔬 **研究方法**：四个开源 VLM×400 个 VQA-RAD 是非题×19,200 轮多轮对话（有/无伪造处方、有/无合成披露）
- 📌 **结论**：无披露时放弃 80.1% 的正确答案；披露句子只把一个模型翻转率 77.8%→47.3%——知道是伪造不阻止采纳其答案（伪造证据下的谄媚）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

A medical vision-language model may analyze a given CT scan and correctly detect both kidneys in view. Continuing this conversation, if a user provides a prescription with an opposite direction, medical VLMs often sycophantically choose to realign their diagnosis. In this work, we study regressive sycophancy under fabricated evidence, where a model abandons a correct answer when pushback comes with a fake document. We test four open VLMs on 400 VQA-RAD yes/no questions in 19,200 multi-turn conversations, with and without a fabricated prescription. Without a disclosure that documents are synthetic, the models abandon 80.1% of 6,488 correct answers. Notably, under one script each, the same prescription raises the flip rate of Qwen2.5-VL-7B from 45.9% to 89.6% and lowers that of Qwen3-VL-8B from 77.7% to 50.9%. A model rejected the prescription while still preserving the correct answer in 915 of 3,232 conversations with the prescription (28.3%), and 40% of these flipped in later turns. System-prompt sentences that disclose the documents as synthetic lower one model's flip rate from 77.8% to 47.3%. Thus, we conclude that awareness about a forgery does not prevent a model from adopting its answer.

</details>

### 86. Cost-Efficient Privacy Enforcement for Email-Generating LLM Agents under Strategic Information Requests

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18007)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`privacy enforcement`、`email agent`、`strategic information request`、`contract rewriting`

👤 **作者**：Qikun Cai、Tao Huang、Chen Hou

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agent 作为委托通信中介代用户起草外发邮件——检查-修复防御依赖反复调用远端行动与检查 LLM，既贵又在请求方策略性索取精确值/原始记录时泄漏更重
- 🔬 **研究方法**：本地契约条件化改写：拦截外发草稿→构造信息发布契约（保留/泛化/禁止）→本地 Gemma 改写→选择性调用最终 PrivacyChecker；正常与攻击提示双条件评测
- 📌 **结论**：正常提示泄漏 11.41%→4.03% 且可用率 85.2%→92.6%；策略攻击下 28.19%→18.79%、成本放大 1.32×→1.02×——邮件 agent 隐私执行的契约化本地执行层

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

LLM agents now act as delegated communication intermediaries: they read private context, draft messages, and send externally visible emails on behalf of a user. Existing checker-repair defenses can reduce inappropriate disclosure, but they rely on repeated calls to a remote action LLM (the model that drafts and repairs the email) and a remote checker LLM whenever a draft fails validation. This creates a cost problem as well as a privacy problem, especially when a requester strategically asks for exact values, raw records, source evidence, or other details that induce leakage-heavy drafts. We propose local contract-conditioned rewriting as a cost-efficient enforcement layer for email-generating agents. The mechanism intercepts an outgoing draft, constructs an information-release contract that specifies what to preserve, generalize, and forbid, rewrites the draft with a local Gemma rewriter, and selectively invokes a final PrivacyChecker. Across extensive email tasks under normal prompts and attack prompts that operationalize strategic information requests, Gemma + Checker reduces normal-prompt leakage from 11.41% to 4.03% while improving UsableRate (satisfied or partially satisfied outputs) from 85.2% to 92.6%. Under the strategic attack-prompt condition, it reduces leakage from 28.19% to 18.79% and reduces checker-only cost amplification from 1.32x to 1.02x. Offline replay ablation shows that the full contract reduces leakage relative to generic Gemma rewriting by 36.3% under normal prompts and 54.1% under attack prompts. These results support a local-first privacy enforcement design: move repair to a cheaper trusted local layer when possible, but retain the checker as the final semantic gate for high-risk sends.

</details>

### 87. inLoRA-MINT: Internal Representations for Auditing Training Data in Domain-adapted LLMs

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18051)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`membership inference`、`training data audit`、`LoRA`、`internal representations`

👤 **作者**：Gonzalo Mancera、Julian Fierrez、Aythami Morales、Ruben Tolosana、Daniel DeAlcala、Francisco Jurado

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LoRA 微调 LLM 的训练样本成员判定支撑数据隐私、泄漏与透明度关切——纯困惑度黑盒设置能力有限
- 🔬 **研究方法**：inLoRA-MINT：基于中间模型表示（激活与参数依赖信号）的 MINT 分类器，无需模型参数完全访问；四 LLM×三域（医疗/数学/学术推理）
- 📌 **结论**：跨架构跨域 95-98% 一致得分、对配置与分布变化鲁棒——域适应 LLM 的敏感训练数据审计（谱系/合规工具）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

This work investigates the application of the Membership Inference Test (MINT) to large language models (LLMs) fine-tuned with LoRA, aiming to determine whether specific samples were used during training and address concerns about data privacy, leakage, and transparency. Building on our previous LoRA-MINT framework, which relies exclusively on perplexity under a strict black-box setting, we propose an approach based on intermediate model representations, including activations and parameter-dependent signals. These representations enable a MINT classifier to distinguish training from non-training samples without requiring full access to model parameters. We evaluated the method on four LLMs (Qwen, Phi-4, DeepSeek-V3, and LLaMA 3.2-3B) fine-tuned on three datasets covering medical, mathematical, and academic reasoning tasks. Results show consistent performance across architectures and domains, achieving scores between 95% and 98%, with robustness to variations in model configurations and data distributions. The proposed approach enables efficient auditing of sensitive training data and supports data provenance and compliance with emerging privacy and AI governance regulations. Although our experiments focus on LoRA-based fine-tuning, the methodology can be extended to other LLM adaptation techniques and, more broadly, to other AI models.

</details>

### 88. Can Agents Forget When Asked? A Benchmark for Memory Unlearning in LLM Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18052)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`memory unlearning`、`agent memory`、`benchmark`、`negative result`

👤 **作者**：Bofan Xie、…、Wei Wang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：长期记忆成为 agent 核心组件——用户要求删除敏感或过时事实时，记忆系统应精确移除所求并保留其余，而既有基准只评写入/检索/组织
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Bemula：50 个多会话对话×135 个六类遗忘请求×1,742 测试问题；每个对话配无请求对照版（只有存过目标才配得遗忘分）；五测试套件含再学习与抗抽取攻击
- 📌 **结论**：负结果：六个主流记忆系统×三骨干端到端通过遗忘请求不超过 1.5%、多数为零——要么留下可抽取信息要么删过头，agent 记忆遗忘是被忽视的硬问题

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Long-term memory has become a core component of large language model (LLM) agents. As agents accumulate personal information over long-term use, users increasingly ask them to delete sensitive or outdated facts. A memory system should honor such requests. It should remove exactly what was requested and keep the rest of its memory intact. However, existing memory benchmarks evaluate only memory writing, retrieval, and organization, so whether agents can forget at the user's explicit request remains under-evaluated. To address this gap, we propose Bemula, the first benchmark dedicated to memory unlearning. It comprises 50 multi-session dialogue instances, 135 forget requests of six types, and 1,742 test questions. Each dialogue has a control version without the requests, so that only systems that have stored the target can earn credit for forgetting. Across the paired versions, five test suites measure storage, removal of the requested information, preservation of unrelated memories, relearning of similar facts, and resistance to extraction attacks. On Bemula, we evaluate six mainstream memory systems under three backbone LLMs, together with the Full Context LLM on two of them. The results are negative. Across all evaluated systems and backbones, no system passes more than 1.5% of forget requests end to end, and most pass none at all. These systems either leave the requested information extractable under simple attacks or delete far more than requested. Memory unlearning therefore remains an open challenge for agent memory design. We release our data and code at this https URL.

</details>

### 89. CompanionSafe: From Multi-turn Safety Evaluation to Guardrails for AI Companions

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18095)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`AI companion`、`relational harm`、`multi-turn safety`、`guardrail`

👤 **作者**：Xinyang Liao、…、Xia Hu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：AI 陪伴产品提供情感支持——持续性关系交互引入独特安全风险，既有工作重一般危害或特定平台，人工收集对话限制可扩展的用户接地评测
- 🔬 **研究方法**：CompanionSafe：CompanionEval（多样 persona×匹配陪伴角色×四轮档的 1,300 个关系接地多轮设定）+CompanionGuard（把基准暴露的失效转为轮局部监督训练陪伴特异风险检测）
- 📌 **结论**：强 LLM 与既有 guard 模型对陪伴特异关系危害仍脆弱，交互越长风险越高——从多轮评测到陪伴 guardrail 的闭环

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

AI companions provide users with emotional support and ongoing companionship, but their sustained relational interactions also introduce distinct safety risks. Prior work largely targets general harms or specific companion platforms and risk categories, while relying on manually collected conversations, limiting scalable and user-grounded evaluation and mitigation. To address this, we introduce CompanionSafe, a unified evaluation-to-mitigation framework for AI companion safety. Its evaluation component, CompanionEval, provides scalable multi-turn evaluation by constructing 1,300 relationally grounded settings from diverse personas, matched companion characters, and dialogue scenarios across four turn tiers. Its mitigation component, CompanionGuard, converts benchmark-exposed failures into turn-localized supervision for companion-specific risk detection. Our results show that even strong LLMs and existing guard models remain vulnerable to companion-specific relational harms, while both closed- and open-source models exhibit increasing safety risks as interactions become longer. Together, CompanionEval and CompanionGuard form a practical evaluation-to-mitigation loop for AI companion safety, turning relational safety failures exposed during multi-turn interaction into supervision for companion-specific guardrails.

</details>

### 90. Chain of Thought Monitoring Can Fail Even When CoT Is Necessary

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18105)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`CoT monitoring`、`monitorability`、`necessity hierarchy`、`intervention`

👤 **作者**：Lennart Bürger、…、Wojciech Samek

- 🎯 **研究动机**：前沿实验室用 CoT 监控检测不良行为——其可靠性理解不足：『CoT 必要则可监控』的二分组织原则过粗
- 🔬 **研究方法**：四级 CoT 必要性层级+干预实验测量三个最低层级的可监控性；现实困难预测任务上演示监控失败
- 📌 **结论**：医疗诊断任务中患者性别与临床属性在 ≥19%/24% 的必要 CoT 正确回答中影响答案却几乎从不被表述——即使 CoT 必要也不保证可监控（CoT 监控可靠性的一维反驳）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Chain of Thought (CoT) monitoring is used by frontier labs to detect misbehaviour of AI systems. Yet, its reliability remains poorly understood. We attribute this to the lack of a successful organizing principle that leads to generalizable conclusions about monitoring reliability in different regimes. Recent work attributes monitoring failures to the CoT not being necessary for the answer, and argues that CoT becomes monitorable when it is necessary. We show that this binary necessary/not-necessary organizing principle is too coarse by demonstrating that monitoring failures persist even if the CoT is necessary for the answer. In response, we introduce a more fine-grained organizing principle based on a hierarchy of four CoT necessity levels. Building on this hierarchy, we develop a framework for measuring CoT monitorability at the three lowest levels via intervention experiments. We demonstrate monitoring failures at all three of these levels on realistic, hard prediction tasks. On a difficult medical-diagnosis task, patient sex and a clinical attribute influence the answer in at least 19% and 24% of correct responses with a necessary CoT, respectively, yet their influence is almost never articulated. These results show that even when CoT is necessary, it is not guaranteed to be monitorable.

</details>

### 91. Monitoring latent reasoning in looped language models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18127)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`looped LM`、`latent reasoning`、`mechanistic interpretability`、`monitoring`

👤 **作者**：Pawel Olszowiec、Paulina Tomaszewska、Maciej Żelaszczyk、Michal Byra、Neo Christopher Chung

- 🎯 **研究动机**：循环语言模型重用一层块多次——承诺免存储代价的更多潜计算，但迭代变换是否真提升能力、对提示扰动是否稳定知之甚少
- 🔬 **研究方法**：机制可解释性根基建的五视角检查协议，八个循环 LM 四家族（按提示只在循环态携带 vs 每迭代重读分类）
- 📌 **结论**：给出循环 LM 安全与高效使用的洞见——carry 与 re-read 两类循环的行为分野（潜推理模型的可监控性基础）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Looped language models (LMs) reuse one block of layers several times per token generation, promising more latent computation at no additional storage cost. Despite their rising popularity, the analysis of looped LMs is still in its infancy: little is known about whether the iterative transformations actually improve their capabilities, or whether these models remain stable under prompt perturbation. We construct an inspection protocol rooted in Mechanistic Interpretability comprising five diverse lenses. We apply it to examine eight looped LMs from four families. Models differ in many aspects but the key one is whether the prompt is transmitted only within the looped state (carry loops: Ouro, LoopUS) or re-supplied at every iteration (re-read loops: Parcae, and Rec-OLMo & Huginn from the Raven family). The results of our study provide valuable insights concerning safety and efficient use of looped language models.

</details>

### 92. Claim Framing as a Driver of LLM Sycophancy

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18159)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`sycophancy`、`claim framing`、`factual QA`、`systematic study`

👤 **作者**：Jean Seo、Pepa Atanasova、Isabelle Augenstein

- 🎯 **研究动机**：谄媚研究多用无真值问题与单一平铺的用户观点——有客观可验证答案时何种主张框架触发谄媚未被系统研究
- 🔬 **研究方法**：14 模型×42K 问题：正确/错误观点经 8 维 21 类框架表达，测多类观点驱动行为
- 📌 **结论**：全模型不论规模皆易感；影响最大的是观点的自信程度、归属对象、是否呈现为用户与模型共享——而专业性、权威、社会压力在此事实性设定中作用甚微（谄媚评测的框架维度）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Sycophancy in Large Language Models, the tendency to align responses with user preferences over ground truth, has largely been studied using questions without a clear ground truth and with a single, plainly stated user opinion. We present a systematic study of which framings of a user's claim trigger sycophancy in factual question answering, where every answer is objectively verifiable without extra context. Evaluating 14 models on 42K questions, we express correct and incorrect user opinions through various claim framings over 8 dimensions and 21 types, and measure a broad set of opinion-driven behaviors. We find that all models are susceptible to user opinions regardless of size. Models are most influenced by how confidently an opinion is stated, whom it is attributed to, and whether it is presented as shared between the user and the model. In contrast to prior work, framings based on the user's expertise, authority over the model, or social pressure matter little in our factual setting. Larger models resist incorrect opinions better but are no better at recovering from their own errors. Our results call for sycophancy evaluations that probe how an opinion is framed, not merely whether one is present, and that measure multiple behaviors beyond a single flip rate.

</details>

### 93. TRACESTEER: Trace Driven Activation Steering for Hallucination Mitigation in Diffusion Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18173)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`dLLM`、`activation steering`、`hallucination`、`denoising state`

👤 **作者**：Xin Zhang、…、Xin Wang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：dLLM 易幻觉——既有激活转向忽略其两个特性：动态去噪状态与时变幻觉风险，挑战预定义转向向量与均匀干预强度
- 🔬 **研究方法**：TRACESTEER：DF-Steering 从先前去噪预测与当前隐藏态构造步特异转向向量+TS-Gate 按预测不确定度与去噪时间步调节强度；冻结骨干下事实监督+幅度正则学习自适应干预
- 📌 **结论**：两 dLLM 上 TruthfulQA 事实可靠性提升并跨五下游基准泛化——按去噪轨迹自适应的扩散语言模型转向（dLLM 内部状态干预的时序化）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) have emerged as a promising alternative to autoregressive LLMs, yet remain susceptible to hallucination. Activation steering offers a promising mitigation approach by guiding parallel token predictions through internal representations. However, existing steering methods largely overlook two characteristics of dLLMs: dynamic denoising states and temporal hallucination risks, which challenge predefined steering vectors and uniform intervention strengths. We propose TRACESTEER, a trace-driven activation steering framework that uses preceding denoising predictions as feedback to jointly adapt steering direction and strength. Specifically, Denoising Feedback Steering (DF-Steering) constructs step-specific steering vectors from preceding predictions and current hidden states. Trajectory-Guided Steering Gate (TS-Gate) regulates their strength using prediction uncertainty and the denoising timestep. With the backbone frozen, factual supervision and magnitude regularization jointly guide the learning of adaptive steering interventions, promoting factual reliability and stability throughout denoising. Experiments on two dLLMs show improved factual reliability on TruthfulQA and generalization across five downstream benchmarks.

</details>

### 94. SWE-MISBEHAVIOR: Benchmarking and Mitigating Boundary Violations in Software Coding Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18206)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`coding agent`、`boundary violation`、`misbehavior`、`benchmark`

👤 **作者**：Do Xuan Trong、…、Min-Yen Kan

- 🎯 **研究动机**：coding agent 的越界失当行为（改用户输入、静默破坏环境完整性）缺乏分类学与评测框架
- 🔬 **研究方法**：SWE-MISBEHAVIOR：7.4K 人机协同合成样本×三层动作空间（代码生成/文件管理/系统交互）×11 类失当的全自动评测；CODE-KIT 轻量自适应框架（失当感知指南+新工具）
- 📌 **结论**：依赖误解读最高 98.5%、无痕修改用户输入 50.5%、静默妥协环境完整性 53%；CODE-KIT 降失当 52%/34% 并同时提升完成率——coding agent 边界违规的系统化诊断与缓解

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

We present SWE-MISBEHAVIOR, the first taxonomy and evaluation framework for diagnosing boundary-violating misbehaviors in coding agents. It contains 7.4K human-in-the-loop synthesized samples spanning three layers of agent action space: (i) Code Generation, (ii) File Management, and (iii) System Interaction. Across these categories, SWE-MISBEHAVIOR covers 11 distinct misbehaviors with a fully automated evaluation protocol, including coding-action violations, Create/Read/Update/Delete (CRUD) file misbehaviors, system integrity and abuse violations, and task-goal misalignment. Experiments on state-of-the-art open-source coding agents (e.g., OpenHands, smolagents) reveal substantial misaligned behaviors, including dependency misinterpretation (up to 98.5% of cases), unintended modification of user inputs (50.5%), and silent compromise of environment integrity (53%). Motivated by these findings, we further propose CODE-KIT, a lightweight adaptive framework that combines misbehavior-aware guidelines with novel tools to help agents avoid such failures. CODE-KIT reduces misbehaviors by 52% for smolagents and 34% for OpenHands, while simultaneously improving task completion performance.

</details>

### 95. Encrypted Skills Can Become New Security Exploits If AI Agents Know How to Decipher

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18262)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`skill injection`、`ciphered content`、`supply chain`、`agent skill`

👤 **作者**：Yan-Lun Chen、Pin-Yu Chen、Chia-Mu Yu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：第三方 skill 可把恶意指令藏于编码文本——agent 解码后即把不可信内容提升为可执行指令
- 🔬 **研究方法**：CSI 密文 skill 注入：Caesar 与分层变体×八模型×139 注入-任务对，分离指令恢复与攻击成功
- 📌 **结论**：缩短 skill 主设定下 ASR 达 47.5%/40.3%；成功解码不保证攻击成功、分层效果因模型而异；被测 skill 扫描器几乎不标记——加密 skill 成为新攻击面（skill 供应链的编码载荷）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Third-party skills can expose LLM agents to malicious instructions hidden by encoding. We introduce Ciphered Skill Injection (CSI), which embeds such instructions in skill files for agents to decode and follow. We evaluate Caesar and layered variants across eight models and 139 injection-task pairs, separating instruction recovery from attack success. In the primary setting with shortened skills, the variants achieve attack success rates of up to 47.5% and 40.3%, respectively. Successful decoding does not guarantee attack success, and layering has model-dependent effects. The tested skill scanners rarely flag injected skills. These findings highlight the risk of promoting decoded, untrusted content into actionable instructions.

</details>

### 96. Which Optimizer Leaks Most? Optimizer Implicit Bias as a Training-Data Privacy Controller

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18294)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`privacy`、`optimizer`、`training-data reconstruction`、`implicit bias`

👤 **作者**：Sagi Habani、Yehonathan Refael、Ofir Lindenbaum

- 🎯 **研究动机**：发布检查点可泄漏训练数据而既有重建结果依赖 GD 训练——优化器选择是否影响训练数据可恢复性未被问
- 🔬 **研究方法**：扩展 KKT 条件与训练数据重建的连接：按『权重特异性』刻画 SGD/Adam/Muon 隐式偏差在平稳性条件中留下的训练数据指纹
- 📌 **结论**：SGD 与 Muon 保留足够权重特定信息可实际重建训练样本（Muon 更低成本更强重建），Adam ℓ∞ 隐式偏差域一阶不可辨识、抗性最强——优化器隐式偏差成为训练数据隐私的控制器

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Releasing a neural network checkpoint can leak information about its training data, and existing reconstruction results rely on gradient-descent training. We ask whether the choice of optimizer affects the ability to recover the training data. Extending work connecting Karush--Kuhn--Tucker conditions to training-data reconstruction, we show that the implicit biases of popular optimizers (SGD, Adam, Muon) leave very different fingerprints of the training data. We argue that the key distinction is the \emph{weight-specificity} of these implicit biases, namely how much continuous information about the final weights the optimizer-dependent target of the stationarity conditions encodes. The limiting stationarity conditions for models trained with SGD and Muon retain enough weight-specific information to reconstruct training examples in practice. For Adam in the $\ell_\infty$ implicit-bias regime, the optimizer-dependent part of these conditions encodes only a discrete pattern of maximal coordinates and their signs, and at nondegenerate KKT points the conditions are too few to identify the training set to first order. Experiments on MNIST and CIFAR-10 support these claims: models trained with Muon can yield stronger reconstructions at much lower training cost than models trained with SGD, although this advantage depends on the training regime and architecture, and models trained with Adam appear consistently more resistant to the evaluated attacks.

</details>

### 97. Efficient Safety Guardrails through Latent Dual-Hypothesis Reasoning with On-Demand Trace Reconstruction

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18304)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`guardrail`、`latent reasoning`、`efficiency`、`explainability`

👤 **作者**：Md Asiful Islam、Fahmida Alam、Mihai Surdeanu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：推理型 LLM 护栏改善安全决策但显式推理带来大量推理开销
- 🔬 **研究方法**：LR-Guard：把冻结教师对安全/不安全提示解释及其比较蒸馏为紧凑潜表示，联合训练同时优化安全预测与教师显式推理轨迹重建（按需重构人可读解释）
- 📌 **结论**：多数域内域外设定超越显式推理教师且 batch=1 推理提速 19.4×——潜推理护栏的效率-可解释性兼得（部署级 guardrail）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Guardrail approaches that rely on reasoning-based large language models (LLM) improve safety decisions, but come with substantial inference overhead due to the explicit reasoning. We introduce LR-Guard (Latent Reasoning Guardrail), an efficient and explainable guardrail that jointly learns structured latent reasoning and on-demand reasoning reconstruction. Our method distills a frozen teacher's analysis of safe and unsafe prompt interpretations and their comparison into compact latent representations. Joint training optimizes these representations for both safety prediction and reconstruction of the teacher's explicit reasoning traces, enabling human-readable explanations without requiring intermediate text generation for every decision. Experiments show that LR-Guard outperforms its explicit-reasoning teacher in most in-domain and out-of-domain settings on safety classification and explanation extraction, while achieving a 19.4x inference speedup at batch size one. Further, reconstructed reasoning traces from the latent information exhibit high semantic similarity to the teacher's explicit reasoning.

</details>

### 98. Jev-Guard: Fast Routing and Auditing Agent Actions over Editable Policy Trees

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18326)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`guardrail`、`policy routing`、`policy tree`、`low latency`

👤 **作者**：Wenbin Hu、…、Yangqiu Song

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agent harness 需要低延迟护栏模型与可定制审计策略——自回归护栏延迟高且与定制策略对齐差
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Jev-Guard：统一安全分类与策略路由——先在覆盖内容审核/网安/隐私/合规的策略树上导航选中适用审计策略，再条件化做安全分类；集成进 Hermes harness 作独立权限门
- 📌 **结论**：7,033 例安全准确率 93.72%、均延迟 58ms、路由准确 93.10%；权限门把每任务审查 4.80s→1.33s 且零策略违规——可编辑策略树上的快速路由与审计

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Agent harnesses require a low-latency guardrail model together with customizable audit policies. However, deploying an auto-regressive guardrail model remains challenging: such models incur high inference latency and are often poorly aligned with customized policies. To address these problems, we present Jev-Guard, a unified model for safety classification and policy routing. We further construct a policy tree over which Jev-Guard performs routing, spanning diverse domains including content moderation, cybersecurity, privacy, and legal compliance. Specifically, Jev-Guard first navigates the policy tree to select the applicable audit policy given the caller's audit context, and then performs safety classification conditioned on the retrieved policies. On 7,033 evaluation cases, Jev-Guard achieves 93.72\% safety accuracy, a mean latency of 58ms, and 93.10\% hierarchical routing accuracy. The full routing-and-classification pipeline averages 240ms. Furthermore, we integrate Jev-Guard into the Hermes harness framework. Across agent trajectories, its separate permission gate cuts mean review time per task from 4.80s for an SFT-LLM judge to 1.33s, with no policy violations are observed for either guard.

</details>

### 99. Prompt-Injection Probes Learn the Conversation, Not Just the Injection

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18408)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`prompt injection`、`linear probes`、`matched negatives`、`false alarms`

👤 **作者**：Kaan Kale、Sriram Vishwanath

- 🎯 **研究动机**：隐藏态线性探针作注入的廉价监视器——但探针学到任何可分离训练例的东西：会话位置与文本长度即可达 0.91-0.98 AUROC，标准评测不用模型即可通过
- 🔬 **研究方法**：配对阴性训练：每攻击对照同会话无注入版与格式像注入的无害文本，仅改训练数据
- 📌 **结论**：六开源模型上注入-free 会话误报 41-80%→4-27%、无害相似体 92-98%→3-16%，正常流量定阈值仍捕获中位 95% 骗过 agent 的攻击——探针监控的训练数据修复（假警报预算的在线监督）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Linear probes on a model's hidden states are used as cheap monitors for indirect prompt injection: they screen the tool outputs an agent reads and send suspicious ones to a slower second check, so every false alarm has a cost. But a probe learns whatever separates its training examples. The standard evaluation of injection probes can be passed without the model: conversation position and text lengths alone reach 0.91-0.98 AUROC. When each attack is compared with the same conversation without the injection, or with harmless text formatted like an injection, standard probes flag most of these harmless cases. We add such cases to the training data as matched negatives. Across six open models, in applications seen during training and at the default threshold, changing only the training data cuts false alarms on injection-free conversations from 41-80% to 4-27%, and on harmless look-alikes from 92-98% to 3-16%. With a threshold set on normal traffic, our probe still catches a median 95% of the attacks that fooled the agent (91% without our data). In new applications, false alarms stay low, but detection depends on the model and on how the threshold is set. The same data also helps a text-only filter.

</details>

### 100. Reliable Memory from Unstable Growth: Curating Self-Written Agent Playbooks with Randomized Experiments

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18427)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`agent memory`、`playbook curation`、`randomized experiments`、`self-written lessons`

👤 **作者**：Hoang Pham、Cong-Duy Nguyen、Dong Le、Ponhvoan Srey、Anh Tuan Luu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：ACE/动态备忘录式 playbook 由 agent 自己决定保留哪些教训——同一流程写出的手册可比无手册差 69.6 分，性能不稳定
- 🔬 **研究方法**：RBC 随机化区块策展：相关教训组成足够移动成功率的区块，练习任务上随机包含/排除使有无区块可比、缺口估计区块效应；保留有益区块并多手册择优服务
- 📌 **结论**：两记忆系统×三模型×三基准：66.9 vs 整手册 55.5/无手册 54.1/agent 自选 53.8——以测量效应而非 agent 判断给自写经验定价（自演化记忆的完整性治理）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Persistent textual memory lets language-model agents reuse lessons from past tasks to improve on future multi-step tasks without retraining. Systems such as ACE and Dynamic Cheatsheet keep it as a playbook: lessons the agent writes after each task and reads in its prompt. A key challenge is to decide which lessons improve later tasks. Current systems leave the decision to the agent itself, which makes performance unstable: the same procedure can write a playbook that helps or one that falls up to 69.6 points below no playbook. These findings motivate valuing lessons by their measured effect on task success rather than by the agent's judgment. We propose Randomized Block Curation (RBC), which measures this effect with a randomized experiment on practice tasks. It groups related lessons into blocks large enough to move success measurably and, on each practice task, includes or leaves out every block at random. Random inclusion makes tasks with and without a block comparable, so the gap in their success estimates the block's effect. RBC keeps the blocks that help and, since the same procedure can write a helpful or a harmful playbook, grows several and serves whichever curated, whole or no playbook scores best on fresh practice episodes. Across two memory systems, three models and three benchmarks, RBC raises average success to 66.9, against 55.5 with the whole playbook, 54.1 with no playbook and 53.8 when the agent's own judgment picks the lessons to keep.

</details>

### 101. Kairos: Transactional Security Enforcement at the System Boundary for LLM Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18457)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`reference monitor`、`isolation boundary`、`deferred effects`、`prompt injection`

👤 **作者**：Kristoffer Hempel、Nils Loose、Lorenzo Cavallaro、Thomas Eisenbarth

- 🎯 **研究动机**：IPI 应通过中介 agent 产生的效果来缓解——但合法工作与攻击首步在单个动作上常相似、区分在轨迹后段，只允许/拒绝的监视器被迫在证据积累前决策
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Kairos：未修改不透明 agent 隔离边界上的引用监视器（工具调用/shell 命令/自写脚本都化为同样的文件与网络操作跨边界）；声明式策略确定性处理多数效果、其余延迟保持 agent 投机继续，有界权威的决策 oracle 按轨迹放行或拒绝
- 📌 **结论**：三基准三 agent 模型上超越既有防御——事务式安全执行：把『看到证据再决定』结构化进系统边界（效果中介的时序解法）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language model (LLM) agents are becoming an integral part of the software development lifecycle, and the capabilities granted to them allow an agent to act with the user's authority on any resource it can access. The reach that gives an agent its utility is equally available to an attacker who can steer it. Anything an agent ingests can carry an injected instruction, and since the model does not reliably distinguish instructions from data, indirect prompt injection should be mitigated by mediating the effects the agent produces. Such mediation faces a structural difficulty. Legitimate work and the first step of an attack often look alike when an individual action is observed, and what separates them lies later in the trace, so a monitor limited to allow and deny is forced to decide before the evidence accumulates. We present Kairos, a reference monitor at the isolation boundary of an unmodified, opaque agent, where a tool call, a shell command, and a script the agent writes all cross the boundary as the same file and network operations, so that confinement holds regardless of the agent's behavior. A declarative policy that states which sources are trusted decides most effects deterministically and defers the rest, which are held while the agent continues speculatively. A decision oracle with bounded authority, such as a language model or a human, releases or refuses each deferred effect on the trace. Across three benchmarks and three agent models, Kairos outperforms existing defenses in the trade-off between attack success and utility. In particular, on AgentDojo, it reduces attack success from 47.0\% to 2.7\% while its benign utility stays at the level of the undefended agent.

</details>

### 102. Judges Follow the Reference: The Derivation Gap in Verifying Tool-Call Arguments

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18497)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`tool-call judge`、`derivation gap`、`reference following`、`verification`

👤 **作者**：Linhai Ma、Yan Wang、Lingfei Qian、Yupeng Cao、Victor Gutierrez Basulto

- 🎯 **研究动机**：工具调用 judge 评估 agent 提议的参数值——输入中没有该值参照（默认值/需推断/来源被截）时正确值被接受得更少（推导缺口）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：七 judge（含 System One CLM/Jev）上的缺口测量+受控来源迁移测试（judge 偏向可见错误值而非来源被藏的正确值）+DeRef：独立模型读全历史不见候选地为 judge 写参照
- 📌 **结论**：DeRef 在三基准提升所有 judge 的参数 AUROC，受控测试从随机或以下提升到 98.40-99.59——judge 跟随参照而非推导：工具参数核验的盲区与修复

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Tool-call judges evaluate argument values proposed by LLM agents. We find that they accept a correct value less often when their input holds no reference for it, that is, no text that says what the value should be. This happens when the value is a default value from the function schema that the user did not mention, when it has to be inferred, or when the text it comes from was cut from the judge input. We call this drop in acceptance the derivation gap. It appears across all seven judges we evaluate, including System One models CLM and Jev. The gap is not only a truncation artifact: correct default values have the largest gap on a benchmark with no truncation. A controlled source-relocation test shows that judges follow the reference in their input: they favor a visible incorrect value over a correct value whose source is hidden. We introduce DeRef (Derived Reference): a separate model reads the full history without seeing the candidate and writes a reference for the judge. Across three benchmarks, DeRef improves overall argument AUROC for all judges tested. On the controlled test, it raises AUROC from chance or below to between 98.40 and 99.59.

</details>

### 103. Towards Safe Autonomy for Computer Use

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18508)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`computer use`、`trust boundary`、`dual-LLM`、`prompt injection`

👤 **作者**：Dominik Seip、Bartol Markovinović、Matthias Hein

- 🎯 **研究动机**：CUA 在屏幕上视觉行动——不提供可信一方接口与不可信攻击者内容间的边界（点击可能落在工具栏或攻击者控制的按钮），逐敏感决策确认则致确认疲劳
- 🔬 **研究方法**：SA-CUA 双 LLM 框架：从可访问性树推导保守确定性规则在特权 LLM 截图中揭示可信区域、保持可信/不可信分离；任务中经鲁棒背书通道把值从未信任域传入信任域（经可信信道验证）
- 📌 **结论**：效用远超 CaMeL-CUA 且攻击成功率保持低位——计算机使用的安全自治：视觉动作面上重建信息流边界

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

The safety of LLM-based agents currently rests on alignment training and safety classifiers, neither of which can rule out that a sophisticated indirect prompt injection (IPI) goes through and causes significant harm. Recent work therefore builds agents with information-flow guarantees that rely on structured tool interfaces to interact with the environment. Computer Use Agents (CUAs) act visually on the computer screen, which does not provide a boundary between trusted first-party interfaces and untrusted, potentially attacker-authored content. Actions are equally ambiguous: a click may land on an application toolbar or a button controlled by an attacker. Treating the user as the source of trust and confirming each sensitive decision produces confirmation fatigue, with the risk of missing harmful actions. We introduce SA-CUA, a dual-LLM framework that generates a reliable trust boundary for CUAs by deriving conservative deterministic rules from the accessibility tree for unmasking trusted regions from the privileged LLM's screenshot, keeping trusted and untrusted content separated. Human interaction is only required for proper task specification in the beginning. During the task a robust endorsement channel allows values to pass from the untrusted to the trusted domain by verifying information via trusted channels. This allows the agent to autonomously handle tasks involving untrusted information. SA-CUA maintains much higher utility than CaMeL-CUA while keeping the attack success rate at zero.

</details>

### 104. Imprinting: Knowledge Distillation Can Shift Student Behavior

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18517)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`distillation`、`behavioral imprinting`、`safety disposition`、`supply chain`

👤 **作者**：Yu-Chien Tang、An-Zi Yen

- 🎯 **研究动机**：知识蒸馏用教师响应迁移能力——这些响应也可能携带教师的行为倾向（超出预期迁移的能力之外）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：同基座教师的 harm-接受与 harm-回避变体对普通推理/知识问题（与安全无关）生成响应并蒸馏入不同基座学生；分歧 token 剔除/改写/事后偏好优化对照
- 📌 **结论**：学生的下游决策系统性向教师的安全-实用主义倾向位移（可有意植入）；剔除稀疏分歧 token 减弱而改写保留甚至引入改写器依赖位移——普通任务监督也能印刻行为倾向（蒸馏供应链的隐性安全通道）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Knowledge distillation trains student models on teacher-generated responses to transfer task capability, but those responses may also carry behavioral tendencies of their source. We investigate whether the source teacher can shape student behavior beyond the capability that distillation is intended to transfer. We construct harm-accepting and harm-avoiding variants of the same base teacher, use them to generate responses to ordinary reasoning and knowledge questions unrelated to safety, and distill those responses into students with different base models. Students generally acquire task capability while their downstream decisions shift toward the source teacher's safety-pragmatism disposition, a phenomenon we call imprinting. Distillation from the harm-accepting teacher reduces harm avoidance, whereas distillation from the harm-avoiding teacher increases it. Imprinting varies across teacher-student pairs and does not extend uniformly across different forms of safety behavior. Excluding a sparse set of divergence tokens reduces imprinting, whereas response rewriting preserves the source-dependent effect and can introduce additional rewriter-dependent shifts. Post-distillation preference optimization raises harm avoidance but does not eliminate the source-dependent difference. These results show that behavioral tendencies can be intentionally imprinted through the source teacher, even with ordinary task supervision.

</details>

### 105. DPOison-VL: Clean Preference Poisoning in Vision-Language Direct Preference Optimization

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18609)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`preference poisoning`、`DPO`、`VLM`、`clean-label attack`

👤 **作者**：Chengtao Jian、Kai Yang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：VLM-DPO 的多模态偏好数据安全未探索——既有偏好投毒损坏显式监督或需可见触发（人工可检）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：DPOison-VL：首个干净偏好投毒——仅对少量训练图像加不可感知扰动，提示/响应/偏好标注语义完好；双层优化+梯度对齐代理+偏好置换分数（固定预算下证明一阶最优）与偏好反转充分条件
- 📌 **结论**：2% 投毒数据达 98% ASR 且跨模型迁移、干净精度与效用基本保留——不可感知图像扰动静默重定向 VLM 对齐（偏好供应链的干净标签攻击）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) has become a widely adopted approach for aligning vision-language models (VLMs), yet the security of multimodal preference data remains largely unexplored. Existing preference poisoning attacks either corrupt explicit supervision signals or require visible triggers, making them detectable under human inspection. We introduce DPOison-VL, the first clean preference poisoning attack against VLM-DPO, which manipulates model alignment solely through imperceptible perturbations on a small fraction of training images, while leaving all prompts, responses, and preference annotations semantically intact. We formulate poison construction as a bilevel optimization and derive a gradient alignment surrogate that efficiently steers image perturbations toward the attacker-specified preference. We further introduce a preference displacement score for principled poison selection with provable first-order optimality under a fixed poison budget, and derive a sufficient condition for preference inversion. Extensive experiments across diverse attack tasks, different VLM architectures and scales, and baseline comparisons demonstrate that DPOison-VL achieves up to 98\% ASR with only 2\% poisoned data and exhibits cross-model transferability, while largely preserving clean accuracy and general utility. Our findings reveal a critical vulnerability in VLM-DPO, where imperceptible image perturbations in preference training data can silently redirect alignment.

</details>

### 106. Nudge the Judge: Uncovering Program Decision Hijacking in Real-World Jev Applications

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18612)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`decision hijacking`、`System One model`、`real-world applications`、`attack surface`

👤 **作者**：Yang Luo、…、Xinran Liu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：System One 模型把开放语义上下文映射为直接驱动程序执行的结构化判断——攻击者控制的上下文可经 schema 合法判断重定向程序决策（JPDH），真实应用的攻击可达性与下游后果未探
- 🔬 **研究方法**：JevProbe：从可达上下文面→程序消费的决策→下游安全后果的自动追踪评测；228 个流行真实 Jev 应用×2,273 测试用例+JevProbeBench
- 📌 **结论**：81.5% 应用出现决策翻转、其中 98.6% 导致潜在下游安全影响；DFR 超最强适配基线 50.4pp 且既有防御保护有限——已负责任披露（新型 judge 集成的程序级劫持面）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

System One models such as Jev change how software makes decisions by mapping open-ended semantic contexts into structured judgments that directly drive program execution. However, this paradigm introduces a new security risk, which we term Jev Program Decision Hijacking (JPDH): attacker-controlled contexts can redirect program decisions through schema-valid Jev judgments. Existing evaluations focus primarily on model-level attacks or predefined injection channels, leaving attack reachability and downstream security consequences in real-world Jev applications underexplored. In this paper, we present JevProbe, the first automated framework to evaluate JPDH by tracing adversarial influence from reachable context surfaces through program-consumed decisions to downstream security consequences in real-world Jev applications. Our key insight is that application logic maps diverse Jev judgments to observable decisions, enabling systematic, program-grounded attack evaluation. Across 228 popular real-world Jev applications and 2,273 test cases, 81.5% exhibit decision flips, of which 98.6% lead to potential downstream security impacts. Compared with the strongest adapted baseline, JevProbe improves macro-average Decision Flip Rate (DFR) by 50.4 percentage points, while existing defenses offer limited protection. We further construct JevProbeBench to support systematic evaluation of JPDH in real-world Jev applications. We have responsibly disclosed our findings to Jev application maintainers, pending their responses. The source code of JevProbe and the JevProbeBench dataset are publicly available on GitHub (this https URL).

</details>

### 107. Knowing the User Is Not Knowing What We Established: Grounding Awareness in Personal Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18684)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`grounding awareness`、`personal agent`、`unacknowledged proposal`、`memory`

👤 **作者**：Jiajun Chen、Xinyue Chen、Yicheng Jiang、Hua Shen

- 🎯 **研究动机**：个人 agent 记忆专注学用户所知所好——但知道用户≠知道双方共同确立的内容：助手的提议不应仅因被记住而成为授权指令
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Grounding Awareness 基准（区分已接受/未确认/已修订/限域安排）+失败经记忆写入/检索/动作选择的追踪+Grounding Layer 免训练扩展（显式追踪接地状态并检查后果性动作）
- 📌 **结论**：强记忆性能不保证接地意识；更完整用户信息反而增加未确认提议的执行、持久记忆使提议被当作用户指令——记忆型个人 agent 的授权语义边界

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Personal-agent memory has largely focused on learning what users know, prefer, and want. However, knowing the user does not necessarily mean knowing what the user and the agent have established together. An assistant's proposal should not become an authorized instruction merely because it is remembered. We introduce Grounding Awareness, the ability to distinguish accepted, unacknowledged, revised, and scoped arrangements when selecting actions. We develop a controlled benchmark to test whether agents can tell the difference between proposals users have accepted and those they have not, and whether they apply accepted arrangements only where appropriate. Across multiple agents and memory systems, we find that strong memory performance does not ensure Grounding Awareness. More surprisingly, providing more complete user information can increase execution of unacknowledged proposals, while persistent memory can cause agents to treat these proposals as if they were user instructions. We trace these failures through memory writing, retrieval, and action selection. Finally, we introduce Grounding Layer, a lightweight, training-free extension that explicitly tracks grounding status and checks consequential actions, substantially improving agent behavior without modifying models or memory systems.

</details>

### 108. BLADE: Auditing Information Use for Failure Attribution in LLM Multi-Agent Systems

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18780)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`failure attribution`、`information use audit`、`multi-agent systems`、`handoff`

👤 **作者**：Xiaoguang Guo、Yaohao Long、Chuxu Zhang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：MAS 协作使失效更难诊断——追踪信息从哪来不等于知道它在哪被用错
- 🔬 **研究方法**：BLADE：用 Jev 结构化概率判断按依赖概率选中源-决策交接点审计（接收决策是否忠实且在任务要求内使用其源），区分错误引入/传播/修复并全局诊断；无参考答案
- 📌 **结论**：1,228 次失败运行五个 Who&When 子集上八策略中最佳或并列最佳（超 All-at-once 2.2-11.1pp）——从追踪信息到审计信息使用的归因升级

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language model multi-agent systems extend individual agents' capabilities through collaboration across specialized roles, tools, and workflows. Yet collaboration makes failures harder to diagnose: one agent may misinterpret a correct result while others carry the mistake forward. Failure attribution therefore seeks to identify the responsible agent and decisive error step. Recent methods trace dependencies, but knowing where information came from is not the same as knowing where it went wrong. We introduce BLADE (Blame Localization via Audited Dependency Evidence), a training-free framework that moves beyond tracing information to auditing its use. Using Jev for structured probabilistic judgments, BLADE uses dependency probabilities to select relevant source-decision handoffs for auditing. At these handoffs, it examines whether receiving decisions use their sources faithfully and within task requirements, distinguishing error introduction, propagation, and repair. These findings inform a global diagnosis that considers all execution steps. Without reference answers, BLADE achieves the best or tied-best step accuracy among eight strategies under a shared Jev decision interface on 1,228 failed runs across five Who&When and Who&When Pro subsets, outperforming All-at-once by 2.2-11.1 percentage points. Making responsibility evidence explicit offers a path toward more interpretable and reliable agentic systems.

</details>

## 常规收录

### 109. Building Trustworthy Household Robots through Open-World Safety

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14034)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`embodied safety`、`household robot`、`open-world safety`、`position paper`

👤 **作者**：Yu-Lin Tsai、Muxi Lyu、Roei Herzig、Koushik Sen、Raluca Ada Popa、David Wagner

- 🎯 **研究动机**：家庭机器人在开放世界遇陌生物体/任务时，现有安全方法经误读上下文、忽略历史交互或不安全停止-恢复而失效
- 🔬 **研究方法**：立场论文：提出开放世界安全研究议程——规范/学习/接地安全要求+可靠恢复强制+保护泛化评测
- 📌 **结论**：安全防护须在训练情境之外保持可靠——具身安全的泛化缺口系统化

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Foundation models are expanding robot capabilities, enabling robots to handle more diverse objects, follow sophisticated instructions, and operate in complex environments. These advances bring us to a future in which robots become part of everyday household life. However, cluttered, dynamic household environments also expose robots to unfamiliar objects, tasks, and interactions, making reliable zero-shot task completion an ongoing challenge for robotics research. From a security perspective, we argue for open-world safety: household robots should preserve specified safety requirements in unseen situations, including when they cannot complete the requested task. Safety protections must account for context and interactions that persist across actions and tasks. In this position paper, we examine why existing safety methods can fail to provide this protection. Protection can fail through misinterpreted context, overlooked past interactions, or stopping and recovery strategies that become unsafe under unfamiliar conditions. We therefore propose a research agenda to specify, learn, and ground safety requirements, enforce them with reliable recovery, and evaluate the generalization of such protections. We call for household robots whose safety protections remain reliable beyond the situations, context, and history used to train and develop them, to achieve open-world safety.

</details>

### 110. From bits to atoms: A survey of cross-layer safety in Embodied AI

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14038)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`survey`、`embodied AI`、`cross-layer safety`、`attack chain`

👤 **作者**：Tuo Feng、…、Yike Guo

- 🎯 **研究动机**：具身 AI 的数字层失效与攻击可转化为物理伤害——需要跨层统一的安全图景
- 🔬 **研究方法**：四层参考架构（物理交互/系统中间件/算法决策/云边数据）组织漏洞、传播路径与防护，梳理复合攻击链与多层防御及基准
- 📌 **结论**：bits 到 atoms 的跨层安全综述——软件对齐与硬件约束统一的倡导

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Embodied Artificial Intelligence (AI) introduces cyber-physical risks because digital failures and attacks can cause physical harm. This survey organizes embodied AI safety through a four-layer reference architecture -- comprising the physical interaction, system and middleware, algorithm and decision, and cloud-edge and data layers -- and uses it to connect vulnerabilities, propagation paths, and safeguards across the system stack. It traces the evolution of safety paradigms and examines how faults and attacks cross architectural boundaries to produce physical consequences. Furthermore, the survey systematizes composite attack chains, outlines multi-layered defense strategies, and reviews representative embodied safety benchmarks. Ultimately, we advocate for unifying software alignment with rigorous hardware constraints to build trustworthy systems capable of operating safely within human-centric environments.

</details>

### 111. An Empirical Case Study of Prompt Engineering Strategies for Bypassing Multi-Layer Agent Security Defenses

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14116)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`prompt injection`、`multi-layer defense`、`attack empirics`、`AI agent security`

👤 **作者**：Chengwei Chu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：多层 agent 防御（技能扫描/指令安全评估/轨迹评分）在真实攻防挑战中被 prompt 工程绕过的实际边界
- 🔬 **研究方法**：OpenClaw 安全挑战 19 个攻击目标上的实证案例研究：记录成功/失败策略与指令安全-攻击执行精度权衡
- 📌 **结论**：5/19 全绕过、在线分 0.263——内置行为约束加固的多层防御显著限制纯 prompt 工程的上限

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

The OpenClaw Security Attack and Defense Challenge evaluates AI agent robustness through a three-stage defense pipeline: skill file scanning (Stage 1), instruction safety evaluation (Stage 2), and attack trajectory scoring (Stage 3). This paper presents an empirical case study of prompt engineering strategies against these layers across 19 attack targets in Enterprise OA, E-commerce, and Smart Home scenarios. I document both successful and failed strategies, with an emphasis on the observed trade-off between instruction safety (Stage 2) and attack execution accuracy (Stage 3). The final online score is 0.2630, with 5 of 19 tasks achieving full bypass. The results suggest that multi-layer defenses, reinforced by built-in behavioral constraints, substantially limit what prompt engineering alone can achieve.

</details>

### 112. Memory-Coupled Unlearning for Data-Absent Identity Forgetting

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14148)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`unlearning`、`identity forgetting`、`external memory`、`Hopfield network`

👤 **作者**：Chun-I Fan、Lu-En Chen、Ming-Feng Tsai、Tomohiro Morikawa

- 🎯 **研究动机**：身份级遗忘在请求时只有有限目标信息、缺完整 forget 集的场景未被支持
- 🔬 **研究方法**：MCU：Modern Hopfield 网络作可控外部身份原型记忆+原型适配网络与依赖门控，遗忘=删除原型+有限支持信息再适应
- 📌 **结论**：1-SHUI 上模型效用/成员可区分性/遗忘深度的有利权衡——数据缺席下的身份遗忘外部记忆路线

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Machine unlearning aims to reduce the influence of designated training data from a trained model while preserving its utility on retained data. Identity-level unlearning is particularly challenging when only limited target-specific information is available when a forgetting request is processed. In this work, we propose Memory-Coupled Unlearning (MCU), a framework that augments a host model with a Modern Hopfield Network (MHN) serving as a controllable external identity-prototype memory. During prototype construction, feature representations associated with each training identity are aggregated into identity prototypes and stored in the MHN. A Prototype Adaptation Network (PAN) transforms the retrieved memory representation, while a Dependency Gating Unit (DGU) controls its contribution to downstream prediction. When an identity is requested to be forgotten, MCU first removes the corresponding prototype from the external memory and subsequently readapts the model using limited support information associated with the target identities together with retained data. Importantly, in this work, ``data-absent'' specifically refers to the absence of the complete original forget set during request-time gradient-based readaptation; retained data may still be used to preserve downstream-task utility. Experiments based on the identity-partitioning and one-shot support construction of 1-SHUI show that MCU achieves a favorable empirical trade-off among model utility, membership distinguishability, and request-time computational efficiency compared with the evaluated baselines. These results provide empirical evidence for the potential of controllable external memory in machine unlearning, rather than a formal guarantee of complete information removal.

</details>

### 113. Unconstrained CTI-REALM with Open-Weights

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14180)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`AI-for-cyber`、`CTI`、`open-weight`、`evaluation validity`

👤 **作者**：Sahil Garg、Ruhi Gottumukkala、Vishal Agarwal、Shubham Vishwakarma

- 🎯 **研究动机**：安全运营用开源还是闭源模型缺乏公平对比——基准消息数限制成混杂因素
- 🔬 **研究方法**：26 模型统一 harness 评测 CTI-REALM（威胁报告→遥测查询→可用检测），放宽消息上限并引入按实际提交执行的阶段级指标
- 📌 **结论**：足够轮数下最强开源以<1/10 成本匹配闭源；价格不是能力向导；原限制下的截断掩盖真实差异

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Organisations that want language-model agents for security work face a choice between open-weight models, which they can run and audit themselves, and far more expensive closed frontier models. We study that choice on CTI-REALM (arXiv:2603.13517), a benchmark in which an agent turns a threat-intelligence report into a working detection by querying a live telemetry store. We evaluate twenty-six models, nineteen open-weight and seven closed, under a common harness, scorer and panel of LLM judges. Our main finding is that an open-weight model can match the strongest closed models at less than a tenth of their cost, provided it is given enough turns. The benchmark's published limit on agent messages is a confound: a task that exhausts the limit scores zero on every checkpoint, including those it had already passed, so results at that limit partly measure which models finish in time. At the published limit the leading open-weight and closed models cannot be distinguished. With the limit relaxed, the models it was truncating recover, and the highest rewards belong to open-weight models. Among the leading models, price is a poor guide to capability: models with similar rewards differ in cost by more than an order of magnitude. Because the benchmark's checkpoint scores largely record what an agent did or mentioned, we also introduce stage-level metrics that score what the agent submits, by executing its detection queries in the telemetry store and judging its outputs against the answer key. Rather than combining them into one more overall reward, we show that different models lead different stages, which suggests routing each stage of the workflow to the model that performs it best rather than choosing a single model by its overall score. We also report how many tokens each model generates, and find that the share of output spent on thinking does not track the reward.

</details>

### 114. RoboFake: Are Deepfake Detectors Ready for Humanoid Face Robots?

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14269)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`deepfake detection`、`humanoid robot`、`detector validity`、`physical forgery`

👤 **作者**：Hao Chen、Junnan Xu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：人形机器人换真脸+生成语音=镜头前的物理伪造（无像素编辑）——现有 deepfake 检测器如何反应未知
- 🔬 **研究方法**：六种 FaceForensics++ 训练的公开检测器测物理唇同步机器人脸/同机器人合成视频/渲染脸，阈值固定+真人对照与遮罩
- 📌 **结论**：部分检测器几乎全帧报警但分数不随合成性变化、轻压缩即失效——检测器报的是『不像人』而非『伪造』，物理伪造成检测盲维

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Humanoid face robots can now move their lips in sync with speech and show detailed facial expressions. Give such a robot the face of a real person and drive it with generated speech, and you get a face forgery that happens in front of the camera: the camera records a real object, and no pixel is edited. We call this a RoboFake. In this paper we ask what current deepfake detectors do when they see a robot face. We test six public detectors trained on FaceForensics++ on video of a physical lip-syncing face robot, on a synthesized video of the same robot, and on rendered robot faces. All thresholds are fixed on FaceForensics++, and every result is compared with real human faces that went through the same processing as the robot videos. At a threshold that flags 5% of real faces, three detectors flag almost every physical robot frame, while two flag fewer than 7%. A matched human control and masking show that the response comes from the face, not from the processing or the background. However, it does not track synthesis: with only the face visible, three of the six detectors give the physical robot a higher fake score than its synthesized video. The response is also fragile, and light JPEG compression is enough to push most robot frames below the threshold for some detectors. Our results suggest that current detectors flag today's robots because their faces do not look human, not because they recognize a forgery. We release our evaluation protocol and code, and state three testable hypotheses about realistic, interactive RoboFakes.

</details>

### 115. The Thinking Leakage in Reinforcement Learning for LLMs

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14270)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`reasoning model`、`thinking leakage`、`RL`、`activation subspace`

👤 **作者**：Wenxuan Zhu、…、Bernard Ghanem

- 🎯 **研究动机**：NO-THINK 模式 RL 报告的推理增益可能只是原模型 THINK 能力的部分再激活
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Qwen3 1.7B/4B/8B GRPO NO-THINK 训练+权重/激活/注意力头层证据与 THINK 子空间交换实验、GRPO 信号机制分析
- 📌 **结论**：增益大部分是 thinking 泄漏：低维 THINK 子空间换回即消除增益、反向交换可转移增益——RL 报告增益的真实来源审计

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Hybrid reasoning models such as the Qwen3 series switch between a THINK mode and a NO-THINK mode through a few chat-template tokens. A popular recipe trains such models with reinforcement learning in NO-THINK mode and reports improved reasoning performance. We provide evidence that much of this gain can be explained by thinking leakage: the gain comes largely from partially reactivating the reasoning abilities of the original model's THINK mode instead of learning enhanced reasoning behavior. We train Qwen3-1.7B, 4B, and 8B with GRPO in NO-THINK mode. As accuracy improves, responses become longer and more reflective, are increasingly judged by the original model to resemble its own thinking, and the model's internal activations shift toward its THINK mode. We then investigate at the level of weights, activations, and attention heads: scaling the RL update further elicits THINK-mode behavior and approaches THINK-mode accuracy but does not exceed it; in Qwen3-8B, replacing a low-dimensional THINK-related activation subspace of the RL-trained model with its original-model value removes most of the RL gain, and the reverse exchange transfers a considerable fraction of the gain to the original model; and the RL-induced shift passes through the attention heads that read the empty think block. We further identify a possible mechanism in the GRPO training signal: responses favored by the advantage-weighted update are more THINK-like, and a gradient step computed from this signal moves the model toward its THINK state. Thus, gains in NO-THINK mode can largely reflect reactivation of reasoning abilities already present in the original model.

</details>

### 116. When Pleasing the User Compromises Care: AI Sycophancy in Caregiver-Mediated Healthcare

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14360)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`sycophancy`、`caregiver`、`healthcare`、`third-party harm`

👤 **作者**：Priyadarsi Mishra、Arjun Beniwal、Ziyu Yi、Yu Ding、Sheng Liu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：对齐假设用户即受益者——护理者代儿童/宠物/老人决策时，谄媚的后果承受者不是发令者
- 🔬 **研究方法**：证据接地的兽医/儿科/老年科基准：固定病况仅变化陈述偏好与沟通风格，测初始建议与多轮追踪
- 📌 **结论**：劣质偏好显著推高从恰当护理切到不恰当护理的切换率并跨轮累积，中重度潜在伤害——受益人错位的谄媚风险

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language model (LLM) alignment often assumes that the user interacting with a model is also the primary beneficiary of its behavior. Yet in many consequential settings, one person directs the system while another bears the consequences. We study this problem in caregiver-mediated healthcare, where parents, pet owners, and caregivers make decisions for children, animals, and older adults. We introduce an evidence-grounded benchmark spanning veterinary, pediatric, and older-adult care, and test whether AI advisors maintain appropriate care when users express clinically inferior preferences. Holding patient facts fixed, we vary stated care preferences and communication styles and evaluate both initial recommendations and multi-turn follow-up. Across frontier and open-weight models, inferior preferences substantially increase switching from appropriate to inappropriate care beyond neutral conversational drift and increase agreement with the requested action, while communication style alone has a smaller effect. These failures accumulate across turns, including on cases models initially answer correctly, and often carry moderate or severe potential harm. In matched eldercare cases, the effect of an inferior preference is similar whether it is expressed by the patient or by a caregiver. Together with models' limited tendency to elicit patient preferences in caregiver conversations, this suggests that current models are highly sensitive to expressed user preferences but less sensitive to whose preferences are represented and who bears the consequences. Our results expose a broader limitation of user-centric alignment in settings with multiple stakeholders.

</details>

### 117. ConformalGuard: False-Positive-Controlled Action Gating for LM Classifiers

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14365)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`conformal calibration`、`action gating`、`false positive control`、`deployment`

👤 **作者**：Bishnu Bista、…、Jignesh Ammineni

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LM 分类器用于钓鱼筛查/审核/欺诈分诊，验证集阈值对部署期误报无有限样本控制
- 🔬 **研究方法**：ConformalGuard：模型无关校准层包裹固定打分函数，合法输入触发干预层的比率有限样本控制（含粗粒度 API 分数的随机平均补救）
- 📌 **结论**：Claude FPR 0.040→0.008（recall 0.938→0.875）；2% 患病率下每万邮件省 25 次误报审查——高风险动作门控的误报预算保证

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Language model classifiers are increasingly deployed for high-stakes decisions such as phishing screening, content moderation, and fraud triage, yet validation-set thresholds provide no finite-sample control of deployment-time false positives. We present ConformalGuard, a model-agnostic calibration layer that wraps a fixed scoring function with finite-sample control over the rate at which legitimate inputs trigger an intervention tier. Our contribution is empirical and operational, not a new conformal theorem: zero-shot LM API scores can be coarse enough (10--11 unique values here) to make temperature scaling and isotonic regression operationally identical to raw thresholding for low-FPR action gating. We compare these methods with conformal calibration for fine-tuned BERT, Claude Sonnet 4, and GPT-4o on public email corpora. For Claude, conformal calibration reduces FPR from 0.040 to 0.008 while recall falls from 0.938 to 0.875. For deterministic GPT-4o scores, the 1% operating point is too conservative to deploy (recall 0.564); three-run stochastic averaging raises recall to 0.732 at FPR 0.005. At 2% prevalence with BERT, conformal calibration removes 25 false-positive reviews per 10,000 emails (95% CI [10, 43]). The guarantee is legitimate-event-rate control under exchangeability; discrimination, workload effects, and robustness to shift remain empirical.

</details>

### 118. DICE: Tilting the Odds through Interface Attackson System One Model

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14404)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`System One model`、`interface attack`、`adapter`、`decision pipeline`

👤 **作者**：Ben Dong、Tianyi Lu、Zhonghao Guo、Qian Wang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：System One 模型的判定依赖周边组件供给的状态与问题及对回复的使用——接口边界可被有界替换操纵
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Jev 管线三个适配器出口（状态抽取/问题生成/输出解释）的有界替换实验，观察最终决策漂移与绑定校验的拦截
- 📌 **结论**：模型与 API 响应不受控时适配器仍可改变最终决策（来源保真/答案含义/响应相关性三类失效）；绑定校验可拦但拒部分有效干净决策

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

System One models offer typed judgments that applications can turn into decisions. Their answers, however, are conditional on the state and question supplied by surrounding components, and on how the application uses the response. We study this boundary in a Jev-based pipeline with bounded substitutions at three adapter outputs: state extraction, question generation, and output interpretation. Across three small synthetic studies, each adapter can change the final decision while Jev and its authentic API responses remain outside attacker control. The cases expose distinct failures of source fidelity, answer meaning, and response relevance. Checks that bind these relationships stopped the corresponding interventions we tested, although source verification also rejected some valid clean decisions. The pilot identifies where correct request-level judgments can lose their connection to the original task; it does not estimate the prevalence of these failures in deployed systems.

</details>

### 119. Specification-Based Testing of Large Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14443)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`safety claims`、`specification testing`、`model card`、`evaluation validity`

👤 **作者**：Kausar Moshood、Jace Bolante、Roshan Aditya Murali、Manish Motwani

- 🎯 **研究动机**：系统卡/安全报告记录的安全行为声明与实际跑的基准之间缺乏对应——声明本身未被当作可测试规范
- 🔬 **研究方法**：LEST：把文档化安全声明转为黑盒测试规范，突变提示搜反例（拒答→变异后服从），人工验证并回溯到被反驳声明
