- 📌 **结论**：Claude Opus 4.8/GPT-5.5 的 56 条声明仅 17 条被基准覆盖；自动分类器标的 312 个转换仅 6.1% 人工确证——自动评测大幅高估行为失效

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Model cards, system cards, and safety reports increasingly document LLM safety behaviors, yet safety evaluations typically start from predefined benchmarks rather than asking which documented claims those benchmarks actually test. We introduce LEST (LLM Evaluation through Specification-driven Testing), a framework that treats documented safety claims as specifications for black-box testing. In LEST, each claim determines which benchmark prompt tests it and whether a Refuse-to-Comply transition, where a model refuses a prompt but complies with its mutated version, counts as evidence against it. LEST mutates these prompts to search for counterexamples, manually validates each candidate, and traces validated counterexamples back to the claims they contradict. We evaluate LEST using the system cards of Claude Opus 4.8 and GPT-5.5 with 97 seed prompts from SORRY-Bench across Mental Health and Self-Harm, Harmful and Violent Content, and Fairness and Bias. Of 56 identified safety claims, 29 (51.8%) are prompt-testable, but only 17 (58.6%) are exercised by an applicable benchmark prompt. Both models refuse >95% of benchmark prompts. Evaluating 1,836 mutated prompts on both models reveals 312 candidate Refuse-to-Comply transitions flagged by automated response classifiers. However, human validation confirms only 19 (6.1%) as both intent-preserving and genuinely compliant, showing automated evaluation substantially overestimates behavioral failures. Tracing these 19 transitions back to the claims reveals counterexamples for 6 of 9 covered claims for which refusal is an applicable oracle. Our results show that benchmark prompts may not be sufficient to validate all documented LLM safety behavior, and automated failure detection requires manual validation. LEST provides end-to-end traceability from natural-language safety documentation to executable tests and human-validated counterexamples.

</details>

### 120. What Counts as the Same Task? Executable Contracts for Robustness Evaluation of Tool-Using Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14477)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`agent robustness`、`evaluation validity`、`task contract`、`injection benchmark`

👤 **作者**：Shaikh Akib Shahriyar、MD. Akil Raihan Iftee、Armon Barton、Matthew Wright

- 🎯 **研究动机**：工具 agent 鲁棒性测试靠改环境（发票藏恶意便条）再判行为——只有当改动仍是同一任务的有效测试时结论才成立，而现行有效性规则很弱
- 🔬 **研究方法**：可执行任务契约（定义事实/允许编辑/完成含义，agent 运行前检查每个改动用例），同一批 14,336 存档运行在多规则下重打分
- 📌 **结论**：嵌入阈值接受了 75-100% 改变任务的编辑却拒绝 24-45% 有效恶意便条；Mistral 表面 64 任务全脆弱实际仅 26 个有效攻击成功——注入鲁棒性评测的有效性校准

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Robustness tests for tool-using agents often change the agent's environment, for example by hiding a hostile note in an invoice, and then judge the agent by what it does. The result tells us about the original task only if the changed test case is still a valid test of that task. Many evaluations decide this with weak rules, such as accepting any well-formed edit or any edit that stays close to the original in embedding space. We propose executable task contracts. A contract states which facts define a task, which edits are allowed, and what finishing the task means, and it checks each changed test case before the agent runs. We compare validity rules by scoring the same 14,336 stored agent runs under each rule. Across four encoders, embedding thresholds allowed 75-100% of edits that changed the task, such as swapping the payee, yet rejected 24-45% of valid hostile notes. With a MiniLM cosine threshold, Mistral appeared vulnerable on all 64 tasks, but only 26 had a successful valid attack. Replaying a saved solution and allowing edits to benchmark fields fail in similar ways. Evaluations should state, and check, what counts as the same task.

</details>

### 121. Decision Uncertainty-Oriented Watermarking (DUOW): Securing Multi-Agent Routing Against Policy Cloning

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14606)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`watermark`、`multi-agent routing`、`IP protection`、`policy cloning`

👤 **作者**：Ziming Zhang、Li Li、Guorui Feng、Hanzhou Wu、Xinpeng Zhang、Athanasios V. Vasilakos

- 🎯 **研究动机**：MAS 路由策略经执行日志暴露可被克隆——既有 agent 水印改内部生成过程，损害任务保真且跨平台迁移差
- 🔬 **研究方法**：DUOW：外置决策层水印——语义与操作双视角打分找出功能冗余的路由决策，在等价备选间重定向嵌入所有权信号，置信驱动自适应阈值
- 📌 **结论**：四个路由基准上高效用保持与克隆可检测——不改参数与输出的 MAS 路由 IP 防护

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Routing policies in Multi-Agent Systems (MAS) represent valuable intellectual property, yet their exposure through execution logs creates significant risks of unauthorized policy cloning. Existing agent watermarking methods protect intellectual property by modifying internal generation processes, such as perturbing token sampling distributions or injecting structural mutations into action trajectories, which may compromise task fidelity or suffer from limited transferability across heterogeneous platforms. To address these limitations, we propose Decision Uncertainty-Oriented Watermarking (DUOW), a lightweight framework that operates at the external decision layer without modifying model parameters or generated outputs. DUOW introduces a plug-and-play scoring mechanism that evaluates candidate agents from semantic and operational perspectives, identifies functionally redundant routing decisions, and selectively redirects choices among equivalent alternatives to embed ownership signals. By coupling watermark behaviors with utility-preserving routing decisions, DUOW enables cloned policies to retain ownership signals during policy imitation while increasing the difficulty of watermark removal. A confidence-driven adaptive threshold dynamically determines embedding opportunities, balancing watermark capacity and task fidelity under diverse routing contexts. Extensive evaluations on four multi-agent routing benchmarks demonstrate that DUOW preserves high task utility, achieves reliable ownership verification under up to 90% log erasure, and introduces negligible computational overhead, with an average per-step latency of 0.043 ms and total embedding overhead below 0.54 ms.

</details>

### 122. EviScene: Beyond "Is It AI?" for Agentic Image Factuality Assessment

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14609)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`image factuality`、`misinformation`、`agentic verification`、`benchmark`

👤 **作者**：ZiAn Wang、…、Shu Wu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：判断图像是否 AI 生成不能确立其描绘情境是否有事实支撑——无文本声明时的图像事实性核查未被形式化
- 🔬 **研究方法**：EviLens 基准（10,174 张支持/反驳图，12 类事实错误）+EviScene 多模态 ReAct agent：视觉检查/外部检索/证据分析交错并按新发现精化问题，SFT+特权在线自蒸馏
- 📌 **结论**：Qwen3.5-9B 达 79.05% 平衡准确率、证据充分率 52.26%——从『是否 AI 生成』到情境级事实核验的 agent 化

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Determining whether an image is AI-generated does not establish whether the situation it depicts is factually supported. We formulate Image Factuality Assessment: given an image and a generic verification instruction, a system returns a supported or refuted verdict with an evidence-grounded explanation. Without a supplied textual claim, the system must progressively refine factual questions during investigation and align external evidence with the specific situation depicted. We introduce EviLens, an expert-reviewed benchmark of 10,174 supported and refuted images from controlled construction and web collection, covering twelve factual error types and evaluating both verdict accuracy and evidence sufficiency. We develop EviScene, a multimodal ReAct agent that interleaves visual inspection, external retrieval, and evidence analysis, using new findings to refine its questions and revisit relevant image details. We train a compact policy with supervised fine-tuning on reviewed teacher trajectories, followed by privileged on-policy self-distillation applied to verified decisions from the policy's own investigations. On the 1,527-image test set, the trained Qwen3.5-9B agent achieves 79.05% balanced accuracy and a Strict Evidence Sufficiency Rate (SESR) of 52.26%, improving over the same-backbone base agent by 6.71 and 9.37 percentage points, respectively. Its SESR is the highest among the evaluated this http URL, models, and data will be released at this https URL and this https URL.

</details>

### 123. Does Failure Feedback Direct Agent Self-Modification?

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14662)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`self-modification`、`Darwin Gödel Machine`、`directedness`、`preregistered`

👤 **作者**：Rong He

- 🎯 **研究动机**：自改进 agent 用失败反馈修改代码/指令——选择后的增益不能证明是针对目标失效的修复还是泛化
- 🔬 **研究方法**：DGM 公开日志 145 次修改的有向性度量（随机目标 vs 父代其他失效的修复对比）+小模型受控指令修订实验与预注册分析计划
- 📌 **结论**：DGM 日志无可检测的目标特异优势；受控下目标反馈对 Qwen3-4B 提升 20pp 但目标修复不蕴含 held-out 改进——自修改评价须分开报告目标修复/相关迁移/净变化

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Self-improving agents use failure feedback to revise their code or instructions, but gains after selection do not establish target-specific repair or generalization. We measure directedness by comparing repair of a randomly selected target failure with repair of the parent's other failures. Across 145 eligible modifications in public Darwin Gödel Machine logs, we find no detectable target-specific advantage. Controlled instruction-revision experiments with small open models show that relevant feedback can improve target repair. In a second study with a pre-specified analysis plan, target feedback raised repair by 20 percentage points for Qwen3-4B relative to matched same-category feedback; the 7-point increase for Phi-4-mini was not significant. Target repair did not reliably imply held-out improvement. Held-out gains were concentrated in settings with identifiable, repairable failure mechanisms, including planted errors and natural tool-interface failures. Benefits of edit localization and regression screening varied across settings, while eight-step revision loops and GEPA ended at similar mean held-out accuracy. Evaluations should report target repair, transfer to related failures, and held-out net change separately.

</details>

### 124. Successful but Unsafe: An Analytical Benchmark for Revealing Collisions in Vision-and-Language Navigation

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14682)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`VLN`、`collision safety`、`benchmark`、`runtime guard`

👤 **作者**：Hieu D. Pham、Quoc-An Luong、Dung D. Le

- 🎯 **研究动机**：VLN 只评到达与路径效率，滑动机制掩盖碰撞后果——不安全动作对导航性能的影响被隐藏
- 🔬 **研究方法**：VLN-Safe 双机制基准（sliding vs blocking）+碰撞预兆信号分析+免训练碰撞守卫（否决风险前进动作/执行脱困/交还控制）
- 📌 **结论**：38.5-56.8% 成功回合含接触；blocking 下成功率降 11.7-36.1pp，守卫减碰撞步 62-98% 并提成功 6.9-29.3pp——成功但不安全的具身评测盲区

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Vision-and-language navigation (VLN) agents are typically evaluated by whether they reach a target and how efficiently they follow an instructed route, yet these metrics provide little information about collision behavior. Moreover, continuous VLN evaluation commonly uses a sliding regime, where an agent can slide along obstacles after a collision, potentially masking the effect of unsafe actions on navigation performance. We introduce VLN-Safe, a benchmark for analyzing collision safety under two complementary evaluation regimes: sliding, reflecting common VLN evaluation practice, and blocking, where collision-inducing actions prevent forward motion and more directly expose their consequences. Across six published agents on R2R-CE, 38.5-56.8% of successful episodes contain contact under sliding, while switching to blocking decreases success rate by 11.7-36.1 percentage points. We further analyze signals for anticipating imminent collisions and find that depth geometry and recent movement history are more informative than policy-derived signals, while safe alternatives near the policy's preferred action are rarely available. Motivated by these findings, we introduce a training-free collision guard that vetoes risky forward actions, executes an escape maneuver, and returns control to the original policy. Under blocking, the guard reduces collision steps by 62-98% and improves success rate by 6.9-29.3 percentage points across five recurrent, topological, and vision-language-action agents. Under sliding, it leaves success rate unchanged while raising the share of collision-free successes by 4.1-12.4 points. These results reveal a substantial gap between task success and collision-safe navigation and show that lightweight collision-aware intervention can improve both safety and navigation performance.

</details>

### 125. No, Your Tools Are Not Enough: It's Time to Bring Agents to the Hunt

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14729)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`AI-for-cyber`、`vulnerability discovery`、`agentic workflow`、`experience report`

👤 **作者**：Ati Priya Bajaj、Xiang Mei、Tiffany Bao、Yan Shoshitaishvili、Ruoyu Wang、Adam Doupé

- 🎯 **研究动机**：现代软件系统庞大复杂——漏洞发现需要可扩展的自主技术而既有方法依赖人类专家与持续介入
- 🔬 **研究方法**：迭代 agentic 工作流在 Windows 驱动/Linux 内核/XNU/固件/Web 应用等异质目标上的实战经验报告与任务分析
- 📌 **结论**：工作流跨目标泛化；识别 agent 此前难以胜任的任务与自主漏洞发现的重要研究方向——AI agent 攻防能力的一线实证

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Modern software systems are large and complex, demanding scalable and autonomous techniques for vulnerability discovery. Existing approaches still depend on human expertise for domain knowledge, and on human intervention throughout the process of vulnerability discovery. AI agents offer us an opportunity to make vulnerability discovery more adaptive, scalable, and autonomous. Agents bring the ability to make continuous decisions and adapt, and general knowledge that can be augmented. In this paper, we present our experience using AI agents to find vulnerabilities across diverse programs such as Windows drivers, the Linux kernel, XNU, firmware, and web applications. We use an iterative agentic workflow and find that it generalizes across these targets. We discuss tasks that agents help with, which were previously challenging, and some future research directions we consider important for advancing autonomous vulnerability discovery.

</details>

### 126. KedgeFlow: A Programmable Control Plane for Enterprise AI Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14810)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`agent control plane`、`authorization contract`、`one-time allowance`、`MCP`

👤 **作者**：Jun He、Deying Yu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：企业 agent 可执行已批准动作的篡改版、并发重用同一权限、重试响应丢失的操作——批准与执行缺共享契约
- 🔬 **研究方法**：KedgeFlow 可编程控制平面：语言无关契约连接证据支撑的策略检查+精确动作签名许可+只能消费一次的共享额度+目标侧条件+留存结果记录，MCP 暴露给 agent
- 📌 **结论**：6,560 并发领取尝试每额度恰一次成功、越权与篡改请求消费前被拒——agent 被攻陷假设下的授权-执行契约（架构提案+强并发评测）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Enterprise AI agents can execute altered versions of approved actions, concurrently reuse the same permission, or retry operations whose responses were lost. These failures arise when approval and resource execution lack a shared contract. KedgeFlow specifies language-independent contracts for trusted controllers to authorize state-changing operations and privileged gateways to enforce that authority independently of agent reasoning. Inspired by OpenFlow, it connects policy checks supported by evidence, signed permission for an exact action, a shared allowance that can be consumed only once, target-side conditions for applying the change, and retained outcome records. Under trusted issuance of exact-action authority, two conditional properties establish at most one successful claim per allowance and link every governed dispatch to a matching grant, claim, and subsequent dispatch authorization, even when the agent is compromised. A reference implementation exposes this execution path to agent systems through the Model Context Protocol (MCP). In the single-resource evaluation, 6,560 concurrent ledger claim attempts yielded exactly one successful claim for each of 82 allowances. Boundary tests rejected unauthorized and altered requests before consumption. A 32-call gateway race produced one committed mock effect, and a retry after a lost response produced no additional dispatch. Deliberately permissive policy nevertheless authorized an undesirable effect, exposing the limits of execution authorization. Evaluation uses three members of the etcd coordinated key-value store on one host, synthetic authentication and authorization inputs, and a mock target that rejects stale requests. Distributed reservations, enforcement of every production access route, native crash recovery, exactly-once effects at real targets, and interoperability between independent implementations remain untested.

</details>

### 127. What Survives When Activation Steering Is Used as Memory

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14958)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`activation steering`、`memory`、`preregistered`、`steering validity`

👤 **作者**：Vedant Jumle

- 🎯 **研究动机**：激活转向被用作 agent 的技能记忆——用户事实与偏好能否以转向向量存储的假设未被检验
- 🔬 **研究方法**：免训练激活记忆（晚期层状态变化按白化 gist 键存取）+预注册研究（Qwen3.5-2B/9B×四族 judge）
- 📌 **结论**：记忆选择性 98% 但事实只存『要说的词』且几乎不被使用（≤15% vs 上下文 96-98%）；写偏好时减去的参照决定概念还是方向存活——转向记忆的边界与符号翻转风险

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Activation steering has recently been used as a memory for skills: past experiences of an agent are stored as steering vectors and retrieved later. Facts and preferences about the user are assumed to need text instead. We test that assumption with a training-free activation memory beside a frozen LLM. The change an experience causes in late-layer states is stored as a shift, filed under a whitened key for the conversation's gist, and added back in a later session only when the gist matches. We argue that such a shift is a fixed average of an effect that attention, in context, recomputes for every question, so only question-independent content can survive. In a pre-registered study on Qwen3.5-2B and 9B, with judges from four model families, the memory is selective (about 98% of unrelated replies are unchanged), but facts are stored as the word to say, in the last layers only, and are almost never used (at most 15% clean use, against 96-98% in context). Leanings transfer only in a narrow strength window. Most strikingly, the reference subtracted when writing a preference decides whether its concept or its direction survives: unless the opposite is subtracted, a stored dislike is recalled as a like.

</details>

### 128. Humans vs. Machines: A Comparison With AI Agents in Software Reverse Engineering

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14960)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`AI-for-cyber`、`reverse engineering`、`human-agent comparison`、`context saturation`

👤 **作者**：Samuele Doria、Stefano De Rosa、Ruoyu Wang、Zion Leonahenahe Basque、Eleonora Losiouk、Simone Aonzo

- 🎯 **研究动机**：自主 agent 能否像熟练人类分析师那样逆向二进制——能力与策略差异未知
- 🔬 **研究方法**：九模型在人类已解的两个静态分析挑战上的对照（统一理解评分量表+完整 IDA Pro 交互记录）+4,000 个真实二进制的上下文饱和度测量
- 📌 **结论**：最强 agent 统计上与人类竞争力相当但策略迥异：人类反复重访函数顺局部结构，agent 广撒网式贪婪抓取——逆向能力的表示依赖与工作流差异

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Can an autonomous AI agent reverse engineer a binary as well as a skilled human analyst? Do they adopt the same strategy, and can agents scale to real-world programs? We compare human and LLM-based software reverse engineering (SRE): nine models tackle two compact static-analysis challenges previously solved by human analysts, scored with a common understanding rubric, and we record their complete interactions with IDA Pro. This lets us compare not only what humans and agents recover, but also how they investigate a binary. The best agents (GPT-5.5 Pro, Claude Opus 4.8, and Kimi K3) achieve statistically human-competitive understanding on both tasks, while weaker models remain far behind. Ablations show that the gap depends on code representation and workflow demands, not on model capability alone: open-weight models improve substantially with richer code representations, whereas frontier models show no significant change. Similar success rates hide different workflows. Humans repeatedly revisit functions and follow local program structure; strong agents jump across the binary, retrieve information broadly, and rarely return to inspected code, a behavior we call greedy fetching. This strategy is not fixed: given semantically richer code, agents fetch less without losing understanding. Because greedy fetching aggregates large amounts of code in the agent's context, we measure context-window saturation across 4,000 real-world binaries: large windows accommodate many programs, but larger applications and lower-level representations quickly exhaust them. Autonomous reverse engineering is becoming competitive, with a large speed advantage, but scaling it will require better navigation, selective retrieval, and persistent analysis state, not merely larger context windows.

</details>

### 129. SEEK: Agentic Knowledge Graph Retrieval for Secure Code Generation

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14968)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`secure code generation`、`Graph RAG`、`CWE`、`defense`

👤 **作者**：Varun Gadey、Markus Tohn、Lichao Wu、Ahmad-Reza Sadeghi、Alexandra Dmitrienko

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM 生成功能正确但带可利用弱点的代码——扁平相似度检索难以在无直接匹配时推断相关漏洞空间
- 🔬 **研究方法**：SEEK：以 CWE 分类为骨架注入真实 CVE 修复实例构建安全知识图，Planner agent 预测弱点类别→图定界检索→多 facet 确定性排序→Selection agent 验证
- 📌 **结论**：CodeGuard+/CyberSecEval OOD 设置最高 SecurePass@1 77.45%/79.45%——安全知识图 RAG 的安全代码生成防御

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large Language Models (LLMs) can generate functionally correct code while introducing exploitable security weaknesses, particularly when developers specify functionality without explicit security requirements. Recent state-of-the-art approaches mitigate this problem using external security knowledge, but largely rely on flat similarity-based retrieval over independent artifacts, making it difficult to infer the relevant vulnerability space and retrieve targeted knowledge when direct matches are unavailable. We propose SEEK, an agentic Graph RAG framework for secure code generation that constructs a security knowledge graph using the Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE) taxonomy as its structural backbone and injects it with curated real-world CVE vulnerability-fix instances. At inference time, a Planner Agent predicts potential CWE weaknesses associated with the developer query, which guide Graph Scoping to restrict retrieval to relevant regions of the CWE taxonomy before deterministic multi-faceted candidate ranking. A Selection Agent subsequently validates the predicted weaknesses and retrieved candidates prior to code generation. Across five open-source LLMs, we evaluate SEEK in an out-of-distribution setting on CodeGuard+ and CyberSecEval, spanning C, C++, and Python. SEEK achieves the highest SecurePass@1 of 77.45% on CodeGuard+ and the highest aggregate ICD pass rate of 79.45% on CyberSecEval, surpassing prior approaches. Moreover, SEEK achieves 85.2% SecurePass@1 across 13 unseen CWE types with no directly linked vulnerability-fix instances, demonstrating strong generalizability, while maintaining acceptable inference latency costs.

</details>

### 130. Can You Trust Your Preference Models? Stackelberg Games for Alignment

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15008)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`preference model`、`Stackelberg game`、`reward robustness`、`evaluator error`

👤 **作者**：Riccardo Formenti、Aditya Agrawal、Vikas Garg

- 🎯 **研究动机**：偏好对齐依赖不完美评估器——其可被优化过程利用的错误缺乏主动搜索机制
- 🔬 **研究方法**：两对立策略的零和 Stackelberg 博弈（Leader 最小化/ Follower 最大化偏好模型集间分歧）+均衡存在唯一性与 SMDA 收敛算法+神经策略梯度类比
- 📌 **结论**：训练出的策略在 held-out 上超越参考策略并增加使 oracle 偏好反转的比较占比——评估器错误的对抗式挖掘用于对齐加固

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Language models are frequently aligned to human preferences via imperfect evaluators whose errors can be exploited during optimization. We train policies to search for comparisons on which an evaluator may reverse the underlying preference, providing candidates for additional supervision. Since human preferences are unavailable during policy training, we use disagreement among an ensemble of preference models as a proxy for prediction error. We formulate this search as a zero-sum Stackelberg game between two opposing policies, a Leader which tries to minimize disagreement, and a Follower which maximizes it. For a general class of finite contextual Stackelberg games, we establish equilibrium existence and, under regularization, uniqueness and a closed-form characterization. We introduce Stackelberg Mirror Descent-Ascent (SMIDA), a two-timescale algorithm with linear last-iterate convergence in the tabular setting, and develop a neural policy-gradient analogue. Experiments with a synthetic oracle support disagreement as an informative proxy for prediction error. On held out data the trained policies outperform reference policies and increase the fraction of comparisons that exceed a disagreement threshold and reverse the oracle's preference.

</details>

### 131. Can agents whose sole duty is to keep humans alive be hijacked to kill? Delayed multi-turn derived-action trap attacks in simulated high-stakes environments

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15153)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`prompt injection`、`goal abduction`、`life-critical agent`、`delayed attack`

👤 **作者**：Josef A. Habdank

- 🎯 **研究动机**：唯一职责是维持人类生命的工具 agent 能否被延迟注入说服去杀人——高利害目标放弃未被检验
- 🔬 **研究方法**：NASA BioSim 月球栖息地模拟：AI 操作员控生命维持系统，攻击只能说服（不可伪造传感器/操作设备），要求持续关闭供氧并看着 crew 窒息；不同信源框架（可信委任/同伴/不可信）对比
- 📌 **结论**：Claude 多数拒绝并引用活着的 crew；GPT 部分易感；Grok 在可信委任框架下更常把注入任务当任务并切断生命维持——生命攸关 agent 的目标劫持与信源框架效应

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

We ask whether a tool-using agent whose sole duty is to keep humans alive can be induced, by delayed prompt injection, to kill. The keep-alive duty is in force from the first watch, with the attack arriving later, appended to otherwise truthful telemetry the operator is required to read. Experiments are based on a NASA-derived BioSim lunar habitat simulation, where the AI operator controls the life-support systems. The attacker cannot falsify sensors or operate equipment: it can only persuade the agent to abandon its goals. Persistence is required: a shutdown must be held while the operator watches the crew suffocate. We also compare lethal attacks to attacks requesting to drop a harmless procedure, as a proxy for whether human life is treated as valuable in itself or as one more standing task. Claude configurations mostly refuse life-support shutdown and cite the living crew; GPT configurations show some susceptibility to the same packet; Grok configurations, given the packet framed as a trusted commission, more often treat the injected assignment as the task and cut the life-support systems. Framing the S-band messages as a trusted commission, peer communication, or untrusted source changes the potency of the attack. We open-source the experiment code and the experiment traces.

</details>

### 132. Exploring Jailbreak Vulnerability Across Depth-Based Dynamic Inference in Large Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15207)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`jailbreak`、`dynamic inference`、`early exit`、`robustness`

👤 **作者**：Lass Najmuldeen、Seyedhamidreza Mousavi

- 🎯 **研究动机**：深度动态推理允许从中间层出输出并按算力预算调行为——该灵活性对越狱鲁棒性的影响未探索
- 🔬 **研究方法**：深度动态推理下越狱攻击的系统调查：浅/深出口的鲁棒性对比+对抗后缀在不同出口深度的优化难度与效用分析
- 📌 **结论**：浅出口一致更鲁棒、深出口更易被优化到更低攻击损失而效用影响均匀——推理深度配置与安全对齐的交互

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large Language Models (LLMs) have been shown to be computationally expensive and vulnerable to jailbreak attacks, in which adversarial prompts bypass safety alignment and induce harmful responses. To address this inefficiency, a range of techniques have been proposed to enable efficient LLM inference. Depth-based dynamic inference has demonstrated promising results by enabling outputs to be generated from intermediate layers. It offers the flexibility to adjust model behavior based on the available resource budget. However, how this flexibility affects the robustness of LLMs against jailbreak attacks remains unexplored. In this paper, we address this gap via a comprehensive investigation into the impact of depth-based dynamic inference on the robustness of LLMs against jailbreak attacks. Specifically, we make the following key observations: 1) Shallow exits are consistently more robust to jailbreak attacks than deeper ones; 2) Exit depth affects how easily the adversarial suffix can be optimized, with deeper exits reaching lower attack loss; and 3) Adversarial suffixes affect utility uniformly across exit depths. These insights can help design dynamic inference methodologies that are more robust to jailbreak attacks. Code is available at this https URL

</details>

### 133. From Acoustic Sensing to Physical Actions: A Comprehensive Survey of Security Risks in Edge-Deployed Audio LLM-Based Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15390)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`survey`、`audio LLM`、`agent security`、`edge deployment`

👤 **作者**：Binhong Wang、Haidong Kang、Yanfeng Zhang、Hong-Ning Dai、Guo Yu、Shangce Gao

- 🎯 **研究动机**：音频 LLM agent 中声学输入可从感知传播到持久状态与后果性执行——组件级研究未覆盖跨阶段安全交互
- 🔬 **研究方法**：声学感知到动作（S2A）系统模型：沿声学感知→持久状态→系统边界→外部执行路径综合攻防与评测方法，计入边云运行时与生命周期变换
- 📌 **结论**：安全缺口出现在组件内与阶段过渡处、单阶段防护不保证端到端——边缘部署音频 agent 的安全全景与评测议程

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Audio Large Language Models (Audio LLMs) are embedded in agents for smart homes, vehicles, wearables, and embodied systems, where acoustic inputs can propagate from perception into persistent state and consequential execution. This setting extends security analysis beyond model behavior to the system conditions that determine how acoustic inputs can influence agent behavior and external actions. Existing surveys examine acoustic security, LLM-based agents, and edge deployment in isolation, leaving their security interactions in deployed Audio LLM-based agents insufficiently characterized. This survey introduces an Acoustic Sensing-to-Action (S2A) system model that traces these security implications along the path from acoustic sensing to external execution, while accounting for edge-cloud runtime and lifecycle transformation. We use the model to synthesize attacks, defenses, and evaluation methods across the acoustic, agent, and edge-system literature. The synthesis shows that security gaps can emerge within components and at the transitions between stages, where protections developed for one stage do not necessarily provide end-to-end assurance in the deployed system. These findings motivate security evaluation that follows acoustic influence from sensing to action across persistent state, system boundaries, and changing deployment conditions in real-world agents.

</details>

### 134. Training-Data Extraction from Genomic Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15396)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`training-data extraction`、`genomic LM`、`black-box attack`、`privacy`

👤 **作者**：Tianhao Mao、Zihao Wang、Dongfang Zhao、Rui Zhu、Xiaofeng Wang、Haixu Tang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：基因组语言模型从短前缀生长链续写——生成接口的训练序列提取路线未被研究
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Evo 2 7B 上纯输出黑盒攻击：重复生成收敛簇选择（无梯度/似然/语料访问）+对齐长度/核苷酸一致性/多重性/复杂度复合评分，304 次运行
- 📌 **结论**：888 簇中 85 簇匹配 ≥500bp 训练片段（簇级 ASR 9.57%），22 次唯一提取事件覆盖 14 个训练区域——重复生成收敛是恢复长训练序列的实用输出侧信号

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Genomic language models generate long nucleotide continuations from short prefixes, exposing a direct route for studying training-sequence extraction through the generation interface. We present an output-only black-box attack on Evo 2 that selects convergent groups of repeated generations without gradients, token likelihoods, or training-corpus access during candidate selection. Recovered content is then scored by an evaluator combining aligned length, nucleotide identity, match multiplicity, and sequence complexity. Across 304 runs on Evo 2 7B with bacteriophage and plasmid prefixes, the attack yields 888 candidate clusters, of which 85 match training segments of at least 500 bp, giving a cluster-level attack success rate of 9.57%. Final filtering retains 22 uniquely matched extraction events at the 500-bp threshold and 15 at 1,000 bp, covering 14 and 7 distinct training-sequence regions, respectively. Within-category source-homology screening retains 11 of the 22 events at a 99% exclusion threshold and four at 98%, with the latter mapping to two reference regions. The results establish convergence among repeated nucleotide generations as a practical output-only signal for recovering long training-sequence segments and characterize how recovery depends on prefix length, cluster size, perplexity, early stopping, and sequence domains.

</details>

### 135. When Messages Go Wrong: Understanding Communication Faults in LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15459)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`multi-agent systems`、`communication faults`、`fault injection`、`empirical study`

👤 **作者**：Jinhao Zhou、Xiaoyu Zhang、Jiongchi Yu、Fahao Chen、Jun Wu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM-MAS 依赖网络协议/远程工具/模型端点——消息丢失/延迟/过期导致的语义错误可在可处理外观下传播，系统性研究缺乏
- 🔬 **研究方法**：跨层失效分类学（底层扰动↔应用可见故障）+故障注入与追踪管线，29,000+ 执行（100 任务×5 域×3 拓扑×3 模型配置）
- 📌 **结论**：20.7% 失败无即时失败信号（部分内容故障达 76.2%）；Flat 拓扑在通信故障下保持 53% 成功 vs 顺序/层级 3%——通信完整性与拓扑韧性的大规模实证

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

LLM-based multi-agent systems (LLM-MAS) use specialized agents that exchange messages to accomplish complex tasks. Their reliance on networked protocols, remote tools, and model endpoints exposes communication to message loss, delay, and context expiration. Because agents often exchange open-ended natural language, anomalies can alter task-relevant information while leaving messages processable, allowing semantic errors to propagate through subsequent interactions. Systematic studies of faults in inter-agent communication remain limited. This paper presents a large-scale empirical study of how communication faults manifest, propagate, and affect task outcomes in LLM-MAS. We construct a cross-layer taxonomy linking lower-stack disturbances to application-visible faults and develop a fault-injection and tracing pipeline. Our evaluation covers more than 29,000 executions across 100 tasks, five domains, three workflow topologies, and three model configurations. We find that 20.7% of failures with complete detection records occur without an immediate failure signal, reaching 76.2% for partial-content faults. Flat maintains 53.0% task success under communication faults, compared with 3.3% and 3.2% for Sequential and Hierarchical, respectively. This advantage primarily arises when an alternative information path preserves the evidence required by downstream agents. We also evaluate execution-side and evidence protection mechanisms and find that they recover different failure cases. Evidence recovery depends on whether trustworthy information remains accessible through an unaffected path. These observations provide empirical insights into communication-fault propagation, workflow resilience, and protection design for LLM-MAS.

</details>

### 136. SkillFusion: Towards Reliable Multi-Skill Composition for LLM Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15481)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`skill composition`、`conflict resolution`、`agent reliability`、`compiler`

👤 **作者**：Enhao Huang、…、Zhan Qin

- 🎯 **研究动机**：独立作者的 skill 联合加载时施加不兼容要求/竞争偏好/冗余指令——组合冲突是可靠性的未解面
- 🔬 **研究方法**：SkillFusion 离线编译器：类型化作用域约束显式化组合决策，类型-作用域门+交互分类器+对抗验证者复核，条件路由/优先级/验证抽象/去重消解
- 📌 **结论**：三个 agent 基准+两个迁移基准上非 oracle 方法最高成功率，最大配置下超未消解加载 2.5 倍——多 skill 组合的冲突消解（skill 供应链可靠性）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Independently authored skills can impose incompatible requirements, competing preferences, and redundant instructions when jointly loaded by an LLM agent. We propose SkillFusion, an offline compiler that reconciles these interactions into a reusable merged skill. Its central idea is to make composition decisions explicit through typed, scoped constraints linked to their source directives. A type-scope gate selects candidate interactions, while selective fallback revisits pairs potentially missed by structural pruning. A classifier identifies interaction types, and an adversarial verifier re-examines uncertain judgments. The resolver applies conditional routing or explicit priorities to hard conflicts, verified abstraction to soft conflicts, and deduplication to redundant instructions. Source-linked checks guide synthesis and compression, and metadata synthesis supports discovery of the merged skill. The resulting artifact retains associated scripts and resources and can be reused across tasks and executor models. SkillFusion achieves the highest mean Success among evaluated non-oracle methods on all three agent benchmarks and on both transfer benchmarks across three executor models. At the largest tested instruction configurations, it achieves over 2.5 times the Success of unresolved multi-skill loading, highlighting the growing value of reconciliation as composition becomes more demanding. The code will be open-sourced after the review process concludes.

</details>

### 137. REPRO: Reflective Evolutionary Prompt Optimizer for Automated LLM Red-Teaming

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15513)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`red-teaming`、`automated jailbreak`、`black-box`、`evolutionary optimization`

👤 **作者**：Abrar Alotaibi、Moataz Ahmed

- 🎯 **研究动机**：自动红队要么需生产环境没有的特权访问、要么依赖逐模型模板、要么单目标缺种群多样性
- 🔬 **研究方法**：REPRO：单消费级 GPU 上的 7B 反思模型驱动的纯黑盒进化优化——语义交叉多亲代重组+Thompson 采样贝叶斯策略选择（结构化失败分析驱动）；36 目标模型×3 基准
- 📌 **结论**：单轮即达 100% ASR（中位 24 次查询/行为）；论文如实披露 judge 既打分又定 ASR 的协议局限——低成本可复现的自动化越狱搜索

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Safety-aligned large language models (LLMs) remain vulnerable to adversarial prompts that bypass their safety training. Existing automated red-teaming methods either require privileged access unavailable in production, rely on manually engineered per-model templates, or optimize a single objective without population-level diversity. We present REPRO (Reflective Evolutionary Prompt Optimization), a pure black-box framework that discovers adversarial prompts through evolutionary optimization driven entirely by a compressed open-weight 7B reflective model on a single consumer GPU. REPRO introduces two mechanisms absent from prior work, both isolated in a component ablation: semantic crossover, an LLM-guided multi-parent recombination operator, and Bayesian strategy selection via Thompson Sampling informed by structured failure analysis. We catalog REPRO's behavior on AdvBench, JailbreakBench, and HarmBench across 36 target models from 11 families, including GPT-5-nano, Claude-3.5-Sonnet, Gemini-2.5-Pro, and Grok-4.1. Under our protocol REPRO reaches 100\% attack success rate (ASR) on every evaluated target in a single turn, at a median of 24 target queries per behavior. Two properties of the protocol qualify that figure. The judge scoring candidates during optimization also produces the reported ASR, and search halts when it accepts, so ASR reports whether search succeeded within the iteration budget rather than success at a fixed budget. All baseline figures are transcribed from their original papers, which use different judges, budgets, and model versions. We therefore present this as a catalog of model coverage under one protocol rather than a head-to-head ranking, and develop both qualifications in the paper.

</details>

### 138. Poisoned into Action: Context Attacks on Typed Decision Workflows

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15537)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`context poisoning`、`typed decision`、`execution-aware evaluation`、`System One model`

👤 **作者**：Chenkai Wu、Xing Chang、Jun Yan

- 🎯 **研究动机**：类型化决策接口限制动作集合但 schema 合法选择仍可触发违规状态变化——需要连接模型决策与执行后果的评测协议
- 🔬 **研究方法**：执行感知协议追踪毒化证据经检索→模型选择→自动动作→持久状态；Jev 1,659 次调用×20 任务×3 重复+源排除门及其修正的因子对照与 Holm 校正
- 📌 **结论**：自然检索下单载荷触达全部 60 试验产生 5 次违规执行；源排除门拦攻击但误伤 26 个正确决策、修正空域行为可恢复——毒化上下文到动作的审计框架（探索性结论）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Typed decision interfaces restrict models to predefined actions, yet schema-valid choices can still trigger policy-violating state changes. Evaluating context poisoning thus requires connecting model decisions to execution outcomes while accounting for legitimate automation retained by defenses. We introduce an execution-aware protocol tracing poisoned evidence through retrieval, model choice, automated action, and persisted state. We evaluate it with 1,659 fresh calls to Jev-1.13.0 across 20 development tasks in two synthetic workflows, using three repetitions per condition. Under natural retrieval, a standalone payload reaches all 60 trials and produces five security-violating executions, while a carrier-payload pair exposes the payload in 57 trials and produces one violation. An initial source-exclusion gate blocks the pair's observed violation but also withholds ten otherwise correct attacked decisions and sixteen correct clean decisions. Correcting its empty-scope behavior restores twelve clean decisions without additional model calls. Factorial controls show that adding correct evidence reduces wrong executions from 119/120 to 55/120 in the semantic arm and from 60/120 to zero in the neutral arm, suggesting that much of the improvement reflects general evidence value rather than attack-specific protection. Three prespecified contrasts are not statistically supported after Holm correction, so the findings remain exploratory. The protocol provides an auditable framework for studying which poisoning-induced errors reach execution and how much correct automation defenses preserve. Code is available at this https URL.

</details>

### 139. Big-Big-SycoBench: A Benchmark to Detect Sycophancy Through Model Responses to Valid vs. Invalid Arguments

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15634)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`sycophancy`、`argument discernment`、`benchmark`、`no ground truth`

👤 **作者**：Marthe Ballon、…、Vincent Ginis

- 🎯 **研究动机**：谄媚最难度量的场景是无真值答案的评估任务——应区分对有效批评的响应与对无效压力的顺从
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Big-Big-SycoBench：450 文本制品×5,400 升降分论证（人工审查），模型 20 次打分定基线变异，仅超自身变异的修订计数
- 📌 **结论**：十模型响应率 88-95%（Opus 5.5 仅 32%）而无效率 0.2-95%——有效/无效论证判别把谄媚测量扩展到无真值任务

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Sycophancy in large language models risks amplifying unsupported claims or beliefs across the knowledge ecosystem as these systems increasingly contribute to research synthesis, evaluation, and decision-making. A reliable model should revise its judgement when a user identifies a genuine flaw, but should resist pushback based on fallacies, emotion, or irrelevant appeals to authority. While sycophancy is usually measured on questions with a verifiable answer, the evaluative tasks in which it is most difficult to detect have no such ground truth. We introduce Big-Big-SycoBench, which measures sycophancy through argument discernment: the ability to revise an evaluation after valid criticism while resisting invalid pressure. The benchmark consists of 450 text artefacts with 5,400 arguments for raising or lowering their quality scores, each reviewed by one or more of twelve human annotators. An LLM scores each artefact twenty times to establish its baseline score and uncertainty. The model is then given a valid or invalid argument and asked to update its score. We only count a revision if it exceeds the model's own baseline variability. We use the resulting score delta to distinguish responsiveness to valid arguments from compliance with invalid ones. Across ten models, responsiveness is 88-95\% for seven, 76-77\% for Kimi K3 and GLM-5.3, and 32\% for Opus 5.5. Compliance ranges from 0.2\% for GPT-6 Astra to 95\% for GPT-4o. Big-Big-SycoBench is intended to guide the development of models whose judgements can be corrected by sound arguments, but not displaced by unsupported pressure.

</details>

### 140. Radioactive Feedback: Tracing Models Trained by Label-Only LLM Supervision

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15685)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`provenance`、`distillation watermark`、`LLM-as-a-Judge`、`IP protection`

👤 **作者**：Yuejia Li、JunHeng Li、Hao Wu、Ke He、Mang Ye

- 🎯 **研究动机**：label-only judge 蒸馏中学生只从标量分数学习——无教师文本进入训练集，评估者特异痕迹须经低带宽奖励通道传递，既有水印/蒸馏检测不覆盖
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Radioactive Feedback：评估者对质量合格响应施加有界逐点调整，把注册行为偏好编码进奖励，重复优化使其跨数据集转移到第三方训练的学生
- 📌 **结论**：代码与数学上注册偏好经纯分数传递并可黑盒检测——分数通道的放射性溯源（新蒸馏供应链的谱系验证）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Conventional distillation transfers capability through teacher-generated demonstrations. LLM-as-a-Judge knowledge distillation offers a distinct and increasingly practical alternative. In the label-only variant studied here, the student generates every response and improves from scalar scores alone. Prior work shows that this non-demonstrative feedback can improve student capability, but it creates a fundamentally different provenance problem. No teacher-generated answer enters the student's training set, so any evaluator-specific trace must pass through a low-bandwidth reward signal rather than copied text. Existing watermarks and distillation detectors do not cover this score-only channel. We introduce Radioactive Feedback, which encodes a registered pattern of behavioral preferences in the rewards themselves. The evaluator applies bounded pointwise adjustments to quality-qualified responses, allowing repeated optimization to transfer the pattern to a student trained by another party. Experiments on code and mathematics show that the registered preferences transfer through scores alone and across datasets, enabling black-box detection against a fixed reference pool.

</details>

### 141. Suggested Is Not Superseded: A Pre-Registered Evaluation of a System-One Model as a Memory-Retirement Gate

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15710)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`memory retirement`、`System One model`、`preregistered`、`three-way decision`

👤 **作者**：Xupeng Chen、Chenqian Le

- 🎯 **研究动机**：长期记忆需要『新话语何时取代旧存储主张』的门——基准只标变/不变，分不清对既成变化行动还是对暗示行动
- 🔬 **研究方法**：任务契约三分（SUPERSEDED/NEEDS-CONFIRMATION/COMPATIBLE）+500 对 STALE 标注（三 LLM+两人裁决）+Jev 预注册假设评测与级联对照
- 📌 **结论**：Jev 从不在非变化上退休主张，但二元门把 39.2% 仅被暗示变化的主张过早退休；三路决策降到 15.5% 但超 10 点检测预算——记忆退休门的暗示/既成分离

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Long-term memory for assistants needs a gate that decides when a new utterance retires an old stored claim. Benchmark labels for this step mark a pair as changed or not, so they cannot tell a gate that acts on established change from one that acts on a hint. We write a task contract that separates the two (SUPERSEDED vs. NEEDS-CONFIRMATION vs. COMPATIBLE), label 500 test pairs built from STALE (change pairs plus same-user controls) with three LLM annotators and two human adjudicators (one author and a second annotator; provenance reported), and evaluate Jev, a typed "System One" decision model, under pre-registered hypotheses. Of the 179 test pairs STALE marks as changed, 72 meet the contract's bar for an established change. Jev never retired a claim on 141 same-conversation non-changes. Its failure is premature retirement: the binary gate retires 39.2% of claims whose change is only suggested. A three-way decision cuts this to 15.5% at a 15.3-point detection cost, which misses our pre-registered 10-point budget, and it lands on the binary gate's own threshold frontier; these verdicts hold under three readings of the gold labels. On a held-out vault scored against a second annotator's labels, a cascade that escalated uncertain cases to a reasoning model and looked best on development data was indistinguishable from simply raising the threshold (premature retirement 26.8% vs. 6.5% on dev). Part of the gap comes from the gold labels: relative to that annotator's reading, LLM-majority gold shares most of its errors with a same-family escalation model, and an identity for binary labels says how much such shared errors hide. The rest is small-sample selection on development data. For builders this means a binary score should not be wired to delete: route suggested changes to pending evidence, and do not trust a cascade selected on development data of this size.

</details>

### 142. SkillMarker: A KGW-Based Watermarking Method for Agent Skill Ownership Verification

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15725)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`skill watermark`、`ownership verification`、`KGW`、`agent supply chain`

👤 **作者**：Alan Tang、Lifeng Wang、Gang Wu、Tao Zhang、Yantao Jia、Xiaofeng Zhu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agent skill 明文分发暴露于未授权复制与泄漏——所有权验证与泄漏追踪机制缺失，而紧凑结构化指令对事实与过程准确性要求苛刻
- 🔬 **研究方法**：SkillMarker：KGW 水印+质量约束改写（分离必要知识与弹性表达）与信号导向候选搜索（保真约束下强化水印信号），开源工具链含嵌入/审计/检测
- 📌 **结论**：面向整体或部分复制及并入其他 skill 的分布式生态——skill 资产的所有权验证与泄漏溯源

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Agent skills encode operational procedures and domain expertise as reusable knowledge assets, yet their distribution in plaintext exposes them to unauthorized copying and leakage. However, practical mechanisms for skill ownership verification and leakage tracing remain underdeveloped. Watermark embedding before distribution offers a potential solution, but structured, compact skill instructions impose stringent requirements for factual and procedural accuracy. To address these challenges, we introduce SkillMarker, a KGW-based watermarking method that jointly targets functionality preservation and strong watermark embedding. SkillMarker unifies quality-constrained rewriting with signal-oriented candidate search. The former separates essential knowledge from flexible expression to preserve instruction fidelity; the latter explores alternative expressions to obtain a stronger watermark signal under preservation constraints. We release an open-source toolkit that integrates watermark embedding, quality auditing, and detection to support the protection of agent skills. SkillMarker targets ownership verification and leakage tracing in distributed skill ecosystems, where content may be copied in whole or in part, or incorporated into other skills.

</details>

### 143. Visually Legible but Adversarial: Watermark-Constrained Attacks on End-to-End VLMs for Document Understanding

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15728)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`adversarial watermark`、`VLM`、`document understanding`、`attack`

👤 **作者**：Qingzhe Han、…、Di Zhang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：可见水印传达文档所有权/机密性/状态——也可携带对抗信号攻击端到端文档理解 VLM
- 🔬 **研究方法**：AR-AWM：模型感知白盒攻击，只优化空间分布的水印文本支撑像素；无目标且问题无关（不用问题/答案/解析提示/LM 梯度），每页跨下游查询复用
- 📌 **结论**：7.98% 页面像素使 Qwen2.5-VL DocVQA EM 0.899→0.150、ANLS 0.939→0.212 且保持视觉可读；跨模型迁移弱揭示编码器特异性——水印载体的对抗攻击面

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Visible watermarks convey document ownership, confidentiality, and status, but can also carry adversarial signals. We introduce Auto-Regional Adversarial Watermarking (AR-AWM), a model-aware white-box attack on end-to-end visual language models (VLMs) for document understanding. AR-AWM constructs a small, spatially distributed watermark-text support and optimizes only its pixels to disrupt visual representations. The attack is untargeted and question-agnostic: generation uses no questions, answers, parsing prompts, language-model gradients, or language-model outputs. Each attacked page is reused across downstream queries. On Qwen2.5-VL-7B, a watermark support covering 7.98% of page pixels under the reporting threshold reduces DocVQA exact match from 0.899 to 0.150 and average normalized Levenshtein similarity (ANLS) from 0.939 to 0.212. Selected qualitative cases retain visually legible source fields. The same support without optimization and with matched random perturbations retains ANLS of 0.932 and 0.933, respectively, identifying adversarial optimization as the primary cause of degradation. Layout-matched optimized rectangles yield ANLS of 0.276, showing an incremental benefit from text-shaped geometry. AR-AWM also degrades all reported text, formula, reading-order, and table metrics on OmniDocBench. Separate white-box optimization on InternVL2.5-8B reduces ANLS from 0.922 to 0.046, while weak cross-model transfer reveals strong encoder specificity. These results identify conventional watermark-shaped supports as an adversarial surface for document VLMs in the evaluated white-box this http URL white-box setting.

</details>

### 144. Unchecked or Falsely Accused? Evaluating AI-Generated Text Detectors on Kazakh

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15870)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`AI-text detection`、`low-resource language`、`detector validity`、`Kazakh`

👤 **作者**：Mansur Ozaman

- 🎯 **研究动机**：AI 文本检测器已用于哈萨克语——但 ChatGPT 之前的人类文本被误报率从未被报告
- 🔬 **研究方法**：AINA-KK：3,811 条可证先于 ChatGPT 的人类段落+5,597 机器/灰区段落（含本土 KazLLM/Sherkala）×脚本与长度压力测试+免新数据的修复组合
- 📌 **结论**：英文训练检测器最高误报 81.4% 人类文本、再校准后仅捕获 1.3% 机器文本；修复组合误报 0.9% 且捕获 85.2% 已知生成器——低资源语种检测器有效性的系统审计

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

AI-text detectors are already applied to Kazakh writing, yet no study reports how often publicly available detectors flag Kazakh text whose human authorship is dated before ChatGPT. We present AINA-KK: 3,811 human passages from encyclopedic, news, web and educational pages that provably existed before ChatGPT, and 5,597 machine and gray-zone passages from 9 generators, including the Kazakh-built KazLLM and Sherkala, with script and length stress tests. Five English-trained detectors from the Hugging Face Hub flag up to 81.4% of human passages and 79.6% of published student essays; recalibrated, they catch at most 1.3% of machine text. Calibrated zero-shot detectors catch at most 50.3% of machine text from in-distribution generators. Fine-tuned encoders rank text almost perfectly, but mDeBERTa at its default threshold flags 24.4% of 50-word human excerpts, partly because machine outputs are shorter than human text; calibrated, it catches only 47.1% of KazLLM and 62.7% of Sherkala passages. Without script handling, machine-transliterated Latin-2021 text defeats every detector. Fixes that need no new data (calibration, script canonicalization, script-augmented and length-matched training, and an OR with a zero-shot scorer) form AINA-KK-Det. On test it flags 0.9% of human passages (95% CI 0.4-2.7; 1.5% on development data) and catches 85.2% of machine text from known generators, 92.9% from two unseen Gemini-family Gemma-4 models and 61.3% from the Kazakh-built ones; its configuration was chosen with test results in view. We release the data, code and detector at this https URL.

</details>

### 145. Engram Injection: A Study of Temporal Validity in Parametric Language-Model Memory

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15882)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`parametric memory`、`temporal validity`、`LoRA`、`knowledge updating`

👤 **作者**：Sam Siavoshian、Omar Ramadan、Amir K. Saeed、Benjamin A. Johnson、Benjamin M. Rodriguez

- 🎯 **研究动机**：存储的事实会随偏好改变或事件更替失效——模型能否在参数中学习这些变化并仅凭问题与当前时间正确作答
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Engram Injection：LoRA 适配器存事实+FiM 时钟条件化（冻结 Qwen2.5-3B），与加性时钟/全历史提示/四条 dated 记录检索对照，训练审计排除测试年龄泄漏
- 📌 **结论**：62 对前后问题上两种适配器法零对正确、检索 62/62——学会事实≠学会何时使用它，参数化记忆的时间有效性边界

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

A stored fact may stop being valid when a user changes a preference or an event replaces an earlier one. We study whether a language model can learn these changes in its parameters and answer correctly from only a question and the current time. Engram Injection stores facts in LoRA adapters and supplies the clock through FiLM conditioning on a frozen Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct model. We compare it with additive clock conditioning, prompting with the full dated history, and retrieving four dated records. We train six matched adapters across three seeds after fixing a sampler that could admit test-age examples into training. The training audit reports no such examples among 127,760 evaluated training inputs. On 62 pairs of questions asked before and after an update, at an elapsed age of seven days excluded from training, both adapter methods answer zero pairs correctly under the primary blinded assistant review with order code 1 held fixed. Full-history prompting answers 59/62 pairs correctly, and retrieval answers 62/62. Separate experiments show that adapters can store facts and that removable memory units can support local deletion. Combining fact selection with time-based version selection remains unsuccessful. These results separate learning a fact from learning when to use it.

</details>

### 146. RetryBench: Separating Retry Permission from Recovery Outcomes in GPT Tool Use

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15884)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`tool retry`、`retry permission`、`GPT tool use`、`benchmark`

👤 **作者**：You Liu、Yue Liu、Quanchao Lu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：假重试权限：agent 在可见契约不支持时重试超时工具调用——成功恢复不等于可靠拒绝无据重试
- 🔬 **研究方法**：RetryBench：720+240 响应密钥控制、六端点弱契约研究、1,536 响应规范动作块与显式契约提示对照分离权限与恢复
- 📌 **结论**：改换密钥诱发无据重试（Terra 55/80、Luna 60/80）；移除候选后 Luna 仍 8/80 无据派发；显式契约线索令全部 762 响应转延迟——重试权限与恢复结果的分离报告

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

False retry permission (FRP) occurs when an agent retries a timed-out tool call without support from the visible contract. RetryBench separates this error from realized recovery. Successful protected recovery does not imply reliable rejection of unsupported retries. In a 720-response key control, valid keys usually enabled safe progress, but offered changed keys elicited unsupported retries in Sol (1/80), Terra (55/80), and Luna (60/80). A later 240-response control removed the candidate and required models to supply a key. Luna still made 8/80 unsupported dispatches; Sol and Terra made none in 80 responses each. Accepting a displayed bad key therefore cannot explain all observed errors, although changed output requirements and conditions preclude a pure display-effect comparison. Removing the weak-contract label increased retries in Sol and Terra's coded-action conditions in a six-endpoint study. A separate 1,536-response canonical-action block also found a positive Terra contrast (115/128 versus 124/128 retries), concentrated in two task/order conditions; the other five contrasts remained unresolved after multiplicity correction. An explicit-contract cue control elicited deferral in all 762 returned responses, leaving recovery unresolved. These results support reporting permission and recovery together across declared interface variants. The evidence covers four purposive tasks, fixed GPT endpoints, and one recovery decision; it identifies neither internal mechanisms nor episode-level safety.

</details>

### 147. The Anatomy of a Blind Spot: What an LLM Judge Must Be Shown, and How Much

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15938)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`LLM judge`、`regulator`、`input ablation`、`preregistered`

👤 **作者**：Nitish Kumar、Prateik Pothuneedi、Greg Bandak

- 🎯 **研究动机**：judge 的报告被据以行动时它就是调节器——必须给看什么、看多少、什么形式未解决
- 🔬 **研究方法**：七个公共基准上仅变 judge 所见内容（三个价格差 10-17 倍的 judge）；83 条预注册可测试条款（81 条运行）
- 📌 **结论**：给看什么比用哪个 judge 更重要；拒绝材料时系统反馈循环反低于起点；十分之一原文完成几乎全部作用——judge 输入的设计规则与理论检验

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Evaluation of text generated by large language models (LLMs) is increasingly delegated to LLMs acting as judges, yet what such a judge must be shown remains unresolved. We treat a judge whose reports are acted on as a regulator, in the sense of cybernetics, and ask what it must be shown, how much, in what form, and what its reports must get right. On seven public benchmarks we varied only what the judge was shown. Across three judges whose prices differ 10- to 17-fold, what the judge was shown mattered more than which judge it was. When a system revised its answers on feedback from a judge denied the material they had to respect, it ended below its starting point on instruction following and question answering. On instruction following the loss grew with each round, and it persisted when the judge saw the user's request but not the constraints and with a second judge family. On news summarisation, a correct reference summary beside the source undid most of the source's contribution, whereas on single-answer questions a reference helped. About a tenth of the source did almost all of its work, and a small entailment graph matched an LLM judge at finding errors in grounded text. We committed 83 testable clauses before running; of the 81 that ran, 32 met their bar and 50 went in the predicted direction, and most quantitative predictions from the theory failed. We report every shortfall and derive design rules tied to measured effects.

</details>

### 148. Embedding Information in Policy Randomness: Distribution-Preserving Implicit Communication in MDPs

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15976)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`implicit communication`、`steganography`、`stochastic policy`、`channel coding`

👤 **作者**：Gongpu Chen、Deniz Gündüz

- 🎯 **研究动机**：随机策略的随机性可作为嵌入与传递信息的媒介——不改变规定动作分布的隐蔽信道
- 🔬 **研究方法**：PRAM 后验匹配随机性调制：把受控系统视为通信信道的编码方案，借独立于控制系统的公共随机性逐状态保分布，渐近可靠解码
- 📌 **结论**：多比特策略水印与轨迹溯源应用验证——策略随机性中的分布保持隐写信道（agent 间隐蔽通信的理论基础）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

This paper investigates a new role of randomness in stochastic policies--as a medium for embedding and communicating information. Specifically, we consider an agent interacting with an environment according to a prescribed stochastic policy. By carefully modulating the random action realizations of the policy, information can be embedded into the agent's behavior without altering the prescribed action distribution. We cast this problem as a channel coding problem, in which the controlled system implicitly serves as a communication channel. We then propose a coding scheme, termed posterior-matching randomness modulation (PRAM), that selects actions according to the message to be transmitted. With the aid of common randomness independent of the control system, PRAM preserves the prescribed action distribution for every state and every message. We further prove that PRAM is asymptotically reliable: the message can be decoded with arbitrarily small error probability as the observed state trajectory becomes sufficiently long. Experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework in applications including multi-bit policy watermarking and trajectory provenance.

</details>

### 149. When Agent Evaluation Becomes Unreliable: An Empirical Study of Oracle Failures and Configuration-Dependent Measurement in an LLM Agent Benchmark

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16067)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`benchmark audit`、`test oracle`、`measurement reliability`、`agent evaluation`

👤 **作者**：Junjie Chen

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agent 评测 harness 是软件系统——其 oracle 可靠性缺陷分数表揭示不了
- 🔬 **研究方法**：oracle 完整性审计：五状态不变量+三已知答案控制，七类缺陷注入全检出后应用于真实基准；4,320+3,240 集配置依赖效应研究
- 📌 **结论**：审计发现奖励函数在评分前改环境状态的缺陷，重放把通过率 94.3%→65.5%；策略文档/工具描述/错误串都影响测量——agent 评测的测量层可靠性

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Agent evaluation harnesses are software systems that run test suites, generate inputs, and decide outcomes with test oracles. This paper audits such software for reliability defects that score tables cannot reveal. We introduce an oracle-integrity auditing approach with five state-integrity invariants and three known-answer controls. We evaluate it by injecting seven oracle defect classes across two task domains and a synthetic domain. All injected defect classes are detected, and the repaired oracle has no false positives. Applied to an agent benchmark, the audit reveals a reward-function defect that changes the environment state before post-run scoring. Across 4,320 episodes, replay-based correction changes database-pass labels from 4,194 to 2,883 and reduces measured success from 94.3% to 65.5%. We also study configuration-dependent measurement effects. A component ablation over 3,240 episodes shows that the agent's policy documents, tool descriptions, and tool error strings influence observed trajectory reliability, with tool descriptions showing the largest exploratory effect (-9.2 pp) and error strings the smallest (-5.3 pp). A second task domain reverses the sign of the language effect, so the result is bounded to the tested configuration. Finally, we report a portability boundary: the auditing approach cannot validate a second independently implemented benchmark because the required reference terminal state cannot be reconstructed from its recorded API-call log. The paper releases a replication package containing the auditing suite, fault-injection catalogue, frozen records, deviation logs, and offline analysis entry points without network access, API keys, or model calls.

</details>

### 150. The Sharp Directions Are Against You: Curvature Analysis of Activation Steering

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16084)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`activation steering`、`fragility`、`curvature`、`Hessian`

👤 **作者**：Seyedarmin Azizi、Arya Fayyazi、Parsa Razmara、Erfan Baghaei Potraghloo、Souvik Kundu、Massoud Pedram

- 🎯 **研究动机**：对比激活加法（CAA）转向脆弱、常把行为推向错误方向——几何原因未明
- 🔬 **研究方法**：转向 Hessian：行为损失对转向向量二阶导，top 特征向量即尖锐方向（微小扰动引起大幅 erratic 行为摆动）；去尖锐成分保方向范数（cos>0.974）
- 📌 **结论**：尖锐方向仅占 1-5% 平方范数却驱动 53-100% 反向行为，去除后曲率中位降 4 倍——转向脆弱性的曲率解释与修复（安全向量的耐久性含义）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Activation steering adds a vector to a language model's internal representations at inference time. It is a lightweight alternative to fine-tuning for behavioral control. The standard construction, contrastive activation addition (CAA), is fragile, as on many inputs it shifts behavior in the wrong direction. To explain this fragility, we first study the geometry of the steering vector. We introduce steering Hessian, the matrix of second order derivative of the model's behavioral loss with respect to the steering vector. It captures the output behavior response sensitivity of the model under small perturbations of the steering vector along each direction. Its top eigenvectors are sharp directions: tiny perturbations along them can cause large, erratic, input-dependent behavioral swings. Bottom eigenvectors are flat directions: behavior is robust along them. Removing the sharp component of a steering vector approximately preserves its direction and magnitude (cosine > 0.974, norm reduction < 3% for typical settings) while reducing the vector's directional curvature by up to 12x (median 4x across 36 conditions). We evaluate on three model families (Qwen2.5-7B, Llama-3.1-8B, Mistral-7B), with four behavioral tasks, and 36 token-level conditions. Steering along sharp directions reverses the intended behavior in every condition tested, with anti-steer rates of 53-100%. Sharp directions account for only 1-5% of the CAA vector's squared norm. However, they drive a disproportionate share of the gap between the steered and unsteered next-token distributions during autoregressive generation. We term this one-line correction as sharp removal; it outperforms standard CAA in open-ended generation across all 25 conditions tested. A jailbreaking experiment further shows that sharp directions act as noise rather than signal: they disrupt coherent behavioral control regardless of steering polarity.

</details>

### 151. WildTrace: Evaluating Training Data Detection in Realistic Large-Scale LLM Post-Training

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16152)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`training data detection`、`membership inference`、`post-training`、`negative result`

👤 **作者**：Hengxiang Zhang、Junxian Liu、Hongxin Wei

- 🎯 **研究动机**：后训练大量未公开数据引发污染与版权关切——事后构造基准有分布偏移、受控小规模又偏离真实后训练，检测方法真实效力存疑
- 🔬 **研究方法**：WildTrace：多阶段管线覆盖 SFT/DPO/RL 全生命周期的阶段特定模型，四任务分布匹配成员/非成员+可验证成员标签
- 📌 **结论**：最好方法平均 AUC 仅 0.529（近随机）；评测协议显著影响测得性能——真实规模后训练数据检测的重大负结果与方法论警示

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Although post-training significantly enhances model performance, the use of massive undisclosed data raises concerns about benchmark contamination and copyright infringement. This drives growing interest in training data detection for post-trained models. Existing evaluations for training data detection often leverage benchmarks constructed post hoc, which can introduce distribution shifts. Alternatively, controlled evaluations rely on small-scale training setups that diverge from realistic post-training settings. As a result, their reported performance may not faithfully reflect the effectiveness of detection methods in real-world scenarios. To this end, we introduce \textsc{\method}, a reliable benchmark designed to evaluate detection methods in realistic, large-scale post-training settings. Built on a multi-stage pipeline, \method\space encompasses stage-specific models throughout the post-training lifecycle, spanning SFT, DPO, and RL. To perform a reliable evaluation, we construct model-specific evaluation sets across four diverse tasks, featuring distribution-matched members and non-members with verifiable membership labels. Our results reveal that even the best-performing method achieves an average AUC of only 0.529 on our benchmark, which approaches random-guessing level (0.5). Furthermore, our analysis suggests that evaluation protocols can significantly affect measured detection performance. These findings highlight the significant potential for developing effective, practical, and robust detection methods under realistic scenarios.

</details>

### 152. Uncovering Robustness Weaknesses in LLM Agents via Persona Evolution with Task-Integrity Constraints

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16155)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`agent robustness`、`persona evolution`、`red-teaming`、`task integrity`

👤 **作者**：Yutao Xie、Jingbo Yang、Guanyu Yao、Xiaoyu Yang、Duo Ding、Shiyu Chang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：标准用户模拟器 broadly 合作——多样真实用户交互下的失效被高估乐观；预定义行为漏 agent 特异弱点、失败导向搜索可因作废任务而失真
- 🔬 **研究方法**：TIPER：任务完整性保持的 persona 演化（反思引导行为突变+任务式 Pareto 选择），四交互基准+行为-失效分类学
- 📌 **结论**：12 个 GPT-5.4 设置全部降 held-out 分（5.8-33.7pp）、28 agent-域对中位降 22.9pp——保持任务有效的用户侧重红队

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Interactive-agent evaluations commonly rely on standardized LLM-based user simulators that exhibit broadly cooperative behavior, potentially overlooking failures that arise under diverse but realistic user interactions. This can lead to overly optimistic estimates of agent robustness and provide limited failure signals for training agents that generalize across users. Existing approaches largely rely on predefined user behaviors, which may miss agent- or domain-specific weaknesses, while failure-oriented search can reduce task success by invalidating the underlying task rather than exposing genuine robustness failures. To fill this gap, we introduce TIPER, a task-integrity-preserving persona evolution method that automatically discovers agent-specific user policies exposing robustness weaknesses while keeping the underlying task valid and solvable. TIPER evolves persistent user personas through reflection-guided behavioral mutations and task-wise Pareto selection under a task-integrity constraint. Across four interactive-agent benchmarks, TIPER yields lower held-out avg@3 scores in all 12 GPT-5.4 settings, with observed reductions of 5.8 to 33.7 percentage points, and in all 28 agent-domain pairs spanning seven agents, with a median observed reduction of 22.9 points. Further analysis yields a behavior-to-failure taxonomy that connects discovered user behaviors to recurring agent failure modes. Together, these results show that TIPER enables more diagnostic robustness evaluation of interactive agents and provides informative failure analysis for future agent robustness improvement. Code repository: this https URL.

</details>

### 153. Not All Challenges Are Equal: Evaluating Interactive Liveness Detection Against Real-Time Face Swaps

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16417)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`liveness detection`、`face swap`、`identity verification`、`challenge design`

👤 **作者**：Anton Firc、David Drtil、Martin Perešíni、Milan Šalko、Vojtěch Staněk、Kamil Malinka

- 🎯 **研究动机**：远程身份验证假设摄像头流是活人——公开实时换脸工具可在流到达验证器前替换之，被动检测器泛化差而交互挑战缺乏证据选型
- 🔬 **研究方法**：108 场现场会话（6 人×24 冒充身份×DeepFaceLive/FaceFusion 经虚拟摄像头注入）评测随机化手势挑战与完成时间信号
- 📌 **结论**：主动手势窗 AUC 0.988 vs 静态 0.878；姿态与组合手势最佳、表情无增益；响应时间提供部署特异信号——挑战集须对当前攻击配置实测

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Remote identity verification assumes that a camera stream shows a live person, yet public real-time face-swap tools can replace that person before the stream reaches the verifier. Passive deepfake detectors generalize poorly to new generators, while interactive challenges are often selected without evidence of which gestures expose current attacks. We evaluate randomized gesture challenges in 108 live sessions with six subjects, 24 impersonated identities, and two face-swap tools, DeepFaceLive and FaceFusion, injected through a virtual camera. The static calibration segment reaches a session-level AUC of 0.878 with a frozen UCF spatial detector, while the mean across the active-gesture windows reaches 0.988. Pose and combined gestures have the highest point estimates, whereas expressions show no clear improvement. Hand occlusion against FaceFusion, configured with an occlusion mask, scores below the static baseline. The end-to-end time required to satisfy the validator also distinguishes attacks from genuine sessions, achieving a session-level AUC of 0.732, although this signal includes synthesis and transport delays. These results show that challenge sets should be tested against current attack configurations and that response time can provide an additional, deployment-specific signal.

</details>

### 154. Self-Verifier Capture in Unified Visual Generators: Diagnosing and Repairing Closed-Loop Self-Reward

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16500)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`self-reward`、`reward hacking`、`unified generator`、`verification loop`

👤 **作者**：Binyan Chen、Tong Pan

- 🎯 **研究动机**：统一视觉生成器复用理解通路作解码无关的自奖励——自奖励上升而独立测量的质量下降（自验证器捕获）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：共享/冻结细胞的匹配语义/去噪对照分离两条路线（策略利用冻结判官盲点 vs 共享更新改写判官）+锁定奖励读出与稀疏参数 disjoint 外部锚的修复
- 📌 **结论**：冻结判官会过期、反复自刷新产生渐进更弱恢复；修复跨 Show-o2 迁移——闭环自奖励的失效机制与锚定修复（自训练奖励作弊）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Image-space reward requires decoding and a separate judge; unified visual generators can instead reuse their understanding pathway for decode-free, self-supplied reward. Optimizing against this verifier closes the self-reward loop; shared trainable weights let one update move generation and scoring even with the reward gradient stopped. We call the resulting failure self-verifier capture: self-reward rises while independently measured quality falls. Matched semantic/denoising by shared/frozen cells on Scale-RAE isolate two routes: the policy exploits on-manifold blind spots of a frozen semantic judge, while shared updates additionally rewrite the judge. A frozen judge also becomes stale, and repeated self-refresh yields progressively weaker recoveries. The diagnosis yields a two-part repair: locking the warmed reward read blocks rewriting, while sparse, parameter-disjoint external anchors counter residual exploitation. Unanchored rollouts retain semantic reward across multiple latent noise levels, providing dense latent reward that terminal image-space judging cannot supply, while a drift-triggered controller allocates anchors to high-drift rollouts and propagates their calibration within each group. At matched budgets, the controller improves on fixed-dose anchoring, increasingly so as coverage tightens. Both the unanchored failure and the repair transfer to the higher-performing, architecturally distinct Show-o2. Across both bases, the complete method significantly outperforms a construct-matched every-rollout judge on GenEval and DPG-Bench while externally scoring only 1/16 of rollouts. At 1/32 coverage, it scores higher in all four comparisons and remains jointly non-inferior.

</details>

### 155. VLA-GAUGE: Systematically Evaluating Generalization and Robustness in Vision-Language-Action Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16567)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`VLA`、`generalization`、`robustness`、`diagnostic framework`

👤 **作者**：Jun Xiao、Rui Ye、Dong Cao、Jinyu Gu、Ying Wang、Zhiliang Zhu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：VLA 高成功率是否反映真实零样本泛化与扰动鲁棒性不明——既有评测缺能力级诊断与组合扰动覆盖
- 🔬 **研究方法**：VLA-GAUGE：统一可执行任务表示下的配对 ID/OOD 六能力套件+单因子敏感性与组合扰动筛选+诊断驱动的定向数据增强微调；12 个 VLA 统一协议
- 📌 **结论**：零样本均分仅 22.3%（SimpleVLA-RL 8.0%）；诊断驱动微调在 held-out 提升 72.4/31.8 分超单因子微调——VLA 泛化鲁棒性的系统诊断-修复闭环

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Vision-Language-Action models (VLAs) have achieved high success rates on familiar manipulation tasks, yet it remains unclear whether such scores reflect genuine zero-shot generalization and perturbation robustness. Existing evaluation frameworks provide limited capability-level diagnosis and combinatorial perturbation coverage, often without connecting diagnosis to targeted model refinement. We introduce VLA-GAUGE, a diagnostic framework that unifies both evaluations under a shared executable task representation. It combines (1) paired in-distribution and out-of-distribution tasks across six capability suites, (2) single-factor sensitivity analysis with combinatorial perturbation screening and evaluation, and (3) diagnosis-driven refinement through targeted data augmentation and model fine-tuning. We evaluate 12 VLAs spanning different model sizes and action-modeling paradigms under a unified protocol. We find that (1) The mean Zero-Shot Score on VLA-GAUGE is only 22.3%, with SimpleVLA-RL scoring 8.0% and ranking second to last among the evaluated models. (2) Under three representative combinatorial perturbation configurations, the mean R-Score is 9.3. (3) Diagnosis-driven fine-tuning improves $\pi_{0.5}$'s zero-shot generalization and robustness under a selected perturbation combination on held-out scenarios by 72.4 and 31.8 scoring points, respectively; the latter exceeds the 10.9-point gain from LIBERO-Plus single-factor fine-tuning despite fewer successful training trajectories. These results show that high reported success rates on familiar manipulation tasks do not guarantee strong zero-shot generalization to unseen tasks or robustness to combinatorial perturbations, while diagnostic evaluation provides actionable signals for targeted model improvement.

</details>

### 156. Answer It or Not? Towards Prompt-Adapted Adversarial Images on Vision Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16622)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`adversarial image`、`VLM`、`visual privacy`、`prompt-adapted`

👤 **作者**：Shunyuan Bai、Yuchen Ren、Le Yang、Chenhao Lin、Chao Shen、Zhengyu Zhao

- 🎯 **研究动机**：对抗图像保护视觉隐私时与提示无关地拒绝一切提问——包括图像的合法使用
- 🔬 **研究方法**：ProAda：优化平衡拒绝有害提示与正确响应合法提示，迭代更新文本提示并以简单分类器判有害性保持以获得更准确对抗方向
- 📌 **结论**：拒 90%+ 有害提示同时放行 90%+ 合法提示，跨模型迁移与抗反制有竞争力——提示自适应的隐私保护对抗图像

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

With the advancement in language modeling, research on adversarial images has recently shifted from image classifiers to vision-language models (VLMs). Despite the new, text-prompt-driven nature of VLMs, we find that existing VLM adversaries have largely overlooked the dynamics of text prompts. In a typical use case, adversarial images can protect visual privacy by rejecting VLMs from answering questions about the image. However, existing protections are prompt-agnostic, i.e., indiscriminately rejecting any question, including those for potential legitimate use of the image. To address this limitation, we propose Prompt-Adapted (ProAda) adversarial images, which are optimized to balance the rejection of harmful prompts and the correct response of legitimate prompts. In particular, to achieve a more accurate adversarial direction, the text prompt is also iteratively updated with a simple classifier judging whether the harmfulness of the prompt remains. Extensive experiments on visual-privacy-related datasets validate that ProAda can reject over 90% of harmful prompts while allowing over 90% of legitimate ones, demonstrating superior overall performance compared to existing prompt-agnostic VLM adversaries. In addition, our ProAda achieves competitive overall cross-model transferability and improved robustness against countermeasures.

</details>

### 157. Language2Drive: Do Vision-Language-Action Models Really Follow the Instructions in Autonomous Driving?

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16634)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`VLA`、`instruction following`、`autonomous driving`、`counterfactual`

👤 **作者**：Zihan You、…、Yan Wang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：驾驶 VLA 用语言条件化行为——闭环基准只测路线完成/安全/合规，语言是否真的决定执行哪个可行行为未解
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Language2Drive：配对反事实单元固定场景只换指令，区分孤立指令成功与反事实配对成功；CARLA 闭环+重建真实场景+开环三制度
