- 📌 **结论**：93.2% 成功 rollout 在替代可行指令下没有成功对应物（单边完成主导）——强驾驶能力与弱语言控制的惊人错配（指令权威的有效性检验）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models increasingly use natural-language instructions to condition autonomous driving behavior. Yet existing closed-loop benchmarks primarily measure route completion, safety, and rule compliance, leaving a fundamental question unresolved: does language actually determine which feasible behavior the vehicle executes? A trajectory may be safe and successful while still violating or even ignoring the passenger's instruction. To address this, we introduce \ltd, a benchmark designed to directly test this capability. Its key evaluation unit, the paired counterfactual unit (PCU), holds the initial driving scene fixed while changing only the instruction. This design enables us to distinguish isolated instruction success from counterfactual paired success across four instruction-following scopes. To test whether this distinction persists across evaluation regimes, we instantiate Language2Drive in complementary closed-loop CARLA simulation, reconstructed real-world scenes, and open-loop driving benchmarks, including our released CARLA-Language2Drive simulator with purpose-built maps. Evaluating representative driving VLAs across these settings reveals a striking mismatch between strong driving competence and weak language control: 93.2% of successful rollouts do not have a successful counterpart under the alternative feasible instruction. This failure is dominated by one-sided completion, where a model repeatedly executes one preferred behavior but fails when the same scene requires the counterfactual alternative. These results expose two fundamental limitations of current evaluation: isolated success and driving scores, which are widely used by conventional driving benchmarks, can substantially overstate language-following capability, while current driving VLAs themselves lack reliable instruction following under matched counterfactual tests.

</details>

### 158. Structural Baselines Matter for Agent Failure Detection and Attribution

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16661)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`failure attribution`、`structural baseline`、`monitor validity`、`agent monitoring`

👤 **作者**：Ramesh Arvind Naagarajan、Stefan Streif

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agent 失败检测与归因两任务的报告性能大部分来自无需模型的规律性：失败轨迹更长、决定性步通常是最后一步
- 🔬 **研究方法**：结构基线（永远报最后一步的规则 67-96% 正确）校正下重测多方法；重放可确认决定步的失败子集与内部状态对照
- 📌 **结论**：对放弃类失败校正后方法零增益；检测上 agent 内部状态优于文本长度/相似度基线——监控评测须报告对结构基线的增益（归因有效性）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

An LLM agent using tools can fail silently. It stops before the task is done, raises no error, and answers fluently. Two kinds of monitor address this, one deciding whether a run failed and the other naming the step where failure became unavoidable. Much of the reported performance on both tasks comes from regularities that need no model. Failing runs are longer, and the step labelled decisive is usually the last. A rule that always names the last step is correct $67\%$ to $96\%$ of the time here, without reading the run at all. That ceiling reflects how the labels were written, not how agents break. On failures whose decisive step replay can confirm, it falls to near chance. Reporting gain over that rule therefore changes what a score means. Under that correction, methods tested here gain nothing on give-up failures, while several published systems keep a clear advantage on other splits. For detection, the agent's internal states beat transcript length and text similarity on the agent benchmark, and hold up where the text baseline does not. Precision stays low when failures are rare, so such a monitor should rank runs for review, not replace it.

</details>

### 159. Mean Accuracy Is the Wrong Estimand for a Release Decision

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16825)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`release decision`、`tail risk`、`evaluation`、`recipe card`

👤 **作者**：Jaewon Choi、Jihye Kang、Namhyuk Ahn

- 🎯 **研究动机**：发布决策是对训练配方下一次重跑的运营承诺——标准报告只给过去种子的平均准确率，而决策依赖灾难尾部风险
- 🔬 **研究方法**：2,124 个微调模型×11 语言模型审计：双峰模式塌缩下高均值掩盖高失败率；recipe cards 用精确有限样本二项界直接认证重跑尾部失败率
- 📌 **结论**：按均值发布违反 10% 部署失败预算的比率达 48.0%；recipe cards 降到 0.6% 并强制样本下限 N≥22——发布决策的正确估计量（评测-部署有效性）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

A release decision about a fine-tuned model is an operational commitment about the \emph{next} rerun of its training recipe. Yet standard benchmark reporting gives only the mean accuracy of past seeds. These target fundamentally different questions: release decisions depend on catastrophic tail risk, whereas standard evaluation reports only central tendency. Because fine-tuning induces bimodal mode collapse, high average accuracy frequently conceals severe failure rates. Auditing $2\,124$ fine-tuned models across eleven language models, we show that shipping models based on mean accuracy violates a $10\%$ deployment failure budget $48.0\%$ of the time. Common heuristics like $\bar b - 2\sigma$ prove equally dangerous: by underestimating variance on small samples, they become most permissive when evidence is weakest. We introduce \textbf{recipe cards}, an evaluation framework that directly certifies rerun tail failure rates using exact finite-sample binomial bounds. Recipe cards reduce held-out budget violations to $0.6\%$, enforce a mathematical sample floor ($N \ge 22$) that prevents certification on thin evidence, and explicitly refuse unverified claims under domain and task shifts.

</details>

### 160. SpecGuardBench: Can Coding Agents Construct Reliable Validation Harnesses from Natural Language Specifications?

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16870)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`coding agent`、`validation harness`、`specification`、`benchmark`

👤 **作者**：Zhiqing Zhong、…、Pinjia He

- 🎯 **研究动机**：coding agent 的正确性证据仍是测试——通过可得测试不等于满足自然语言规范，agent 能否自行构造忠实且充分的验证 harness 未测
- 🔬 **研究方法**：SpecGuardBench：291 任务×13 真实仓库×8 类验证逻辑（功能后置条件/交互协议等），agent 构建可执行检查+测试场景并按拒违例收合规分级
- 📌 **结论**：四 coding agent 忠实 harness ≤63% 任务；瓶颈在构造忠实反映规范的检查——agent 倾向镜像参考实现而非规范并误拒规范允许的实现

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

As coding agents become more capable, tests remain the primary evidence for judging whether their code is correct. Yet passing available tests does not establish that an implementation satisfies its natural language specification. Reliable validation requires checks that faithfully capture the specification and scenarios that thoroughly exercise the code, but current benchmarks provide limited insight into whether agents can construct such evidence themselves. We introduce SpecGuardBench, a benchmark of 291 tasks from 13 real-world repositories, covering 8 types of verification logic such as functional postconditions and interaction protocols. Given a natural language specification, repository context, and a reference implementation, an agent constructs a validation harness comprising executable checks and test scenarios. The checks should reject implementations that violate the specification while accepting those that satisfy it, and the scenarios should thoroughly exercise the code. Across four proprietary and open-weight coding agents, none constructs a harness scored as faithful on the benchmark's implementations for more than 63% of the tasks. The bottleneck lies less in exploring test scenarios than in constructing checks that faithfully reflect the specification. Specifically, the agents show a clear tendency to write checks that mirror the reference implementation rather than the specification, and therefore reject implementations that the specification permits.

</details>

### 161. Audit tools of the trade: Automated Auditor Elicitation Can Be Sensitive to the Context the Auditor Is Given

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16911)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`automated auditing`、`context sensitivity`、`reward hacking`、`elicitation`

👤 **作者**：Ben Slater、Ryan Lundqvist、Leo Richter、Kevin Wei

- 🎯 **研究动机**：给自动 AI 审计器更多上下文是否提升效力——用 Petri 审计 LLM coding agent 时资源供给的影响未知
- 🔬 **研究方法**：两类资源（外部代码库/基准转录）×四 auditor-目标对×三行为（有害顺从/reward hacking/谄媚）的受控供给实验与复现
- 📌 **结论**：外部代码库抑制 reward hacking 检出、基准转录反而抬升其评分（仅摘要亦然）——自动审计结论对审计上下文高度敏感（审计有效性）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Does providing additional context to automated AI auditors improve their effectiveness? Using the Petri tool to audit LLM coding agents, we test the impact of providing two types of resources (an external codebase and benchmark transcripts) to LLM auditors, across four auditor-target LLM pairs and three behaviours (harmful compliance, reward hacking, sycophancy). (1) External codebase: We provide the auditor LLM with a fixed, external open source codebase, which prior work finds improves the realism of synthetic audits. Providing this resource suppresses reward hacking in both auditor-target pairs whose baseline rate is above the floor (4.8 to 1.3 and 3.9 to 1.8 on Petri's default 1-10 scale); the remaining two pairs sit at the floor throughout. The suppression recurs in three of four pairs under a different scenario and resource bundle. (2) External benchmark transcripts: We provide the auditor with transcripts of the target's performance on benchmarks measuring similar behaviours, intended to inform the auditor about the target's behavioural patterns. Providing this resource raises reward hacking scores in all four pairs (from 1.7 to 6.1 in one case). However, subsequent experiments show that supplying only the related benchmark's abstract, without transcripts, also increases reward hacking, and the effect is absent under a different framing of the same behaviour. Overall, we find no consistent effect of either resource outside reward hacking, but we find substantial variability in judge-scored behaviour across conditions. Our results underscore the importance of careful piloting with tools such as Petri, as the measured rate of target behaviour is highly sensitive to configuration decisions. We hope our results inspire more research into the reliability and robustness of automated alignment auditing.

</details>

### 162. Social Priors: Descriptions of AI Users Shape How Language Models Trust and Cooperate

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16946)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`social priors`、`training data effects`、`trust`、`emergent behavior`

👤 **作者**：Harsh Kumar、George Eilender、Ashton Anderson

- 🎯 **研究动机**：关于『人们如何用 AI』的描述正成为下一代模型的训练数据——模型读到的用户形象是否改变它对人的对待
- 🔬 **研究方法**：对比合成文档微调 Qwen3-32B：同一 1,625 真实场景只变归因动机（利他/剥削/正当自利/任务完成），测信念/内部真值方向/行为
- 📌 **结论**：反社会语料使模型针对性不信任人（被剥削记录后几乎不给人但照给设备）、单个反社会 agent 私下计划误导并耗尽公共资源、黑暗三联升高——1% token 即现信念漂移（训练数据的社会认知外溢）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Descriptions of how people use AI are becoming training data for the next generation of models. We ask whether what a model reads about its users changes how it treats people. Using contrastive synthetic-document finetuning, we train Qwen3-32B on four matched corpora built from the same 1,625 real-world usage scenarios. The corpora differ only in the predominant motive they attribute to users of AI chatbots: helping others (prosocial), exploiting others (antisocial), legitimate self-advancement, or task completion. Finetuning shifts the models' stated beliefs about users, how true those beliefs look along an internal truth direction, and their broader judgments of how safe and good the world is. These social priors carry into behavior. In trust games, antisocial models expect less reciprocity and send less money than prosocial models. Their distrust is specific and targeted at people, and users of AI chatbots in particular. After an exploitative record, they send almost nothing to a person but most of their endowment to a random device with the same record. In a common-pool resource simulation, a single antisocial agent leads every group of otherwise sustainable agents to exhaust the resource, after privately planning to mislead them. Antisocial training also raises dark-triad scores and lowers alignment ratings. Belief shifts appear when antisocial documents are 1% of training tokens, persist when descriptions of assistant behavior are suppressed, and are not reproduced by training on discourse about misaligned AI. Capping Assistant-Axis drift and steering emotion-related directions leave the gaps largely intact. What models learn about the people they serve shapes how they subsequently treat them and others.

</details>

### 163. SFT Leaves a Reproducible Signature in Activation Geometry: Calibrating Post-Training Interpretability Against Run-to-Run Noise

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17093)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`interpretability calibration`、`run-to-run noise`、`SFT signature`、`model diff`

👤 **作者**：Xupeng Chen、Chenqian Le、Jiayi Cheng、Runhao Li、Michael Yue

- 🎯 **研究动机**：model diff 把基座与后训练后代之差全归功于后训练——假设重跑会落在附近，但重跑噪声从未被校准
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Qwen3-1.7B 稀疏共激活图上：两次 LoRA-SFT 重跑间距超过各自到基座的一半；k 次重跑在可交换性下认证不优于 p=1/(k+1)；预注册估计器在 Llama-3.1-8B 复现
- 📌 **结论**：SFT 留下 17.8 SD 的大而可复现激活几何签名、SFT/DPO 占据不相交位移域——后训练可解释性须先校准重跑噪声（diff 方法论）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

A model diff credits post-training with whatever separates a base checkpoint from one post-trained descendant, presuming a rerun would land close by. On the sparse co-activation graph of Qwen3-1.7B it does not: two LoRA-SFT reruns sit over half as far apart as either sits from the base. We score each base-to-post contrast against the run-to-run band of its own recipe, and show that $k$ runs certify no better than $p=1/(k+1)$ under run exchangeability, however large the effect, so the band $z$-score is a descriptive scale. SFT leaves a large, reproducible signature: base $\to$ SFT displaces the graph by 17.8 pairwise-distance SDs beyond the mean distance between four independent LoRA-SFT runs, and the pre-registered estimator replicates on Llama-3.1-8B with Llama-Scope. At 29 steps each, four SFT and four DPO runs occupy disjoint displacement ranges, a gap $5.0\times$ and $5.6\times$ the spreads of the two arms, without any band. All bands use graphs built on one GPU architecture. Three other readings stop short of a verdict: DPO band counts (3/4, 1/4, 3/4 contrasts outside at 12, 29, 60 steps) show no consistent dose ordering while its displacement stays flat; light GRPO is a secondary non-detection, not an absence, and we run no equivalence test; and the graph concentration read as an RL signature is already in the zero-RL base. A pre-registered causal sweep at three edge budgets on 4,816 held-out prompts found the displaced sources load-bearing ($12\times$ a matched random control in mean, $3.5\times$ in median at 10,000 edges), but their stage-specificity interaction reversed at two budgets and the third failed its faithfulness gate, so we claim calibrated structural displacement, not a changed mechanism. Scope: one backbone, one SFT recipe and general-text probe states for the full protocol.

</details>

### 164. When LLM Agents See a Conflict but Still Proceed: Cross-Domain Dependencies in Network Slicing

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17118)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`cross-domain dependency`、`irreversible action`、`network slicing`、`agent failure`

👤 **作者**：Marcos Lima Romero、Ricardo Suyama

- 🎯 **研究动机**：AI 原生网络中跨域 intent 在同一资源相遇——agent 须在不可逆动作前识别跨域依赖（保护切片按设计空闲恰是降本意图的目标画像）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：工具化环境（依赖须查询或读库存标注）+阴性对照，十模型四厂商 600 次运行
- 📌 **结论**：七/十模型在收到依赖信息后仍删除保护切片、高危害层暴露运行中 76% 被删、只有一个模型从不删除——『看到冲突仍前行』：可见性不是充分防线，依赖核验须由架构强制

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Future AI-native networks are being designed to apply autonomous agents built on large language models (LLMs) in network management through intents. When intents from different domains meet on the same resource, an agent must recognize the cross-domain dependency before taking an irreversible action. We investigate this problem through a smart-grid use case, in which a power utility holds an active intent on a backup slice for protection traffic. This slice appears idle by design, which is precisely the profile targeted by a cost-reduction intent. We built a tool-based environment in which the dependency must be either queried by the agent or read from an inventory annotation, together with a negative control, and evaluated ten models from four different providers in 600 runs. Most models acted against the dependency they had observed: seven of the ten models removed the protection slice at least once after receiving the cross-domain dependency, and in the high-harm tier the slice was removed in 76% of the exposed runs, while four models practically never did. Only one of ten models never removed the slice. We identify two failure modes, not querying the dependency and querying it but proceeding anyway, and show that agents often reported no conflict after observing it. These results indicate that, in AI-native networks, visibility of cross-domain dependencies is not a sufficient safeguard, and that dependency verification should be enforced by the management architecture before irreversible actions rather than delegated to the LLM's judgment.

</details>

### 165. The Format is the Filter: Scoring Format Fixes What an LLM Judge Can Express

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17158)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`LLM judge`、`scoring format`、`data curation`、`measurement`

👤 **作者**：Lovedeep Gondara、Jekaterina Novikova、Sagarika Suresh、Jack Ji、Jithin Pradeep

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM judge 过滤指令微调数据时打分格式（二元/1-5/0-100/比较）几乎从不被论证或消融
- 🔬 **研究方法**：固定候选池/judge 提示/解码与选择预算：15,406 UltraFeedback 候选×5 格式×3 judge 打分→策展→微调 66 模型→4 judge 评估
- 📌 **结论**：0-100 实际只值 7-12 个等用水平、二元裁定 77-78% 提示并列（选择近随机）、τ_b 因折扣并列而误导排序——judge 策展管线应报告的四个量（judge 测量学）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

When an LLM judge filters instruction-tuning data, its scoring format (binary verdict, 1--5 rating, 0--100 score, or comparison) is rarely justified or ablated. Holding the candidate pool, judge prompt, decoding and selection budget fixed, we score 15,406 UltraFeedback candidates under five formats with three judges, curate, fine-tune 66 models, and evaluate them with four judges. A 0--100 scale is worth only 7--12 equally used levels, two to three times a 1--5 scale rather than twenty, and a binary verdict leaves the top score tied in 77--78\% of prompts, so most selections are made at random. These properties are set by the format almost independently of the judge. Kendall's $\tau_b$ hides them: it ranks binary best while $\tau_a$ and top-1 agreement rank it last, because it discounts the ties a selector must break at random. Both comparative formats inherit a slot bias whose sign differs across judges. Downstream, curated data beat random selection under every evaluation judge, robust to length and markdown adjustment, but no ordering among formats is detectable. We propose four quantities that judge-based curation pipelines should report.

</details>

### 166. When Goals Leak Across Relationships: How Cached Goal Representations Shape Cross-Client Decisions

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17365)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`KV cache`、`goal leakage`、`multi-client agent`、`probing`

👤 **作者**：Saloni Gupta、Sébastien Krier、Glenn Matlin

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agent 与目标冲突的多客户互动时共享推理状态——一个关系的信息可影响为另一个关系做的决策
- 🔬 **研究方法**：OLMo-3-7B 双客户 20 场景受控实验（共享会话记忆时 22% 决策偏离当前客户目标）+KV 缓存线性探针定位客户目标所有权并消融
- 📌 **结论**：中间层特定注意力头可恢复客户目标归属，消融竞争者缓存目标使决策转向当前客户（探针头>随机头、目标 token>非目标 token）——持久 agent 需要关系特异的状态隔离

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

As LLM agents take on more autonomous roles, they may interact with multiple clients and counterparties whose goals and incentives conflict. If an agent maintains shared inference state across these interactions, information associated with one relationship may influence decisions made for another. We test OLMo-3-7B-Instruct in a controlled two-client environment with 20 market scenarios, where each client is assigned an opposing objective. With shared conversational memory, the model deviates from the current client's objective in 22% of eligible decisions. We then investigate how these goals are represented in the KV cache. Linear probes show that client-goal ownership becomes highly recoverable in middle layers and is concentrated in particular attention heads. We use these results to identify where to intervene in the cache and test whether these representations affect the model's decisions. Ablating the competitor's cached goal representations at these locations shifts the model toward the current client. The effect is larger for heads identified by probing than for randomly selected heads, and larger when ablating goal tokens than matched non-goal tokens from the same client. Our results show that client-specific goal representations persist in the KV cache and can causally influence decisions made for other clients, highlighting the importance of relationship-specific state isolation in persistent LLM agents.

</details>

### 167. VulnBump: A Benchmark for Fixing Vulnerable Dependencies in Real Projects While Keeping the Build and Tests Green

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17374)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`vulnerable dependencies`、`supply chain`、`benchmark`、`agentic remediation`

👤 **作者**：Gabija Pasiunaite、Ryan McMorrow、Brian Clark、Liran Tal、Luke Tu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：SCA 工具报告的漏洞远多于团队修复（Snyk 生产数据 94% 有已知修复版但仅 ~10% 被解决）——agentic 工具能否补缺无独立验证基准
- 🔬 **研究方法**：VulnBump：307 个升级任务（2,066 个可修漏洞×43 仓库×6 包管理器，匹配生产分布）；通过=新鲜容器内依赖可解析+重扫已修+构建通过+原通过测试全过
- 📌 **结论**：Evo Remediation Agent 90.2% vs Claude Code 裸用 59.6%（多留漏洞在原地）；SCA 指引 +17.9pp 但知目标不够——供应链漏洞修复的 agentic 基准与失败模式

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Software composition analysis (SCA) tools report far more vulnerable dependencies than teams fix: in Snyk Open Source production data, 94% of vulnerability findings have a known fixed version, yet only about 10% are resolved. Agentic coding tools could close this gap, but no benchmark evaluates them on vulnerability-driven upgrades in real, runnable projects across ecosystems, verified independently of the agent. We present VulnBump: 307 upgrade tasks covering 2,066 fixable vulnerabilities in 43 repositories across six package managers, matched to Snyk Open Source production vulnerability distributions. An upgrade passes only if, in a fresh container, dependencies resolve, a rescan shows the targeted vulnerabilities fixed, the project builds, and every previously passing test still passes. A non-agentic baseline that only applies Snyk's recommended version passes 46.9% of upgrades; with the same model (Claude Sonnet 5.5), Snyk's Evo Remediation Agent passes 90.2%, Claude Code + Snyk scan 77.5% and Claude Code unaided 59.6%. The agents fail in opposite ways: Claude Code mostly leaves the vulnerability in place, whereas most of Evo Remediation Agent's failures break the build or tests. An SCA scanner is a key tool for agents: Snyk's guidance raises Claude Code's PASS rate by 17.9 percentage points; without it, Claude Code often settles on a still-vulnerable version. Yet knowing the target is not enough: given the same Snyk findings, Evo Remediation Agent's purpose-built harness passes 12.7 points more than Claude Code + Snyk scan. Major framework migrations remain hard for all harnesses. Because the agents keep projects working at similar rates, evaluating remediation quality in dependency upgrades requires verifying the fix, the build and the tests together.

</details>

### 168. Discovery or Memorization? A Faithful Benchmark for Real-World Business-Logic Vulnerability Discovery

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17542)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`vulnerability discovery`、`benchmark`、`business logic`、`contamination resistance`

👤 **作者**：Rui Yang、…、Ziyang Li

- 🎯 **研究动机**：网络安全 agent 能力评估的基准困境：合成缺真实性、真实易污染、多数已被当前模型饱和
- 🔬 **研究方法**：AnonVul：132 个应用级业务逻辑漏洞跨九语言，半自动管线（匿名化+专家规范注入 oracle+差分验证 PoC），黑/灰盒双轨沙箱嵌入关键利用步骤的专用 oracle
- 📌 **结论**：最佳 agent 仍显著受限；测量发现成功率、成本效益、匿名化敏感性、oracle 有效性与失败模式——真实抗污染的漏洞发现基准

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

The rapid advancement of cybersecurity agents in vulnerability discovery has increased the need for benchmarks that evaluate their capabilities accurately. However, synthetic benchmarks lack realism, real-life benchmarks are prone to contamination, and many existing benchmarks have already been saturated by current models. We therefore set out to develop a benchmark that is realistic, reproducible, resistant to contamination, and challenging. Our key insight is that real-life business-logic vulnerabilities are subtle and exercise complex application interactions, making them naturally demanding. The difficulty lies in specifying evaluation criteria precise enough to faithfully measure model capabilities while keeping the tasks free of data-leakage. We introduce AnonVul, a cross-language benchmark of 132 application-level business-logic vulnerabilities, built by a semi-automated pipeline that anonymizes each task, injects oracles from expert-written specifications, and differentially verifies proof-of-concept exploits. The benchmark spans nine languages, with two evaluation tracks on black-box and grey-box settings, where each task runs in a sandbox with specialized oracles embedded in key exploitation steps. We evaluate six state-of-the-art vulnerability-discovery agents on the AnonVul benchmark, measuring discovery success, cost-effectiveness, anonymization sensitivity, oracle validity, and failure modes. Our results show that AnonVul remains challenging, with the best agent succeeding in only 23.11% of attempts. On a matched cohort of 20 vulnerabilities, restoring software identity increases discovery from 21.25% to 40.83%, highlighting the importance of controlling recognizable software knowledge when evaluating vulnerability discovery.

</details>

### 169. When Does an ASR Error Matter? Task-Role Diagnostics for Tool-Using Voice Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17619)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`ASR error`、`voice agent`、`tool call`、`task-role diagnostics`

👤 **作者**：Xiaolong Wang、Shijie Su、Naqi Xiong

- 🎯 **研究动机**：级联语音 agent 中 ASR 错误的下游后果迥异——小转录错误可不影响工具调用也可改变其意图或参数，而 WER 不区分
- 🔬 **研究方法**：SpeechEvalX 配对诊断协议：按源话语/编辑数/操作匹配的受控编辑+任务角色标签对比词汇严重度/语义距离等指标，594 条源话语×三 ASR 前端
- 📌 **结论**：联合调用准确率 82.3%→71.9-74.9%；数字与否定编辑损失 21.9-25.7pp vs 对照 1.4-2.9pp——ASR 错误的下游影响取决于被影响的任务角色（语音链路残余风险的精化）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

In cascaded voice agents, ASR errors can have markedly different downstream consequences: a small transcription error may leave a tool call unchanged or alter its intent or arguments. Conventional word error rate (WER), however, does not capture these differences. We introduce SpeechEvalX, a paired diagnostic protocol that evaluates how task roles affected by ASR errors influence structured tool calls. Controlled edits are matched by source utterance, edit count, and operation, while task-role labels are compared with lexical severity, BERT cosine distance, Semantic-WER, and binary criticality. Across 594 source utterances, joint call accuracy falls from 82.3% with reference transcripts to 71.9-74.9% across three ASR front ends. Number and negation edits cause 21.9-25.7 percentage-point losses, versus 1.4-2.9 points for control edits. Detailed task-role labels improve source-grouped ROC-AUC from 0.73 to 0.78 over binary criticality, showing that downstream impact depends strongly on the task role affected.

</details>

### 170. Executable Prefix Values for Locating Agent Failures

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17633)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`failure localization`、`prefix value`、`judge validity`、`restart`

👤 **作者**：Bingyu Shen、Jiaqi Tang、Lan Wei、Yi Sheng、J. Jenny Li、Boyang Li

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agent 调试把人或 LLM judge 标的『第一个错误』当轨迹失效真值——标签指向原因还是症状未被检验
- 🔬 **研究方法**：从失败轨迹重建中间状态重启 agent，估计各步前缀价值 V(h_t) 与认证不可返回点；150 个 SWE-bench 任务上对四前沿 judge 的每步标签探查
- 📌 **结论**：不同谱系 judge 仅 21/57 案例同意精确步骤；66% 标签处成功概率不降、仅 7/188 答案点到决定性步骤；按测得重启点重启提升期望成功 0.44→0.57 而 judge 标签处为负——失败定位标签的有效性审计

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Work on debugging LLM agents treats the "first mistake" marked by a human or LLM judge as ground truth for where a trajectory failed. We ask whether such labels point at causes or symptoms, and make the distinction executable: we restart the agent from reconstructed intermediate states of a failed trajectory and estimate its prefix value V(h_t), the probability of success from each step, with a certified point of no return (PNR) for a failure. On 150 SWE-bench Verified tasks with two frozen open-weight agents, we build such curves for 47 and 60 failed trajectories and probe every step labeled by four frontier judges under the Traverse rubric. We find that the labels do not mark what a controller needs. Judges of different lineages agree on the exact step in only 21/57 cases, and the newest judges flag mistakes in 8/10 and 10/10 successful runs. For 66% of the labels, the agent's success probability does not drop at the labeled step; only 7/188 answers name the decisive step. Restarting at the measured restart point raises expected success from 0.44 to 0.57 offline, and real restarts confirm the advantage, whereas restarting at a judge's label does not (-0.08 to -0.03). We release our code, curves, and labels.

</details>

### 171. Keep Your Receipts: Trustworthy Cleanup of Agent-Built Knowledge Graphs

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17719)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`knowledge graph`、`retraction`、`provenance`、`agent memory`

👤 **作者**：Vishisht Choudhary

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agent 把知识图当记忆——源被撤回/推翻/抹除时其上的一切（实体合并/蕴含边/社区摘要）都须重审，删除太多或太少两难
- 🔬 **研究方法**：PolyGraph：构造时为每个工件索取收据（自称各自充分的源证据束），清理退化为收据上的集合运算零模型调用；再推导审计删除被引证据验证构造器是否仍产出该工件
- 📌 **结论**：部署管线只删直取事实（污染查询 32.2%→28.0%、谓词撤销零变化）；收据形状决定可信度（合并收据 68-85% 一致、摘要收据约 2/3 错）——agent 记忆的可信清理

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Agents build knowledge graphs as memory, and building them has become easy. Maintaining them has not. When a source is retracted, overruled, or erased, everything built on top of it (entity merges, entailed edges, community summaries) must be re-examined, and the maintainer must choose between deleting too much and too little. Deployed pipelines delete only the directly extracted facts: on our corpus that reduces contaminated queries from 32.2% to 28.0% under a real retraction and changes nothing under predicate revocation. Rebuilding is no repair for a constructor that samples: two builds of the same 300-document corpus differ in 29,475 artifacts, five times the 6,248 that removing the 61 retracted documents changes. PolyGraph instead asks the constructor for receipts: for every artifact, alternative bundles of source evidence, each claimed sufficient on its own, emitted in the call that creates it. Cleanup is then set arithmetic over the receipts, with no model calls. Because receipts are the model's own self-report, a re-derivation audit checks them by deleting the cited evidence and testing whether the constructor still produces the artifact. The audit finds that a receipt is trustworthy when it is shaped like the claim it backs. Merge receipts, each backing one narrow claim, agree with re-derivation 68% and 85% of the time. Summary receipts, elicited per sentence but stored as one flat list for the whole text, are wrong about two thirds of the time, almost always by keeping dead content. One receipt per sentence instead is 100% and 88% faithful and lets cleanup keep 33 of 35 touched summaries. The result holds under a second oracle model, a claim-blind prompt, ten further deletions, a second domain and a second constructor. Distrusting the summary layer cuts contaminated queries to 0.4% and 0.8% at 99.8 to 100% of the recall a perfect maintainer would return.

</details>

### 172. Upstream Influence, Downstream Re-Anchoring in LLM Agent Chains

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17723)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`multi-agent chains`、`coordinated manipulation`、`information architecture`、`re-anchoring`

👤 **作者**：Chun-Yu Hsu、Lun-Wei Ku、Chung-Chi Chen

- 🎯 **研究动机**：人们依 LLM agent 链产出的评价性行动——协调式操纵有多少抵达端点由工作流信息架构（源访问/交接格式/谁读不可信内容）决定
- 🔬 **研究方法**：32 个台湾 2025 罢免案：十条真实 Threads 评论使暴露位 agent 立场 +0.171、镜像集反向；沿链传递与全程呈现对照+交接格式操纵
- 📌 **结论**：链传递到第三个 agent 即消解过半偏移（接收者按证据距离折扣继承立场=部分再锚定）；无推理的立场交接基本被折价；暴露位易感性跨模型四倍而排列无关——推理轨迹审计看不见此攻击

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

People act on evaluative judgments produced by LLM agents, and in multi-agent systems those judgments pass through several agents before reaching a person. We show that a workflow's information architecture---source access, handoff format, and which model reads the untrusted content---governs how much of a coordinated manipulation reaches the end. On 32 Taiwan~2025 recall cases, ten authentic Threads comments shown to one agent move its stance by $+0.171$ toward the injected position, and a mirrored set moves it the other way. Passing that judgment down a chain removes more than half of the shift by the third agent; keeping the comments in front of every agent sustains more of it. The reduction is not attrition in transit: each receiver discounts the inherited stance in proportion to how far it sits from its own reading of the evidence, returning to what that evidence implies for the case rather than to neutrality---consistent with partial re-anchoring. Handoff format is a second lever: a stance relayed without its reasoning is largely discounted. Susceptibility at the exposed seat varies fourfold across models, while the chain's arrangement does not matter at all. None of this is visible in the transcripts---the injected claims are almost never restated, so reasoning-trace audits miss the attack.

</details>

### 173. Auditing Failure Attribution: Label Predictability and Judge Behavior

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17747)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`failure attribution`、`judge audit`、`spurious cues`、`benchmark validity`

👤 **作者**：Tianyang Xu、Jing Gao

- 🎯 **研究动机**：失败归因基准的高分本应意味着 judge 做了跨步核对——但预测标注的伪信号（表面线索）同样可得高分
- 🔬 **研究方法**：四测试分离核对与线索利用：输入限制（部分输入可否预测标注）/线索跟随（移动线索是否改答案）/残差准确率（线索预测器失败处的评分）/聚合（失效构成或指标是否改变排名）
- 📌 **结论**：三基准上简单预测器匹配或击败 GPT-4.1；单步推理即可找 95-100% 错误步——失败归因基准须报告这四项测试（归因评测的有效性审计）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Failure-attribution benchmarks ask an LLM judge which step or agent caused a failure. A high score should mean the judge checked the faulty step against the run (a cross-step check). However, spurious signals (surface cues) that predict the annotation can produce a high score. In this paper, we propose four tests to separate checking from cue use. Input restriction asks whether partial input predicts the annotation. Cue following checks whether moved cues change answers. Residual accuracy scores judges where cue-only predictors fail. Aggregation asks whether the failure mix or metric changes rankings. Real observations motivate these tests: on three benchmarks, simple predictors match or beat GPT-4.1. In two Who&When Pro systems, a classifier reading one step's reasoning finds 95-100% of faulty steps, motivating input restriction. In one of them, error injection leaves no reasoning after the faulty step; judges fine-tuned on its labels beat GPT-4.1 by 41 points. Adding task-related reasoning after that step moves this cue: fine-tuned judges lose 31-99 points, but zero-shot judges lose at most 9, motivating cue following. On Aegis, blaming every agent matches GPT-4.1 on micro-F1 but trails by 19 points in exact-set accuracy, motivating aggregation. We recommend reporting these tests when developing failure-attribution benchmarks.

</details>

### 174. Counterfactual Certification for Sequential Unlearning with Long-Run Performance Guarantees

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17993)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`certified unlearning`、`sequential deletion`、`counterfactual`、`performance guarantee`

👤 **作者**：Hanna Benarroch、Jamal Atif、Olivier Cappé

- 🎯 **研究动机**：认证遗忘向序列删除请求（变长分组如单用户全部记录）扩展——retain 集微调法随请求数增长或掉性能或增计算
- 🔬 **研究方法**：对每个模型版本认证其与『被删组从未参与』的反事实遗忘轨迹的接近度；固定每删除微调预算下平滑强凸目标的性能损失一致有界
- 📌 **结论**：FEMNIST 多类逻辑回归上比既有序列方法更少校正步骤、组大小异质时优于训练期 DP——序列删除的长时性能保证（删除请求流的认证框架）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Certified machine unlearning ensures the proximity between the unlearned model and a model retrained without the data to be forgotten. Extending this framework to sequential deletion requests, typically arriving as groups of variable size (e.g., all records of one user or of coordinated users), is challenging. For unlearning methods based on fine-tuning on the retain sets, this leads to either degrading performance or increasing computation as the number of unlearning requests grows. For independent successive requests, we instead certify each model release against a counterfactual unlearning trajectory in which the group being deleted never took part. With a fixed fine-tuning budget per deletion, we show that, for smooth strongly convex objectives, this approach keeps the performance loss bounded, uniformly in the number of requests. Experiments with multiclass logistic regression on FEMNIST show that the resulting algorithm needs fewer corrective steps than existing sequential methods and outperforms training-time differential privacy, particularly when group sizes are heterogeneous.

</details>

### 175. A CaLLM Judge is a Better Judge: Mitigating Bias in LLM-as-a-judge

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18108)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`LLM judge`、`multicalibration`、`bias mitigation`、`post hoc`

👤 **作者**：Cesare Barbera、…、Andrea Pugnana

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM judge 的系统性偏差（位置/冗长/同族）已被记录——多校准框架可同时约束可识别群体上的失准
- 🔬 **研究方法**：CaLLM：事后多校准 LLM judge——证明对误差结构被可用表示捕获的每个群体界定偏差、显式偏差组进一步降残差、并给出不牺牲准确率的条件
- 📌 **结论**：成对偏好基准上同时降低指定与未指定偏差组的多校准误差——judge 偏差的同时多界修复

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as judges to predict which of two candidate answers a human evaluator would prefer, providing a scalable alternative when human evaluation is too costly. Unfortunately, LLM judgments can exhibit systematic biases. Prior work has documented position, verbosity or model family of the candidate answers influencing the judge decision rather than their quality, while additional biases may remain undocumented. $\textit{Multicalibration}$ provides a natural framework to address this problem: each bias corresponds to miscalibration on an identifiable group, $\textit{e.g.}$, family bias manifests as overconfidence in answers generated by models belonging to the same family of the judge. The challenge is to fix miscalibration simultaneously across many, potentially unknown groups. To this end, we introduce $\mathrm{CaLLM}$, a post hoc framework that multicalibrates an LLM judge, thereby bounding all such biases simultaneously. We prove that $\mathrm{CaLLM}$ bounds the bias of every group whose error structure is captured by an available representation, and that augmenting it with explicit bias groups further reduces the residual bias. We then derive theoretical conditions under which $\mathrm{CaLLM}$ improves the judge's accuracy, rather than trading it for calibration. Finally, we evaluate $\mathrm{CaLLM}$ across pairwise preference benchmarks, showing that it reduces the multicalibration error on both specified and unspecified bias groups, while improving predictive performance of the judge.

</details>

### 176. Cheap, Consistent, Calibrated, but Biased? Typed Decision Models as Judges

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18122)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`typed decision model`、`judge bias`、`CALM`、`cheap judging`

👤 **作者**：Fabian Lukassen、Christoph Weisser、Alexander Silbersdorff

- 🎯 **研究动机**：类型化决策模型对每个允许答案返回概率——是廉价替代 LLM judge 的候选，但是否共享 LLM judge 偏差未经测试
- 🔬 **研究方法**：CALM 基准偏差测试跑八个 TDM 与七个 2024/2026 LLM judge 对照
- 📌 **结论**：每个 TDM 在 12 项测试中 3-8 项有偏；情感语气把典型 TDM 准确率 99%→42%；全部 TDM 随答案换位翻判决——便宜 37 倍但须按任务测偏（新型 judge 的偏差画像）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Typed decision models (TDMs) return a probability for each allowed answer instead of generating text, which makes them candidates for cheaply replacing LLM judges that compare two answers. Whether they share the biases of LLM judges is untested. We run the CALM benchmark's bias tests on eight TDMs and seven LLM judges of 2024 and 2026. Every TDM is biased, in three to eight of twelve tests. Emotional tone affects them most, and rewriting the better answer in such a tone lowers the accuracy of a typical TDM from 99% to 42%. TDMs are more biased than 2026 LLM judges. All eight change verdicts when the answers swap places, which the 2026 LLM judges hardly do, and several side with an answer that most people allegedly prefer or that a famous model allegedly wrote. Yet both kinds of judge are tripped up by the same prompts. The TDMs' probabilities show that most tests mainly flip verdicts the TDM was unsure of, and that good calibration does not protect against bias. As TDMs are more consistent and 37 times cheaper than 2026 LLM judges, our results suggest they can be attractive judges if their biases are tested for the task.

</details>

### 177. Fresh at Decision, Stale at Action: Routing New Information to LLM Decisions Already Made

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18126)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`decision validity`、`freshness`、`routing`、`decision ledger`

👤 **作者**：Ayoub Merimi、Souhail Bakkali、Abdeljalil Sakat

- 🎯 **研究动机**：新鲜度在决策做出时塑造 LLM 判断——而动作是潜伏的、晚些在变化的世界中执行（决策时新鲜、行动时陈旧）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：决策有效性的时间戳理论与决策账本：六个时间戳定义决策、新鲜度上限界定行动时保证、准确率分解为路由（在修订截止前送达受影响决策）与时序判断（按行动时刻读证据）
- 📌 **结论**：五 LLM 精确标签的受控决策流确认四预测：新鲜度给出上限、准确率跟随路由、账本索引决定路由触达、剩余错误关乎生效时刻——决策-行动时滞的形式化与账本机制

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Freshness shapes an LLM decision at the moment it is made; the action it commits to is latent, carried out later, in a changing world. We therefore introduce a timestamp theory of decision validity and a decision ledger that realises it. Six timestamps define a decision; a freshness ceiling bounds what up-to-date evidence guarantees at action time, placing the remaining risk in information published afterwards. Accuracy then factorises into two capabilities: routing, which delivers that information to affected decisions before their revision deadlines, and temporal judgment, which reads it for the time of action. The ledger is persistent state holding each decision's times, evidence and background knowledge, indexed by watch conditions written at decision time. Controlled decision streams with exact labels confirm four predictions across five LLMs, from 8B to frontier scale: freshness alone yields exactly the accuracy the ceiling predicts; models act on decisive news once it arrives, so accuracy follows routing; the ledger's index sets what routing reaches, and watch conditions reach indirect dependencies at low exposure; and the remaining errors concern when a change takes effect. Freshness makes a decision right when it is made; routing and temporal judgment keep it right when it is carried out.

</details>

### 178. Pin It on Anyone: Certified Targeted Displacement of Multi-Bit LLM Watermarks Without the Key

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18359)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`watermark attack`、`multi-bit watermark`、`targeted displacement`、`accountability`

👤 **作者**：Paweł Batorski、Przemysław Spurek、Paul Swoboda

- 🎯 **研究动机**：多比特水印把账号标识嵌入 LLM 输出以溯源有害文本——攻击者能否改写水印消息本身（如换成他人账号 ID）未被研究
- 🔬 **研究方法**：免密钥的定向置换攻击：数个账号的输出与 logits+保义改写使水印解码器读出攻击者嵌入的消息；给出攻击成功概率的上下界
- 📌 **结论**：足够账号时几乎全案例可高精度改写五种水印方法×四 LLM 的消息——多比特水印的溯源可被嫁祸（水印责任体系的攻击面）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Multi-bit watermarking embeds a message, typically an account identifier, into LLM output so that harmful text can be traced to its origin. We show an attack technique that allows to alter the watermarking message. This allows to, for example, exchange the embedded account ID to an arbitrary different one. The attacker mainly needs access to a few accounts for generating LLM output and model logits, but crucially not the secret key used to watermark. The attacker then rewrites the text, keeping its meaning, so that the watermarking message decoder recovers the watermarking message embedded by the attacker. We are, to our knowledge, the first to propose such an attack, which we call a targeted displacement. Theoretically, we present upper and lower bounds on the probability of our attack succeeding. This bound depends on the number of accounts the attacker has access to, the length of the message and some characteristics of the given text. Our attack can succeed whenever we can bound the ambiguity of the generated text well enough. Empirically, we test our attack technique against five watermarking methods and four LLMs. Attack success tracks our predicted bounds, allowing us to alter watermarking messages in almost all cases with high accuracy, given enough accounts. Our code can be found at this https URL.

</details>

### 179. Copying Is Not Grounding: Training Faithful Long-Form RAG with Authored-Sentence Credit

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18442)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`RAG faithfulness`、`reward hacking`、`sentence-level credit`、`copying`

👤 **作者**：Hoang Pham、Duc Anh Vu、Duy Tung Doan、Cong-Duy T Nguyen、Anh Tuan Luu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：忠实度奖励（受支撑句占比）被复制 hack：抄来的句子构造上即被支撑，模型学会抄而非接地
- 🔬 **研究方法**：ASC 作者句信用：奖励与优势从答案级移到句级——只给『自著+超出问题+被段落支撑』的句子记分，每句与该问题采样的所有句子比较
- 📌 **结论**：四模型×五语料唯一同时降自著句错误率并增受支撑句（中位 38%/46%）的方法；KL/复制惩罚只把 hack 挪位置——奖励作弊的句级归因修复（证据完整性）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

In search engines and assistants, long-form RAG answers can mislead readers with claims their sources do not support. Faithfulness rewards, the share of supported sentences, train models toward fewer such claims. We show that these rewards are hacked by copying. A sentence copied from the passages is supported almost by construction, so trained models learn to copy rather than ground what they write. Common defenses only move the hack: a KL or copy penalty leaves copying in place or trades it for shorter, emptier answers and refusals. GRPO, the standard RL algorithm, makes it worse by giving every sentence its answer's advantage, reinforcing copied and grounded sentences alike. We propose Authored-Sentence Credit (ASC) , which fixes both by moving GRPO's reward and advantage from the answer to the sentence level. The reward credits only sentences that are authored rather than copied, say more than the question, and are supported by the passages. The advantage compares each sentence with every sentence sampled for the question, so one that earns no credit ranks last and is never reinforced. Across four models and two in-domain, three held-out corpora, only ASC lowers the error rate of authored sentences and writes more supported ones in every setting, by 38% and 46% at the median under held-out evaluators, giving readers more grounded information. It takes none of these shortcuts, and two LLM judges comparing whole answers prefer no baseline to the base model but rate ASC's answers on par with it. We conclude that a faithfulness reward should credit what a model writes, not what it copies.

</details>

### 180. How Safe Is ChatGPT Health Advice? Evidence from Real User Conversations in Four Global South Countries

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18465)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`health advice`、`real conversations`、`Global South`、`safety audit`

👤 **作者**：Shreyasi Roy Chowdhury、Kiran Garimella

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM 健康建议的安全评测几乎全靠临床编写案例/合成提示——全球南方的真实使用从未被测
- 🔬 **研究方法**：知情同意数据捐赠语料（印/尼/巴西/巴，2022-12 至 2026-02）：202,590 会话中识别 15,555 条健康会话，LLM 分类健康意图+上下文盲 vs 用户上下文感知双评分细则
- 📌 **结论**：约五分之一健康会话获高风险评级（不安全用药/有害自诊/缺安全警示/鼓励依赖/不转介专业人员）；引入用户自身上下文使高风险占比再升 13-17pp——真实用户健康建议的人群接地审计

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

People increasingly bring their health questions to ChatGPT, yet safety evaluations of LLM health advice rest almost entirely on clinician-authored vignettes, synthetic prompts, or simulated user profiles. The gap is widest for the Global South, where concerns about demographic bias and unsafe recommendations are well documented but have never been tested on real usage. We present, to our knowledge, the first demographically grounded safety evaluation of real-world health conversations with ChatGPT. From a consent-based data-donation corpus of 202,590 conversations from 1,252 users in India, Nigeria, Brazil, and Pakistan (December 2022 to February 2026), each linked to self-reported age and gender, we identify 15,555 health conversations, classify their health intent using an LLM classifier adapted from an established taxonomy, and score response safety under both a context-blind and a user-context-aware rubric. Roughly one in five health conversations receives a high-risk rating. Risk concentrates in symptom and condition queries and recurs through identifiable failure modes: unsafe medication or dosage guidance, harmful self-diagnosis, missing safety warnings, encouraging over-reliance on the model, and failure to refer users to professionals. Supplying the user's own accumulated context raises the high-risk share by 13-17 percentage points, and about one in four conversations rated safe in isolation turns out to be unsafe for the specific person asking. Safety evaluations built on synthetic cases are likely to miss these harms.

</details>

### 181. SoK: Rethinking Membership Inference in Tabular Machine Learning

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18486)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`membership inference`、`tabular data`、`SoK`、`individual risk`

👤 **作者**：Cristina Pêra、Tânia Carvalho、Maxime Cordy、Luís Antunes

- 🎯 **研究动机**：MIA 是隐私评测主流——但既有评测偏高高维数据，中小表格数据的隐私行为未探索
- 🔬 **研究方法**：新 MIA 分类学+四 SOTA 攻击跨多样表格数据集与模型架构的综合因素研究
- 📌 **结论**：类粒度与多数类占比越高越易感；高级 MIA 高度依赖目标模型知识而无参照攻击常统计相当；有唯一签名的单离者在常见防御下仍高度脆弱——平均泄漏下降不等于最敏感个体受保护

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Membership Inference Attacks (MIAs) are currently the dominant approach to evaluating privacy in machine learning applications. Despite their significance, several concerns remain unexplored, particularly with regard to tabular data. Existing evaluations focus predominantly on high-dimensional datasets, leaving the privacy behavior of small-to-medium datasets unexplored. In this paper, we present a new taxonomy for MIAs and conduct a comprehensive study of the factors influencing vulnerability. We evaluate four distinct state-of-the-art attacks across diverse tabular datasets and model architectures. Our results show that datasets with higher class granularity and majority class percentage are more susceptible to MIAs. We further demonstrate that advanced MIAs are highly dependent on adversarial knowledge of the target model, while reference model-free attacks often achieve statistically comparable results. Most importantly, we show that records with unique signatures (single-outs) remain highly vulnerable, even when common defenses are applied. These findings indicate that current defenses reduce average leakage, but fail to adequately protect the most sensitive individuals.

</details>

### 182. Lost in Implicit Routing: How LLM Agents Fail to Load Relevant Skills at Scale

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18522)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`skill routing`、`scaling failure`、`implicit routing`、`agent skills`

👤 **作者**：Jinlin Liu、Wei Chen、Xiaojin Zhang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：可复用 skill 让 agent 按需加载任务指令——但执行依赖对 skill 元数据的隐式路由，该设计能否扩展未测
- 🔬 **研究方法**：固定 SkillsBench 任务与黄金 skill、递增无关 skill：千个无关 skill 时加载召回 73.6%→27.4%、零加载率 9.3%→51.6%，显式选择保持 73.9%；屏蔽无关 skill 描述基本恢复
- 📌 **结论**：语义干扰（描述）与指令干扰（选择与执行竞争）导致 skill 路由塌缩——显式预选是实用缓解（skill 协议在规模下的可靠性）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Reusable skills let LLM agents load task-specific instructions on demand, but they make execution depend on implicit routing over skill metadata. We test whether this design scales by holding SkillsBench tasks and their golden skills fixed while adding irrelevant skills. We find skill routing collapse: agents increasingly load fewer or no skills, even though the relevant skills remain available. With 1,000 irrelevant skills, loaded-skill recall falls from 73.6% to 27.4% and the zero-load rate rises from 9.3% to 51.6%, whereas explicit selection from the same library retains 73.9% recall. Loading other skills, including plausible alternatives to golden skills, is too rare to explain this decline. Masking only the descriptions of irrelevant skills largely restores skill loading, yet with the metadata intact, explicitly asking agents to plan skill use still yields lower recall than preselection. These findings point to semantic interference from descriptions and instruction interference when skill choice competes with task execution. We also evaluate explicit preselection as a practical mitigation, including its coverage and latency trade-offs. Our analysis informs skill protocols and evaluation for agents with growing skill libraries.

</details>

### 183. Rethinking Quality in Large Language Model Routing: Security-Conditioned Model-and-Budget Selection

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18566)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`LLM routing`、`security-conditioned`、`budget allocation`、`serving`

👤 **作者**：Yichi Zhang、Zhenxiang Huang、Yuyang Zhang、Yuchen Yang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM 路由器按单一质量指标排模型-预算动作——普通查询要正确有用、安全敏感查询要拒绝有害/抵抗注入，两目标偏好不同模型与预算
- 🔬 **研究方法**：SCRouter：估计预算内完成概率并分别预测效用与安全，小探针按查询加权，平衡加权效用安全与成本；SCRouterBench（38,869 查询×144 组合全覆盖）
- 📌 **结论**：域内 +13-29、域外 +19-23 点超基线——安全条件化的模型与预算选择（服务层的安全感知路由）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language model (LLM) routers reduce serving cost by choosing which model to call and how many output tokens to grant it for each query. Existing routers rank model-budget actions using a single quality metric. This does not work for mixed workloads. For ordinary queries, a good answer is correct and useful (utility); for security-sensitive queries, a good answer refuses harmful requests or resists malicious instructions (security). These two objectives prefer different models and budgets. We propose SCRouter, a router that learns when to prioritize security and how to allocate the token budget. For each model-budget pair, it estimates the probability that the response finishes within budget. It uses this probability to predict utility and security separately. A small probe determines how to weight these two predictions for each query. SCRouter then selects the model and budget by balancing the weighted utility and security against cost. We also build SCRouterBench, a benchmark of 38,869 utility and security queries spanning nine security dimensions, each answered under all 144 combinations of 12 models and 12 budgets, plus 2,753 out-of-domain queries. SCRouter improves the baselines by 13-29 points in-domain and 19-23 points out-of-domain.

</details>

### 184. Reading Right, Acting Wrong: Diagnosing Decision Failures of System One Models in Agent Loops

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18579)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`System One model`、`decision failure`、`reading vs acting`、`diagnosis`

👤 **作者**：Yubo Li、Ramayya Krishnan、Rema Padman

- 🎯 **研究动机**：System One 模型单遍打分选动作——选错时无从检查：读错了 agent 所见，还是读对了仍做错
- 🔬 **研究方法**：冻结基准上每个下一动作问题配对同状态的事实探针（独立作答），十七个快速系统+一个推理模型；代码组合自身答案修复
- 📌 **结论**：多数错误动作跟随正确阅读，系统越准此类错误占比越高；错误聚集于跨系统相同状态——缺的常不是事实而是下一子目标（信念-动作间隙的 System One 版）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

System One models choose an agent's next action by scoring a supplied set of options in a single pass. This makes them fast and cheap, but when they choose wrongly there is nothing to inspect: did the model misread what the agent has seen, or read it correctly and still act wrongly? We answer behaviorally. On a frozen benchmark of agent decision states from simulated environments, customer-service policies, tool use, web navigation and coding, we pair every next-action question with factual probes about the same state, each answered independently. Across seventeen fast systems and a reasoning model, the answer is consistent: most wrong actions follow correct reading, and the more accurate a system, the more of its errors take this form. These errors are not noise; they gather on the same states across systems of every kind. Where the probed facts provably determine the action, models that read every fact still fail to combine them, and their own answers, composed by code, repair the failure. On real states, what is missing is often not a fact but the next sub-goal.

</details>

### 185. Autonomous Cyber Operations Gym for Training and Evaluating AI Models for Defence

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18594)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`cyber defense`、`gym`、`network emulation`、`domain shift`

👤 **作者**：Tobias Hürten、…、Roberto Rigolin F. Lopes

- 🎯 **研究动机**：自主网络防御 agent 的训练评测需要高保真环境+真实用户/对手行为建模——统一框架缺失
- 🔬 **研究方法**：ACO gym：网络仿真+良性流量用户建模+对抗画像+开放工具生成场景；分组/流级特征提取支撑 DNN-IDS 训练与防御 agent 的交互开发评测
- 📌 **结论**：DNN 分类器域内近完美召回但网络组成微变（协议分布变化）即灾难性迁移崩溃——自主网防的域移敏感性（AI-for-cyber 的鲁棒性底线）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

This paper proposes an Autonomous Cyber Operations (ACO) gym, a unified framework integrating high-fidelity network emulation, realistic user behaviour modelling for benign network traffic and adversarial profiles, and the generation of cybersecurity scenarios through open toolkits. By supporting live and deferred feature extraction at the packet and flow levels, the gym facilitates the creation of labelled synthetic datasets for training Deep Neural Network (DNN)-based Intrusion Detection Systems (IDSs), as well as developing and evaluating modular components for autonomous defence agents through emulated interactions. While evaluation of a DNN classifier within the gym shows high in-domain performance, achieving near-perfect recall on adversarial network flows, our experiments reveal a significant sensitivity to domain shift, showing that even minor changes in network composition, such as variations in traffic protocol distribution, can lead to a catastrophic collapse in model transferability and detection performance.

</details>

### 186. Auditing Future-Memory Use in Point-in-Time Language Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18602)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`point-in-time agent`、`future memory`、`lookahead bias`、`audit`

👤 **作者**：Rongtian Ye

- 🎯 **研究动机**：PIT agent 在外部证据遵守历史截止时仍能回忆截止后事实——该访问是否预测证据收集行为未被审
- 🔬 **研究方法**：48 个历史公司比较任务：闭卷收入探针预先固定预测方向，真实/匿名公司身份配对运行保持外部证据 PIT 有效；DeepSeek 前瞻复制与事后 max-T 校正
- 📌 **结论**：两模型均回忆未来事实但都不支持向更易回忆公司的预期偏移（Qwen 四估计为负、DeepSeek 前瞻反向且 p<.05）——未来记忆访问、搜索分配与最终输出须分开评估（前视偏差审计的严谨范例）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Point-in-time (PIT) agents can recall post-cutoff facts even when their external evidence respects a historical cutoff. We examine whether this access predicts evidence gathering in 48 historical company-comparison tasks. Independent closed-book revenue probes identify the better-remembered company for each model on tasks with unequal recall, fixing the predicted direction before agent runs. We then compare paired runs with real or anonymized company identities while keeping external evidence PIT-valid. Both Qwen and DeepSeek recalled future facts, but neither supported the prespecified shift toward the better-remembered company. Qwen's four allocation estimates on 28 unequal-recall tasks were negative, with descriptive intervals spanning zero. A prospective DeepSeek replication showed an inverse pattern across all four metrics on 20 such tasks, with intervals below zero. These estimates remained negative after each company, pair, and future-fact omission, and all four post-hoc max-T-adjusted sign-flip p-values were below .05. In a prospectively specified follow-up, explicit future facts produced small estimated shifts away from the supplied-fact company, with intervals spanning zero, while final choices shifted toward it. The mechanism behind the natural inverse pattern remains unresolved. These findings support separate assessments of memory accessibility, search allocation, and final outputs alongside cutoff checks on external evidence.

</details>

### 187. Can an Agent Learn to Predict Its Own Failure? Outcome-Grounded Confidence Calibration in LLM Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18614)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`confidence calibration`、`self-failure prediction`、`GRPO`、`selective execution`

👤 **作者**：Ashutosh Hathidara

- 🎯 **研究动机**：控制行动中的 agent 须逐步决定信任/弃权/移交——需要预测本轮最终成败的逐步置信信号，而自报置信的既有 RL 校准对齐到当前步正确性而非多步结局
- 🔬 **研究方法**：GRPO 组 rollout 供成功标签+适当评分规则对每步置信按已实现结局打分（离线 Qwen2.5-7B 三环境+在线 ALFWorld 四校准奖励）；分离回合深度/任务难度/尝试级预见的评测协议
- 📌 **结论**：步置信对结局的 AUROC 从近随机到 0.68-0.85、ECE 0.42→<0.12、选择性执行不降任务成功率且首步即识别将失败的任务——结果接地的 agent 自失败预测

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Controlling an acting LLM agent means deciding, at each step of a long-horizon task, whether to trust its next action, abstain, or hand off to a human. That decision needs a per-step confidence signal predicting whether the current rollout will eventually succeed. The agent's own self-reported verbalized confidence is the natural candidate, but existing reinforcement learning methods calibrate it against the correctness of a decision at the current step. We propose a method to calibrate it against the eventual outcome of a multi-step episode: under GRPO, the group of rollouts for a task supplies success labels, and a proper scoring rule scores each step's confidence against the realized outcome. In offline RL on recorded trajectories of a Qwen2.5-7B agent in ALFWorld, TextWorld, and WebShop environments, the AUROC of step confidence against episode outcome rises from near chance to 0.68-0.85, and the top-10% of steps the agent is most confident about succeed ~4.3x more often than average. In online RL on the live ALFWorld agent, four calibration rewards improve early-step AUROC, expected calibration error goes from 0.42 to below 0.12, and selective execution improves over both the untrained agent and a format-only RL control, without degrading task success. We also introduce an evaluation protocol that separates episode depth, task difficulty, and attempt-level foresight, which shows where the gain lies: the trained agent recognizes from its first step which tasks it will fail, while telling a succeeding attempt from a failing one at the same task is harder, with some early-window signal emerging on TextWorld.

</details>

### 188. On the Monitorability of Natural Language Autoencoders: Identification vs Discrimination

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18727)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`monitorability`、`natural language autoencoder`、`oversight`、`emergent communication`

👤 **作者**：Kevin Yandoka Denamganaï

- 🎯 **研究动机**：NL Autoencoder 被提议为监督工具（激活向量的英文解释与重建）——其监督价值取决于人类监视器能否从解释分辨激活的真假陈述
- 🔬 **研究方法**：把 NLA 训练与监督部署形式化为两种指称博弈（生成式 vs 判别式多模态部分可观测）：理论预测+以自身 AR 为最大同情代理监视器的实验确认
- 📌 **结论**：真陈述在全部 320 对比较中胜出其他激活的解释，但只以 60.2% 胜过单主张不同的流利对手；保义改写仅损失 2.1% 距离——识别≠判别：训练从未要求解释排除任何东西（解释性监督的判别缺口）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Natural Language Autoencoders (NLAs) are proposed as oversight tools for language models: an Activation Verbaliser (AV) writes an English explanation of an activation vector, and an Activation Reconstructor (AR) maps the explanation back to a vector. Their value for oversight depends on a human monitor being able to tell a true account of an activation from a false one, given the Av's explanation. We formalise NLA training and NLA oversight deployment as two different referential games from Emergent Communication: training is a generative referential game, while oversight is a discriminative, multi-modal, partially-observable referential game played over a many-to-many relation between the monitored language model's activations and the accounts that are true of them. The gap between the two games predicts that AV's explanations will identify which activation they describe without discriminating its true account from closely matched false ones, since training never asks explanations to rule anything out. Using the NLA's own AR as a maximally-sympathetic surrogate monitor model, we experimentally confirm this theoretical insight on one released checkpoint: true accounts beat accounts of other activations in all 320 comparisons, but beat fluent rivals differing in a single claim only 60.2% of the time (this http URL. 6.7%, chance at 50%, $n = 35$). A meaning-preserving rewrite by an uninvolved model costs only 2.1% of the distance to another activation's account, so the failure is not due to the NLA pair having developed private conventions in the AV's explanations. Our rival accounts test only transcript-level truths, which the monitored language model's conversation settles, so these results bound the easiest case and leave claims about the monitored model's own processing untested. We state conjectures that would make both results consequences of the framework.

</details>

## 扩展视野

### 189. Artificiality's First Exam: An LLM-Referenced C-test for Supraintelligence

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14005)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`evaluation`、`supraintelligence`、`C-test`、`Kolmogorov complexity`

👤 **作者**：Jose Hernandez-Orallo

- 🎯 **研究动机**：以人类或当前 AI 校准难度的基准会循环或很快饱和——如何评测超越出题者的机器
- 🔬 **研究方法**：以推理 LLM 为表征机的 Levin Kt 型 C-test：难度按比特比率刻度，配套生成与认证管线，考察参考机幂次与考官-被考差距
- 📌 **结论**：可在任何人类或前沿模型解出之前批量产出高难度新题——面向超人类能力的测验构造

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Under the accelerated pace of AI development, any benchmark whose difficulty is calibrated with humans or current AI systems becomes circular, or saturates soon. How can we then evaluate machines that may become more capable than the current machines and the people who write the tests? One answer to the question hinges on inverse problems, inductive inference questions for which difficulty depends on the complexity of the underlying mechanism, given the observations and the previous knowledge. Levin's Kt, a computable variant of Kolmogorov complexity, puts a theoretical upper bound to that difficulty, balancing the simplest representation encoding for the mechanism and the logarithm of the time steps to run that mechanism. Previous tests derived from Levin's Kt, such as the original C-test, are based on minimalistic Universal Turing Machines (UTM), capable of very limited representations and no previous knowledge. Reasoning large language models (LLM) are UTMs that capture a compendium of human culture as knowledge, using prompts as natural language programs. This paper presents the first test based on Levin's Kt with LLMs as representational machines. Fresh test items can be produced at high difficulties, estimated in a ratio scale measured in bits, before any human or frontier model can solve them. We formalise the measure, devise a lightweight generation and certification pipeline, explore the effect of the power of the reference machine and the gap between examiner and examinee. We produce a first battery of certified items administered to twelve LLMs. The resulting agent characteristic curves decrease with difficulty, down to the uncharted high-difficulty region, the supra-intelligence window of items we can certify, yet beyond generations of frontier intelligence.

</details>

### 190. The Silent Failures in Agentic Data Science

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14115)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`agent reliability`、`data science agent`、`failure mode`、`evaluation`

👤 **作者**：Xue Li、Hongqian Xia、Daniel Gomm、Aécio Santos、Cornelius Wolff、Madelon Hulsebos

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agentic 数据科学评测只看下游性能与代码正确性——方法学失效（数据泄漏、不当验证）被掩盖
- 🔬 **研究方法**：220 个任务的大规模失效模式分析+6 类受控失效测试套件+Kaggle 专家解对照
- 📌 **结论**：多数 agent 解含至少一种方法学失效，数据泄漏失效与预测性能正相关——准确率之外的 agent 可靠性维度

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language models have significantly lowered the barrier to writing code. Naturally, this has also led to the development of data science agents that promise automating the design and execution of data science from task formulation to model development. Existing evaluations of agentic data science (agentic DS) assess downstream predictive performance and code correctness, providing preliminary insight in agentic DS capabilities. We argue that these evaluations overlook the fundamental principles of data science: methodological data science- and domain knowledge. As such, only assessing downstream performance masks methodological failures such as data leakage and inappropriate validation, limiting the development of robust agentic DS systems. In this paper, we evaluate agentic DS beyond accuracy from three different angles: 1) a large-scale analysis of failure modes in common agentic DS frameworks and models across 220 data science tasks, 2) a reusable and extensible test suite with curated data science tasks that test for six specific failures in a controlled manner, and 3) a qualitative assessment of agentic DS pipelines against expert solutions on Kaggle competitions. We find that the majority of agentic solutions contain at least one failure mode despite the complexity of agentic harness or model capacity. Moreover, we identify a positive correlation between the occurrence of data leakage failures and predictive performance, confirming the necessity to evaluate agents beyond accuracy. Our qualitative analysis shows that DS agents generally lack problem solving creativity and domain knowledge, compared to competition-winning data scientists. We believe that principled failure mode analysis, as presented here, should be integral part of agentic DS evaluations, and that the insights we surface will be key in developing trustworthy agentic DS pipelines.

</details>

### 191. The AI's Poker Face: Contrasting Persuasion and Sycophancy in Voice-Based Multi-Turn Conversations

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14124)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`persuasion`、`sycophancy`、`voice agent`、`RCT`

👤 **作者**：Ilia Frantzi、Bernie Hogan、Max M. Lang、Paul C. Bauer

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM 影响用户信念的能力在持续多轮语音对话中如何展开缺乏受控证据
- 🔬 **研究方法**：N=616 随机对照实验：非指导/双对立立场指导三条件多轮语音对话，分离论证语义与交互语用策略
- 📌 **结论**：指导条件不对称地改变观点；社交谄媚近乎普遍但不驱动观点变化而塑造主观体验——说服与谄媚的角色分离

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

As interactions with large language models (LLMs) become increasingly routine, concerns about their capacity to influence users' beliefs grow. Yet how such influence unfolds in sustained dialogue remains insufficiently understood. We conduct a randomized controlled experiment (N=616) where participants engage in multi-turn, spoken dialogue with an LLM under three conditions: non-directive, and two directive conditions maintaining opposing stances on a polarizing topic (the merits of feminism). We examine opinion change, rhetorical strategies, and sycophancy, distinguishing the semantic content of LLM argumentation from the pragmatic strategies used to manage the interaction. Both directive conditions shifted opinions toward their assigned stances, but asymmetrically: shifts were larger for pro-feminist positions, which also elicited distinct rhetorical strategies. The non-directive condition, likewise shifted opinions pro-feminism. Social sycophancy, though near-universal, was not associated with opinion change; instead it shaped users' subjective experience. Our findings highlight distinct roles for argumentation and sycophantic accommodation in AI influence.

</details>

### 192. Long-Horizon-Terminal-Bench 2.0: Automating Diverse Task Construction and Measuring Sustained Agent Progress

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14146)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`benchmark`、`terminal agent`、`long-horizon`、`process reward`

👤 **作者**：Zongxia Li、…、Leowei Liang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：长时程终端基准只做终态二值检查——持续进展与过程奖励未被测量
- 🔬 **研究方法**：LHTB 2.0：46 原任务修复+31 扩展，参数化参考实现生成多场景、密级进展阶梯防记忆化，3 小时窗口每 30 分钟记录 verifier 检查点
- 📌 **结论**：1,145 次运行：二值分 0.221 vs 密集阶梯分 0.653——仅看结果丢弃大量中间进展（终端 agent 持续进展度量）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Long-horizon terminal benchmarks measure whether an agent can sustain progress through extended workflows with process rewards, not only whether its final state passes a binary check. We present Long-Horizon-Terminal-Bench 2.0 (LHTB 2.0), an extension of Long-Horizon-Terminal-Bench that preserves its 46 original tasks while correcting task and evaluation-infrastructure defects and extending 31 tasks. LHTB 2.0 contains 77 tasks across 13 domains and eight task types. The extended tasks are produced through a systematic reconstruction-task pipeline. We start from a working scientific or engineering artifact, then use a parameterized reference implementation to generate multiple scenarios, reveal six as behavioral evidence, hold out three for evaluation, and use dense progress ladders to reward partial progress while preventing memorization of public outputs. We also extend the evaluation horizon from 90 minutes to three hours and record verifier checkpoints every 30 minutes, enabling direct measurement of sustained progress within a run. Across 1,145 scored model-task runs from 15 frontier models, GPT-6-astra leads with dense mean reward 0.69 and 37 tasks at R at least 0.95, followed by GPT-5.6-sol with dense mean reward 0.67 and 32 tasks at the same threshold. On the 30 reconstruction tasks, binary verifier scores average 0.221, whereas dense ladder scores average 0.653, showing that outcome-only evaluation discards substantial intermediate progress. LHTB 2.0 provides a broader, hardened, and process-aware testbed for studying reliable agent execution over hours.

</details>

### 193. Should SLMs be in the Driver's Seat? A Study of Local Models as Interpreters in Cloud-Written Agent Programs

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14209)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`privacy`、`agent architecture`、`local model`、`data locality`

👤 **作者**：Boris Radovič、Marco Canini、Veljko Pejović

- 🎯 **研究动机**：云端大模型 agent 读用户隐私 vs 端侧小模型效果差的权衡——云写程序端执行遇运行时内容解释即失败
- 🔬 **研究方法**：中间架构：云写程序将有界的数据内问题经普通函数调用延迟给端侧小模型判读
- 📌 **结论**：AppWorld 六种骨干×分区组合全部提升（最高 +14.9pp），数据留在设备上收回相当部分差距——隐私与能力的架构折中

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

The most accurate agents executing user-provided tasks are driven by large, cloud-hosted models, which solve them by reading the private data of the user. Agents powered by models small enough to run on edge devices keep that data local, but yield far worse results. A third option is to have cloud models write a program that devices execute: the data stays local, but as the program is written before any data is seen, it fails whenever a step depends on interpreting content that appears only at run time. We explore an intermediate architecture, in which the cloud-written program defers bounded questions over user data to a small local model through an ordinary function call. On AppWorld this improves the success rate of a code-only agent in all six combinations of backbone model and test partition we evaluate, by up to 14.9pp, recovering a substantial part of the gap to a cloud-hosted ReAct loop while keeping user data on the device.

</details>

### 194. Harness^2: Recursive Agent Harnessing for an Open World

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14224)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`recursive self-improvement`、`harness`、`open-world agent`、`self-evolution`

👤 **作者**：Ruiyao Xu、…、Tomas Pfister

- 🎯 **研究动机**：RSI 自动演化 harness 在开放世界缺真值 verifier 且任务特定不泛化
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Harness^2：任务级递归（对比 Propose-Probe-Compose 无真值引导编辑）+harnessing 级递归（经验提取为持续改进 harness）
- 📌 **结论**：单步即提升 GLM-5/Gemini 3.5 Flash（WorkBuddy +11.5 等）——自改进 harness 的双层递归架构（自演化风险的可行性参照）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

LLM agents' capabilities depend not only on their underlying models but also on their execution harnesses. Recursive self-improvement (RSI) offers a promising approach to automatically evolve these harnesses. However, deploying RSI in open-world settings still faces two critical challenges. First, open-world tasks are typically unseen during the harness design phase and novel at test time, leaving no ground-truth verifiers to reliably guide online harness edits. Second, the RSI process remains highly task-specific and fails to generalize to novel scenarios. We introduce Harness^2, a plug-and-play, recursive agent harnessing framework that addresses these challenges through two connected levels of recursion. Task-level recursion refines the current task's harness using a contrastive Propose-Probe-Compose step that reliably guides edits without ground-truth verifiers, and can be scaled further via parallel or sequential recursion. Harnessing-level recursion then extracts experience from these edits to update a continual improvement harness, enabling the agent to better adapt to future tasks. Across three professional benchmarks and four model-harness configurations, a single step consistently improves GLM-5 and Gemini 3.5 Flash over their base harnesses, yielding gains of up to 11.5 points on WorkBuddy-Bench, 4.5 points on JobBench, and 11.8 percentage points on LAB, with further gains as the task-level recursion budget increases.

</details>

### 195. Diagnosing Generalization Boundaries in AI-Based Vulnerability Detection via Calibrated Embedding Distances

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14296)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`vulnerability detection`、`distribution shift`、`OOD`、`diagnostics`

👤 **作者**：Konstantin Ievtushenko、Ralf Lämmel、Jan Jürjens

- 🎯 **研究动机**：漏洞检测的高分掩盖分布偏移下的严重退化——需要不依赖辅助模型的退化诊断信号
- 🔬 **研究方法**：校准嵌入几何：白化中间层嵌入+局部最近邻打分，严格配对的易损/补丁代码上测模式/项目/时间偏移
- 📌 **结论**：OOD AUROC 0.739、跨项目准确率掉 35pp+、补丁误报与几何邻近系统相关——表示几何直接映射模型退化边界

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

High benchmark scores in learning-based vulnerability detection mask severe degradation under distribution shifts. This paper demonstrates that calibrated embedding geometry exposes these hidden generalization boundaries on syntactically similar vulnerable and patched code pairs. An evaluation of CodeBERT and Qwen2.5-Coder across pattern, project, and temporal shifts on a strictly paired dataset reveals three main findings. First, whitened intermediate-layer embeddings with local nearest-neighbour scoring separate unfamiliar samples with a mean out-of-distribution AUROC of 0.739 (RQ1). Second, distance scores reveal a shift-specific reliability gradient where accuracy drops by over 35 percentage points and cross-project shifts cause the sharpest degradation, even though temporal shifts drift geometrically farthest (RQ2). Third, false positives on patched code systematically correlate with geometric proximity to vulnerable training neighbourhoods, increasing from 9.4% in the lowest-risk decile to 58.8% in the highest (RQ3). These calibrated distances establish a diagnostic signal that maps representation geometry directly to model degradation without requiring auxiliary models.

</details>

### 196. Actionable Meta-Requirements for AI Robustness Auditing

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14356)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`robustness auditing`、`meta-requirements`、`governance`、`SLR`

👤 **作者**：Joshua Owotogbe、Roya Nasiri、Indika Kumara、Damian Andrew Tamburri、Willem-Jan van den Heuvel

- 🎯 **研究动机**：AI 鲁棒性审计框架应支持什么要求从未被系统整合——审计结论的证据充分性无从判断
- 🔬 **研究方法**：设计科学路线：SLR 保留 74 篇提炼 43 项元要求（16 类），128 位从业者 5,504 评分验证精炼
- 📌 **结论**：44 项元要求获 81.2% 同意，从业者补充外部组件与供应链依赖要求——审计框架设计的经验校准基线

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

AI-enabled systems must remain dependable when the conditions under which they operate change (e.g., due to input perturbations, distribution shifts, or changes in external dependencies). This makes robustness auditing of such systems important for judging whether the available evidence is sufficient to support a defined robustness conclusion. However, designing an AI robustness auditing framework is challenging. A key unresolved issue is determining the requirements such a framework should support; these requirements have not yet been consolidated. We address this gap through meta-requirements: higher-order requirements specifying what a class of auditing frameworks should support.
