In design science research, literature and domain experts are common sources of meta-requirements; accordingly, we derive the initial set from literature and validate and refine it with practitioners. Our systematic literature review retained 74 of 2,890 records and derived 43 meta-requirements across 16 categories. We evaluated them with 128 practitioners, obtaining 5,504 ratings and 586 substantive comments. Overall, 81.20% of ratings indicated agreement or strong agreement. In particular, 41 of the 43 literature-derived meta-requirements were classified as strongly supported. Practitioner feedback refined the requirements and identified an additional requirement concerning external components and supply chain dependencies. The final set comprises 44 meta-requirements across 16 categories, of which 43 received quantitative validation. These meta-requirements can help organizations implement AI robustness auditing frameworks, assess existing frameworks for completeness, and refine them.

</details>

### 197. Tokenization-Invariant Watermarking: A Byte-Local Method for LLM Text

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14378)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`watermark`、`byte-local`、`tokenization-invariant`、`robustness`

👤 **作者**：Tung-Ling Li

- 🎯 **研究动机**：token 级水印对分词器/嵌入管线敏感——词级编辑即可破坏检测
- 🔬 **研究方法**：TI-WM：生成时嵌入但以规范化 UTF-8 字节为键与打分，检测器不调用分词器，局部编辑只影响有界打分窗口
- 📌 **结论**：5% 词编辑预算下攻击后 AUC 高于 Unigram 基线，但可见插入仍可击败默认设计——字节局部水印的构造型结果而非部署保证

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

We introduce Tokenization-Invariant Watermarking (TI-WM), a method that embeds a watermark during token generation but keys and scores it on canonicalized UTF-8 bytes. The detector does not invoke the tokenizer. Canonicalizer-preserving edits leave its score unchanged; other localized canonical-byte edits affect a bounded number of scoring windows. Across three fixed keys and 128 prompts per key and model, a deterministic white-box attack at a 5% word-edit budget leaves mean attacked-AUC 0.530 on Qwen3-8B and 0.664 on Gemma 4 E4B, compared with 0.410 and 0.489 for Unigram with detection-side deletion. These operating points are not jointly matched on quality and clean detectability. Bias sweeps show model-dependent distortion costs, and shared-rule controls support a locality interpretation without establishing a unique advantage of bytes. Visible insertion outside the canonicalizer's deletion set defeats the default design. Alphabetic filtering closes that channel but does not resolve source shift or calibrated detection power. TI-WM thus provides a concrete construction with local scoring guarantees and measured attack-ranking gains, not a general robustness or low-false-positive-rate deployment guarantee.

</details>

### 198. ByteMark: A Unified Byte-Level Watermark for Detection Across Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14427)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`watermark`、`byte-level`、`cross-model detection`、`provenance`

👤 **作者**：Xiaopu Wang、Runze Li

- 🎯 **研究动机**：文本来源未知时，不同提供方的分词器/嵌入差异使各自水印互不兼容且无法从文本判断
- 🔬 **研究方法**：ByteMark：信号定义在发布文本的 UTF-8 字节上，不同分词器模型按同一公共规范与密钥嵌入，单一检测器凭文本+密钥验证
- 📌 **结论**：基线检测器换分词器即退化而 ByteMark 保持检出率，参与模型续写/改写仍可检——跨模型统一水印规范

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

As watermarking of LLM-generated text enters deployment, users increasingly need to check text whose source is unknown. Existing detectors read the watermark through a specific tokenizer or embedding pipeline, and even if all providers adopted the same watermarking method, differences in these representations would leave their watermarks incompatible, and the text alone does not reveal which representation produced it. We propose ByteMark, a unified watermarking scheme that defines its signal on the UTF-8 bytes of published text. Models with different tokenizers embed this signal under one public specification and key, and a single detector verifies the text from the text and key alone. When the tokenizer or embedding model of a baseline detector is replaced, its detection degrades sharply, whereas ByteMark, which uses neither, retains its detection rate; ByteMark also remains detectable when participating models continue or rewrite the text.

</details>

### 199. Re-executing Coding-Agent Evaluations: Unchanged Decisions and Unresolved Policy Risk

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14460)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`evaluation audit`、`coding agent`、`preregistered`、`grader reliability`

👤 **作者**：Tianyu Ding、Yaocheng Tong、Muzhi Yang、Fei Jiang、Lei Yang、Boya Yu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：可执行测试给 coding agent 补丁打分驱动排行榜/奖励/自改进——评分配置变更后判决是否稳定未被预注册检验
- 🔬 **研究方法**：两项预注册重执行研究：SWE-bench Pro 59 个已接受 Go 补丁+DGM 档案 1,929 次评测重跑，附探索性宿主/镜像/评分器对照
- 📌 **结论**：主判决与排序保持稳定但 3% 暴露补丁风险未解决、镜像重建改变 0.83% 多数判决——评分器审计的负面结果与边界透明报告

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Executable tests grade large language model (LLM) coding-agent patches for leaderboards, rewards and self-improvement. Two preregistered re-execution studies returned negative results. SWE-bench Pro v1's offline option rejected none of 59 accepted Go patches whose grading configuration changed. Their exploratory, unweighted upper confidence limit is 4.95% under candidate independence, leaving a 3% exposed-patch risk unresolved; the preregistered estimator uses inverse sampling-probability weights. An extension was inconclusive. In a selected stratum, a reconstructed module cache built from the reference solution's dependencies rejected accepted alternatives from four models that passed with their own dependencies. Re-executing 1,929 selected agent-version/task evaluations from the Darwin Gödel Machine's archive preserved best-agent selection and system-variant ranking, with search history and scores of evaluations not rerun fixed. Exploratory host, image and adapted-grader comparisons also preserved them. Seven of 23 disagreements with archived verdicts did not recur on a second host; rebuilt images changed 16 three-run majority verdicts (0.83%) under four-CPU execution. We provide an evidence-to-claim summary, lessons for grader audits and an anonymous replication package. These results concern accepted patches and fixed-history decisions; general policy safety and adaptive-learning robustness require broader evaluations.

</details>

### 200. Evaluating Bias in Chinese Large Language Models across Tasks and Registers: Task Operationalization Effects and Irony Classification Risks

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14464)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`bias audit`、`Chinese LLM`、`irony register`、`content governance`

👤 **作者**：Mo Zhou

- 🎯 **研究动机**：中文内容治理用 LLM 识别毒性/歧视表达——任务操作化与反讽语体（阴阳怪气）会改变偏差读数
- 🔬 **研究方法**：预注册多任务行为审计：正式 vs 阴阳语体的分类错误率对比+29 个命题锚点三任务形式操作化与操纵检查
- 📌 **结论**：核心面板错误率正式 10.9%→阴阳 23.5%（+12.7pp），反讽感知提示降 9.6pp——审核模型在反讽语体下的失效与任务形式敏感度

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

In Chinese content governance, large language models are increasingly used to identify toxic and discriminatory expressions, yet both task operationalization and ironic expression can alter bias measurement readings. We conducted a preregistered multi-task behavioral audit of multiple Chinese large language models. In the content classification experiment, the core panel's classification error rate rose from 10.9% in the formal register to 23.5% in the yinyang (an echoic-irony register) version, a risk difference of +12.7 percentage points. (95% CI [+9.7, +15.7]). Inter-rater agreement was lower in the yinyang register (majority-vote kappa = 0.299), so the increase contains a component of label uncertainty propagation, but the direction of increase was consistent under both adjudicated and majority-vote reference standards. Explicit irony-aware prompts reduced the intervention panel's error rate by 9.6 percentage points. We operationalized the 29 shared proposition anchors into three task forms: social consensus estimation (Form A), high-stakes decision (Form B), and controlled association rating (Form C); independent manipulation checks found that all 58 A-C comparison units received unanimous agreement, whereas the low A-B convergence was confirmed by calibrated majority adjudication (87.8% supporting the minority judgment); on the irony-probe materials, the interactions of B and C relative to A were +0.577 and +1.121, respectively. Thus, when evaluating large language models for Chinese content governance, the effect of task operationalization on bias readings is an empirical question that requires data to answer, and safety evaluation should incorporate the ironic register as a test condition.

</details>

### 201. SEPM: Self-Evolving Procedural Memory Grounded in Task Graphs for Multi-Agent Collaboration

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14515)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`self-evolving memory`、`procedural memory`、`multi-agent`、`task graph`

👤 **作者**：Yi Liu、Junwei Zhao、Li Sun、Jiansong Zhang、Sen Su

- 🎯 **研究动机**：长时程多 agent 协作的记忆需同时保留成功动作与协作结构——过程记忆演化与任务依赖和执行状态脱节
- 🔬 **研究方法**：SEPM：任务依赖图为共享表示，执行期黑板记录结构化证据支持目标/计划/世界态分歧检测与证据对齐，事后蒸馏可复用过程并按效用与安全约束演化
- 📌 **结论**：ALFWorld/WebArena/OfficeBench 六设置最高分——自演化过程记忆的安全约束与退役机制（agent 记忆自演化的能力参照）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Long-horizon multi-agent collaboration requires memory that preserves both successful actions and the coordination structure underlying them. Existing memory systems support information sharing or procedural reuse, but often leave the evolution of shared procedures disconnected from the task dependencies and execution states of collaborating agents. This coupling also limits the direct transfer of procedural memory designed for individual agents to team settings. We propose Self-Evolving Procedural Memory (SEPM), a framework that uses task dependency graphs as a common representation for online coordination and procedural memory evolution. During execution, a lightweight shared blackboard records structured evidence alongside task dependencies, supporting the detection of goal, plan, and world-state divergence and evidence-based realignment. After execution, verified actions and dependencies are distilled into reusable procedures, while recurring failures inform warnings and constraints. The shared procedure pool evolves through creation, refinement, specialization, and retirement, guided by success and cost utility and constrained by evidence and safety requirements. Together, these mechanisms preserve the collaborative structure and applicability conditions needed to reuse experience across tasks. Evaluations on ALFWorld, WebArena, and OfficeBench under AutoGen and DyLAN show that SEPM achieves the highest task scores among the compared methods in all six benchmark settings. Under AutoGen, it improves task scores over the no-added-memory baseline by 29.85, 25.86, and 13.00 percentage points, respectively. The codebase is available at this https URL.

</details>

### 202. Who to Trust in Multi-Robot Systems: Joint Robot and Object Hypothesis Trust Inference via Neural-Symbolic Cross-Validation

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14530)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`multi-robot trust`、`adversarial interference`、`neural-symbolic`、`Bayesian filtering`

👤 **作者**：Ruiyang Wang、Hao-Lun Hsu、Haocheng Meng、Songqiao Xie、Miroslav Pajic

- 🎯 **研究动机**：多机器人可靠协作依赖对信息源及其报告假设的信任推断——部分可观测与潜在对抗干扰下既有方法依赖预置信任或侧信道
- 🔬 **研究方法**：NeST-Bayes：仅凭机器人间观测一致性构建自我中心关系图，神经符号推理出瞬时信任证据并 Beta 分布贝叶斯累积
- 📌 **结论**：激进优化攻击下仍达 98.8% 机器人精度/93.8% 召回——无预置信任的多机器人对抗信任推断

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Multi-robot systems (MRS) offer significant advantages over single-robot platforms by enabling information sharing and coordination for different tasks in complex environments. However, reliable cooperation critically depends on inferring trust over both information sources and their reported hypotheses, particularly under partial observability and potential adversarial interference. Existing approaches frequently depend on pre-established trust relationships or side-channel information, which may not be available or reliable in realistic MRS deployments. This work introduces NeST-Bayes, a neural-symbolic framework that jointly infers robot and hypothesis trust using only inter-robot observation consistency, without requiring preassigned robot trust, reputation information, or side-channel identity signals. NeST-Bayes infers instantaneous trust evidence scores for robots and object hypotheses by reasoning over ego-centric relational graphs constructed from cross-robot observation consistency. These evidence scores are accumulated over time through Bayesian updates of Beta-distributed trust beliefs to maintain temporally consistent trust estimates. Extensive evaluations show that NeST-Bayes achieves the highest mean object precision and robot recall across all benchmark settings, and the highest robot precision and object recall in most settings, compared to trust-based methods. Under aggressive optimized attacks, it achieves 98.8\% robot precision with 93.8\% recall and 98.3\% object precision with 95.4\% recall. In an occlusion-rich Webots warehouse, it maintains 92.5\% robot precision with 92.0\% recall and 97.3\% object precision with 85.0\% recall. These results demonstrate joint improvements in the reliability and coverage of retained information.

</details>

### 203. Are Multimodal Benchmarks Really Useful? Item-Level Multimodal Benchmark Diagnosis via Structure-Response Co-Calibration

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14542)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`benchmark validity`、`multimodal`、`modality dependence`、`diagnosis`

👤 **作者**：Shiqi Zhang、Weixin Zeng、Ziheng Zhang、Jiuyang Tang、Xiang Zhao

- 🎯 **研究动机**：多模态基准含单模态可答或选项规律可答的捷径条目——既有诊断只看条目结构或模型响应行为单一视角
- 🔬 **研究方法**：SRCoD：从基准内容与目标答案估计样本级多模态信息结构作软锚，模态条件化项目反应建模产出条目级模态依赖画像
- 📌 **结论**：两种验证协议下优于 SOTA 诊断方法——条目级模态依赖校准（多模态评测有效性的细粒度工具）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Multimodal benchmarks are central to evaluating whether models can reason over information from multiple modalities. However, existing literature has revealed that some benchmarks may contain shortcut items that can be answered from a single modality or from answer-option regularities alone, thus failing to reflect models' true cross-modal inference ability. Existing methods for assessing benchmarks typically rely either on intrinsic item structure or extrinsic model response behavior, which are not adequate for a comprehensive diagnosis. To fill in this gap, we propose SRCoD, a structure-response co-calibrated diagnosis framework for item-level diagnosis of modality dependence in multimodal benchmarks. SRCoD estimates sample-wise multimodal information structure from benchmark content and target answers, and uses this signal as a soft structural anchor for modality-conditioned item response modeling. The learned model produces a calibrated item-level diagnostic profile that reflects how strongly an item depends on different forms of modality evidence, enabling fine-grained benchmark analysis beyond aggregate accuracy. SRCoD also provides interpretable attribution of the diagnosis results. Extensive experiments under two complementary protocols, i.e., benchmark-refinement oriented validation and human-labeled item validation, demonstrate that SRCoD outperforms state-of-the-art benchmark diagnosis methods.

</details>

### 204. Flow-Support-Weighted Proposals for Test-Time Verified VLA Policies

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14588)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`VLA`、`test-time verification`、`proposal contraction`、`sampling`

👤 **作者**：Chen Zhao、Zipeng Ye、Haitao Shen、Guanlin Li、Yuchen Xie、Jing Zhang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：测试时采样本应给 verifier 多样候选——微调使 flow-matching 解码器收缩到主导示范动作，重复采样高度冗余（proposal contraction）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：ADS 训练期动作动态采样：每步从当前策略积分 K 条新轨迹按流支撑 Hutchinson 迹估计加权，有限集 KL 项校正局部动作分布
- 📌 **结论**：RoboCasa oracle best-of-8 57.6%→63.0%，真机 TokenPB +13 点——测试时验证 VLA 的候选多样性约束与训练侧解法

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Test-time sampling promises to turn one action prediction into a set of distinct executable candidates that a verifier can choose among. Downstream fine-tuning works against that promise. A vision-language-action model trained to solve one task distribution concentrates its flow-matching decoder around the dominant demonstrated action, so repeated samples differ only marginally and the verifier receives many copies of one decision. We call this proposal contraction and identify it as a key constraint on test-time scaling: repeated samples can remain highly redundant as the candidate budget grows. Action Dynamic Sampling (ADS) alleviates the constraint on the proposal side, during training. At each training step, ADS integrates $K$ additional trajectories from the current policy under fresh base noise, scores each by the flow support along its path with a Hutchinson trace estimator, and corrects the policy's local action distribution toward this weighted set through a detached finite-set KL term. Training cost rises; deployment cost is unchanged. Across simulation and four physical-robot tasks, ADS raises proposal spread without the success penalty that Gaussian perturbation incurs, lifts oracle best-of-8 success on RoboCasa from 57.6% to 63.0%, improves fixed verifiers by 2.4 to 4.9 points in simulation, and raises TokenPB by up to 13.0 points on real-world manipulation.

</details>

### 205. From Existing Methods to New Research Ideas: Two Perspectives on Recursive Self-Improvement

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14625)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`recursive self-improvement`、`taxonomy`、`survey`、`self-modification`

👤 **作者**：Leijun Zhou、…、Junhao Hu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：RSI 使语言 agent 保留对参数/记忆/技能/代码的修改——设计空间缺乏统一刻画
- 🔬 **研究方法**：九维四组统一分类（更新目标/组织/调度与范围/反馈与选择）+与深度学习传统机制（优化/正则/数据增强/持续学习/对抗学习）的映射与基准综述
- 📌 **结论**：既有 RSI 方法的共享设计空间与可借鉴学习机制——自改进能力与风险的地图（对应失控行为评估基础）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Recursive self-improvement (RSI) offers a route to AI systems that participate in their own continued development. Recent methods allow language agents to retain changes to their parameters, memory, skills, and code for subsequent execution and improvement. We examine how these methods can inform new research ideas through two complementary perspectives. First, we introduce a unified taxonomy with nine design dimensions organized into four groups: update targets, update organization, update schedule and scope, feedback and selection. This taxonomy places existing methods in a shared design space, where examining individual dimensions and their interactions can inspire new research ideas. Second, we examine how existing RSI methods draw on traditional deep learning, for example through optimization, regularization, data augmentation, continual learning, and adversarial learning. We develop further ideas by examining more specific learning mechanisms and drawing on evolutionary computation. We also review how existing benchmarks assess self-improvement under online, offline, and offline-to-online settings. Together, these perspectives help researchers develop new RSI research ideas from existing designs and learning mechanisms. RSI papers and resources are available at this https URL.

</details>

### 206. PVFH-Bench: Benchmarking Premise-Induced Visual Fixation Hallucinations in Vision-Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14635)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`hallucination`、`false premise`、`visual fixation`、`benchmark`

👤 **作者**：Xinhao Wang、…、Ge Yu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：认知启发的前提诱导视觉固着幻觉（PVFH）：模型锚定易混淆视觉证据、接受虚假前提后描述不在场但视觉合理的物体
- 🔬 **研究方法**：PVFH-Bench：自动构造视觉易混淆但缺席的目标+诊断子集追踪从识别到固着的路径，10 个 LVLM 评测
- 📌 **结论**：全部模型高度易感且显式 CoT 进一步加剧（把弱视觉线索过度解读为支持证据）——前提诱导的多模态幻觉新失效模式

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have achieved remarkable progress across a wide range of multimodal tasks. However, object hallucination remains a persistent challenge, undermining model reliability in applications such as visual question answering, embodied agents, and human-AI decision support systems. In this paper, inspired by cognitive science studies on perceptual bias, we investigate a new failure mode termed Premise-Induced Visual Fixation Hallucination (PVFH). Specifically, PVFH refers to a premise-induced reasoning error in which models become anchored to visually confusable evidence, accept a false textual premise, and subsequently generate responses about an absent yet visually plausible object. To systematically study this phenomenon, we introduce PVFH-Bench, a benchmark that automatically constructs visually confusable but absent targets and pairs the core hallucination setting with diagnostic subsets to trace how models move from object recognition to Premise-Induced Visual Fixation Hallucination. Experimental results on 10 state-of-the-art LVLMs demonstrate that all evaluated models are highly vulnerable to Premise-Induced Visual Fixation Hallucination. Moreover, we find that explicit chain-of-thought reasoning can further exacerbate this failure mode by encouraging models to overinterpret weak visual cues as supporting evidence for hallucinated objects.

</details>

### 207. VitaBench: Memory Lifecycles Beyond Retrieval

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14731)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`agent memory`、`lifecycle`、`benchmark`、`counterfactual evaluation`

👤 **作者**：Muquan Yu、Dihan Hu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：长期记忆基准只评『能否检索使用』——未来用途未知前的保留/压缩/合并/删除表示决策未被评测
- 🔬 **研究方法**：VitaBench：重复记忆审查+全时域反事实评测；诊断层隔离未来使用模式+自然层组合成客服式交互流；三个诊断量（生命周期余量/时域反转/最优动作多样性）
- 📌 **结论**：生命周期结构显著：56-60% 状态的偏好动作随短/全时域改变——记忆生命周期决策成为可测对象

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Long-term memory benchmarks for language agents primarily evaluate whether information from earlier interactions can be retrieved and used when relevant. Persistent memory systems face an additional problem before future use is known: deciding whether a memory should remain exact, be compressed, consolidated, or deleted. Existing benchmarks capture long-range retrieval, temporal reasoning, evidence reuse, and incremental interaction, but do not directly evaluate these earlier representation decisions. We formalize memory lifecycle evaluation and introduce VitaBench, a benchmark that makes lifecycle decisions measurable through repeated memory reviews and full-horizon counterfactual evaluation. VitaBench contains a controlled Diagnostic tier that isolates future-use patterns and a Naturalistic tier that composes them into generated customer-service-style interaction streams. We characterize lifecycle decision structure with three diagnostics: lifecycle headroom, measuring the advantage of adaptive choices over the best fixed strategy; horizon reversal, measuring when short-horizon decisions change under delayed consequences; and optimal-action diversity, measuring whether different memory states favor different lifecycle operations. Across both tiers, VitaBench exhibits substantial lifecycle structure: lifecycle headroom is 1.364 in Diagnostic and 1.405 in Naturalistic, and 60.2% and 56.5% of states, respectively, change their preferred action between short and full horizons. A learned delayed lifecycle policy further improves over the best fixed strategy on VitaBench-Naturalistic, reaching 101.5 versus 83.4 joint utility. These results show that retrieving past information and deciding how it should persist are distinct evaluation problems, and that lifecycle management requires evaluation of the delayed consequences of representation decisions.

</details>

### 208. Pre-Registered Genesis Accounting for Recursive Self-Improvement: First Audited Operator-Genesis Events in a Physics-Discovery Runtime

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14843)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`recursive self-improvement`、`audit`、`genesis events`、`preregistered`

👤 **作者**：Wenbin Mao、Pei Zou

- 🎯 **研究动机**：RSI 声明易做难证——改进来自复用设计者机制还是系统自 authored 机制无从分辨
- 🔬 **研究方法**：预注册双层记账：M1 机器生成内容实例、M2 机器 authored 机制类型（新算子注册+held-out 验证证书）；物理发现运行时首报审计过的 M2 事件
- 📌 **结论**：从 NIST 氢光谱数据自生成 Rydberg 形式算子（held-out R²=1.0 vs 标准库 -0.546）、独立恢复开普勒速度律；审计捕获并修复协议缺陷——RSI 自我改进声明的可证伪记账

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Claims of recursive self-improvement are easy to make and hard to falsify: did improvement come from re-using designer-provided mechanisms, or from mechanisms the system authored itself? We introduce a pre-registered, two-tier accounting: M1 counts machine-generated content instances within a designer-provided vocabulary, while M2 counts machine-authored mechanism types (new operators registered into the runtime's own vocabulary, with a held-out-validation certificate). We report the first audited M2 genesis events. In the primary event, depth-limited compositional search over elementary operators, judged by held-out R^2 on real NIST hydrogen Balmer measurements, produced the operator sq(inv(n)) = 1/n^2 -- the Rydberg-level form -- which no standard-library transform could express (held-out R^2: -0.546 best standard transform vs. 1.0000000 composed). A protocol flaw (independent variable built by inverting the very law under test) was caught in audit and repaired via an independent-label protocol; extrapolation to unseen levels (n=8,9 trained on n<=7) attains relative error ~10^-6, four to six orders beyond the best standard operator. A second event independently recovered sqrt(inv(a)) -- Kepler's velocity law -- from JPL ephemeris vectors. Also incorporated: an audited compounding channel (first domain-level measurement: 1.109x accuracy ratio on a real held-out domain) and blind, decurated genesis exams whose honest tally is zero. The Kepler event passes line audit; the hydrogen gap reproduces on He II; the certificate is threshold-stable. Against eleven prior systems, the contribution is the measurement and governance of machine-authored mechanisms, not the search algorithm or rediscovered physics. Limitations: exams were curated by agents who knew the textbook answer, triggers manual, grammar observer-injected; both events demonstrate capability to author, not yet autonomous genesis.

</details>

### 209. Social Bias in Chinese Large Language Models under Social Media Expressions

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14879)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`social bias`、`Chinese LLM`、`sarcasm`、`measurement validity`

👤 **作者**：Mo Zhou

- 🎯 **研究动机**：中文社会偏差基准依赖规范化/模板表达——网络俚语、表情与反讽的影响未被检验
- 🔬 **研究方法**：73 组配对探针（性别/地域/职业：中性表达+社媒风格表达+身份反转对照），任务重构为社会共识估计，区分风格包装与反讽包装的配对差异
- 📌 **结论**：全部模型显著身份偏差、五个模型风格包装显著为负、反讽位移跨模型梯度——表达形式不是透明载体，低分不等于偏差缓解

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Existing Chinese social-bias benchmarks for large language models largely rely on normalized or template-based expressions, leaving the influence of internet slang, emoji, and sarcasm insufficiently examined. Safety alignment may further compress conventional plausibility ratings toward the lower end of the scale. This study constructs 73 paired probes across gender, region, and occupation. Each probe contains a neutral expression, a social-media-style expression, and a reversed-identity control. The task is reframed from direct plausibility judgment to social consensus estimation. Social-media expressions are divided into stylistic packaging, which preserves semantic polarity, and sarcastic packaging, which reverses surface polarity. Six Chinese-capable large language models are evaluated through paired differences measuring identity bias, stylistic packaging, and sarcasm shift. All models show significant identity-bias effects; five show significantly negative stylistic-packaging effects; and sarcasm shifts display a clear cross-model gradient. These findings demonstrate that expression form is not a transparent carrier in bias evaluation and that lower scores under sarcasm should not be interpreted directly as bias mitigation. The proposed paired design offers a measurement approach for Chinese social-media contexts while also revealing the construct boundary and prompt sensitivity of social consensus estimation.

</details>

### 210. SkillForgeBench: Evaluating Agent Reliability Grounded in Agent Skill Specifications

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14918)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`agent reliability`、`skill specification`、`benchmark`、`requirement satisfaction`

👤 **作者**：Zhengxi Li、…、Yufei Wang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agent 能否严格满足多样且过程复杂的要求决定实用可靠性——专家基准深而不广、众包基准广而不严
- 🔬 **研究方法**：SkillForgeBench：以 agent skill 为统一执行蓝图的三阶段协同设计（资源准备/技能驱动任务合成/整体评测设计），842 个沙箱任务五专业域
- 📌 **结论**：严格完成率呈陡峭能力阶梯（Opus 4.8 领跑 40.9%），全约束满足暴露被单项通过率掩盖的瓶颈——技能规范锚定的可靠性评测

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Evaluating an agent's ability to strictly satisfy diverse and procedurally complex requirements is essential to determining its practical reliability. Conducting such an evaluation demands agentic tasks that reconcile broad domain coverage with deep procedural complexity. Existing paradigms face a fundamental trade-off: expert-curated benchmarks achieve procedural depth but suffer from domain diversity bottlenecks, whereas crowdsourced benchmarks span diverse scenarios but lack execution rigor. To resolve this tension, we present SkillForgeBench, which leverages agent skills as unifying execution blueprints across three co-designed stages: (1) Resource Preparation uses domain-balanced skills to guide artifact retrieval, establishing broad workflow coverage; (2) Skill-driven Task Synthesis employs skills as procedural anchors to partition artifact roles, synthesize naturalistic task instructions, and package sandboxed scenarios for deep procedural execution; and (3) Holistic Evaluation Design translates skill-embedded requirements into fine-grained completion and process rubrics to enable strict requirement-level scoring. Comprising 842 sandboxed tasks across five major professional domains, SkillForgeBench reveals a steep capability hierarchy among eight mainstream agents, led by Opus 4.8 ($40.9\%$ strict completion). Requiring all constrains satisfaction uncovers severe capability bottlenecks across all model tiers that remain masked under individual rubric pass rates ($70.0\%$ to $85.7\%$ vs. $12.6\%$ to $40.9\%$). Crucially, under the external guidance from skills, top-tier agents obtain larger gains than low-tier agents (i.e., $\Delta 19.2\%$ for Opus 4.8 vs. $\Delta 5.8\%$ for Haiku 4.5). Finally, agents satisfy process rubrics far less frequently than completion rubrics (under $3\%$ for Haiku 4.5), proving that completion-only checks overestimate real-world agent reliability.

</details>

### 211. What Drives AI Citations? Content, Retrieval and Model Knowledge in Generative Engine Optimization (GEO)

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15033)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`GEO`、`AI citations`、`generative search`、`platform manipulation`

👤 **作者**：Mahe Chen、Nick Koudas

- 🎯 **研究动机**：生成式搜索引擎决定用户看到的来源——什么驱动被引用（GEO 产业的基础）缺乏机制性证据
- 🔬 **研究方法**：两市场 90 天 158,099 回答 468,037 引用+API 重放：同站页面内比较内容因素，分解引擎自有检索与模型知识的作用
- 📌 **结论**：引擎自己的搜索与改写子查询解释 32% 变异（页面属性仅 2.8%），品牌可被无页面凭记忆点名并重排同质产品——生成引擎引用生态的操纵面

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Generative search engines such as ChatGPT, Gemini and Perplexity now decide which Web pages users see as sources, and a growing industry of generative engine optimization advises publishers on how to earn these citations. We study what shapes citation in two 90-day collections of answers to commercial questions in contrasting markets: 158,099 answers with 468,037 citations. Comparing pages of the same website, content matters in one way: pages whose title and paragraphs match the questions people ask are cited more, whereas FAQ blocks, structured data, summary boxes and page speed appear effective only across websites. Page properties nevertheless explain little of the variation between pages of a website. Replaying the questions through the engines' APIs shows why. Every page ChatGPT cites is one its own search retrieved, and which pages that search retrieves, for sub-queries the model rewrites on every answer and that rarely coincide with Google's or Bing's results, explains far more of the variation than page properties (32% against 2.8%). Content acts mainly through this step, by matching the engine's queries in meaning. The model's own knowledge shapes it too: ChatGPT writes brands it knows into its searches, prefers websites it knows, and names brands without any supporting page, and controlled experiments show that brand names reorder its rankings of products with identical facts. Pages are thus cited when searched; brands are also named when remembered. The findings replicate in a market of small B2B vendors. We release data and code.

</details>

### 212. Trace Evolution Bench: Measuring Whether Agents Improve from Their Own Environment Traces

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15055)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`self-evolving agent`、`measurement validity`、`frozen rescore`、`benchmark`

👤 **作者**：Fangfei Li、Chenyang Zhao、Long Wang、Feng Tian、Lv Guo

- 🎯 **研究动机**：自演化 agent 用端点增益（更新后减更新前成功）打分——冻结重打分即可冒充演化
- 🔬 **研究方法**：TEB 协议：分离重打分与上下文/权重更新，更新仅在验证不降、held-out 标识不入更新时接受；三模型×九种文本更新单元的种子带对照
- 📌 **结论**：DeepSeek 冻结重打分即可移动 5-12.5pp；九个 GEPA/ACE/ExpeL 单元七个超出自身种子带但无一在全部种子保持——自演化测量有效性的严格协议

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Self-evolving agents are usually scored by endpoint Gain: held-out success after an update minus success before. That difference can credit a frozen rescore as evolution. We introduce Trace Evolution Bench (TEB), a protocol that separates that rescore from a context or weight update, together with three frozen 80/20/40 packs. An update is accepted only when validation does not fall, held-out identifiers never enter the update, and a text update is not ranked with a weight update. The primary scoreboard is coding, graded by hidden unit tests in a closed workspace. On that pack (40 held-out tasks, one family), DeepSeek v4 Pro under no_evolve never changes its policy yet moves 7.5, 5.0, and 12.5 percentage points across three seeds. Of nine text-update cells inspired by GEPA, ACE, and ExpeL, seven have positive excess above that seed's own band, and one gepa_llm seed moves down by the width of its band (-12.5, regression 0.48). GLM and MiniMax do not reproduce that seven-of-nine pattern. No method stays above its own band on every seed of all three models: GLM has two paired cells above their own bands, and MiniMax expel_llm seed 44 is a third (regression 0.30). The methods were fixed on a validation screen before held-out was read. Office, graded by rule-bounded delivery, and manufacturing table QA are separate packs: office DeepSeek does not exceed its own bands (widest 17.5 points), and manufacturing has no paired positive excess beyond its seed band.

</details>

### 213. GAMW: Generation-Aware Mel-Domain Watermarking for TTS Traceability

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15082)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`watermark`、`TTS`、`traceability`、`generation-aware`

👤 **作者**：Yihang Zhang、Weicai Yan、Qifei Zhang、Yangyang Wu、Tao Jin

- 🎯 **研究动机**：事后波形水印在复合扰动下脆弱——合成语音的主动溯源需要在生成过程内注入
- 🔬 **研究方法**：GAMW：波形合成前把内容自适应多比特水印残差嵌入生成表示，解码目标融合焦点调制与逐比特动态加权，双 TTS 骨干分别训练评测
- 📌 **结论**：16 比特载荷下个体/复合扰动解码精度超最强基线 9.2/23.5pp——生成感知的语音水印溯源

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

As text-to-speech (TTS) systems continue to advance, provenance tracking and security verification of synthesized speech have become increasingly important. Audio watermarking provides active provenance by embedding imperceptible, machine-recoverable information into speech. However, existing post-hoc methods typically modify the waveform after synthesis, leaving the watermark vulnerable to compound perturbations. To address this limitation, we propose GAMW, a watermark injection framework integrated into the TTS generation process. Before waveform synthesis, GAMW embeds a content-adaptive multi-bit watermark residual into the generated representation, and its decoding objective combines focal modulation with dynamic per-bit weighting. We train and evaluate GAMW separately on two TTS backbones. With a 16-bit payload, GAMW improves decoding accuracy over the strongest baseline by 9.2 and 23.5 percentage points under individual and compound perturbations, respectively, while maintaining competitive speech quality. Reliable clean-speech decoding is also retained at higher payloads. Code is available at this https URL.

</details>

### 214. Have You Been Convinced? A Neuro-symbolic Argumentation Reasoning Approach to Detecting Persuasiveness in ChangeMyView Discussions

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15091)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`persuasion detection`、`argumentation`、`ChangeMyView`、`neuro-symbolic`

👤 **作者**：Siddharth Bhargava、Sara Tonelli、Patricia Martín-Rodilla

- 🎯 **研究动机**：论证结构如何传播影响以检测在线讨论中的说服力——形式化表示缺失
- 🔬 **研究方法**：NARS：检索增强迭代 LLM 抽取双极论证结构+渐进论证语义传播论证影响；CMV2BAS 数据集（delta 事件标注）
- 📌 **结论**：F1=0.57 超 zero-shot 大模型；支持主导与长链是限制攻击影响传播的关键失效——说服检测的形式语义路线（社交影响测量）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Argument structures represent how individual arguments interact within a discourse. However, leveraging these structures to infer meaningful knowledge remains challenging, as it requires a formal representation of how argumentative influence propagates across the structure. We propose Neuro-symbolic Argumentation Reasoning System (NARS) for performing persuasiveness detection directly from online discussions. The neural component employs retrieval-augmented iterative LLM reasoning to extract bipolar argument structures, while the symbolic component applies gradual argumentation semantics to propagate argumentative influence over the structure. Using the Webis-CMV-20 corpus, we construct CMV2BAS, a dataset of long, multi-threaded ChangeMyView discussions annotated with LLM-derived bipolar argument structures and labeled with delta events (view change vs.\ no change). We investigate whether gradual argumentation semantics can effectively detect persuasiveness, whether augmenting them with argument quality and graph-based centrality improves performance, and whether symbolic reasoning remains necessary in the presence of modern LLMs. NARS achieves F1=0.57 and AUC=0.56 under its best configuration, outperforming zero-shot Qwen3.6:27B and GPT-oss:20B. We identify support-dominant discussions and long argumentative chains as key failure modes limiting attack influence propagation. Our work demonstrates the feasibility of operationalizing formal gradual argumentation semantics over noisy real-world discourse for scalable macro-level persuasion analysis, while highlighting key challenges and directions for future research in neuro-symbolic argumentation reasoning. We release both CMV2BAS dataset and the NARS framework for public use.

</details>

### 215. Anchors Enhance Controllability and Reasoning of Diffusion Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15097)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`diffusion language model`、`controllability`、`anchor`、`RL`

👤 **作者**：Feiyu Qu、Zheng Huang、Andrea Zanette

- 🎯 **研究动机**：DLM 双向注意力允许控制 token 从任意位置引导生成——控制内容与位置的兼容性决定引导效果
- 🔬 **研究方法**：AnchorDLM：锚生成器产出短片段+位置作为控制 token 初始化冻结 DLM 输出序列，RL 联合优化锚内容与放置；离散流匹配视角解释为重塑初始速度场
- 📌 **结论**：五个 DLM 骨干上代码与数学推理的可控性与性能双提升——dLLM 可控生成机制（扩散语言模型生态）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Diffusion language models (DLMs) offer new opportunities for controllable text generation. However, their controllability, defined as the ability to satisfy task constraints while preserving their original capabilities, remains underexplored. DLMs' bidirectional attention allows control tokens to guide generation from arbitrary positions in the output sequence, but effective guidance depends on the compatibility between their content and placement. To this end, we propose AnchorDLM, a framework in which an anchor generator produces an anchor consisting of a short output fragment and its position for each query. The anchor is placed as control tokens at the specified position to initialize the output sequence of a frozen DLM and guide subsequent denoising. We train the anchor generator with reinforcement learning (RL) to jointly optimize anchor content and placement, using rewards computed from the DLM's generated outputs based on answer correctness and constraint satisfaction. Moreover, through the lens of discrete flow matching (DFM), we interpret anchor placement as helping to reshape the initial velocity field that guides generation. Experiments on code generation and mathematical reasoning across five DLM backbones show that AnchorDLM improves controllability and further yields gains in reasoning performance across diverse benchmarks.

</details>

### 216. Sanzaru: Benchmarking Protocol Formation under Epistemic & Communication Asymmetry

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15163)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`multi-agent communication`、`coordination`、`benchmark`、`protocol formation`

👤 **作者**：Yiping Li、Zhiyu An、Wan Du

- 🎯 **研究动机**：单模型任务能力不保证团队在不等的信息与通信访问下仍能协作
- 🔬 **研究方法**：SANZARU：拆弹式合作谜题（一听不见/一不能说/一看不见），120 实例七族，1512 回合+交互轨迹分析
- 📌 **结论**：总体成功率 49%，60% 团队失败归因通信且 agent 试图使用队友不可用信道——认知与通信不对称下的协议形成评测

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

A language model's ability to solve a task individually does not ensure that a team can solve it under unequal access to information and communication. SANZARU examines this gap through cooperative bomb-defusal puzzles adapted from BOMBANANA! (Lefto Studio, 2026). Task information and action authority are divided among three agents: one cannot hear, another cannot speak, and the third cannot see. The benchmark contains 120 puzzle instances across seven families, each targeting a distinct coordination ability. Across 1512 episodes, 7 models achieve an overall success rate of 49%, ranging from 20% to 86% under the evaluated configurations. Teams develop or adopt diverse communication protocols, from simple gesture codes to Morse and encodings of entire manuals. Yet trace analysis attributes about 60% of 771 team losses to communication, and agents attempt to use channels unavailable to their teammates despite explicit role instructions. Together, task outcomes and interaction traces enable evaluation of both collaborative performance and perspective taking throughout cooperation. The benchmark is available at this https URL.

</details>

### 217. UDHRBench: Benchmarking Human Rights in Large Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15179)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`human rights`、`benchmark`、`LLM judge`、`alignment audit`

👤 **作者**：Alak Raad、Noreldin Morssy、Zeerak Suhail、Serin Atiani、Mustafa Jarrar

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM 与国际公认人权标准的一致性缺乏操作化评测
- 🔬 **研究方法**：UDHRBench：覆盖全部 30 条宣言的 1,124 问题+4,496 条四模型回复专家标注（κ=0.801）+LLM-as-a-Judge 自动化与对齐宽宥分
- 📌 **结论**：系统性非平凡错位且违规多以效率/风险管理/科学中立等看似理性的推理出现——人权重维度的对齐审计

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language models are increasingly deployed in contexts requiring adherence to ethical and legal norms, yet their alignment with internationally recognized human rights standards remains underexplored. We introduce UDHRBench, a large-scale benchmark for operationalising the evaluation of large language model compliance with the Universal Declaration of Human Rights. The dataset comprises 1,124 human rights confronting questions covering all 30 articles and 4,496 responses from four large language models (ChatGPT, Grok, Gemini, and Fanar), manually annotated by experts for alignment, with high inter-annotator agreement ($\kappa = 0.801$). Our analysis reveals systematic and non-trivial misalignments, showing that violations typically arise through seemingly rational reasoning: appeals to efficiency, risk management, or scientific neutrality, rather than explicit rejection of rights. We further propose an LLM-as-a-Judge framework for automated evaluation using zero-shot and few-shot prompting, achieving 89.8\% weighted F1-score in detecting human-rights misalignment, alongside a novel Alignment Leniency Score that quantifies how judge models resolve ambiguous cases. The dataset, annotations, and code will be released as open-source resources (this https URL).

</details>

### 218. HarnessNet: Feedback-driven Hierarchical Joint Harness Evolution for Multi-agent Systems

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15221)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`harness evolution`、`multi-agent systems`、`self-improvement`、`local optimization`

👤 **作者**：Zhaoyuan Qiu、Sachith Seneviratne、Yu Xia、Saman Halgamuge

- 🎯 **研究动机**：MAS 的 harness 联合演化：全局优化器随规模膨胀且访问限制禁止共享 agent 内部或全局反馈
- 🔬 **研究方法**：HarnessNet：Agentron（可演化前向 harness+基础模型+仅可观察修改该 harness 的局部优化器）分层组图，环境反馈沿图反向传播节点特异反馈
- 📌 **结论**：AgentBench 上与全局优化器相当收益且守本地隐私、更少 token/时间——MAS 自演化的分层架构（自改进基础设施）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

AI agents can evolve by using environment feedback to revise their harnesses, the software managing model inputs, tool use, memory, and execution, while keeping foundation models frozen. Existing studies focus on single agents, leaving joint harness evolution in multi-agent systems (MAS) underexplored. A single global optimizer faces growing context and workload as the system scales, while access restrictions may prevent sharing agent internals or system-wide feedback. We propose HarnessNet, a framework for hierarchical harness evolution in MAS. Its basic unit, Agentron, comprises an evolvable forward harness, a foundation model, and a local optimizer that can observe and modify only that harness. Agentrons form a layered graph through which environment feedback propagates backward, with upstream optimizers receiving node-specific feedback from the downstream layer. This decomposes joint harness evolution into smaller, coordinated tasks with separate contexts, reducing individual optimizers' context and workload while keeping access local. Optimizers need not access all agent trajectories or raw environment feedback. Experiments on AgentBench with multiple foundation models demonstrate the feasibility of training HarnessNet. Compared with a single global optimizer, HarnessNet achieves comparable performance gains while respecting local privacy and requiring fewer tokens and less time per optimizer call. A migration study shows that the evolved harnesses remain jointly effective after replacing the foundation models, suggesting transferability across models.

</details>

### 219. Accuracy and Uncertainty in Machine Deception Detection (Research White Paper)

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15314)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`deception detection`、`uncertainty`、`calibration`、`white paper`

👤 **作者**：Heng-Sheng Chang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：读内部激活的欺骗检测器依赖所选层、宽测试退化且不报告警报的不确定性——警报可信度决定可用性
- 🔬 **研究方法**：机器欺骗检测器白皮书：读全部层、仅用响应标签学习携带欺骗证据的 token、校准警报阈值控 FPR、集成一致性门控
- 📌 **结论**：以 Liars' Bench 五个未见欺骗任务×六个 Qwen3 规模为目标的评测设计与层-规模分析框架——欺骗监控的准确率+不确定性双目标提案

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

A deception detector is only as useful as the trust its alarms deserve. Like a polygraph, detectors that read a language model's internal activations can catch deceptive responses that look honest. Yet current detectors depend on which layers they read, weaken on broader tests, and do not state how certain their alarms are. This white paper proposes the Machine Deception Detector, which targets both detection accuracy and the uncertainty of detector scores. The detector reads every layer of the language model, learns which tokens carry evidence of deception using only response labels, and calibrates its alarm threshold so that its false--positive rate stays below a prescribed level. An ensemble of such detectors measures the uncertainty of each score and raises an alarm only when its members agree, with a confidence level that sets how certain each alarm must be. The proposed evaluation trains on instructed honest and deceptive statements, tests on five unseen deceptive tasks of Liars' Bench read by six Qwen3 models from 0.6B to 32B parameters, and is designed to also show which layers carry the deception feature and how this changes with model size.

</details>

### 220. Adversarial Detection for Pre-trained Classifiers via Lipschitz-Margin Analysis with Certification

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15359)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`adversarial detection`、`certification`、`Lipschitz`、`classifier`

👤 **作者**：Rui Zhang、Xinle Cao、Huazhen Yu、Jian Liu、Youwen Zhu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：认证 ℓ2 防御在随机平滑的查询成本与 Lipschitz 网络的架构约束间两难——检测导向是漏报代价更高的部署的对齐选择
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Lipschitz-边缘归约把认证鲁棒性转化为认证检测（单 Lipschitz 常数处理）；两族检测器（扰动对估计 L/加局部边缘质心）可部署于任意预训练分类器免重训
- 📌 **结论**：MNIST/CIFAR/ImageNet 四骨干上匹配或超越基线——『球内对抗输入必被检出』的证书式检测路线

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Deep classifiers deployed in safety-critical services like autonomous-driving perception remain vulnerable to small adversarial perturbations. Certified $\ell_2$ defenses today face a costly trade-off: randomized smoothing (RS) requires hundreds of noisy queries per input, while Lipschitz networks (LN) constrain the architecture and sacrifice clean accuracy. We take a detection-oriented approach instead: rather than certifying that predictions are invariant inside an $\ell_2$ ball, we certify that adversarial inputs within an $\ell_2$ ball are \emph{detected} --- a guarantee aligned with security-critical deployments where missing an attack is costlier than flagging a clean input. To realize this, we prove a Lipschitz-margin reduction from certified $\ell_2$ robustness to certified detection, which reduces the problem to handling a single Lipschitz constant $L$. We introduce two detector families: $\mathbb{D}_{Adv}$, which estimates $L$ from a single defender-side perturbed pair, and $\mathbb{D}_{lc}$, which augments that pair with a local margin centroid to keep the estimate above what the certificate requires. Both deploy on any pretrained classifier without retraining. With a valid global bound, the detector is fully certified at that radius; with a local estimate $L'$, certification is sample-wise and applies only when our certified condition holds. Across MNIST, CIFAR-10, and ImageNet-1k on four backbones (LeNet-5, VGG16, ResNet-50, ViT-B/16), our detectors match or outperform five baselines (CTT, SortNet, CRM, ContraNet, A$^2$D). For CIFAR-10 with 1-Lipschitz SortNet, our reduction delivers $100\%$ certified detection at the lowest FPR among compared certified detectors; on the standard CIFAR-10 VGG16, our detectors reject all evaluated in-budget adversarial examples, with 62-82\% both rejected and formally certified.

</details>

### 221. MemeLogos: Knowledge-Driven Retrieval-Augmented Reasoning for Harmful Meme Detection

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15463)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`harmful meme detection`、`knowledge graph`、`RAG`、`moderation`

👤 **作者**：Hexiang Gu、…、Zhaofeng He

- 🎯 **研究动机**：有害 meme 的危害隐含在视觉/文本/语境线索的复杂交互中——相似实例检索式 RAG 无法捕获可复用危害知识与推理机制
- 🔬 **研究方法**：MemeLogos：把复发危害模式与推理结构抽象为知识单元组织成星形知识图，结构化检索+认知启发的多阶段显式推理
- 📌 **结论**：持续超越既有有害 meme 检测方法并生成更连贯可解释推理——结构化危害知识的多模态审核

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

With the rapid proliferation of social media, harmful memes have become a growing threat to online ecosystems. Their harmfulness is often implicitly conveyed through complex interactions among visual, textual, and contextual cues, making accurate detection challenging. Recent studies have introduced Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) and external knowledge into harmful meme detection. However, existing methods mainly retrieve similar instances or auxiliary information, limiting their ability to capture reusable harmfulness knowledge and underlying reasoning mechanisms, and thus constraining both detection performance and interpretability. To address these challenges, we propose MemeLogos, a knowledge-driven retrieval-augmented reasoning framework for harmful meme detection. MemeLogos abstracts recurring harmfulness patterns and reasoning structures from diverse harmful cases into reusable knowledge units and organizes them into a dedicated star-shaped knowledge graph. By combining structured knowledge retrieval with an explicit multi-stage reasoning process inspired by cognitive mechanisms, MemeLogos leverages retrieved knowledge to guide harmfulness reasoning, improving both detection reliability and interpretability. Extensive experiments show that MemeLogos consistently outperforms existing harmful meme detection methods while generating more coherent and interpretable reasoning, demonstrating the effectiveness of structured harmfulness knowledge for multimodal content understanding.

</details>

### 222. Security in LLM-Assisted Coding: A Systematic Literature Review

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15482)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`survey`、`LLM coding`、`security`、`SLR`

👤 **作者**：Liang Yuan、Maryam Khanian Najafabadi、Mashud Rana、Varvara Vetrova

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM 加速开发但引入严重安全漏洞——自动编码安全的系统综述缺失
- 🔬 **研究方法**：系统文献综述：安全风险、基准局限与人类开发者角色三线
- 📌 **结论**：模型因训练数据偏差频繁生成不安全代码与错误补丁且自身可被直接攻击；评测受数据污染与安全指标缺失所困，人类监督仍不可或缺——LLM 编码安全全景

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large Language Models accelerate software development but introduce severe security vulnerabilities. This systematic review investigates security risks in automated coding, benchmark limitations, and the role of human developers. Findings indicate that models frequently generate insecure code and faulty patches due to training data biases. Additionally, the models themselves are susceptible to direct attacks. Current evaluation methods suffer from data contamination and lack robust security metrics. Consequently, human oversight remains essential. Safer integration of automation will require more security-aware model architectures, stronger security-specific benchmarks, and more effective human-AI collaboration frameworks to mitigate these systemic risks.

</details>

### 223. Screening Collapse under Recursive Synthetic Data

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15617)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`synthetic data`、`model collapse`、`variable screening`、`theory`

👤 **作者**：Jia Zhang、Xin Chen

- 🎯 **研究动机**：递归合成数据训练对模型拟合前变量筛选阶段的退化效应未明——伪标签目标漂移可侵蚀或反转活性分数边缘
- 🔬 **研究方法**：高斯线性模型+最小范数递归生成器下建立有限样本筛选界、晚代信息损失极限与 minimax 不可能结果；两种缓解对照
- 📌 **结论**：筛选崩溃可先于并加剧普通模型崩溃，被遗漏变量带来协方差加权损失——合成数据递归的数据完整性理论（供应链数据质量）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Recursive synthetic-data training can degrade a fitted model, but its effects on the variable-screening stage that precedes model fitting are less understood. When each generation labels data for the next, a marginal screener ranks variables against the current pseudo-label target rather than the original response. Across generations, this target drift can erode or reverse the active--inactive score margin and cause screening to discard variables that clean-label screening would retain. We call this process \emph{screening collapse}. In a Gaussian linear model with minimum-norm recursive generators, we establish finite-sample screening bounds, characterize a late-generation information-loss limit, and derive a minimax impossibility result. Screening collapse can precede and compound ordinary model collapse: omitted variables incur a covariance-weighted loss that no refit restricted to the selected variables can eliminate, while recursive labels further degrade estimation on the retained variables. We also examine two approaches to mitigating screening collapse. Fixed-fraction real-label retention preserves a clean-label component but leaves resolution fixed, whereas data accumulation can avoid screening collapse if the pooled target preserves a positive margin and the growing sample resolves it. Numerical experiments on gene-expression data support the theoretical findings.

</details>

### 224. Riemannian Suppression of Cross-Session Identity Leakage in Motor-Imagery EEG

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15626)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`EEG privacy`、`identity linkage`、`Riemannian geometry`、`BCI`

👤 **作者**：Sheikh Samit Muhaimin、Aarthy Nagarajan

- 🎯 **研究动机**：运动想象 EEG 携带跨会话持存的主体识别结构——分别发布的会话可被链接（隐私泄漏）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：黎曼变换均衡主体条件几何统计（均值协方差+切空间散布白化），两会话拟合一次不变应用到留出会话，五个攻击者×四协方差表示评测
- 📌 **结论**：最凶攻击者从 90.8% 降到 15.6%/9.9%，MI 解码代价 7.0/2.1 点，无聚合规则超随机两分——协方差几何上的免学习身份抑制

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Electroencephalography (EEG) recorded for motor-imagery (MI) brain-computer interfaces (BCIs) carries subject-identifying structure that persists across sessions, allowing separately released sessions from the same person to be linked. Existing EEG identity protections use learned or additive perturbations and operate on representations other than the covariance geometry underlying MI pipelines. We ask whether cross-session identity leakage can be suppressed directly in this covariance representation without learning a privacy model. We introduce a Riemannian transform that equalizes two subject-conditioned geometric statistics: the Riemannian mean covariance and the tangent-space scatter around it, with two optional band-specific refinements. Ablations show that mean alignment and scatter whitening carry nearly all of the suppression, while the optional stages contribute small, dataset-dependent refinements. Across two public datasets and five attackers spanning four covariance-space representations, the worst attacker falls from 90.8% to 15.6% on BCI Competition IV-2a and from 90.8% to 9.9% on OpenBMI, at a cost of 7.0 and 2.1 points of MI decoding accuracy relative to the 8-30 Hz input, and no aggregation rule exceeds chance by more than two points at any batch size. The transform requires no learned privacy model, surrogate network, or gradient-based training, and is fitted on one session and applied unchanged to a held-out later session.

</details>

### 225. Robust Detection of Directional Adversarial Attacks in Deep Neural Networks for Radiological Imaging

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15628)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`adversarial detection`、`medical imaging`、`directional attacks`、`defense`

👤 **作者**：Tristan Malatyński、Joanna Jaworek-Korjakowska

- 🎯 **研究动机**：放射影像深度系统对对抗扰动高度脆弱（文献 >90% ASR）——误诊风险要求可靠检测
- 🔬 **研究方法**：随机噪声后续攻击框架：比较干净/攻击图像与不同对抗变体间的 Sigmoid 预测差异识别对抗方向性
- 📌 **结论**：三个医学数据集多攻击场景最高 99.8% 检测准确率——医疗影像对抗攻击的方向性检测路线

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Deep learning is now central to radiology, helping detect changes on X-rays, CTs, and MRIs. However, these systems are highly vulnerable to adversarial attacks - small, crafted perturbations that mislead models while appearing unchanged to humans. Such errors risk missed cancers or false positives. Studies show over 90% attack success on medical scans. Almost 30% of US hospitals use AI in imaging in 2023. Thus, an effective method of detecting such attacks and their directionality is crucial to ensure the credibility and reliability of medical systems based on DNNs. We propose a novel detection framework that leverages subsequent attacks using random noise to identify adversarial perturbations. Our approach uses the analysis of variations in the Sigmoid function to distinguish genuine medical images from adversarially manipulated ones. Specifically, we compare prediction differences between clean and attacked images, as well as between different adversarially altered versions, to improve detection accuracy. We evaluated our method on three popular medical datasets including 'Chest X-Ray Images for Classification', 'Retinal Fundus Multi-disease Image Dataset' and 'Brain Tumor Dataset' under various attack scenarios, including random noise. Our framework achieved up to 99.8% ACC in identifying adversarial directional attacks, significantly outperforming existing defense mechanisms. It consistently identified adversarial samples across varying attack strengths while keeping false positives low, showing strong reliability and potential for clinical use. Furthermore, our proposal highlights its potential for real-world deployment.

</details>

### 226. Recovering Low-Bit Failures in Vision-Language-Action Policies via Quantization-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15675)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`VLA`、`quantization`、`deployment gap`、`reinforcement learning`

👤 **作者**：Seongmin Park、…、Jungwook Choi

- 🎯 **研究动机**：量化降低 VLA 部署成本但损害任务完成——QAT 与 PTQ 在长时程与偏移下仍有大缺口
- 🔬 **研究方法**：五模型 4,050 真机试验系统量化失败模式+QCRL 离线 RL：进度优势估计+量化感知源条件（FP rollout 供参照经验、进度惩罚区分部分完成）
- 📌 **结论**：INT4 W4A16 达全精度 99.3% 均分、NVFP4 达 100.7% 且 1.73× 加速——VLA 量化部署缺口的实测与恢复（部署配置×安全关键策略）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Quantization lowers deployment costs of vision-language-action (VLA) policies but can compromise task completion. Across five VLA models and 4,050 real-world trials, we systematically reveal that quantization-aware training (QAT) generally improves on post-training quantization (PTQ) but leaves substantial gaps to full precision (FP) on long-horizon tasks and under visual and semantic shifts, while its imitation-based objective does not explicitly use rollout success or partial completion. To exploit these outcome signals, we introduce Quantization-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning (QCRL), an offline RL method coupling progress-based advantage estimation with quantization-aware source conditioning. FP rollouts supply reference experience beyond action-level distillation, progress-scaled terminal penalty distinguishes partial completion, and quantization-aware conditioning selects advantageous reference behavior within the quantized policy. We evaluate weight-only INT4 W4A16 on $\pi_{0.5}$ and GR00T N1.5 and joint weight-activation NVFP4 W4A4 on $\pi_{0.5}$. Using only in-distribution data, QCRL reaches 99.3% of FP's mean score across three visual-shift conditions for $\pi_{0.5}$ with INT4 W4A16, alongside 42.9% model-memory savings. With NVFP4 W4A4, it reaches 100.7% of the same FP baseline, alongside a $1.73\times$ speedup using NVFP4/FP8 deployment. Hardware gains are relative to FP16 on Jetson Thor.

</details>

### 227. Towards Autopilot Labeling for Short Video Content Governance

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15687)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`content governance`、`autopilot labeling`、`short video`、`moderation`

👤 **作者**：Junwen Chen、…、Yang Song

- 🎯 **研究动机**：短视频爆炸式增长使人工标注低效昂贵——内容治理的自动化分级缺乏渐进框架
- 🔬 **研究方法**：四级自动驾驶式标注框架：判别 MLLM（L1）→域适应零样本（L2）→带工具 agentic 工作流（L3）处理复杂/模糊案例
- 📌 **结论**：各级精度渐进提升、真实应用人工成本降 85%——内容审核的 agentic 自动化生产级实践

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

The explosive growth of short videos has presented significant challenges to content governance. Traditional manual labeling is inefficient, slow to iterate, and costly. Therefore, we explore and develop a multi-stage autopilot labeling framework aimed at progressively increasing the level of automation in short video content annotation. To move beyond the heavy reliance on human expert labeling at the initial L0 stage, we first introduce a discriminative MLLM labeler at the L1 stage, which is fine-tuned with supervised data to directly predict labels for short videos. Advancing to the L2 stage, we shift to a domain-adaptive MLLM pretrained on short video data and use it for zero-shot labeling, enabling flexible generalization without task-specific supervision. In the L3 stage, we propose an agentic annotation workflow, and equip the agent with several key tools, which empower the agent to handle complex, unfamiliar, or vaguely defined cases, significantly reducing the need for human intervention. We conduct extensive experiments across various content issues, and results demonstrate that each stage of the autopilot labeling framework progressively improves labeling accuracy, and has significantly reduced manual labor costs in real-world applications by up to 85%.

</details>

### 228. Generalizable Model Editing Could Be More Than Direct Generalization

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15707)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`model editing`、`generalization`、`compositional`、`paradigm analysis`

👤 **作者**：Xiaotian Ye、…、Shu Wu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：模型编辑的直接泛化范式（输入输出映射式）被共识认为困难——真实 chatbot 部署中经典方法却展现流利泛化，矛盾未被解释
- 🔬 **研究方法**：理论（可辨识性问题）+实践（构造偏差歧义与被优化动力学覆盖）双重约束分析，提出组合泛化替代范式并跨模型跨方法验证
- 📌 **结论**：现代 LLM 的推理组合能力支撑广泛的组合式编辑泛化——编辑泛化的范式修正（知识更新的边界与机制）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

A central desideratum in model editing is generalization to complex tasks, which prior work formalizes under a direct generalization paradigm: treating and evaluating LLM behavior as an input-output mapping and expecting a factual update to alter outputs on reasoning tasks. Yet despite substantial empirical effort, the field consensus remains that such generalization is highly challenging and elusive. However, when we deploy the latest models in an in-the-wild chatbot-user setting, we surprisingly find that even classical editing methods exhibit remarkably fluent generalization behavior. This motivates us to revisit the obstacles to generalization and the limitations of the prevailing paradigm. We find that direct generalization is fundamentally constrained both theoretically and practically: theoretically, it suffers from an identifiability issue; and practically, existing approaches attempting to circumvent this are further hindered by the ambiguity of their constructed biases and by vulnerability to being overridden by other factors such as optimization dynamics. We instead identify compositional generalization, empowered by the increasingly strong reasoning and composition abilities of modern LLMs, as a theoretically grounded and empirically validated alternative. We validate this phenomenon across multiple models and editing methods, showing that such generalization behavior is widespread. Overall, we argue that generalizable model editing could be more than direct generalization, and call for a paradigm shift toward more generalizable and practically deployable knowledge update techniques.

</details>

### 229. SpecForge: Recursive Self-Improvement for Coding Agents via Behavioral Specifications

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15708)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`recursive self-improvement`、`coding agent`、`specification`、`tree search`

👤 **作者**：Yutong Cheng、Haifeng Chen、Peng Gao、Wei Cheng

- 🎯 **研究动机**：冻结模型的 coding agent 靠外部上下文自改进——行为规范（自然语言文档）递归精修的实体间耦合（输出耦合）造成打地鼠回归
- 🔬 **研究方法**：SpecForge 多前沿树搜索：Pareto 引导状态选择防过早承诺+被调者先于调用者的依赖约束选择+错误多样化扩展
- 📌 **结论**：DevEval+ 上有效克服输出耦合实现持续规范改进——行为规范驱动的 RSI（自改进基础设施的能力参照）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Coding agents based on frozen language models can improve their performance by refining the external context they rely on, without updating model weights. We study recursive self-improvement through behavioral specifications: natural-language code documentation that describes the behavior of program entities and guides agents in generating correct code. We formulate a self-improvement loop in which an agent generates code from its current specifications, evaluates it with execution-verified tests, and refines the specifications based on observed failures. The improved specifications then guide subsequent generation, progressively improving the agent's understanding of the codebase. However, recursive refinement is challenging because program entities are interdependent. Improving the specification of one entity can inadvertently degrade the correctness of others, a phenomenon we call output coupling. This creates a whack-a-mole effect in which local improvements cause regressions elsewhere, preventing consistent progress. We introduce SpecForge, a multi-frontier tree search framework that addresses this challenge with three mechanisms: (i) Pareto-guided state selection preserves complementary specification states and prevents premature commitment; (ii) dependency-constrained entity selection prioritizes callee-before-caller refinement; and (iii) error-diversified expansion explores multiple refinements using different subsets of test failures. On DevEval+, SpecForge achieves the best performance across five backbone models, including a 94.9% solve rate with Claude-4.5-Sonnet and a 5.7-percentage-point average improvement over the strongest baseline. The improved specifications also make the agent better at tasks outside the loop: they transfer to cross-language translation and to new-feature implementation, where they outperform the benchmark-provided requirement documents.

</details>

### 230. MedHarnessBench: A Unified Benchmark for Healthcare Agent Evaluation

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15714)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`healthcare agent`、`harness evaluation`、`process dimensions`、`benchmark`

👤 **作者**：Rui Tang、Hongqiu Wang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：医疗 agent 须把纵向临床证据转化为可靠动作——终态结果导向基准无法定位失效来自模型、harness 还是交互
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Medical Harness Protocol：七个医疗过程维度（含临床审计性与行为边界）+规范化干预轨迹追踪运行时支持是否被采纳传播；342 可执行任务 16,000+ 受控执行
- 📌 **结论**：统一协议在终态成功、子任务完成与过程质量上全面提升，单故障扰动归因 harness-模型交互——医疗 agent 的 harness 级评测

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Healthcare agents must transform longitudinal clinical evidence into reliable actions through heterogeneous tools and stateful workflows, conditions under which model-centric systems may lose context, misuse tools, fail to verify consequential transitions, or cross authorization boundaries. Existing medical-agent benchmarks primarily emphasize terminal task outcomes, making it difficult to determine whether execution failures arise from the agent model, the runtime harness, or their interaction. We present the Medical Harness Protocol, an outcome-preserving framework that independently evaluates seven healthcare-specific process dimensions: Care Action Fulfillment, Clinical Resource Competence, Patient-Evidence Alignment, Clinical Workflow Continuity, Clinical Auditability, Closed-Loop Verification, and Clinical Conduct Boundaries. A normalized intervention trace further tracks whether runtime support is available, activated, delivered, adopted by the agent, and propagated into subsequent behavior. We instantiate the protocol as MedHarnessBench, comprising 342 executable tasks across FHIR/EHR operations, healthcare-administration interfaces, and multimodal clinical tools. Across more than 16,000 controlled task executions, MedHarness yields broad improvements in terminal task success, intermediate subtask completion, and healthcare-specific process quality. We further evaluate MedHarness against multiple general-purpose harnesses and use controlled single-fault perturbations to verify the sensitivity and discrimination of the seven-dimensional evaluation. These results demonstrate that healthcare-specific runtime support can substantially improve both task completion and execution quality, while revealing failures that terminal outcomes alone cannot capture. Code and resources are available at this https URL.

</details>

### 231. Visible but Not Avoided: State-Dependent Re-Proposal of Rejected Candidates in Catalog-Based LLM Optimization Loops

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15738)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`LLM optimization loop`、`rejection avoidance`、`measurement`、`self-improvement`

👤 **作者**：Jianlin Chen、Wenhui Chen、Chi Man Vong

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM 优化循环把已试已拒候选展示给提议者——静态探针测的回避能否预测真实轨迹状态中的回避未知
- 🔬 **研究方法**：目录式循环（40 编辑选一改 GUI agent）中按编辑名精确识别重提议：循环-探针差+历史/失败证据/一句拒绝状态标注的对照
- 📌 **结论**：干净探针低估重提议 0.32；已评组合消耗 28-63% 有效迭代；一句『此候选已被拒』在所有模型降重提议——自我改进循环的拒绝遗忘与廉价修复

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

LLM optimization loops often show the proposer the candidates it has already tried and rejected, but leave it to the proposer to avoid them. We ask whether avoidance measured in a static probe predicts avoidance in the states an optimizer's own trajectory produces. In catalog-based loops where three open LLMs improve a GUI agent by naming one of 40 edits, a re-proposal is identified exactly by the edit's name. Clean base-state probes underpredict re-proposal in the proposers' own loops (macro-averaged loop-minus-probe rate 0.32 [0.27, 0.37]), and proposals of already evaluated harness-edit pairs consume 28-63% of valid proposer iterations. The two settings reject different edits: a probe's rejected edits are mostly ones the proposer did not choose, whereas a loop's are its own earlier choices, which remain preferred when the history is removed. Ordinary history lowers re-proposal for two proposers but raises it for the third, and the effects of failure evidence are model-specific. Stating the rejection status in one sentence lowers re-proposal in every model (-0.16 [-0.22, -0.09] relative to a neutral list of the same IDs). For catalog-based loops with exact edit identities, rejection avoidance should be measured on the rejected sets the loop produces.

</details>

### 232. Agent Sociodrama: Social-Dynamics-Guided Persona Recasting for LLM Agent Teams

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15788)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`multi-agent systems`、`social dynamics`、`persona recasting`、`team repair`

👤 **作者**：Yirui Qi、Xiaoming Zhang、Jaehong Yoon

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM 团队以社交方式失败：知情者沉默、支配性发言锚定错误、违规不受制裁
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Agent Sociodrama：统一外部 judge 每轮更新情感与有向非对称关系+社会保真度验证；轨迹引导的 persona 重铸诊断群体动态失败后重跑
- 📌 **结论**：四协作基准上提升最高 83.3pp、讨价还价 Pareto 率超 SOTOPIA/AgentVerse——MAS 社会性失效的建模与修复

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly deployed as teams, yet they fail in characteristically social ways: information holders stay silent, dominant speakers anchor groups on wrong answers, and norm violations go unsanctioned. We present Agent Sociodrama, a framework for modeling and validating evolving social dynamics in LLM agent teams. Under fixed personas, agents' emotional states and directed, asymmetric relationships are updated every round by a unified external LLM judge and shape their subsequent interactions. Across six LLM backbones and five social scenarios, we assess social fidelity through classical social-theory patterns and agreement with human judgments. The resulting trajectories exhibit structured social patterns and model-specific differences. We then introduce Trajectory-Guided Persona Recasting (TPR), which diagnoses group-dynamic failures from interaction transcripts and social trajectories and recasts agent personas before rerunning the task. Across four collaborative benchmarks, TPR improves performance from both heterogeneous and randomly initialized agent persona compositions, yielding up to 83.3%p of accuracy improvement over the initial casting. On representative distributed-information cooperation and mixed-motive bargaining tasks, we outperform SOTOPIA and AgentVerse in three of four comparisons, by up to 37.5%p in Pareto rate. These results show that validated social trajectories provide actionable signals for repairing LLM agent teams.

</details>

### 233. Review Rarely Beats the Stronger Model: Separating Verification from Capability Transfer in LLM Pipelines

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15897)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`verification`、`LLM pipeline`、`capability transfer`、`evaluation validity`

👤 **作者**：Yirui Zhang、Yash Raj Shrestha

- 🎯 **研究动机**：以对生成器的增益评价审查者混淆了验证（超出双模型）与能力转移（更强者本可自答）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：12 个开源模型 48 对在数学/知识/代码/推理上以『更强模型单独』为基准重新度量审查收益并分析机制
- 📌 **结论**：96 个审查方向仅 9 个胜过更强模型单独（差距越大越差）；审查成本 2.6-2.8 倍而同两次调用的选择器更准更省——验证研究的报告基准修正

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Qwen3-8B reviewing Llama-3.1-8B in math adds 33.2 points, yet Qwen3-8B alone is 6.4 points more accurate than the result. Reviewing another model's answer is usually judged by its gain over the generator. We show that this confuses verification, which adds accuracy beyond either model alone, with capability transfer, where a stronger reviewer supplies answers it could have given on its own. Measured against the stronger model alone, across 48 pairs of 12 open-weight models (3B to 72B) in math, knowledge, code and reasoning, review beats it in only 9 of 96 review directions, and does worse the larger the capability gap. The reason is that the reviewer mostly follows the generator, discarding its own correct answers. Review costs 2.6 to 2.8 times as much as the stronger model alone, while a selector using the same two calls is 2.7 to 4.2 points more accurate than review and cheaper. Studies of verification should report the stronger model alone, not only the gain over the generator. Code and per-question results: this https URL

</details>

### 234. Mind Your Own Role: Reducing Role Overstepping for Effective and Efficient Multi-Agent Collaboration via Dynamic Role Activation Steering

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15907)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`multi-agent systems`、`role overstepping`、`activation steering`、`runtime intervention`

👤 **作者**：Xingke Yang、…、Miao Pan

- 🎯 **研究动机**：MAS 中 agent 越权执行其他角色的子任务扰乱工作流——角色相关激活模式未被利用
- 🔬 **研究方法**：DRAS：发现角色指令诱导一致方向位移+角色遵从/越轨轨迹激活模式可分，构建角色转向向量与轻量越轨检测器按激活状态动态调强度
- 📌 **结论**：越轨轨迹降 86.6%、任务质量 +10-11.1%、提速 1.5-1.6×——激活空间的 MAS 角色治理（推理时干预）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Multi-Agent Systems (MAS) have shown strong capability in solving complex tasks by assigning different agents specialized roles and corresponding subtasks. However, agents may overstep their assigned responsibilities and perform subtasks belonging to other roles, which can disrupt workflow, reduce system efficiency, and degrade task performance. To address this issue, we investigate role-related patterns in the model's internal activation space and uncover two key observations: (i) role instructions induce consistent directional shifts in activation space, and (ii) role-adherent and role-overstepping trajectories exhibit distinct activation patterns. Based on these observations, we propose Dynamic Role Activation Steering (DRAS), a lightweight and effective inference-time activation intervention approach to reduce role overstepping in multi-agent collaboration. Specifically, DRAS constructs a role steering vector and a lightweight role-overstepping detector for each role. During LLM generation, the detector estimates the probability of role overstepping from the current activation state, and DRAS dynamically adjusts the steering strength accordingly to guide the agent toward its assigned responsibilities. Experimental results show that DRAS effectively reduces the number of role-overstepping trajectories by up to 86.6% compared with Vanilla MAS, thereby improving task quality score by 10.0%--11.1%, achieving a 1.50$\times$--1.61$\times$ task-completion speedup, and reducing token consumption by 26.8%--38.7%.

</details>

### 235. LURE: a Phase-Labelled Synthetic Corpus of Online-Grooming Exchanges

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15908)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`online grooming`、`synthetic corpus`、`child safety`、`moderation`

👤 **作者**：Emanuele Florindi、Valentina Franzoni、Ludovico Guercio、Alfredo Milani、Mattia Polticchia

- 🎯 **研究动机**：阶段标注的 groomer-受 害者对话是研究诱骗进展与早期预警的关键——唯一公开源小、过时且冻结
- 🔬 **研究方法**：LURE：检索增强管线+去拒答 8B 模型按五阶段负责任合成协议生成 2,121 组四阶段交换（ChatCoder 八标签映射），人工在环与两级验证
- 📌 **结论**：标签/阶段准确率 0.85/0.93，可迁移到真实 ChatCoder 罪犯语料的自动与人工编码——未成年人保护的合成语料与预警研究基础

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Phase-annotated conversational data is essential for studying the progression of online grooming of minors and for developing early warning tools. However, the only publicly available source (i.e., Perverted-Justice archive, which contains a subset coded using ChatCoder) is small, outdated, and frozen. We present a synthetic corpus of groomer-victim exchanges, generated using a retrieval-augmented pipeline running on institutional hardware. This pipeline uses an openly available, eight-billion-parameter, refusal-ablated language model. The corpus was created using the five stages of the responsible-generation protocol for synthetic datasets: ontology-driven design based on the ChatCoder luring-communication scheme (eight communicative tags mapped onto four grooming phases); multi-model prompt testing; human-in-the-loop supervision; semantic and feature-level validation; and modality-aware documentation. The released corpus contains 2,121 unique exchanges across seven tags and four phases, each accompanied by a Plutchik emotion field.
