Validation quantifies the integrity and lexical profile of the corpus, as well as the reproduction of real seed lines, in-domain label recoverability (tag accuracy: 0.85; phase accuracy: 0.93) and its transfer to real ChatCoder offender lines, which were coded automatically at the eight-tag level (31,640 lines) and by human coders at the three-construct level (10,876 posts). Accuracy was measured for lexical and sentence-embedding features, while a real-versus-synthetic discriminator was able to separate the two domains with 91% accuracy. The scope of the corpus includes controlled, phase-conditioned experimentation, pipeline development and augmentation alongside real data and awareness material. The corpus, its datasheet and the audit code are released with vetted access.

</details>

### 236. Small but Mighty: Test Suites as Improvement Signals for Self-Evolving Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15964)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`self-evolving agent`、`test suite`、`improvement signal`、`quality diversity`

👤 **作者**：Yutong Cheng、Wei Cheng、Wenchao Yu、Dawei Zhou、Haifeng Chen

- 🎯 **研究动机**：自改进 agent 重写自己的 harness——改进信号是重写必须通过的检查：rollout 昂贵主观而仓库测试太稀疏（仅拒 1/4 行为改变重写）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：TeSearch：质量多样性进化搜索小而强的测试套件（规模作行为描述子+转移核去噪 LLM 执行器），129 真实模块三骨干
- 📌 **结论**：拒 59.9% 行为改变重写（仓库测试 25.3%）、误报 4.0%、3.5s/决策——可执行测试作为自演化 agent 的守门信号

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

A self-evolving agent rewrites its own harness and must decide which rewrites to keep, so its improvement signal is the check a rewrite has to pass. Verifying a rewrite by agent rollout is expensive and subjective, while executable checks such as the repository's own tests are too sparse to catch behavior-changing rewrites, rejecting only a quarter of them. We instead take test feedback as the executable signal, and search for test suites that are both small and strong with a quality-diversity evolutionary algorithm, so that the signal is dense and informative. TeSearch treats suite size as a behavior descriptor, so that quality is pursued at every size and a small, strong suite is reachable, and learns a transition kernel during the search that denoises the LLM as an actuator on that descriptor. Across 129 real-world modules and three backbones, TeSearch delivers the highest-quality suites of any method compared, LLM-driven evolutionary search included, and the smallest of any LLM-based one. Used as the improvement signal of a self-evolving agent, it rejects 59.9% of behavior-changing rewrites against 25.3% for the repository's tests, at a 4.0% false-alarm rate and 3.5 s per decision. The code and data are available at this https URL.

</details>

### 237. AutoSec-RAN: Specification-Grounded LLM Fine-Tuning for Automated Security Analysis of O-RAN

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15995)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`O-RAN`、`security triage`、`LLM fine-tuning`、`telecom`

👤 **作者**：Anoop Mishra、Joshua Moore、Aly Sabri Abdalla、Vuk Marojevic

- 🎯 **研究动机**：O-RAN 测试的安全分诊依赖静态规则或原始抓包特权访问——规范接地的自动化分析缺失
- 🔬 **研究方法**：AutoSec-RAN：69 份 O-RAN/3GPP 标准文档两阶段 LoRA 微调 Mistral-7B+同索引 RAG，5G/O-RAN 测试床上的 DDoS 重放场景验证（judge 打分忠实/正确/可行动）
- 📌 **结论**：313 评测项均分 0.713（超 0.70 阈值），缓解与威胁建模推理最强——LLM 作为电信基础设施安全机制的能力刻画

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

This paper presents AutoSec-RAN, a specification-grounded large language model (LLM) framework for automated security triage of Open Radio Access Network (O-RAN) testing. Unlike approaches relying on static rule sets or privileged access to raw packet captures, AutoSec-RAN fine-tunes Mistral-7B-Instruct through two Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) stages over a curated corpus of 69 O-RAN, 3GPP, and adjacent standards documents, then selectively augments inference with retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) over the same specification index. At inference, the model receives only an investigator-style question and a scenario metadata block, simulating an automated analyst operating without raw packet evidence. AutoSec-RAN is validated on a purpose-built 5G/O-RAN testbed across labeled fronthaul distributed denial of service replay scenarios. A Gemma-4-31B judge LLM scores each candidate's answer on faithfulness, correctness, and actionability relative to a reference authored from full packet capture and log evidence. AutoSec-RAN achieves a mean judge overall score of 0.713 across all 313 evaluation items (0.755 restricted to the five supervised taxonomy classes), clearing the 0.70 reporting threshold, and a blended score of 0.778 (0.808 supervised-only) when cosine similarity over Beijing Academy of Artificial Intelligence (BAAI) General Embedding (BGE) is incorporated as a secondary semantic signal. The framework performs strongest on mitigation and threat-model reasoning (with a mean overall of 0.849 and 0.848, respectively) and identifies a measurable gap on the held-out attack-direction class (0.515), a controlled result that exposes a specific training data requirement. These results establish specification-grounded LoRA fine-tuning combined with RAG retrieval as a viable foundation for automated security validation of AI-native O-RAN.

</details>

### 238. Adversarial Robustness of Camera-Radar Sensor Fusion for Autonomous Driving

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16012)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`adversarial robustness`、`sensor fusion`、`autonomous driving`、`camera-radar`

👤 **作者**：Ce Zhou、Yubo Wu、Khang Nguyen、Liyang Xiao、Thomas Hou、Qiben Yan

- 🎯 **研究动机**：AD 多传感器感知的相机-雷达融合在对抗条件下的鲁棒性未被系统测量
- 🔬 **研究方法**：nuScenes 统一评测管线：8 种相机图像扰动+3 种雷达点云扰动×5 种融合算法×检测/深度估计双任务
- 📌 **结论**：相机扰动中等强度即严重退化双任务、雷达扰动小且任务依赖（检测几乎不受扰）——融合架构依赖稠密相机特征，融合不内禀鲁棒性

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Autonomous driving (AD) relies on multi-sensor perception to interpret the surrounding environment. However, the robustness of camera--radar fusion under adversarial conditions remains largely unexplored. This paper presents a systematic measurement study of the robustness of camera--radar sensor fusion in AD. We construct a unified evaluation pipeline on the nuScenes dataset that simulates attacks on both sensing modalities, including eight camera-image perturbations and three radar point-cloud perturbations. We evaluate five camera--radar fusion algorithms across two perception tasks, 3D object detection and depth estimation, and quantify their performance under clean and attacked inputs using standard metrics. Our evaluation reveals a clear asymmetry in the robustness of fusion systems. Camera perturbations substantially degrade both object detection and depth estimation, and moderate attack strengths are already sufficient to cause severe performance degradation. In contrast, the evaluated radar perturbations produce smaller and task-dependent effects, with object-detection models showing particularly limited degradation. Our analysis attributes this difference to the strong dependence of existing fusion architectures on dense camera features, together with the filtering, association, and confidence-weighting mechanisms commonly applied to sparse radar measurements. These results demonstrate that sensor fusion does not inherently guarantee robustness and that the contribution and reliability of each sensing modality must be explicitly considered under adversarial conditions. Based on four empirical findings, we derive measurement-guided design implications for future robust camera--radar fusion, including modality-specific reliability estimation, cross-modal consistency checking, adaptive fusion, and graceful failover under severe sensor degradation.

</details>

### 239. Computational Reflective Equilibrium for Agentic AI: A Rawlsian Alignment Scaffold for Governance

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16015)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`governance`、`reflective equilibrium`、`alignment scaffold`、`prompt injection`

👤 **作者**：Quanyan Zhu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agentic AI 每个部署案例有各自的指令/策略/证据/干系人——对齐须逐案形成而非全局一次性
- 🔬 **研究方法**：ARE 计算反思均衡框架：类型化支持/攻击关系连接原则/判断/工具证据/候选动作，出处编码指令层级；均衡即互适博弈 Nash 均衡+单调性/词典序/稳定证书/注入隔离定理
- 📌 **结论**：『不择手段赢退款』的助手在均衡下提交诚实保修申请——治理经价值优先级/来源权威/禁止/人环触发进入 harness（治理架构提案）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

The rapid deployment of agentic AI has introduced complex challenges for the ethics and governance of AI systems. Agents built on large language models act through tools on behalf of people, and each deployment case brings its own instructions, policies, evidence, and stakeholders, so alignment must be formed case by case. To this end, this work proposes agentic reflective equilibrium (ARE), a computational reflective equilibrium framework that serves as an ethical alignment scaffold in the agent harness. Drawing inspiration from the reflective equilibrium of John Rawls, the scaffold links principles from the system prompt and operator policy, considered judgments, tool evidence, and candidate actions through typed support and attack relations, with provenance encoding the instruction hierarchy. The agent acts on an equilibrium of this state and calls tools or asks questions only while doing so is worth its cost, unless a due-diligence mandate requires them. Governance enters through value priorities, source authority, prohibitions, human-in-the-loop triggers, and mandates, and certificates attribute each action to the sources whose commitments drove it. We show that equilibria are Nash equilibria of a mutual-adjustment game and establish monotonicity, lexical priority, stability certificates, and a quarantine theorem for prompt injection. Two case studies illustrate the framework: a computer-use assistant told to win a refund "whatever it takes" files an honest warranty claim, refuses a fake review, and ignores an injected request for personal data; a hiring agent showcases how authority, oversight triggers, audit mandates, and hard prohibitions shape discrimination, privacy violations, and escalation. The framework offers traceability, coherence, and adaptivity, facilitating safe, secure, and trustworthy agentic AI.

</details>

### 240. Of Errors and Echoes: Responsibility-Aware Credit Assignment for Tool-Use Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16027)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`credit assignment`、`responsibility`、`tool-use agent`、`RL`

👤 **作者**：Xingjian Diao、…、Jiang Gui

- 🎯 **研究动机**：观察到的错误可能继承自更早决策、多个责任决策共享同一后果或参与后续恢复——细粒度信用分配混淆责任
- 🔬 **研究方法**：ECHO：冻结 judge 结合回溯轨迹分析与策略可见前缀的决策时评估，区分新引入/继承/错过纠正/成功纠正；共享后果只计一次、自致错误修复受限
- 📌 **结论**：六工具基准 4B/8B 双规模五比六最高分——决策责任作为工具 agent 信用分配的基础（训练与归因共用框架）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Reinforcement learning improves tool-use agents, yet fine-grained credit assignment remains challenging even with step-level feedback. An observed error may be inherited from an earlier decision, while multiple responsible decisions may share the same downstream consequence or participate in later recovery. We introduce Error-Chain-Aware Optimization (ECHO), a responsibility-aware credit assignment framework that separates responsibility identification from credit allocation. A frozen judge combines retrospective trajectory analysis with decision-time assessment from the policy-visible prefix to distinguish newly introduced errors, inherited consequences, missed corrections, and successful corrections. ECHO then counts shared downstream consequences once across responsible decisions and constrains rewards for repairing self-induced errors through source-funded recovery, producing bounded action-level supervision alongside terminal supervision. Experiments on six diverse tool-use benchmarks at both 4B and 8B scales show strong and consistent improvements over the compared baselines, with ECHO achieving the highest score on five of six benchmarks at each scale. These results support decision responsibility as a useful basis for fine-grained credit assignment in tool-use agents. Our code is available at this https URL.

</details>

### 241. Adaptive Compression and Targeted Perturbation: A Unified Framework for Generalized Audio Deepfake Detection

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16049)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`audio deepfake`、`generalization`、`detection`、`adversarial perturbation`

👤 **作者**：Jianqiao Cui、Haoxian Zhu、Hanbiao Meng、Yanghao Zhang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：既有音频深伪检测固定瓶颈架构无法适配多样伪造伪影的异质信息密度，标准增广致鲁棒-精度病态权衡
- 🔬 **研究方法**：AAFI 自适应特征整合（压缩池+随机探索动态选潜维）+Mel-BAP 频域定向正则学不变决策边界
- 📌 **结论**：Whisper-small 达平均 EER 1.51%、参数比 3B SOTA 少 91.6% 且 EER 改善 60.7%，跨五未见语言 3.91-5.65%——轻量泛化音频深伪检测

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Existing audio deepfake detection (ADD) methods frequently exhibit limited generalization, as fixed bottleneck architectures fail to adapt to the heterogeneous information densities inherent in diverse spoofing artifacts. Moreover, standard augmentation often leads to a pathological robustness-accuracy trade-off. In this paper, we propose a unified ADD framework to address these challenges. Our approach integrates: (1) an Adaptive Audio Feature Integration (AAFI) module that dynamically selects optimal latent dimensions via a compression pool and a stochastic exploration mechanism, ensuring flexible representations across multiple types of dynamic attacks; and (2) a Mel-Band Adversarial Perturbation (Mel-BAP) strategy that applies targeted regularization in the mel-spectrogram domain to encourage the model to learn invariant decision boundaries. Extensive evaluations on benchmarks including ADD 2022, FakeOrReal, and In-the-Wild demonstrate that our model based on Whisper-small achieves a state-of-the-art average Equal Error Rate (EER) of 1.51%. Compared to existing SOTA models such as the 3-billion-parameter Resemble-Detect-3B-Omni, our framework reduces the parameter count by up to 91.6% while achieving a 60.7% improvement in average EER. In a separate cross-lingual evaluation, the Whisper-base configuration achieves EERs of 3.91-5.65% on five unseen languages. The framework also maintains strong resilience to real-world acoustic distortions. This work provides a scalable and efficient "plug-in" solution for enhancing trust and security in the audio Web ecosystem.

</details>

### 242. Dream2Discern: Learning from Hard Latent Variants for Runtime Failure Prediction in Multi-Agent Systems

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16071)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`runtime monitoring`、`failure prediction`、`multi-agent systems`、`data augmentation`

👤 **作者**：Boxuan Zhang、jianing Zhu、Xiaotian Han、Ruixiang Tang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：MAS 运行时审计器从执行前缀预测终局——训练语料受限于昂贵的执行验证，且覆盖广度无难点选择准则
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Dream2Discern：按自身预测损失选择表示算子扰动生成困难潜变体（Dream），用原标签学预测（Discern），变体随学习再生成
- 📌 **结论**：AFTraj-2K/StepShield 上超竞争审计器、双基准 90%+ 轨迹级加权准确率并平衡及时性——无需新轨迹收集的运行时失败预测

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Reliable deployment of LLM-based multi-agent systems calls for runtime auditors that predict terminal outcomes from observed execution prefixes. These auditors typically learn from a limited corpus of trajectories with verified outcomes. Expanding this corpus requires costly execution and verification, yet broader failure coverage alone provides no explicit criterion for identifying prefixes that the current auditor finds difficult to classify. To this end, we propose Dream2Discern, a runtime auditor trained through implicit augmentation guided by its own prediction loss. During Dream, we select perturbations of the auditor's representation operators to increase prediction loss on recorded prefixes, yielding hard latent variants. During Discern, the auditor learns to predict execution outcomes from these variants using the original labels. The variants are regenerated as the auditor learns, turning a limited corpus into a renewable source of hard examples and requiring no additional trajectory collection or outcome verification. Extensive evaluations on AFTraj-2K and StepShield show that Dream2Discern outperforms competitive failure auditors and achieves over 90% trajectory-level weighted accuracy on both benchmarks while balancing prediction accuracy and timeliness. Project page: this https URL

</details>

### 243. Memorization in image classification: test set behavior and scaling laws

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16090)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`memorization`、`scaling laws`、`interpolation`、`privacy`

👤 **作者**：Jeevesh Attri、Florentin Guth

- 🎯 **研究动机**：理解泛化须理解记忆——记忆网络是否只是查询表、行为与动力学有何差异
- 🔬 **研究方法**：随机标签/像素训练 CNN：测试集行为跨初始化对比+数据集规模对容量与训练时间的需求标度
- 📌 **结论**：同随机标签不同初始化学得不同预测函数（泛化网络则一致）；完整记忆所需呈现次数可随数据集增大而减少——记忆化的机制刻画（隐私记忆研究基础）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Deep neural networks are capable of both learning generalizing solutions on natural tasks and memorizing arbitrary mappings. We argue that understanding the former requires understanding the latter, which is much less studied. In what ways do networks that generalize or memorize differ in their behavior or their training dynamics? Do memorizing networks simply function as a lookup table where input-output mappings are stored independently? We investigate these questions in the context of CNNs trained on image classification tasks with randomized labels or pixels. We first study the behavior of such memorizing networks on unseen images. We find that independently initialized networks trained on the same random labels learn different predictive functions: they make different predictions on test examples, in contrast with generalizing networks. We then examine the effect of dataset size on memorization through the required network capacity and training time to interpolate the training set. Counter-intuitively, we find that the number of presentations of a given example required for complete memorization can decrease with dataset size even when input-output pairs share no common structure. Although memorizing networks do not learn a universal solution, they exhibit interesting behaviors which call for further investigations into the underlying mechanisms.

</details>

### 244. Beyond Perfect Teammates: Multi-Agent Collaboration with Unreliable Colleagues

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16117)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`multi-agent collaboration`、`unreliable agents`、`robustness`、`benchmark`

👤 **作者**：Chen Huang、Yiyang Zhang、Yuhao Wu、Yang Deng、Wenxuan Zhang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：MAS 评测隐含完美同事假设——现实中同事会答非所问、信息不足或失联，任务能力与证据链恢复能力被混淆
- 🔬 **研究方法**：UnCoBench：错位/不完整/缺席三设置组织行为失效+可验证可扩展自动生成管线（答案唯一性与角色必要性执行检查）
- 📌 **结论**：十模型显示相似可靠设定性能对应不同不可靠鲁棒性；恢复行为低效等待与冗余查询——不可靠同事下的协作鲁棒性分离

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

LLM-based multi-agent collaboration is increasingly used to solve complex tasks by dividing work among specialized agents and integrating complementary expertise. Yet existing systems and evaluations largely rest on an implicit perfect-colleague assumption: collaborators provide correct and complete information and respond promptly to requests. In realistic teamwork, however, collaborators may return information that does not answer the intended question, provide insufficient detail to support a decision, or fail to respond to a request. To separate task-solving competence from the ability to recover an unreliable evidence-gathering process, we introduce the Unreliable Colleagues Benchmark (UnCoBench), which organizes behavioral failures through Misaligned, Incomplete, and Missed settings. The main agent need to verify contextual correctness, diagnose information gap and track unresolved dependencies in order to obtain the correct evidence for solving the task. We also introduce a verifiable and scalable automated generation pipeline that pairs LLM-based scenario generation with executable checks of answer uniqueness and role necessity. Results across ten models show that similar reliable-setting performance can result in different robustness to unreliable evidence. Trace analysis reveals frequent omissions of corrective follow-up, and the recovery behavior involves inefficient waiting and redundant queries. These findings show that robust collaboration requires not only sound reasoning, but also targeted action to diagnose evidence failures, recover relevant information, and integrate it into decisions. Our data and source code are available at this https URL.

</details>

### 245. Omni-Modal LLMs Know More Than They Say

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16135)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`omni-modal LLM`、`knowing vs saying`、`modality alignment`、`token-level`

👤 **作者**：Na Min An、Taekyung Kim、Jaemyung Yu、Hyunjung Shim、Dongyoon Han

- 🎯 **研究动机**：全模态 LLM 常正确感知非文本模态却无法经语言表达——知道但说不出（knows-but-cannot-say）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：TMA token 级模态对齐度量（模态表示与响应 token 对齐+注意力分配）+OmniCatalyst 感知→推断策略（自生成文本补充原模态输入）
- 📌 **结论**：非文本表示与响应 token 对齐更弱且注意力更少；七 Omni-LLM 上 +7.29% 且模态偏好更均衡——感知知识与语言表达间的接口失配

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Omni-modal large language models (Omni-LLMs) extend LLMs to process heterogeneous modalities (e.g., vision, audio, and IMU signals) alongside language, enabling a broader range of multimodal tasks. Yet we find that these models often fail to express information from non-textual modalities even when they perceive it correctly. We term this disconnect knows-but-cannot-say. To investigate this phenomenon, we introduce Token-level Modality Alignment (TMA), which measures the alignment between individual modality representations and response representations. Our analysis shows that non-textual representations are less aligned with response tokens and receive less attention than textual representations, suggesting a potential explanation for uneven modality preferences. Motivated by these findings, we propose OmniCatalyst, a simple perceive $\rightarrow$ infer strategy that complements the original modality inputs with self-generated text, making non-textual knowledge more accessible. Across seven Omni-LLMs, OmniCatalyst consistently improves performance (+7.29%) and yields more balanced modality preferences, with further gains from diverse training strategies. Further analysis demonstrates that the knowledge extraction phases increase TMA, suggesting that improved alignment makes non-textual information more accessible during response generation. In summary, our findings uncover how Omni-LLMs can better express modality knowledge through language. Our code is available at this https URL.

</details>

### 246. Pollard: Governed Semantic Execution Trees for Framework-Independent AI Agent Control and Replay

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16145)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`agent runtime`、`governance`、`replay`、`execution tree`

👤 **作者**：Muntaser Syed、Marius C. Silaghi

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agent 框架的检查点/工具调用追踪/资源限制给执行、治理与重放各自分配不同身份——统一缺位
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Pollard 治理语义执行树：步身份承诺规范输入/种类/尝试/父身份，同树记录完成/拒绝/资源结算/分支史；严格重放+事务存储+签名确定性冻结
- 📌 **结论**：双副本零不变量违反、严格重放免外部调用、全部语义变异被检出且控制组零误报——框架无关的 agent 控制与重放机制

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Agent frameworks can persist checkpoints, trace tool calls, and limit resource use, but these facilities often assign different identities to execution, governance, and replay. We present Pollard, a framework-independent runtime based on governed semantic execution trees. A step identity commits to canonical input, kind, attempt, and parent identity; the same tree records completion, refusal, resource settlement, and branch history. Strict replay returns a verified stored result without invoking the wrapped operation, while a transactional store coordinates reservations across concurrent workers. Under a signed deterministic freeze, two state-model replicas each executed the evaluation transitions without an invariant violation, retained-lease PostgreSQL groups were tested against exact limits, strict replay avoided external calls, and all semantic mutations were detected with no false alarms on the controls. A multi-pair framework matrix preserved reference behavior and verified the trees. A separate cost-capped hosted study exercised live provider paths, and every completed Pollard trial produced a verified tree. Together, the studies support the core mechanisms under explicit lease, boundary, and seal-custody assumptions.

</details>

### 247. Geometric Parrots: Rule Absence vs. Rule Fragility in Spatial and Physical Reasoning for Vision-Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16170)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`VLM`、`spatial reasoning`、`capitulation`、`dual-loop evaluation`

👤 **作者**：Bilal Jawaid、…、Rizwan Qureshi

- 🎯 **研究动机**：VLM 单轮准确度分不出有益修正与向自信错误断言的投降——规则缺失与规则脆弱两类失效被混同
- 🔬 **研究方法**：GRIP：34 个程序化生成域 10 万图 60 万问题（确定性代码出真值），双环评测（组合问题阶梯定位失效+受控挑战下重取答案并给符号）
- 📌 **结论**：把答案变化签名为修正或投降，分离规则缺失与规则脆弱——交互一致性维度的 VLM 推理评测

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Vision-language models (VLMs) can answer many geometric and physical reasoning questions correctly, yet still behave inconsistently in interaction, for example, abandoning a correct answer when confronted with a confident but incorrect user assertion. Standard single-turn accuracy cannot distinguish such capitulations from beneficial corrections because it scores each response in isolation. We introduce GRIP, a controlled benchmark with 34 procedurally generated reasoning domains, 100,000 images, and 600,000 questions spanning geometric, spatial, visual, and physical reasoning. Each scene is generated by deterministic code, and every answer is derived directly from the underlying scene state, providing exact ground truth without human annotation. On GRIP, we propose Dual-Loop Evaluation. A closed loop probes each image with a five-step compositional question ladder to localize where reasoning fails. An open loop then re-elicits the model's answer under controlled challenges, including peer-model responses and unsupported user assertions. Because ground truth is known exactly, answer changes can be signed as corrections (wrong-to-right) or capitulations (right-to-wrong), separating two failure modes: rule absence and rule fragility. The dataset is available at this https URL, with further details at this https URL.

</details>

### 248. Same Goal, Different Words: Spatial Instruction Sensitivity in World-Action Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16242)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`world-action model`、`instruction sensitivity`、`grounding`、`evaluation`

👤 **作者**：Ali-Adeeb Abbas、Esmaeil Seraj、Behrad Toghi、Taskin Padir

- 🎯 **研究动机**：世界-动作模型应在空间目标的等价描述间保持意图一致——措辞敏感性未被测量
- 🔬 **研究方法**：864 次 rollout 三模型四场景固定初始态与目标，对照参考物/被移物表述方式；独立语言理解测试与 rollout 行为分解
- 📌 **结论**：以参考物表述平均降 12.2pp 稳定放置；移错物体可满足所求关系、稳定放置从任意时刻 43.2% 掉到回合末 21.8%——WAM 的语言敏感性评测议程

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

World-action models should preserve the intended manipulation across equivalent descriptions of a spatial goal. We evaluate this consistency in 864 rollouts from three models across four RoboLab scenes, holding initial states and task goals fixed. Describing relations through the reference object rather than the object-to-move lowers the rate of reaching a stable placement by 12.2 percentage points on average. The effect of instruction rephrasing varies across spatial relations, so aggregate scores can hide sensitivity to wording. Standalone tests of the policies' language understanding identify relation reversals and show that adding images can disrupt otherwise correct text-only interpretations. Rollout analysis also distinguishes spatial placement from instruction following: moving the wrong object can satisfy the requested relation, and rates of stable placement fall from 43.2% at any time to 21.8% at episode end. These findings motivate a more thorough evaluation of world-action models' steerability, language understanding, and grounding. Evaluations should test consistency across equivalent instructions, verify that the intended object moved, and check whether the requested arrangement persists to the end of the rollout.

</details>

### 249. REVEAL: Reverse Engineering Agentic Latents from Frontier-Model Traces

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16288)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`agentic latents`、`trace distillation`、`steering`、`training-free`

👤 **作者**：Yuetian Du、…、Qiang Zhu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：前沿 agent 的推理成本促使经验迁移到小开源 agent——蒸馏需训练、潜通信需源内部访问
- 🔬 **研究方法**：REVEAL：受协议逆向工程启发定义 Agentic Latents（锚定状态+目标模型重放引出的反馈-回应动态画像），从同任务成败 rollout 的回应转变对比得出目标特异转向方向
- 📌 **结论**：Terminal-Bench 2.1/HLE-with-tools 三目标上超未修改 agent——前沿轨迹作为冻结 agent 的局部激活控制（轨迹资产复用）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Frontier LLM agents perform complex interactive tasks, but their inference costs motivate transferring their experience to smaller open-source agents. Distillation and latent communication typically require target-model training or access to source-model internals. We study how textual frontier-model trajectories can provide reusable local controls for a frozen agent. Inspired by protocol reverse engineering, we introduce $\textit{Agentic Latents}$, comprising anchored states and dynamic profiles of feedback--response cycles elicited by replay in the target model. Our training-free framework, $\textbf{REVEAL}$ ($\textbf{REV}$erse $\textbf{E}$ngineering $\textbf{A}$gentic $\textbf{L}$atents), uses these profiles and trajectory outcomes to match and select local events from successful and failed rollouts of the same task and source model. Their response-transition contrasts yield a target-specific steering direction, applied before each response. Within-benchmark evaluations on Terminal-Bench 2.1 and Humanity's Last Exam with tools report higher accuracy than unmodified agents across three targets. Representation analyses and behavioral case studies support reusing frontier-model experience as local activation control.

</details>

### 250. Strong yet Controllable LLM Abstention without Learning to Abstain

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16318)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`abstention`、`selective answering`、`coverage control`、`decomposition`

👤 **作者**：Junwei Zhang、…、Fei Sun

- 🎯 **研究动机**：模型外选择性回答（阈值控覆盖率）vs 模型内弃答训练（训练后覆盖率固定）——匹配覆盖率公平对比缺失
- 🔬 **研究方法**：风险分解为作答能力与区分能力+TAPS：标准后训练+现成置信估计器的事后选择，保持覆盖可控
- 📌 **结论**：弃答训练的优势主要来自答得更准；TAPS 匹配覆盖率下风险低 1.5-9.4pp 且模块化受益于组件进步——强而可控的弃答基线

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language models (LLMs) can reduce hallucination by abstaining from questions they would likely get wrong. Abstention can be implemented outside the model (selective answering) using a confidence threshold that controls the answer rate (coverage), or learned inside it (abstention training), which can perform better but fixes coverage once trained. To compare the two, we match their coverage and decompose risk (error rate) into answering ability and distinguishing ability, i.e., how well the model answers and how accurately it abstains, respectively. The decomposition shows that abstention training performs better mainly by answering better: with strong confidence estimators, the two have nearly equal distinguishing ability. This motivates Train for Accuracy, then Post-hoc Select (TAPS), a modular approach that keeps coverage controllable, needs only standard post-training and an off-the-shelf confidence estimator, and benefits from advances in either component. Across three LLMs and three factual QA benchmarks, TAPS with a probing-based confidence estimator attains 1.5--9.4 percentage points lower mean risk than three representative abstention training methods at matched coverage. Within TAPS, accuracy training often weakens the model's own confidence signals, yet refitting the estimator largely preserves distinguishing ability. Together, these results establish TAPS as a strong yet controllable baseline and raise the bar for future abstention training: sacrificing control over coverage should yield gains beyond what can be achieved by optimizing answering and abstaining separately.

</details>

### 251. Doctor Trigger: A Framework for Release-Bound Face Verification

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16336)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`face verification`、`provenance`、`publisher`、`watermark`

👤 **作者**：Chia-Ming Lee、Chia-Yu Lin、Hung-Kai Huang、Yi-Ting Ku、Yu-Chen Liang、Chih-Chung Hsu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：换脸与生成编辑使在线流通的人脸脱离发布者监督——溯源/检测/水印/完整性都不建立与特定发布版本的绑定
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Doctor Trigger：发布前嵌入弱键控傅里叶相位信号并存频谱参照，验证时比较流通脸与参照并用键投影测发布特异信号保留
- 📌 **结论**：FF++/DFDC/Celeb-DF 上频谱一致分离换脸与生成操纵、键控信号区分已发布版与未发布原版——发布绑定的人脸验证补充溯源元数据

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Face swapping and generative editing enable realistic facial manipulation, while online redistribution complicates publisher oversight of circulating face images. Existing approaches establish provenance, detect manipulation, recover embedded watermarks, verify image integrity, or disrupt unauthorized face manipulation. These capabilities do not necessarily establish consistency with a specific publisher-enrolled release, limiting the evidence available for verification and human review. To address this gap, we introduce Doctor Trigger, a framework for verifying circulating faces against private publisher-held release records. Before publication, the publisher embeds a weak keyed Fourier-phase signal in each face while preserving visual fidelity and stores its spectral reference. Afterward, verification compares the circulating face with that reference and uses key projection to measure retention of the release-specific signal. Therefore, relation and key evidence jointly help publishers identify suspicious copies for review, potentially reducing the circulation of manipulated images. Experimental results across FF++, DFDC, and Celeb-DF show that spectral agreement separates tested face swaps and generative manipulations from enrolled releases, while the keyed signal distinguishes published versions from their unreleased originals under the evaluated key-preserving benign transformations. Doctor Trigger complements provenance metadata with release-specific face verification and record-linked evidence for further assessment.

</details>

### 252. RoboBugBench: Benchmarking Automated Repair of Real Robot Software Under an Audited Behavioral Oracle

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16369)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`robot software`、`automated repair`、`audited oracle`、`counterfeit patch`

👤 **作者**：Alexey Tuzikov

- 🎯 **研究动机**：语言 agent 越来越多修改机器人软件——能否修复未被测量（机器人缺陷表现为行为而仓库几乎没有测试）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：RoboBugBench：33 个执行验证的真实 ROS 2 导航栈缺陷，补丁按物理仿真驾驶评级；oracle 为构造而非继承故作对抗审计
- 📌 **结论**：三前沿模型修复 24.2-36.4%；难度跟 oracle 而非领域；审计分解出『抑制失败表象而非修复』的伪补丁被出厂规则 11/11 接受而分布规则拒 4——构造 oracle 的可观测性界限

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Language agents increasingly modify robot software, but whether they can repair it is unmeasured, because the domain supplies no oracle to inherit: a robot bug manifests as behavior, and robot repositories ship almost no tests that capture it. We present RoboBugBench, a benchmark of 33 real, execution-validated defects in a pinned ROS 2 navigation stack, in which a candidate patch is graded by compiling it and driving the robot through a physics simulation rather than by running a unit test. Three frontier models, each given 1 attempt per instance, repair 24.2%-36.4%, with no pairwise difference significant; difficulty tracks the oracle rather than the domain, at 66.7% on instances graded by process survival against 22.2% on those graded by what the robot did (instance-level p = 0.0027). Because the oracle is constructed rather than inherited we audit it adversarially, and report the decomposition that audit yields: counterfeit patches which suppress a failure without repairing the defect are accepted by the shipped rule 11/11, while a distributional rule over the same data rejects 4 at no cost in false rejects. The residual is an observability bound (what the observation channel discards, no predicate over it can recover), so discrimination must be bought with instrumentation rather than stricter criteria, and our repair rates are bounds: inflated by what the oracle cannot distinguish, deflated by unanimity's rejection of correct fixes under run-to-run noise. We release the corpus, criterion batteries, counterfeit suite and cached telemetry.

</details>

### 253. Rule vs.Consequence: Dissociable Internal Representations of Moral Reasoning in LLMs

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16371)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`moral reasoning`、`internal representations`、`probing`、`steering`

👤 **作者**：Eugenie Shi

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM 对规则框架与后果框架道德问题的差异化反应是内部结构还是表面词汇敏感未知
- 🔬 **研究方法**：2×2 因子数据集（推理风格×判断极性）+干净重述测试集与冲突对；线性探针与激活转向定位残差流方向
- 📌 **结论**：两个近正交方向（Dir-A 风格/Dir-B 极性，互相关 <0.04）跨三开发者模型可复现且因果有效——道德推理风格的内部结构分离（安全相关推理的可操纵底座）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language models are increasingly deployed in morally and legally sensitive contexts, yet little is known about how they internally represent distinctions between moral reasoning styles. We ask whether a model's differential response to rule-framed and consequence-framed moral questions reflects distinct internal structure or merely surface lexical sensitivity. We construct a 2$\times$2 factorial dataset crossing reasoning style (rule-based vs. consequence-based framing) with judgment polarity (permissive vs. prohibitive), drawn from three established moral reasoning benchmarks and evaluated on a cleanroom test set of novel phrasings that includes conflict pairs where the two framings predict opposite labels. Using linear probing and activation steering, we identify two orthogonal directions in the residual stream: Dir-A tracks reasoning style and Dir-B tracks judgment polarity, each near-blind to the other factor (cross-correlation $|r| < 0.04$). This double dissociation replicates across three instruction-tuned models from different developers at comparable proportional depth, and it is not knife-edged in layer: the directions are recoverable and causally effective throughout an extended band of the network. Dir-A can causally change judgments, and in conflicting scenarios, it selectively corrects errors in rule-based judgments without changing consequence-based judgments, suggesting that it does not simply make the model more likely to accept everything. Generalization beyond the training templates is partial: the direction transfers across benchmark sources, but its advantage over lexical baselines on novel phrasings is modest. We therefore frame the result as moral reasoning style sensitivity, a geometrically dissociable and causally effective structure, rather than internalized normative reasoning.

</details>

### 254. What UI Design Can Do For "Good" Oversight Work: A Three-Month Action Research Study

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16410)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`human oversight`、`UI design`、`action research`、`work quality`

👤 **作者**：Cedric Faas、…、Anna Maria Feit

- 🎯 **研究动机**：AI 自动化后果性任务要求人类监督——监督工作的真实体验（枯燥/影响不明/联结有限）影响『好监督』的设计却缺乏纵向证据
- 🔬 **研究方法**：三个月行动研究跟随 8 位监督 AI 学习助手的导师：12 次周会+两轮工作坊+四次问卷的主题分析与 UI 修改试验
- 📌 **结论**：激发/掌控/联结是关键挑战，界面修改收效不一——真实监督工作的特征与界面可支持边界的纵向记录（Human-AI oversight）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

With AI automating consequential tasks, human oversight is required for ethical, legal, and practical reasons. Yet these oversight tasks can be demanding and often make for poor work. Empirical evidence on overseers' lived work experiences remains underexplored, yet critical for designing ``good'' oversight. We provide longitudinal, in-the-wild insights through action research accompanying (N=8) tutors who oversaw an AI-supported learning assistant over three months. Our thematic analysis combines insights from twelve weekly meetings and two workshop iterations and was enriched by four repeated questionnaires. We identified stimulation, mastery, and relatedness as key challenges, stemming from the task's repetitiveness, unclear impact of interventions, and limited connections with students and peers. Informed by our initial findings, we deployed targeted UI modifications with mixed success. This paper documents the long-term evolution of the work characteristics of a real-world oversight task and discusses which oversight work characteristics can and cannot be supported by interface design.

</details>

### 255. Not Every Cue Is Evidence: Cue Supportability for Forensic Distillation in Face Forgery Detection

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16452)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`face forgery`、`explainability`、`evidence faithfulness`、`distillation`

👤 **作者**：Binjia Zhou、…、Yijun Bei

- 🎯 **研究动机**：可解释伪造检测需要输入图像可支撑的取证证据——有限质量分辨率下细粒度线索不可观察，特权监督教学生说出其感知不到的证据
- 🔬 **研究方法**：双支撑性审计在蒸馏前过滤特权线索：候选线索须可从其余特权上下文预测且因果关联当前图像高归因区域
- 📌 **结论**：八基准上维持或提升检测精度并显著提升解释忠实度——『答案对证据错』（CAWE）失效与线索支撑性过滤

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Explainable face forgery detection requires not only correct judgments but also forensic evidence supported by the input image. However, training cues are typically derived from the forgery generation pipeline. Since images available to the model are of limited quality and resolution, it is difficult to observe the fine-grained traces these cues refer to. Training methods that further supply such cues as privileged supervision exacerbate the problem, as the student is taught to articulate evidence it cannot perceive. The student consequently resorts to templated or over-generalized expressions, giving rise to the correct-answer, wrong-evidence (CAWE) failure. We propose a dual supportability audit that filters privileged cues before distillation. Each candidate cue must be predictable from the remaining privileged context and causally tied to high-attribution visual regions in the current image. Only cues satisfying both conditions receive substantial distillation weight, preventing unsupported details from leaking into the student. Experiments across eight benchmarks show that our method maintains or improves detection accuracy while significantly raising explanation faithfulness compared to existing paradigms.

</details>

### 256. Drafting Agents, Not Just Tokens: Speculative Recursive Self-Improvement for Agentic Systems

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16491)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`recursive self-improvement`、`speculative execution`、`tree search`、`verification`

👤 **作者**：Xin Xu、…、Zhouchen Lin

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agent 逐步修订需在有限预算下平衡探索与评估——窄搜索漏有用编辑序列、全评估耗尽预算
- 🔬 **研究方法**：SpecRSI：前代完全评估前起草可执行后继+采用前完整验证；VATS 按预测已验证回报分配预算；GVO 从完成搜索学改进器而真实评估器固定
- 📌 **结论**：主科学对比终局增益超 Janus 33.3-40.0%、50 个骨干-任务对全胜——投机式自改进的预算分配（RSI 工程化）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Improving an agent through successive revisions requires balancing exploration and evaluation under a limited budget. A narrow search can miss useful sequences of edits, while fully evaluating every intermediate version can exhaust the budget before those sequences yield a verified improvement. We introduce Speculative Recursive Self-Improvement (SpecRSI), which drafts executable successor agents before their predecessors are fully evaluated, while requiring complete verification before adoption. Verification-Aware Tree Search (VATS) allocates the budget between further edits and outstanding checks using the predicted verified return of a continuation. Group Verification Optimization (GVO) learns the improver from completed searches while keeping the real evaluator fixed. In the main scientific comparison, terminal gains exceed Janus by 33.3%--40.0%. Additional evaluations show that SpecRSI outperforms Janus in all 50 scientific backbone--task pairs and exceeds MetaRSI-v1 by 1.85%--2.38% in macro-average system score across twenty backbones. Coding-validation success reaches 69.4% versus HGM's 64.3% at a 20.8% lower target-cost index.

</details>

### 257. From Caricature to Collective Artifact: How Persona Construction Drives Polarization in Generative Social Simulations

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16498)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`generative social simulation`、`persona`、`polarization`、`validity`

👤 **作者**：Dimitris Michailidis、Petter Törnberg、Fernando P. Santos

- 🎯 **研究动机**：GSS 的 persona 被压缩为刻板漫画——这些漫画如何经交互演化为集体产物（如极化）缺乏系统研究
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Twitter 式 GSS 平台+ANES 派生 persona，消融情感极化标记/党派认同/投票选择三层党派身份
- 📌 **结论**：删少量显式党派陈述即从近完全隔离转为接近真实 Twitter 的跨党连接；匹配单一结果不等于行为效度——persona 构建的评估清单（仿真社会风险的效度基础）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Generative Social Simulation (GSS) leverages Large Language Models (LLMs) to create sophisticated personas that define agents with demographics, emotions, and political opinions. While prior work shows that personas compress outputs into stereotypical caricatures, there is no comprehensive study of how these caricatures evolve into collective artifacts when they interact. Using a Twitter-like GSS platform with personas derived from the American National Elections Study (ANES), we ablate three layers of partisan identity: affective polarization markers, party identification, and vote choice, while retaining demographics, ideological positioning and important issues. We find that collective outcomes depend heavily on how survey responses are translated into personas. Removing a few explicitly partisan statements moves the network from near-complete segregation to cross-party ties approaching those of real Twitter networks. We argue that matching one outcome is not evidence for behavioral validity and point to salience as a possible explanation. Surveys ask for a position on every question regardless of its importance to the respondent, and persona construction turns each answer into a direct behavioral instruction. We conclude with a checklist for more comprehensive evaluation of personas in future works.

</details>

### 258. Intent-Triggered Anchoring for Multi-Token Format Units in Parallel Decoding of Diffusion Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16516)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`dLLM`、`parallel decoding`、`format units`、`medical report`

👤 **作者**：Junhyeok Lee、Kyu Sung Choi

- 🎯 **研究动机**：dLLM 并行解码每步提交多 token——同提交 token 互相不可见，破坏解析器所需的多 token 格式单元（CXR 报告 32.7-36.7% 缺可解析 Impression）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：意图触发锚定：免训练解码规则在模型提交格式单元首 token 后补全（只插入有唯一无内容补全的单元），停止/循环时回退直写
- 📌 **结论**：三 CXR 评测集可读率 63.3-67.3%→100%，五 dLLM 数学答案标记准确率 +5.8-20.8 点——dLLM 并行解码的结构性失效与修复

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

In parallel decoding, diffusion language models commit several tokens per forward pass, so co-committed tokens cannot see each other, breaking multi-token format units that parsers need. On ECHO, an open-weight one-step chest X-ray (CXR) report generator, 32.7-36.7% of evaluation reports lack a parseable Impression heading and score as empty. We propose intent-triggered anchoring, a training-free decoding rule that completes a format unit once the model commits its first token, inserting only units with a unique, content-free completion; fallbacks write the unit when the model stops or loops without starting it. On three CXR evaluation sets, the rule raises readable rates from 63.3-67.3% to 100.0% with no regressions and a 95th-percentile latency ratio of 0.68-1.28 against official decoding. Broken headings state findings absent from the reference; adding the heading at the end leaves them in 15.5-31.0% of reports, our rule in at most 0.6%. For math answer markers, the rule raises accuracy in the most parallel setting of five diffusion language models by 5.8-20.8 points on GSM8K and 5.1-17.1 on GSM-Symbolic, held out from tuning. With lenient answer extraction, gains are 3.3-12.5 and 2.7-6.3 points; the rule adds 1.4-8.6 points over a stop-only fallback for four of the five models.

</details>

### 259. Trust-Calibrated VLA Policy Post-Training with Evolving Imagination

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16517)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`VLA`、`trust calibration`、`world model`、`RL post-training`

👤 **作者**：Wenzhan Li、Yiran Qin、Heng Zhou、Huirui Wang、Yulan Guo、Ruimao Zhang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：VLA 的 RL 后训练存在可靠性-效率张力：无模型精修交互密集、有模型可利用不可靠想象
- 🔬 **研究方法**：MERL：在线更新的世界模型+奖励代理头组成仿真器，仿真器可靠性控制想象数据比与 rollout 时域、块级信任控制重放选择，残差预测器免查询未来真值估计信任
- 📌 **结论**：扰动 LIBERO-PRO 均分 79.7%、高阈值学习提速 10.9%、两真机任务 80.0%——信任校准的 VLA 后训练（想象可靠性的显式治理）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are often pretrained via imitation learning, making reinforcement learning (RL) post-training important when deployment moves beyond demonstration coverage. Existing refinement paradigms expose a reliability--efficiency tension: model-free refinement avoids simulator bias but is interaction-intensive, whereas model-based refinement can exploit unreliable imagination even as its simulator adapts. We propose Model-Evolving Reinforcement Learning (MERL), a trust-calibrated framework that couples policy optimization with an online-updated simulator comprising a world model and a reward proxy head. Across stages, simulator reliability controls the imagined-data ratio and rollout horizon; within rollouts, chunk-level trust controls replay selection and optimization weight. For recursive imagination without exact grounded continuations, a residual predictor estimates trust without querying future ground truth. On perturbed LIBERO-PRO tasks, MERL reaches 79.7% average success, improves high-threshold learning speed by 10.9% over the matched model-free control, and outperforms same-protocol external baselines across three training seeds. Fixed-data adaptation from 50 demonstrations per task achieves 80.0% average success on two physical-robot tasks.

</details>

### 260. Does a Hosted Decision Model Respect Irrelevant Alternatives? A Pre-Registered Test of Option-Set Dependence in Jev

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16655)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`IIA`、`decision model`、`preregistered`、`API validity`

👤 **作者**：Dushyant Rajput、Nirdesh Chauhan、Siddharth Kosaraju

- 🎯 **研究动机**：决策 API 返回的选项概率被当作选项自身属性——只有满足无关替代品独立性（IIA）时该解读才成立
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Jev 1.13.0 预注册研究：299,463 次调用（意图/主题分类），操纵添加选项测金标签与最强错标签的对数几率移动
- 📌 **结论**：每个添加选项都移动对数几率（RMS 0.20-0.52 nats、达运行间变异 1.2-3 倍），畸变随选项数对数增长至 ~1 nat——托管决策模型的选项集依赖性

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Decision APIs that return a probability for each option are routinely used as if those probabilities were properties of the options themselves. That reading is valid only if the model satisfies independence of irrelevant alternatives (IIA): adding an option that is neither the right answer nor its strongest rival should leave the odds between those two unchanged. We test this in a pre-registered study of jev-1.13.0, a hosted decision model, using 299,463 calls on intent classification (BANKING77, CLINC150) and topic classification (AG News). Every added option moves the log-odds between the gold label and the strongest wrong label: the root-mean-square systematic shift is 0.20-0.52 nats, 1.2 to 3 times the run-to-run variation, even when the added option takes at most 0.1% of the probability mass. The shifts are directional. An option similar to the wrong answer draws probability from it (+0.10 nats toward the gold label). A paraphrase of the wrong answer produces an intermediate pattern: the gold label's probability is almost conserved, but the paraphrase receives only about a third of the wrong answer's share rather than the half that strict substitution implies. The distortion grows steadily with the logarithm of the number of options, reaching about 1 nat at 255 options, and off-domain padding pushes the odds against the gold label. In contrast, questions packed into one call do not interact (bias < 0.001). Scoring each option with an independent yes/no question removes the option-set dependence but picks the right answer 8.7 points less often. Jev sits between two local baselines, an option-independent scorer and a prompt readout. Its probabilities are best read as relative to the option list they were computed over.

</details>

### 261. Lost in Contrast: Instruction-Preserving Decoding for Reliable Visual Question Answering

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16722)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`contrastive decoding`、`abstention`、`VQA`、`decision utility`

👤 **作者**：Haonan Zhang、Ting Jin、Wenzhong Tang、Shuai Wang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：视觉对比解码用原图与参照条件的预测差纠错——同一对比会把有依据的弃答变成错误答案
- 🔬 **研究方法**：指令保持解码：保持弃答指令对原图预测的作用完整、对比强度仍可调；IP-VCD/IP-M3ID 两实现+决策效用（同时认可正确答案与有据弃答）评测
- 📌 **结论**：九模型 VizWiz 全部超越非 IP 基线——对比解码与弃答保持的解耦（可靠 VQA 的解码层修复）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Visual contrastive decoding corrects answers using the prediction difference between the original image and a reference condition, yet the same contrast also turns supported abstentions into incorrect answers. We find that abstentions are lost at two stages: contrastive decoding with candidate filtering can exclude the abstention token before any comparison takes place, and the reference branch's response to the abstention instruction is removed when the reference prediction is subtracted. We therefore propose instruction-preserving decoding, which keeps the effect of the abstention instruction on the original-image prediction intact while leaving the contrast strength freely adjustable. Using a perturbed image and an image-free prediction as references, we obtain IP-VCD and IP-M3ID. On decision utility (DU), which credits both correct answers and supported abstentions, both methods outperform all compared non-IP baselines on every one of nine models on VizWiz, and IP-VCD also leads on eight of nine models on Food-101. Candidate measurements and paired per-question comparisons show that computing the instruction effect and the visual contrast term separately lets contrastive decoding support both correct answers and supported abstentions.

</details>

### 262. Trace-to-Architecture: Capability Bottleneck Diagnosis for Harness Revision

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16725)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`harness revision`、`failure diagnosis`、`execution evidence`、`agent engineering`

👤 **作者**：Wenfeng Wang、…、Jing Li

- 🎯 **研究动机**：改进 agent 须知道失败时改什么——相似失败需不同修复、解决一个问题可暴露另一个
- 🔬 **研究方法**：T2A：开发 agent 用成败任务的执行证据诊断失效、修订对应 harness 组件、检查目标任务改进/成功任务保持/编辑实际触发，新证据引导下一轮
- 📌 **结论**：ALFWorld/ScienceWorld/AppWorld 三任务 agent 最高总分（超最强基线 6.2-30.3 点）——执行证据驱动的 harness 定位修订

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Improving an LLM agent requires knowing what to change when it fails. Similar failures can require different fixes, and resolving one problem can expose another. We introduce Trace-to-Architecture (T2A), a framework that uses execution trajectories to determine what to change in an agent's harness and when those changes apply. A development agent diagnoses failures using execution evidence from successful and failed tasks, revises the corresponding harness components, and then checks whether target tasks improve, selected successful tasks remain successful, and the edits are actually triggered. New execution evidence guides subsequent diagnosis and revision. Across ALFWorld, ScienceWorld, and AppWorld, T2A achieves the highest aggregate score for all three evaluated task agents, exceeding their respective strongest baselines by 6.2--30.3 points. We also integrate T2A's diagnosis and validation procedures into Meta-Harness, improving the resulting harnesses' mean ScienceWorld score by 21.6 points from the same starting harness.

</details>

### 263. The Multilingual Artificial Hivemind: Do All LLMs Think Alike in Every Language?

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16755)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`homogenization`、`multilingual`、`diversity collapse`、`LLM output`

👤 **作者**：Xaver Raphael Davey、Shubhra Mishra、Anton Adelöw、Siyao Liu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：英语 LLM 生成的模型内/模型间塌缩已知——同质化是否延伸到其他语言及是否匹配人类模式未知
- 🔬 **研究方法**：23 开源模型×13 语言的输出多样性测量：三个嵌入空间+无嵌入词汇度量排除测量伪影，与 held-out 翻译能力关联
- 📌 **结论**：人工蜂群效应普遍但不均，同质化缺口与语言能力稳健相关——多语言性能-多样性权衡（内容生态多样性风险）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Text generated by Large Language Models (LLMs) often remains limited in diversity compared to that produced by humans. Recent advances demonstrate the presence of inter- and intra-model collapse in open-ended generation of English text, raising concerns about the long-term homogenization of LLM, and more importantly, human thought. However, our understanding of whether this homogenization extends to languages beyond English is limited. In this work, we investigate the role of language on output diversity. Specifically, we aim to understand whether multilingual LLM-generated text is also homogeneous, and whether this homogeneity matches what we observe in humans. Across 23 open-weight models and 13 languages, we find that the artificial hivemind effect is pervasive but uneven in its extent. The intra-model effect holds in three different embedding spaces and under a lexical metric that uses no embedding model, so it is not an artifact of how diversity is measured. The size of the homogenization gap is associated with a model's competence in a language, measured on a held-out translation benchmark, robustly across embedding spaces. Thus, we demonstrate a performance-diversity tradeoff in multilingual LLMs.

</details>

### 264. Unsupervised Data Drift Monitoring for Deployed Deepfake Speech Detectors

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16826)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`deepfake speech`、`drift monitoring`、`deployment reliability`、`unsupervised`

👤 **作者**：Lukáš Prokeš、Vojtěch Staněk、Anton Firc、Kamil Malinka

- 🎯 **研究动机**：部署中的深伪语音检测器面对演化的攻击/编解码/说话人/录音条件且无标签——何时可信赖未知
- 🔬 **研究方法**：检测器内部嵌入上的无监督漂移监视器：正则 Mahalanobis 距离对 bona fide 与欺骗参照比较，划分可信/歧义/新颖区域并路由复核
- 📌 **结论**：对数据集失配告警并大幅降低接受深伪的安全关键案例（代价是自动覆盖率下降）；漂移双向影响两类——部署检测器的可靠性问题化

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Deepfake speech detectors are typically evaluated as binary classifiers on fixed benchmarks, but deployed systems face evolving attacks, codecs, speakers, and recording conditions, with labels unavailable during operation. This makes it difficult to know when a detector operates in familiar conditions and can be relied on. We propose an unsupervised drift monitor that operates on the internal embeddings of a deployed detector. The monitor compares each input against bona fide and spoof references using regularized Mahalanobis distances. These distances define trusted, ambiguous, and novel regions of the detector embedding space, allowing unfamiliar inputs to be routed for review. We evaluate the monitor on four modern detectors on three datasets. The monitor raises alerts for dataset mismatches and substantially reduces the security-critical case of accepting deepfakes at the cost of reduced automatic coverage. The results also show that drift affects both classes: bona fide recordings can move away from the training reference, increasing alert rates even in in-domain settings. Our findings suggest that deployed deepfake speech detectors should be treated not only as a binary classification, but also as a reliability problem.

</details>

### 265. Beyond User-Side Defenses: Image Protection in Open-Weight Diffusion Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16835)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`image protection`、`open-weight diffusion`、`tamper resistance`、`personalization`

👤 **作者**：Jihye Kang、Jiwon Park、Namhyuk Ahn

- 🎯 **研究动机**：图像保护多在用户侧——开源权重部署下模型提供方还能做什么未被系统化
- 🔬 **研究方法**：四要求框架（保护效力/干净保真/兼容性/抗篡改）+长时域攻击模拟强化对保护移除的抵抗
- 📌 **结论**：单一保护工具在提升抗再对齐代价下损伤干净保真——模型侧图像保护的研究议程与初步权衡（开源生态的防护责任）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Model providers can support image protection, but open-weight deployment introduces practical constraints. We propose four requirements: protection effectiveness, clean fidelity, compatibility, and tamper resistance. We incorporate long-horizon attack simulations to strengthen resistance to protection removal. Experiments with a single protection tool show improved resistance to re-alignment at a cost to clean fidelity, motivating further research toward practical model-side protection.

</details>

### 266. Label Collapse in Diffusion Language Model In-Context Learning

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16839)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`dLLM`、`in-context learning`、`label collapse`、`evaluation validity`

👤 **作者**：Ziye Guo、Ye Yuan

- 🎯 **研究动机**：标签排列与模型偏差的交互未被系统检验——定义标签塌缩（生成与查询无关的标签）并考察其普遍性
- 🔬 **研究方法**：五模型跨自回归（Llama-3/Qwen3.5）与离散扩散（LLaDA/iLLaDA/LLaDA-Instruct）族，类平衡交替排列下测量+分离度（查询位两标签词对数几率）诊断与防 tokenizer 伪影协议
- 📌 **结论**：8-9B 规模架构普遍：准确率近随机却生成查询无关标签且表面分数不可分辨——AR 与 dLLM 共有的隐蔽 ICL 失效模式

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

In-context learning (ICL) applies large language models to new tasks by prepending a few labeled demonstrations to a query. Prior work has studied how demonstration ordering affects ICL, but the interaction between label arrangement patterns and model biases has not been systematically examined. We define label collapse as the failure mode in which the model generates query-independent labels, and show that it is architecturally universal at the tested 8-9B scale: five models spanning autoregressive (Llama-3, Qwen3.5) and discrete diffusion (LLaDA, iLLaDA, LLaDA-Instruct) families all exhibit label collapse even under the class-balanced alternating arrangement, with some label word pairs collapsing in virtually every tested condition and others flipping their collapse direction with arrangement. Critically, accuracy alone cannot detect this collapse: models can achieve near-chance accuracy while generating query-independent labels, a score indistinguishable from balanced performance, so the failure can persist undetected in deployment. We measure separation, the log-odds of the two label words at the query position (the quantity that label-bias calibration operates on), as a complementary diagnostic that exposes collapse hidden beneath surface accuracy, together with a measurement protocol that guards label extraction against tokenizer and formatting artifacts. For autoregressive models, collapse persists where the majority-label-bias account does not apply, and arrangement patterns can reverse the collapse direction; for diffusion models, we present a systematic evaluation of ICL failure modes, complementing contemporaneous studies of dLLM context comprehension and demonstration-order sensitivity, and identify open questions about how iterative denoising interacts with label biases.

</details>

### 267. EviStress: Evidence Stress Learning for Generalizable Face Forgery Detection with Textual Explanations

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16879)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`face forgery`、`generalization`、`textual explanation`、`evidence stress`

👤 **作者**：Jiuyao Jing、Xuanyu Yi、Yu Zheng、Chunlei Peng

- 🎯 **研究动机**：网络人脸伪造来自演化中的生成管线并经传播处理——未见操纵与视觉变化下的可靠鉴伪困难；CLIP 检测器证据依赖高度不均
- 🔬 **研究方法**：EviStress：证据定向块扰动（全局/关系/语义线索估计依赖度并正相关扰动概率）+判别变化保持+内在证据指令调优迁移 LLaVA 出文本解释
- 📌 **结论**：高依赖视觉证据的训练期压力测试提升泛化并生成解释——伪造检测的证据鲁棒化与可解释输出

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

The rapid spread of face forgeries on the Web challenges the credibility of visual content. In practical Web environments, manipulated media originate from evolving generation pipelines and undergo diverse processing during dissemination, making reliable authenticity assessment under unseen manipulations and visual changes a critical challenge. We observe that a standard LoRA-adapted CLIP detector exhibits uneven patch-level decision dependence, concen trating a disproportionate share of class-specific relevance on a small fraction of visual patches. To improve robustness to variations in such highly dependent evidence, we propose EviStress, a two-stage framework for generalizable face forgery detection with textual explanations. In the first stage, Evidence-Targeted Patch Perturbation (ETPP) estimates patch dependence from global, relational, and semantic cues and assigns positively correlated perturbation probabilities, explicitly stress-testing highly dependent visual evidence during training. We further introduce Discriminative Variation Preservation (DVP), which constrains the residual between clean and perturbed representations to retain authenticity discriminative information. In the second stage, Intrinsic Evidence Instruction Tuning (IEIT) transfers the adapted CLIP encoder into LLaVA as its vision tower and performs parameter-efficient multimodal instruction tuning, enabling authenticity prediction and textual explanation directly from the learned forgery-sensitive representation without an external detector. Experiments demonstrate improved cross-dataset and cross-method generalization and corruption robustness, with the multimodal model providing authenticity predictions and human-readable explanations.

</details>

### 268. Acceptable Misalignment

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16881)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`value alignment`、`acceptable misalignment`、`open-ended learning`、`position paper`

👤 **作者**：Maud van Lier、…、Richard Duro

- 🎯 **研究动机**：开放学习的自主机器人在设计时未知部署/学习/行为——完全对齐理论与实践皆不可能，错位事件将持续发生
- 🔬 **研究方法**：立场论文：从对齐最大化转向『错位事件何时可接受』的条件化视角，给出可接受错位的规范要求与机制含义
- 📌 **结论**：剩余错位风险的社会可接受性应成为设计变量——对齐方法的边界与治理转向（残余风险框架）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

There is a specific challenge designers face in developing autonomous robots capable of open-ended learning. This challenge is how one can develop such systems in an ethically sound way when there are many unknowns during design time about their manner of deployment, what they can and will learn, and how these systems will act on the basis of what they have learned. One response to this challenge is to align, as far as possible, the activities and goals of autonomous robots with human values, raising the question of how to make this alignment possible, a question known as the value alignment problem. We argue that the value alignment problem faces a number of challenges that show that full alignment is both theoretically and practically impossible, meaning that misalignment incidents will keep occurring. This undermines current alignment approaches, as the attempt to maximize alignment cannot properly deal with the possibility that the risk of misalignment that remains after alignment methods have been implemented might still be unacceptable to society. To overcome this difficulty, we argue that a shift in perspective is required from a focus on the maximization of alignment to the conditions under which misalignment incidents become acceptable. In this paper, we explore what this shift in perspective entails. We introduce the concept of acceptable misalignment, formulate normative requirements for alignment mechanisms aimed at bringing misalignment incidents to an acceptable level, and we conclude by presenting a new methodological framework that consists of alignment mechanisms that can meet these requirements.

</details>

### 269. Transparency as Answerability: Role-Dependent Information Flow Modeling

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16887)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`transparency`、`answerability`、`information flow`、`governance`

👤 **作者**：Mattias Brännström、Themis Dimitra Xanthopoulou、Lili Jiang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：AI 决策系统的透明性被广泛要求却定义含混——系统/组件属性式评估跨组织不迁移且答不了干系人问题
- 🔬 **研究方法**：透明性即关系：信息流网络上形式化『合法问题可从结构被回答』；结构/组合/角色三维可答性+公共部门招聘工具的真实预集成评估
- 📌 **结论**：跨 Provider-Deployer 边界的角色依赖透明问题可被形式化陈述与分析——透明性的可计算定义与真实案例（治理机制）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Transparency in AI-supported decision systems is widely required but poorly defined. Existing approaches treat transparency as a property of systems or components, producing characteristic failures: assessments that do not transfer across organizational boundaries, stakeholder questions that cannot be formulated within the available frame of analysis, and component-level findings that say nothing about system-level behavior. We propose treating transparency as a relation: a system is transparent to a stakeholder to the degree that their legitimate questions are answerable from its structure. We formalize this using information flow networks, in which transparency questions are expressed in terms of network structure and answerability is the condition that the system settles them. The framework supports three dimensions of analysis: structural answerability from the network alone, compositional answerability across organizational boundaries for deployed systems, and role-dependent answerability given a stakeholder's accessible sub-network. We demonstrate the framework on a real pre-integration assessment of an AI recruitment tool in a public sector municipality. The assessment uses participatory design and interviews to model the decision system structure. We show how structural and role-dependent transparency questions can be formally stated and analyzed across the Provider and Deployer boundary where the AI system is composed together with the deployer's decision process.

</details>

### 270. Transparent Information Flow Modeling for Role-Aware AI Act Assessment

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16916)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`AI Act`、`compliance`、`information flow`、`accountability`

👤 **作者**：Mattias Brännström、Themis Dimitra Xanthopoulou、Lili Jiang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：EU AI Act 的高风险系统治理强调透明/风险管理/角色问责——现行评估依赖整体视图或割裂清单，捕获不了社会技术复杂性
- 🔬 **研究方法**：信息流方法论 IFM：决策系统建模为信息位点与转换通道网络，结构化分析信息/决策/责任跨组织与技术边界的流动；公共部门招聘工具真实案例
- 📌 **结论**：IFM 桥接组件与整体视角、识别风险路径并支持意图-实际使用的对齐分析——超越清单式 AI Act 合规评估

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

The European Union's Artificial Intelligence Act introduces a structured framework for governing high-risk AI systems, emphasizing transparency, risk management, and role-specific accountability. However, current assessment practices often rely on monolithic system views or disconnected checklists, limiting their ability to capture the socio-technical complexity of real-world deployments. We explore the use of the Information Flow Methodology (IFM), a modeling-based approach to AI Act compliance assessment. IFM represents decision systems -- whether algorithmic, human, or hybrid -- as networks of information sites and transformation channels, enabling structured analysis of how information, decisions, and responsibilities flow across organizational and technical boundaries. We apply IFM in a real-world case involving a recruitment support tool used by a public-sector organization, evaluating both the Provider's system and the Deployer's recruitment process. Our results demonstrate that IFM can serve as a scaffold to bridge both component-based and holistic perspectives, and how technical systems are situated within socio-technical contexts. In doing so, IFM supports transparency, identifies risk pathways, and enables alignment analysis between intended and actual use. We argue that such modeling provides a foundational tool for effective, context-sensitive AI governance and accountability -- offering capabilities beyond conventional checklist-based assessments.

</details>

### 271. Lexical Redefinition Reveals Context-Sensitive Instruction Following

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16952)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`instruction following`、`lexical redefinition`、`context sensitivity`、`behavioral paradigm`

👤 **作者**：Roksana Goworek、Haim Dubossarsky

- 🎯 **研究动机**：模型如何调和提示内的冲突上下文证据——显式指令的解读是否被后续使用证据改变
- 🔬 **研究方法**：词汇重定义范式：显式指令规定词义+后续句子经用法提供证据，870 词对上系统测量
- 📌 **结论**：重定义不被当固定规则：上下文用法可保持/削弱/完全反转指令——指令-证据整合的受控行为范式（指令层脆弱性研究工具）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

How do language models reconcile conflicting contextual evidence within a prompt? We study this question using lexical redefinition, where an explicit instruction specifies how a word should be interpreted and a subsequent sentence provides additional evidence through its usage. Across 870 word pairs, we find that lexical redefinitions are not applied as fixed rules. Instead, the behaviour depends systematically on the relationship between the explicit instruction and the intervening sentence: the contextualised usage can preserve, weaken, or completely reverse the instruction. These results suggest that the interpretation of an explicit instruction remains sensitive to subsequent contextual evidence. Lexical redefinition therefore provides a controlled behavioural paradigm for studying how language models integrate multiple sources of information within a prompt.

</details>

### 272. SarcasTraps: Effect of Sarcasm on Turkish Hate Speech Detection

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16957)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`hate speech detection`、`sarcasm`、`Turkish`、`moderation robustness`

👤 **作者**：Niyazi Ahmet Metin、Dilara Keküllüoğlu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：仇恨言论数据集偏重显式有害语言——反讽经嘲讽表扬/夸张/幽默/表面礼貌间接传达伤害
- 🔬 **研究方法**：SarcasTraps：479 条仇恨×反讽双维标注的外部测试集，BERTurk/XLM-R 微调检测器与 GPT-4.1/Llama-3.3-70B 零样本评测
- 📌 **结论**：微调编码器在讽刺仇恨上掉召回、零样本 LLM 保召回但精度大跌——反讽包装的间接伤害是双类检测器的共同盲区

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Standard hate speech detection datasets often emphasize explicit harmful language, but directed harm can also be expressed indirectly, without clearly hateful wording. Sarcasm is one such form of indirect expression, as it can convey harmful intent through mock praise, exaggeration, humor, or apparent politeness. In this paper, we introduce SarcasTraps, a targeted external test set for analyzing the effect of sarcasm on Turkish hate speech detection. SarcasTraps contains 479 entries annotated along two dimensions: hate speech and sarcasm. We train classifiers using BERTurk and XLM-RoBERTa on two existing Turkish hate speech datasets and evaluate them on SarcasTraps, using it strictly as an external test set. We also evaluate GPT-4.1 and Llama-3.3-70B in a zero-shot setting on SarcasTraps. Results show that sarcasm substantially affects hate speech detection. Fine-tuned encoder models lose recall on sarcastic hate, while zero-shot LLMs maintain higher recall but suffer substantial precision drops on sarcastic examples. These findings show that both fine-tuned hate speech detectors and zero-shot LLMs struggle when sarcasm is involved, and SarcasTraps provides quantitative evidence of this challenge through a targeted Turkish test set. Due to the presence of sensitive and hateful language, SarcasTraps will be shared upon request for academic research purposes. Warning: This paper has examples from the dataset which contains offensive and hateful content towards groups of people.

</details>

### 273. Glasseek: A Transparent Recipe for Training and Trustworthy Evaluation of DeepSearch Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16998)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`deep search agent`、`transparency`、`test-set leakage`、`evaluation validity`

👤 **作者**：Cong Pang、…、Xin Lou

- 🎯 **研究动机**：开源深搜 agent 开发三大瓶颈：轨迹构造管线不透明难审计、上下文压缩致训练-推理失配、评估中 agent 可从开放网检索基准内容造成污染虚高
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Glasseek 全透明框架：可验证轨迹策展管线+CM-GSPO（同一策略搜索与摘要、只优化执行子轨迹跨压缩边界训练）+首个开放网 agent 测试集泄漏系统审计与模块化防泄漏插件
- 📌 **结论**：严格协议下 9B agent 在多数深研基准超前沿专有 agent——透明配方+防泄漏评估（评测有效性的工程化）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Deep search enables language agents to solve complex information-seeking tasks through iterative web exploration. However, developing robust open-source agents remains challenging for three reasons. First, opaque trajectory-construction pipelines make training data difficult to audit and reproduce. Second, context compression can create a substantial mismatch between training and inference. Third, agents may retrieve benchmark content from the open web during evaluation, contaminating the results and overstating their performance. To overcome these bottlenecks, we introduce Glasseek, a fully transparent deep search framework spanning the complete development lifecycle, releasing all data, model weights, training recipes, and code. Specifically, for data construction, we establish a verifiable trajectory curation pipeline that systematically generates high-quality exploration paths. For training, we propose Compaction-Managed GSPO (CM-GSPO), which uses the same policy for search and summarization while optimizing only execution sub-trajectories, enabling end-to-end search-policy training across context-compaction boundaries. For evaluation, we conduct the first systematic audit of test-set leakage in open-web agents and release a modular anti-leakage plugin that seamlessly integrates into existing evaluation harnesses. Under this rigorous evaluation protocol, our 9B agent outperforms frontier proprietary agents across almost all deep research benchmarks spanning diverse task types, and achieves the best overall performance among recent open-weight agents, setting a new state of the art at the 9B scale.

</details>

### 274. Governing Copyright Responsibly in the Era of Generative AI

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17002)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`copyright`、`governance`、`generative AI`、`policy analysis`

👤 **作者**：D. Kochetkov

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM 训练语料可能含学术作品而作者缺乏作品级使用信息——版权治理如何框定并操作化对作者的保护
- 🔬 **研究方法**：40 项立法/司法/软法工具（2019-2026）按 RRI 理论框架演绎编码，1,337 类赋值；欧美为主英中与国际组织对照
- 📌 **结论**：两大法域都以程序而非权利框定保护、语料无一般性作者署名义务；从编码缺口导出六工具治理设计与能力本位责任分配——生成式 AI 版权治理的实证政策分析

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language models are trained on collections that may include scholarly works, while authors generally lack work-level information about whether and how their works were used. This paper asks how copyright governance frames and operationalises the protection of such authors, and what a responsible alternative would require. Forty legislative, judicial and soft-law instruments issued between 2019 and 2026 were coded deductively against a framework derived from Responsible Research and Innovation theory, yielding 1,337 category assignments across 835 segments. The European Union and the United States are the primary cases; the United Kingdom, China and international bodies serve as comparators. Normative orientation and the distribution of responsibility dominate the corpus, while reflexivity is the least represented category and reaches its minimum in the US sub-corpus. Both primary jurisdictions frame protection procedurally rather than as an entitlement, and no instrument in the corpus creates a general author-facing attribution duty for the use of works in AI training. From the coded gaps, the paper derives a six-instrument governance design and a capacity-based allocation of responsibility among developers, publishers, collecting societies, funders, institutions, regulators, and individual authors.

</details>

### 275. LLM Evaluation is a Forecasting Problem

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17018)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`evaluation`、`non-stationarity`、`forecasting`、`model selection`

👤 **作者**：Rahul Thomas、Adam Block、Micah Goldblum

- 🎯 **研究动机**：随机分割模拟 IID 抽样——实践中模型训练于过去部署于未来，分布随时间漂移
- 🔬 **研究方法**：网络文本非平稳性测量+跨时间性能连续变化观察+时间序列预测器 vs 历史性能的部署选择对照
- 📌 **结论**：未来性能由近期评测与历史性能的时序预测最好预测——时间应作为 LLM 评测的基本变量（评测有效性的时间维度）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Standard procedure in machine learning is to randomly split train and test data, emulating IID draws from one underlying data distribution. In practice, models are trained on past data and deployed in the future, which may come from distinct distributions. In this paper, we find that web text is indeed highly non-stationary, as text distributions shift over time. We further observe that model performance changes continuously over time across diverse benchmarks. Consequently, future model performance is better predicted by measurements made on recent evaluation data, and is best predicted by time series forecasters applied to historic performance rather than by historic performance itself. Choosing which model to deploy based on forecasted accuracy enables more reliable selection of performant models, thus improving downstream accuracy in the regime practitioners care about: deployment. Taken together, our experiments suggest that time should be treated as a fundamental variable in LLM evaluation. We release our code and data.

</details>

### 276. Superintelligence and the Triple Risk of an AI Race

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17085)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`AI race`、`superintelligence`、`governance`、`position paper`

👤 **作者**：Matthew Rendall、Max Hellrigel-Holderbaum、Leonard Dung

- 🎯 **研究动机**：AGI 可能数十年内出现——先发者全球主导的叙事有引发中美激烈竞争之虞
- 🔬 **研究方法**：三重风险论证：被超越/触发战争/失去对未来的控制；对照历史军备竞赛与当前政策表态
- 📌 **结论**：竞逐 AGI 争先恐后带来安全实践与监管的逐底竞赛；美国应优先单边与合作的安全改进——AI 竞赛动力学的政策分析

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

On many estimates, artificial general intelligence (AGI) is likely to emerge within the next few decades or even sooner.
