Once it does, it may quickly surpass human intelligence, possibly by a huge margin. Some believe that the country that develops AGI first could attain global dominance. This prospect risks setting off intense competition between the United States and China. Indeed, a narrative has already developed that the superpowers are locked in an AI race, with the Trump administration proclaiming its determination to 'dismantle unnecessary regulatory barriers' to winning it. We argue that this approach presents three profound dangers. The first two are familiar from past arms races: being overtaken by a rival or triggering a war. The third is new: losing control of human beings' future to agents far more intelligent than we are, which could endanger our very survival. Striving to get to AGI first risks a race to the bottom on safety practices and regulation concerning them. China does not yet appear bent on racing to superintelligence. The United States should prioritise both unilateral and co-operative efforts to improve AI safety and avoid provoking a race that all humanity could lose.

</details>

### 277. What the Machine Invented: Mining New Mathematical Instruments from OpenAI's October 2026 Release of AI-Generated Mathematics

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17090)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`AI-generated mathematics`、`release analysis`、`capability assessment`、`mining`

👤 **作者**：Ying Xie

- 🎯 **研究动机**：OpenAI 2026 年 10 月公开发布 722 篇 AI 写作数学手稿（372 问题族，含多个公开问题声称解）——从中提取真正的数学新工具是能力评估的原料
- 🔬 **研究方法**：38 个族的仪器挖掘：提取引理/构造/不等式/证明机制并区分于既有工具与其新组合，记录声明/仪器/前身/警示与 11 个横切模式
- 📌 **结论**：新颖性评估初步且混合、部分源比较修正了原稿错误——AI 生成数学的系统性开采框架（自动化研究能力的外部审计视角）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

On 6 October 2026 OpenAI published an initial 722 AI-written mathematical manuscripts, organized into 372 problem families, in the public repository this http URL. The release includes claimed solutions to many prominent open problems. We approach this collection as a treasure mine of mathematical ideas. Our aim is to extract new lemmas, constructions, inequalities and proof mechanisms introduced in the source arguments, and to distinguish them from established tools and new combinations of those tools. We call these mathematical instruments. Extraction comes first; their validity, novelty and possible reuse then become subjects for further study. The resulting descriptions also help readers understand the selected papers without having to reconstruct every argument at the outset. We examine 38 families in two groups of 17 and 21, spanning number theory, geometry, analysis, combinatorics, complexity, algebra, operator algebras, topology and mathematical physics. Each entry records the claim, extracted instruments, antecedents and caveats. Eleven cross-cutting patterns connect the entries, and a scoreboard records formalization status and dependencies. Novelty assessments are provisional and sometimes mixed. Selected source comparisons correct mathematical statements, hypotheses and counts; they do not independently establish priority or certify the underlying proofs. No Lean development was compiled or kernel-checked for this survey. We also briefly describe a subsequent application of the signed restriction lemma from family 112 to circuit lower bounds at every fixed depth.

</details>

### 278. D-World: An Explicit World-Language-Action Model with Recursive Self-Improvement under Dual Control

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17114)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`world model`、`recursive self-improvement`、`dual control`、`digital twin`

👤 **作者**：Xuan Wen、Baichao Wang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：机器人策略背后的世界模型隐式藏于权重或预测视频、无法直接检查与纠正——显式化可审计
- 🔬 **研究方法**：D-World：感知 agent 写可执行数字孪生+规划 agent 仿真搜动作并按实测修订孪生（重放不超残差容差才接受）；任务推进与误差暴露的动作选择建模为双控制问题
- 📌 **结论**：测量接地的纠正-修订循环（LLM 冻结而其行动的世界自改进）+廉价局部修订对抗谨慎控制器的关断——具身 RSI 的显式世界模型路线

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Frontier large language models (LLMs) now reason about space well enough to program robots, yet the world models behind robot policies are implicit, held in network weights or predicted video, and cannot be inspected or corrected directly. We present D-World, an explicit world-language-action model built on the Sense, Plan and Act decomposition. A perception agent writes the scene as an executable digital twin using generated vision and geometry code. A planning agent searches for actions in simulation and, after each executed action chunk, revises the twin's object states and named physical parameters against what the robot measured. A parameter revision is accepted only if replaying the latest chunk does not increase any measured residual beyond its tolerance, and every chunk is checked in the updated twin before it runs. Structural mismatches return to perception for reconstruction. We call this measurement-grounded cycle of correction and revision recursive self-improvement (RSI), in which the LLM stays frozen while the world it acts in improves. We model the choice of actions that both advance the task and expose model error as a dual control problem and show that the cost of incorporating evidence into the twin sets the threshold at which probing pays, so cheap local revision counteracts the turn-off of cautious controllers. Each episode runs a real-to-sim-to-real loop, and its recorded twins can be refitted and re-rendered offline into higher-fidelity environments for post-training. Because perception and planning communicate only through the twin and language, either can be replaced by a learned model, and since inference dominates the loop, the loop speeds up with faster models. On a dual-arm robot, towel and toaster tasks show local revisions, such as one reducing the bread's rendered-depth residual from 22.4 to 1.5 mm, that let execution continue without reconstruction.

</details>

### 279. ToolHACE: A Dataset for Detecting Hallucinations in Tool-Based LLM Responses

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17125)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`hallucination`、`tool-augmented LLM`、`benchmark`、`post-tool generation`

👤 **作者**：Nikolay Ivanov、…、Ilseyar Alimova

- 🎯 **研究动机**：工具增强模型即使调对工具且返回准确信息仍可矛盾/遗漏/编造——既有幻觉基准只评工具调用阶段
- 🔬 **研究方法**：ToolHACE：工具增强响应的 span 级幻觉检测基准（五细类），现代检测器实验与工具无关数据迁移对照
- 📌 **结论**：工具后幻觉广泛且难检，有效检测需专用工具增强语料训练、工具无关迁移表现差——工具 agent 响应生成段的可靠性空白

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Tool-augmented language models ground their responses in external tool outputs, improving reliability beyond parametric knowledge alone. However, even when the correct tool is called and returns accurate information, models may still contradict such outputs, omit critical details, or generate unsupported claims. Existing hallucination benchmarks for tool-augmented dialogue primarily focus on the tool-calling stage, evaluating whether the correct tool and arguments are selected, while overlooking errors that arise during post-tool response generation. To address this gap, we introduce ToolHACE, a span-level benchmark for hallucination detection in tool-augmented LLM responses, covering five fine-grained hallucination categories. Through extensive experiments with modern hallucination detectors, we show that post-tool-call hallucinations are widespread and remain challenging to detect. Our results further demonstrate that effective detection requires training on specialized tool-augmented corpora, while transfer from tool-agnostic hallucination datasets performs poorly in this setting. These findings highlight the need for dedicated benchmarks and models for reliable hallucination detection in tool-augmented language systems.

</details>

### 280. Who is the 'I' in AI? Establishing Decentralized Identity and Accountability for Autonomous Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17126)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`agent identity`、`accountability`、`self-sovereign identity`、`delegation`

👤 **作者**：Ahammod Munim Ramil、…、Md Sadek Ferdous

- 🎯 **研究动机**：自主 agent 在去中心化生态扩散——缺乏标准化的『自我』定义造成信任与问责缺口
- 🔬 **研究方法**：ABCD 框架：算法指纹（架构与谱系）/行为指纹（动态性能度量）/密码身份（DID）/委托（动作密码绑定到人类问责）四维正交的统一身份演算
- 📌 **结论**：从简单认证到复杂责任归属的粒度化信任策略——自主经济活动中 agent 身份与问责的去中心化基础设施

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

The rapid proliferation of autonomous AI agents in decentralized ecosystems challenges existing identity paradigms. While human identity is traditionally anchored in biological and/or self-asserted attributes and government credentials, AI agents lack a standardized definition of selfhood, creating critical gaps in trust and accountability. This paper introduces a novel framework for establishing Decentralized AI Identity rooted in Self-sovereign identity (SSI) principles. We propose a formal model that clearly distinguishes human users and AI agents, enabling distinct verification logic for each class while maintaining a unified identity calculus. The central to our contribution is the ABCD Framework, a multi-layered approach to agent identification and verification: (A) Algorithmic Fingerprint, which binds the identity to static model architectures and provenance; (B) Behavioural Fingerprint, which verifies dynamic performance metrics over time; (C) Cryptographic Identity, which anchors the agent via Decentralized Identifiers (DIDs) and (D) Delegation, which cryptographically binds agent actions to respective human accountability. By orthogonalizing these dimensions, we model how systems can enforce granular trust policies ranging from simple authentication to complex liability assignment, thereby solving the black box identity problem in the autonomous economic activities of AI agents.

</details>

### 281. Does a Governed Confidence Score Know When a Text-to-SQL Answer Is Right? An Exploratory Study on BIRD and a Pre-registered Confirmation on Spider

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17256)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`confidence audit`、`text-to-SQL`、`preregistered`、`conversational analytics`

👤 **作者**：Raghu Venkat、Tricha Anjali

- 🎯 **研究动机**：生产系统给每个数据库答案附治理置信分决定展示/警示/扣留——分数权重与阈值由设计拍板而从未被审计
- 🔬 **研究方法**：BIRD 上探索性审计（AUROC 0.624、错误扣留 35.7% 正确答案）+冻结规则（样本一致性为主、确定性检查作否决与上限）后在 Spider 上的预注册确认
- 📌 **结论**：预注册假设成立：AUROC 0.566→0.725、误扣 49.0%→8.8% 且 PASS 准确率 95.7%——置信分设计约定的可审计性与修复

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Conversational analytics lets people ask questions of records spread across many systems and get answers in plain language; it is useful only if each answer can be trusted and explained. A production system of this kind attaches a confidence score to every answer it writes over a database and uses it to show the answer, show it with a warning, or withhold it. The score's weights, caps and thresholds were set by design. This paper audits that design. On the 1,534 questions of BIRD dev, answered by GPT-5.1 with a benchmark-adapted production prompt and five samples per question, the designed score separates right from wrong answers weakly (AUROC 0.624, 95% CI 0.595-0.652) and withholds 35.7% of correct answers. Nearly all of its ranking signal comes from agreement among the samples, and most of it is lost where agreement is combined by a minimum with a checklist that drops to zero on any unsupported claim, including claims in commentary. We froze a rule in which agreement with the shown answer is the score and deterministic checks act as vetoes and caps, and pre-registered a test on Spider dev before any Spider answer existed. The primary hypothesis held: AUROC rose from 0.566 to 0.725 (paired difference +0.159, 95% CI +0.097 to +0.221), and correct answers withheld fell from 49.0% to 8.8% with PASS accuracy 95.7%. Under one matching policy the choice of agreement statistic matters little; the gain comes from how agreement enters the score. Whether added checks help depends on how correctness is defined, while they always cost unqualified coverage. A pre-registered empty-result check did not meet its effect-size criterion on Spider. Correctness here means the core answer; labels are model-judged, and a blinded two-person audit, not yet complete, will be reported in a revised version.

</details>

### 282. RL Post-Training Diffusion Language Models Along Their Own Generation Paths

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17266)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`dLLM`、`RL post-training`、`denoising trajectory`、`consistency`

👤 **作者**：Vaibhav Singh、Eugene Belilovsky

- 🎯 **研究动机**：dLLM 经多中间状态生成——既有 RL 后训练近似不可达序列似然或对每个去噪步同权，对轨迹中间状态如何影响期望奖励视而不见
- 🔬 **研究方法**：PACT：奖励倾斜参考分布启发，联合学策略与为每个中间状态打分的标量势（终局奖励锚定），采样转移上一致性损失把揭示 token 的策略-参考对数比匹配到势变化
- 📌 **结论**：LLaDA-8B-Instruct +GSM8K 9.9/MATH500 12.0/Countdown 55.7/Sudoku 72.6——沿自身生成路径的 dLLM 强化后训练（扩散模型训练栈扩展）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Diffusion language models are a promising and more efficient alternative to autoregressive models. However, most RL post-training techniques are not easily adapted to these models. One issue is that diffusion language models generate answers through many intermediate states, deciding at each step which masked positions to reveal and which tokens to place in them. Many existing RL methods used in this setting approximate intractable sequence likelihoods or weight every denoising step by the same advantage, without explicitly distinguishing how these intermediate states of the denoising trajectory affect the expected reward. To address this issue of trajectory blindness, we introduce PACT (Potential-Anchored Consistency of Transitions). Motivated by a reward-tilted reference distribution, PACT jointly learns the policy and a scalar potential that scores each intermediate state, anchored by terminal reward. For each sampled denoising transition, a consistency loss matches the policy-to-reference log-probability ratio of revealed tokens to the change in this potential. This formulation requires neither sequence-likelihood estimation nor importance-ratio clipping. On four tasks, PACT improves LLaDA-8B-Instruct by +9.9 points on GSM8K, +12.0 on MATH500, +55.7 on Countdown, and +72.6 on Sudoku, and reaches the best average accuracy across the four tasks reported for this model, +3.7 points over the next best method. Further compared to AGRPO, it reaches that baseline's best accuracy in about half the training compute on GSM8K.

</details>

### 283. Reasoning for Propaganda Detection via Label-Guided CoT Optimisation

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17271)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`propaganda detection`、`chain-of-thought`、`interpretability`、`misinformation`

👤 **作者**：Zeba Afroz、Harsh Vardhan、Md. Shad Akhtar

- 🎯 **研究动机**：宣传检测系统是黑箱缺解释——说服技术在特定社会政治语境下有定义性特征
- 🔬 **研究方法**：ReCoT：推理条件化检测框架经群体级精化与性能引导重采样捕获定义线索（无需额外解释标注）
- 📌 **结论**：INDI-PROPspan 宏 F1 0.364、PTC 达 SFT 上界 85% 且保持可解释——宣传检测的推理化（误导信息的可解释识别）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Propaganda detection systems usually operate as black boxes, lacking explanations. To address this, we hypothesise that persuasive techniques contain definitional characteristics with respect to their social-political setting. We introduce ReCoT, a reasoning-conditioned detection framework that captures definitional cues of persuasive techniques via population-level refinement and performance-guided resampling, without requiring additional explanation annotations. On INDI-PROPspan, ReCoT achieves a macro F1_LCS of 0.364, surpassing baselines and reaching 88% of the SFT upper bound on Avg F1_LCS. Similarly, on PTC, ReCoT reaches 85% of the SFT upper bound on Avg F1_LCS, closing much of this gap while remaining interpretable. Importantly, the analysis of the reasoning pool's convergence and update pattern for ReCoT shows effective refinement of reasoning CoTs.

</details>

### 284. Beyond Successful Play: Diagnosing Opponent Modeling in Large Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17310)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`theory of mind`、`opponent modeling`、`benchmark`、`diagnostics`

👤 **作者**：Hanbo Xie、Yue Zhao、Robert C. Wilson

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agent 对行为随交互变化的他人的适应可能依赖学到的行为模式而非心智理论（推断信念与意图）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：重复捉迷藏对显式递归信念更新的算法对手：真值信念与动作概率区分适应性游玩/动作预测/信念追踪，经交互/固定历史/受控信息干预+行为 SFT
- 📌 **结论**：游玩改进但对手预测与信念追踪受限；告知对手类型可改善而须从历史推断时变差——LLM 对手建模的诊断框架（欺骗/博弈能力的底座测量）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Language-model agents are expected to adapt to others whose behavior changes through interaction. Yet adaptation may rely on learned behavioral patterns rather than theory of mind (ToM): the ability to infer others' mental states, including beliefs and intentions. We introduce a benchmark and diagnostic framework for repeated hide-and-seek against algorithmic opponents with explicitly defined recursive belief updates. Their ground-truth beliefs and action probabilities let us distinguish adaptive play, action prediction, and belief tracking through live interaction, fixed-history evaluation, and controlled information interventions. With structured elicitation and behavioral supervised fine-tuning (SFT), we find improved play in several tested settings, but diagnostics reveal limitations in opponent prediction and belief tracking. We test whether supervising opponent action probabilities can address this predictive deficit in Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct. Predictions improve when the model is told the opponent's type and parameter settings, but become less accurate when it must infer the opponent from history without this information. Training on examples that also omit these details reduces the gap, although improvements vary across opponents. Is this limitation specific to smaller open-weight models, or does it also appear in a frontier model? GPT-5.4 also benefits from correct opponent beliefs: supplying them improves its predictions and choices, whereas deeper explicit belief reports and self-generated memory do not consistently improve forecasting from interaction history. This contrast suggests that using useful belief information is easier than inferring it autonomously, even for a frontier model. Together, our findings distinguish improved play and action prediction from reliable belief tracking, providing a framework for evaluating mentalizing and testing targeted improvements.

</details>

### 285. SSM-Attack: Learning Where to Perturb for Black-Box Face Privacy

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17323)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`face privacy`、`black-box protection`、`state space model`、`perturbation allocation`

👤 **作者**：Tunahan Parlayıcı、Erchan Aptoula、Yücel Saygın

- 🎯 **研究动机**：在线照片可被人脸识别系统无授权匹配——生成式保护依赖重型骨干/多阶段推理/可见语义编辑
- 🔬 **研究方法**：SSM-Attack：选择性状态空间模型一次线性时间前向预测扰动预算的空间分配（标记携带身份证据的面部位置），短投影细化在逐像素预算内定值
- 📌 **结论**：三个评测集最高黑盒攻击成功率（87.10/86.90/81.15%），同等扰动上限下结构相似性+0.044——知道在哪花小预算胜过大生成先验

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Face recognition systems are deployed widely enough that a photograph shared online can be matched without its subject's consent. Generator-based protection methods rely on heavy pretrained backbones, multi-stage inference or visible semantic edits. We introduce SSM-Attack, a black-box face privacy framework built on Selective State Space Models. Rather than synthesizing the perturbation, the state-space model predicts in one linear-time pass a spatial allocation of the perturbation budget, marking which facial locations carry identity evidence; a short projected refinement then sets the values within this per-pixel budget. Against published benchmark results, SSM-Attack attains the highest average black-box attack success rate on all three evaluation sets: 87.10 percent on CelebA-HQ, 86.90 on LADN and 81.15 on FFHQ, the last from a network that never saw that dataset. The allocation network holds 5.73 million parameters, or 16.13 million with the frozen networks used by its quality terms. Under the same cap of 0.10 on the maximum per-pixel change, allocation improves structural similarity by 0.044, peak signal-to-noise ratio by 1.62 decibels and Frechet Inception Distance by 11.2 points over a uniform map while spending 37 percent of its capacity. Strong facial privacy protection therefore does not require a large generative prior; it requires knowing where to spend a small one.

</details>

### 286. Human Situational Awareness and Workload during Heterogeneous Autonomous Vehicle Hazard Monitoring: A Cross-Platform Study

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17339)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`situational awareness`、`supervisory control`、`human factors`、`autonomous vehicles`

👤 **作者**：Ziyue Wang、Yang Xing、Argyrios Zolotas、Adolfo Perrusquia、Weisi Guo、Antonios Tsourdos

- 🎯 **研究动机**：操作者从直接控制转向监督监控——异质自主平台罕见安全关键事件中的态势感知（SA）维持是根本挑战
- 🔬 **研究方法**：18 人被试×6 组内条件（视觉/触觉/视听×地面车/无人机）的 SART 与 NASA-TLX 测量
- 📌 **结论**：空中监控 SA 显著更低且负荷更高；触觉与视听组合在不增负荷下显著提升 SA——监督接口设计可在既有机制内增强觉察（Human-AI oversight）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

As autonomous ground and aerial vehicles become increasingly capable, human operators are transitioning from direct control to supervisory monitoring roles. Maintaining Situational Awareness (SA) during rare but safety critical hazard events remains a fundamental challenge, particularly when operators must monitor heterogeneous autonomous platforms with different spatial and cognitive demands. This study develops a unified experimental framework for investigating human SA and workload during supervisory monitoring of autonomous ground and aerial vehicles under hazard conditions. Eighteen participants completed six within-subject conditions combining three interface modalities, namely, visual-only, haptic-only, and visual-haptic, with two platform types, ground vehicle and aerial drone. SA was measured using Situation Awareness Rating Technique (SART), while workload was assessed using the NASA Task Load Index (NASA-TLX). The key findings are: 1) Aerial monitoring produced significantly lower overall SA, higher situational demand, and greater workload than ground monitoring, driven by elevated Demand and reduced Understanding rather than diminished Supply. 2) Workload was consistently coupled with situational demand across all conditions, suggesting a stable SA-workload mechanism. 3) Both haptic-only and visual-haptic significantly improved overall SA relative to visual-only without increasing workload, demonstrating that interface design can enhance awareness within the existing workload envelope. These findings establish a cross-platform behavioural baseline for designing multimodal interfaces that enhance operator SA without elevating workload in heterogeneous autonomous vehicle systems.

</details>

### 287. claimcheck: Measuring and Improving Specification Faithfulness

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17358)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`specification faithfulness`、`formal verification`、`informalization`、`benchmark`

👤 **作者**：Martin Amster、One An、Holly Baker、Fernanda Graciolli、Simon Henniger、Nada Amin

- 🎯 **研究动机**：形式验证证明程序满足规范却不保证规范捕获了作者本意——LLM 同时写代码与规范时这一缺口成为现实风险
- 🔬 **研究方法**：四形式语言 3,234 行基准（含三分之一保义改写防编辑检测）+claimcheck：未见要求的模型把形式工件非形式化回英文、另一模型判定是否表达同一保证
- 📌 **结论**：四语言上宏召回 +8.2-9.8 超单次调用——规范忠实性的可测量化（形式化管线的语义边界）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Formal verification proves that a program satisfies its specification, but says nothing about whether the specification captures what the author meant. As LLMs increasingly write both code and specifications, a gap between the author's natural-language requirement and the specification becomes a live risk. A machine-checked proof of a property narrower than the one requested is worthless but passes every check. Closing this gap is solving the spec formalization problem: given a natural-language requirement, produce a formal artifact whose meaning matches it. To measure the gap, we curate an end-to-end benchmark for spec formalization spanning four formal languages: Dafny contracts and Lean 4, Isabelle and Rocq theorem statements. Each of its 3,234 rows pairs a natural-language requirement with a formal artifact and a label established by execution, by proof, or by the benchmark it is drawn from. A third of the rows are meaning-preserving rewrites of the artifact that keep the faithful label, so a judge cannot score by detecting that a row was edited. We introduce claimcheck, a tool that detects incorrect formalizations: the formal artifact is translated back into plain English by a model that never sees the requirement, and a separate model, the judge, decides whether the informalization and the requirement express the same guarantee. On all four languages it gains 8.2 to 9.8 points of macro recall over a single call shown the requirement and the artifact, and 4.2 to 6.0 over that call handed the same list of meaning-preserving rewrites. Showing the judge the artifact as well costs 1.4 to 4.5 points. Across four judges that also see the artifact, the gain shrinks as the judge gets stronger, to within a point of the informed single call for the strongest.

</details>

### 288. PIG: Parallel Inconsistency Guidance for Accelerating Diffusion Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17375)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`dLLM`、`parallel decoding`、`inconsistency estimation`、`acceleration`

👤 **作者**：Quentin Fruytier、Anton Xue、Sanjay Shakkottai、Sujay Sanghavi

- 🎯 **研究动机**：dLLM 并行解码多掩码位时偏离缓慢昂贵的串行 oracle 轨迹致精度下降
- 🔬 **研究方法**：PIG：~2M 参数辅助模型单次前向估计并行-串行不一致，按置信度降序遍历逐 token 加入并行解码集直到估计不一致超阈值
- 📌 **结论**：LLaDA-8B/NemotronDiffusion-3B 上扩展五基准的精度-TPF 前沿——轻量不一致估计的 dLLM 并行解码（扩散解码栈）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Diffusion language models accelerate generation by decoding multiple masked positions per forward pass. However, accuracy is known to drop when parallel decoding trajectories deviate from their sequential counterparts, which are slow and expensive "oracles" that adhere to the joint distribution of language. In this paper, we propose Parallel Inconsistency Guidance (PIG), a new paradigm that uses a trained auxiliary model to directly estimate the inconsistency between parallel and sequential generation from a single model forward pass. During inference, masked positions are traversed by decreasing confidence, and tokens are added to the parallel decode set one-at-a-time until the total estimated inconsistency between the parallel and sequential generation exceeds a threshold. Surprisingly, it suffices to train a lightweight ~2M parameter auxiliary model on a small mixture drawn from OpenCoder, GSM8K-train, MATH-train, and MBPP-train. For LLaDA-8B Instruct and NemotronDiffusion-3B Instruct, PIG extends the accuracy-TPF frontier on GSM8K, MATH500, MBPP, HumanEval, and MMLU, when compared to the industry standard Fast-dLLM and measures favorably on LLaDA-8B Instruct against DAPD, KLASS, EB-sampler, and other learned unmasking policies.

</details>

### 289. Get In, Decide, Prove: A Merchant-Side Readiness Benchmark for Agentic Commerce

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17378)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`agentic commerce`、`merchant readiness`、`decision sufficiency`、`benchmark`

👤 **作者**：Adrian de Valois-Franklin、Alex Bogdan、Anas Ibrahim、Ibrahim Shaer、Tamaz Andguladze

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agent 代人浏览购买——交易的另一侧（商家网站是否放 agent 进、条款是否足以决定购买、是否留有约定证据）几乎未知
- 🔬 **研究方法**：商家侧三问基准+试点：决策充分性按买家授权的具体标准评定（确定/不确定/未知）并携带证据覆盖与缺失界；555 站点访问普查+330 商家确定性规则评定
- 📌 **结论**：四大商家三个在报价页拒绝声明式 profile、无一授权可确认满足（总价或退款条款不确定）；18.9% 站点首页对声明 profile 不可读——agentic 商务的商家侧准备度

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

AI agents now browse and buy on people's behalf, and benchmarks measure how well the agents perform. Far less is known about the other side of the transaction: whether a merchant's website lets an agent in, publishes terms precise enough to decide a purchase, and leaves evidence of what was agreed. This paper presents a merchant-side benchmark built on those three questions and reports a pilot of it. The central measurement, decision sufficiency, rates each criterion of a concrete buyer mandate (total cost, cancellation or return terms, service date, variant identity) as determinate, indeterminate, or unknown from the merchant's own published evidence. Readiness scores carry their evidence coverage and missingness bounds; access is reported separately for a declared bot, a declared headless browser, and a user-present browser; and each observed term is mapped to the RAILS admissibility class of the evidence that could support it in a dispute. Four large merchants received the full assessment: three refused the declared profiles at their offer pages, and no mandate could be confirmed as met, because each merchant left a total or a refund term undetermined. An access census of 555 sites of merchants that two payment processors publicly name as clients found the home page unreadable to both declared profiles at 105 sites (18.9%). A declared assessment of 330 merchants, scored by deterministic rules without a rater, found that evidence coverage rather than the score decides who can be ranked: 115 merchants (35%) reached the ranking threshold, rankable profiles scored a median of 89 to 90, and material terms was the one pillar well below the others, because the all-in total was decidable at 18 merchants. The pilot shows that the measurements can be taken; it does not validate them or rank merchants, and the weights and the projected clearing band await validation against outcomes.

</details>

### 290. Mitigating Black-Box Score-Based Query Attacks via Gradient Confusion

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17382)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`query attack`、`gradient confusion`、`defense`、`black-box`

👤 **作者**：Ali Javeri、Javad Hezareh、Radin Shahdaei、AmirMahdi Sadeghzadeh

- 🎯 **研究动机**：分数型黑盒攻击靠重复查询估计梯度——随机输出扰动防御挡不住加大预算或平均的攻击者
- 🔬 **研究方法**：GCD：免重训确定性测试时包装器，解 Jacobian 接地的约束优化把暴露的 logit 梯度对齐到与真实攻击损失梯度最大失配的混淆方向
- 📌 **结论**：理论证明可证放缓梯度估计型攻击并诱发随机搜索型系统错误；CIFAR/ImageNet 四 SOTA 攻击上 SOTA 鲁棒且零干净精度损失——主动塑造查询接口的防御

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Score-based black-box adversarial attacks pose a serious threat to deployed machine learning systems by exploiting repeated model queries to craft adversarial examples, requiring neither access to model parameters nor architecture. Existing defenses predominantly rely on stochastic output perturbations, which fail to prevent determined attackers from recovering stable gradient estimates by increasing query budgets or averaging over randomness. In this work, we propose the Gradient Confusion Defense (GCD), a retraining-free, deterministic test-time wrapper that actively shapes the probability vector returned to querying adversaries. By solving a constrained optimization problem grounded in the model's Jacobian, GCD aligns the exposed logit-gradient with a target confusion direction that is maximally misaligned with the true adversarial loss gradient---increasing the query complexity of attacks without sacrificing predictive utility or top-1 accuracy. We provide rigorous theoretical analysis demonstrating that GCD provably slows gradient-estimation-based attacks and induces systematic accept/reject errors in random search-based attacks, with the confusion effect amplifying as the fraction of defended queries increases. Extensive experiments on CIFAR-10 and ImageNet across multiple architectures (ResNet-18, ResNet-50, ViT-B/16) and four state-of-the-art attacks confirm these predictions: GCD achieves state-of-the-art robustness, while incurring zero clean accuracy degradation. %Crucially, unlike randomization-based defenses, GCD's deterministic structure provides resilience against adaptive attacks that exploit stochasticity, making it well-suited for security-critical deployment settings.

</details>

### 291. JustOPD: On-Policy Distillation from Autoregressive to Diffusion Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17397)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`AR-to-DLM`、`on-policy distillation`、`diffusion LM`、`decoding alignment`

👤 **作者**：Zikun Zhang、Jiayuan Sheng、David D. Yao、Wenpin Tang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：强 ARM 到弱 DLM 的在策蒸馏因 ARM 左到右解码与 DLM 灵活生成顺序的错配而未被探索
- 🔬 **研究方法**：JustOPD：训练中用自回归 rollout 使学生生成过程与 ARM 教师的左到右解码对齐，推理时保留 DLM 灵活生成顺序
- 📌 **结论**：Qwen3-14B→Dream-7B 跨多种散度选择一致提升数学推理——AR→DLM 蒸馏的解法（安全属性跨范式迁移的基础设施）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

On-policy distillation (OPD) is a post-training approach for large language models (LLMs) that enables a student model to learn a teacher model's distribution from sequences sampled from the student's own policy. Despite its promising capability, OPD for diffusion language models (DLMs) remains underexplored and existing studies primarily focuses on DLM-to-DLM distillation. The limited availability of strong teacher DLMs and the flexible generation order of DLMs impose additional technical challenges as well. However, it remains unclear how to effectively distill knowledge from a strong autoregressive language model (ARM) into a weaker DLM, given the mismatch between the left-to-right decoding of ARMs and the flexible decoding order of DLMs. Inspired by Ni et al. (2026), we propose JustOPD, an ARM-to-DLM on-policy distillation method that uses autoregressive rollouts during training to align the student's generation process with the ARM teacher's left-to-right decoding. This alignment enables effective distillation from ARM teachers while preserving the DLM's flexible generation-order capability at inference time. Experiments using Qwen3-14B as the teacher and Dream-7B as the student demonstrate that JustOPD consistently improves mathematical reasoning performance over the base DLM across multiple divergence choices.

</details>

### 292. Why Agentic Workflows Fail: A Taxonomy Derived from Literature and Empirical Data

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17418)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`failure taxonomy`、`agentic workflow`、`root cause`、`systems reliability`

👤 **作者**：Xin Li、Rami Bahsoon、Tao Chen

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agentic 工作流同一用户可见症状可源自模型/编排/工具/基础设施或其交互——失败难诊断
- 🔬 **研究方法**：来源感知分类学：来源归因+失效字典+工作流阶段定位+agent 能力定位的双轴矩阵，学术文献与 GitHub 证据双重应用
- 📌 **结论**：文献偏规划/推理/学习而 GitHub 偏执行/致动/工具/API/基础设施，热点是迭代执行与致动——agentic 可靠性应作系统问题对待

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Agentic workflows combine large language models with planning, memory, tools, execution environments, and iterative feedback. Their failures are difficult to diagnose because the same user-visible symptom may originate from model behavior, workflow orchestration, external tools, infrastructure, or their interaction. This paper presents a source-aware taxonomy of agentic workflow failures and applies it to evidence from academic literature and GitHub. The taxonomy combines source attribution with three empirical dimensions: a concrete failure-type dictionary, workflow-stage localization, and agent-capability localization. Its central structure is a two-axis localization matrix: workflow stages form the horizontal process axis and agent capabilities form the vertical functional axis. This design moves beyond taxonomies that mainly classify final failure manifestations by localizing intermediate process states inside agent operation. Our empirical comparison shows that literature-derived failures emphasize planning, reasoning, learning, perception, and knowledge-related mechanisms, whereas GitHub-derived failures concentrate on execution-stage, actuation-related, tool, API, and infrastructure failures. The dominant hotspot is iterative execution and actuation. These findings indicate that agentic workflow reliability should be treated as a systems problem rather than only a model-output problem. The taxonomy provides a basis for structured evaluation, root cause analysis, failure injection, and engineering triage.

</details>

### 293. Large Language Models Extend Known Research But Do Not Create New Research Directions

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17439)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`scientific novelty`、`AI research capability`、`direction vs distance`、`measurement`

👤 **作者**：Zhihua Liu、Kurt Butler、Damian Machlanski、Danilo Bueno、Sotirios A. Tsaftaris

- 🎯 **研究动机**：人类评审常评 LLM 想法比专家更新颖——但模型能否开创全新研究方向还是只能延伸既有知识未解
- 🔬 **研究方法**：区分距离（离既有工作多远）与方向（是否追求新线路）：2,690 万人类论文×1,325 主题共享概念空间嵌入 524,308 条 LLM 假设，正交新颖性得分（ONS）量化方向
- 📌 **结论**：LLM 想法比人类论文更远（+16%）但方向新颖性不足——扩展已知而非创造新研究方向（自动化研究能力的外部测量）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

The success of autonomous research systems relies on the quality of the scientific hypotheses they generate. Recent studies report that human reviewers often rate LLM-generated ideas as more novel than those from human experts, raising hope that AI can accelerate scientific discovery. Yet it is unclear whether these models can do more than extend existing knowledge. Can LLMs drive genuine innovation by revealing new research directions, or are they limited to repeating and recombining what science already knows? This unresolved question lies at the heart of discussions about AI and the future of science. To address this, we distinguish two types of novelty: distance, which measures how far a hypothesis lies from existing work, and direction, which captures whether the hypothesis pursues new lines of inquiry beyond those already present. These two measures diverge sharply for LLM-generated work. We represent scientific papers in a shared conceptual space using a pretrained scientific-paper embedding model. Within this space, we embed 26.9 million human-authored papers across 1,325 research topics in six natural-science fields and 524,308 LLM-generated hypotheses from eight models. Distance is measured using existing metrics. To quantify direction, we introduce the orthogonal novelty score (ONS). LLMs consistently generate ideas more distant from existing work than human-authored papers, up to 16\% further when the LLM is given the papers farthest from the centre of the topic. However, LLM hypotheses have a 15--23\% lower median ONS than published papers from the same topic. Thus, LLMs move further from what is already known, but remain within established directions. This pattern persists across changes in various configurations. As a result, LLMs excel at extending the frontier but the creation of new directions is still led by human scientists.

</details>

### 294. Revisiting the 16x Pretraining Compute Gap between Diffusion and Autoregressive Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17590)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`dLLM`、`scaling laws`、`pretraining compute`、`block diffusion`

👤 **作者**：Guanghan Wang、Bing He、Zhan Shi、Benoit Dumoulin、Hanqing Lu、Volodymyr Kuleshov

- 🎯 **研究动机**：广被引用的『DLM 需 16 倍 ARM 训练算力』使 dLLM 从零预训练显得不可行——其方法学（ELBO 打分+全序列 decoder-only）已脱离当前实践
- 🔬 **研究方法**：以模型自身解码顺序的精确似然+块扩散训练+编码器-解码器架构+实用块尺寸扫描重访标度律（~1,700 个模型）
- 📌 **结论**：16×→12.8×（精确似然）→7.8×（块 32）→4.6×（块 8）→3.0×（块 4）——dLLM 训练远比想象便宜（扩散语言模型生态的算力基线修正）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Diffusion language models (DLMs) have attracted wide attention for their fast, parallel decoding, but they are believed to be far less efficient to train than autoregressive language models (ARMs): a widely cited study reports that a DLM needs roughly 16x the training compute of an ARM to match its likelihood, a figure that has made pretraining DLMs from scratch look prohibitive at scale. We find that the methodology behind this figure has fallen out of step with current DLM practice: it scores the model with its evidence lower bound (ELBO) and trains a full-sequence, decoder-only model. We therefore revisit the scaling laws of DLMs, adopting the exact likelihood under the model's own decoding order, block diffusion training, an encoder-decoder architecture, and a sweep over the block sizes used in practice. Under this new protocol, we study across ten model variants, about 1,700 models in total, and find that pretraining DLMs is far cheaper than previously thought. Concretely, scoring with the exact likelihood reduces the compute multiple to 12.8x; block diffusion training and an encoder-decoder architecture further reduce it to 7.8x at block size 32, and smaller block sizes narrow it to 4.6x at block size 8 and 3.0x at block size 4. We complement these scaling analyses with an empirical rule for the optimal decoder depth of encoder-decoder block diffusion, a held-out compute budget beyond the fitting range at which the gap between variants persists, and downstream benchmark results where likelihood improvements carry over to accuracy. Overall, the 16x figure substantially overstates the pretraining cost gap between DLMs and ARMs, and pretraining DLMs from scratch is far more viable than previously assumed.

</details>

### 295. Facts Don't Speak Louder than Words: The Behavioral Essence of Long-CoT Distillation

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17607)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`CoT distillation`、`behavioral alignment`、`reasoning`、`mechanism`

👤 **作者**：Yongcan Yu、…、Ran He

- 🎯 **研究动机**：长 CoT 蒸馏提升推理的机制不明——管线假设事实正确与覆盖广度不可或缺
- 🔬 **研究方法**：行为假说检验：仅用有缺陷轨迹或受限提示集训练性能相近；仅在已掌握问题上微调隔离行为对齐与新知识注入；概率损失与交叉熵的复杂度分层交互
- 📌 **结论**：长 CoT 蒸馏的本质是与结构化推理模式的行为对齐而非事实记忆——推理模型训练的机制澄清（推理行为习得的本质）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

While long chain-of-thought (CoT) distillation significantly improves the reasoning abilities of language models, its underlying mechanism remains poorly understood. Current pipelines generally rely on rejection sampling to gather reasoning traces across diverse prompts, assuming that factual correctness and broad problem coverage are indispensable. Surprisingly, we find that training exclusively on flawed reasoning trajectories or a restricted prompt set yields nearly comparable performance. Driven by this counterintuitive finding, we propose the Behavioral Hypothesis: the essence of Long-CoT distillation is behavioral alignment with structured reasoning patterns, rather than factual knowledge memorization. We then validate this by demonstrating substantial reasoning gains even when models are fine-tuned exclusively on previously mastered problems-effectively isolating behavioral alignment from novel knowledge injection. Having established that reasoning patterns are the true bottleneck, we investigate how to learn them across varying levels of complexity optimally. We reveal a critical interplay: while the recent probability-based loss excels at learning simple patterns, the standard cross-entropy loss is essential for scaling to complex CoTs. We believe these insights demystify CoT distillation and provide a principled foundation for the future development of reasoning models. Code is available on this https URL.

</details>

### 296. Verifier-Harness Co-Evolution for Training-Free Self-Improvement on Open-Ended Tasks

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17609)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`self-improvement`、`verifier evolution`、`training-free`、`open-ended tasks`

👤 **作者**：Tianyi Xu、…、Huazheng Wang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：免训练自改进在开放任务上依赖模型 verifier——verifier 错则响应更糟且误导经验跨任务流持久化，永不更新又会随 agent 进步而陈旧
- 🔬 **研究方法**：UCoE：可执行 verifier 程序与 harness 状态共同演化（模型参数冻结），在声明单属性差异的响应对比上测候选 verifier 的最弱族判别力（驱动容量）并择优
- 📌 **结论**：六开放基准上平均 59.00%/57.19% 偏好胜过直接生成——verifier 与 agent 的免训练共同演化（自改进中的验证器漂移治理）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Training-free self-improvement lets an agent use feedback to revise the response it is about to return and to update the harness state that shapes how it handles later tasks. On open-ended tasks that feedback rarely comes from reference answers or executable tests, so the agent has to rely on a model-based verifier. When the verifier is wrong, the current response can get worse, and misleading experience can persist across the task stream. A verifier that never changes also grows stale as the agent's responses improve and task demands shift. We introduce Unified Co-Evolution (UCoE), a training-free method that evolves an executable verifier program together with the agent's harness state while all model parameters stay fixed. UCoE synthesizes candidate verifier programs and tests them on response contrasts, pairs of responses that differ in one declared property and have a known preferred side. It selects the candidate whose discrimination on its weakest contrast family is highest, a quantity we call driving capacity. The selected verifier diagnoses one weakness in each draft, guides a single repair, decides which response is served, and determines whether the accepted repair may enter the harness state. As tasks complete, their diagnostics seed new candidate verifiers, which must beat the current verifier on the same contrasts before adoption. Across six open-ended benchmarks, UCoE averages 59.00% preference against matched direct generation with Qwen3-8B and 57.19% with Qwen3.8-27B, margins of 9.00 and 7.19 percentage points. On three verifiable benchmarks, the same method raises the mean from 36.06% for direct generation to 38.48%. Code is available at this https URL.

</details>

### 297. MAS-Gate: Unlocking Multi-Agent System Lifecycle via Node-Centric Quantification

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17613)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`multi-agent systems`、`lifecycle`、`node attribution`、`quantification`

👤 **作者**：Hehai Lin、…、Chengwei Qin

- 🎯 **研究动机**：MAS 研究碎片化于设计时编排/运行时干预/事后归因各阶段——目标与信号难以跨阶段调和
- 🔬 **研究方法**：MAS-Gate：执行建模为 DAG，节点内效用（参考答案似然变化）+节点间强度（语义 uptake 与下游触达）的统一节点中心量化，适配三目标（编排器密集奖励/前缀编辑器/风险排序辅助归因）
- 📌 **结论**：交互环境与多跳问答上验证——跨生命周期的共享量化信号（MAS 归因与干预的统一底座）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems (MAS) can solve complex problems beyond the reach of a single agent by decomposing tasks and coordinating complementary capabilities. Despite extensive research on MAS, existing work largely focuses on specific aspects or individual phases of their development and operation, resulting in fragmented perspectives with objectives and signals that are difficult to reconcile across these different settings. To bridge these disconnected perspectives, we conceptualize MAS development and operation as a three-phase lifecycle spanning design-time orchestration before execution, runtime intervention during execution, and post-hoc attribution after execution, and introduce MAS-Gate, a unified node-centric quantification applicable across these phases. Modeling each execution as a directed acyclic graph, MAS-Gate combines within-node utility, which estimates a node's contribution through changes in reference-answer likelihood, with inter-node strength, which approximates semantic uptake and potential downstream reach. We adapt this shared quantification to three objectives: providing a structure-aware dense reward for Orchestrator training, training an Editor to act on execution prefixes using paired rewards from completed edited and unedited trajectories, and supplying risk-ranked nodes as auxiliary evidence for LLM-based post-hoc attribution. Experiments spanning interaction environments, knowledge-intensive multi-hop question answering, general reasoning, and post-hoc attribution demonstrate that MAS-Gate improves workflow construction, targeted runtime editing, and LLM-assisted error localization over corresponding phase-specific baselines. Together, these results establish MAS-Gate as a unified structural interface for improving MAS generation, dynamic editing, and error attribution throughout the lifecycle.

</details>

### 298. Beyond Bias Scores: Answerability Confounds in Activation Steering with Gradient-Constrained Direction Design (GCD)

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17641)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`activation steering`、`answerability confound`、`bias measurement`、`direction design`

👤 **作者**：Weitong Li、Donglin Song、Junkai Lin、Xi Xuan

- 🎯 **研究动机**：偏差评分混淆模型偏好什么与是否作答——BBQ 歧义分可分解为可答性与带符号偏好
- 🔬 **研究方法**：五模型 28,824 项分析对比转向的可答性通道占比+GCD 选项梯度构造（一阶未知 logit 约束）
- 📌 **结论**：对比转向在 11/11 类降歧义分但仅 8/11 降明确偏好分；GCD 把答案率失真降 14 倍且偏好变化大 4 倍——转向评测须与分数并报可答性

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Bias scores in language-model evaluations often conflate what a model prefers with whether it answers at all. We identify this confound in \textsc{BBQ}, whose ambiguous score factorizes into answerability and signed preference. Across 11 categories, 28,}824 items, and five models, contrastive steering lowers the signed ambiguous score in 11/11 categories but lowers the signed disambiguated preference score in only 8/11. Across 99 configurations, the answerability channel exceeds half of the observed score change in 17\% and accounts for the entire change in 11%. In a six-category disambiguated evaluation, 20.8\% of 1,500 items flip to the unknown option, where abstention is incorrect; \textsc{StereoSet} shows no analogous answer-rate shift. The effect reflects selection and near-orthogonality between the contrastive and preference-gradient directions. We introduce Gradient-Constrained Direction Design (\textsc{GCD}), an option-gradient construction with a first-order unknown-logit constraint. Under a matched Qwen-7B protocol, GCD reduces answer-rate distortion 14-fold and yields a 4-fold larger preference change, while ablations show similar gains for alternative and unconstrained option-gradient directions; nulling the unknown gradient alone is not responsible for the gain. We recommend reporting answerability alongside published scores.

</details>

### 299. textGrain watermarking is harder to detect: can sum-tests still do it optimally?

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17655)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`watermark theory`、`detection boundary`、`textGrain`、`minimax`

👤 **作者**：Swapnaneel Bhattacharyya、Soham Bonnerjee

- 🎯 **研究动机**：textGrain 水印以显式熵预算校准信号——人类编辑后何时以及多高效可检的最优性未知
- 🔬 **研究方法**：稀疏混合编辑模型（保水印位比例 ε_n≍n^-p、token 不确定度 n^-q）下的极小极大检测边界与和检测器最优性分析
- 📌 **结论**：textGrain 边界 p+q=1/2 严格小于 Gumbel-max 的 2p+q=1（源于构造而非熵校准）；固定列数时和检测器恰在边界最优——水印检测的理论刻画

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Watermarking embeds an imperceptible signal in text from large language models that is statistically detectable with a secret key. The recent textGrain watermark (Li et. al. (2026b)) calibrates this signal against the model's sampling entropy through an explicit entropy budget, combining the stochasticity of Red-Green and the unbiasedness of Gumbel-max watermarks, and its accompanying report proposes a detector that sums pivotal statistics. We study when, and how efficiently, textGrain can be detected after human edits, modeled as a sparse mixture in which a fraction $\varepsilon_n\asymp n^{-p}$ of positions retain the watermark and token uncertainty decays as $n^{-q}$. First, entropy calibration comes at a cost: the minimax detection boundary of textGrain is $p+q=1/2$, so its detectable region is strictly smaller than that of Gumbel-max and maximal-coupling watermarks, whose boundary is $2p+q=1$. This cost stems from textGrain's construction rather than from entropy calibration itself: an unbiased scheme with the same entropy loss remains detectable where textGrain is not. Second, perhaps surprisingly, this loss makes the sum detector optimal at the boundary when the number of transport columns is fixed, unlike for Gumbel-max. When the number of columns grows and the entropy budget does not vanish, the detectable region expands while that of the sum detector does not. Third, for a fixed edit fraction, we characterize the optimal exponential rate of type II error decay and show that the sum detector falls strictly short of it at every fixed alternative. Finally, we propose a weighted multiscale binomial scan, valid in finite samples and needing no knowledge of the next-token distributions, entropy budget, or edit fraction; it attains every detection boundary above and the optimal exponent.

</details>

### 300. HelpMeOut: A Neuropsychology-Grounded Benchmark for Error-Aware Monitoring of Household Task Execution

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17694)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`error monitoring`、`household tasks`、`neuropsychology`、`embodied AI`

👤 **作者**：Haoran Wang、Jingwei Huang、Anqing Duan、Ho Seok Ahn、Bruce Macdonald

- 🎯 **研究动机**：既有家庭基准假设人类正确执行任务——认知障碍者与老年人的日常活动产生结构化执行错误，监护 agent 须尽早发现
- 🔬 **研究方法**：HelpMeOut：日常动作错误研究操作化为 20,000 对活动片段×九类神经心理学错误类别，流式轨迹监控+学得监控器/符号/变点/前沿 LLM 基线
- 📌 **结论**：各基线作为错误监控器表现均差、常漏判执行出错——错误感知辅助 agent 的测试床（具身监控的有效性缺口）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

We introduce HelpMeOut, an embodied AI benchmark for error-aware monitoring of household task execution. Existing embodied household benchmarks typically assume that humans execute tasks correctly, but this assumption does not hold for cognitively impaired persons or older adults whose everyday activities produce structured execution errors. To address this gap, HelpMeOut operationalizes research on everyday action errors into 20000 paired activity episodes, covering nine neuropsychologically motivated error categories. In each episode, agents observe a streaming human activity trajectory and must monitor whether the execution is unfolding correctly, diagnosing any structured errors as early as possible. We evaluate learned-monitor, symbolic, change-point, and frontier LLM prompting baselines. Results show that these methods perform poorly as error monitors, often missing when execution goes wrong. These results suggest a gap left by standard goal-inference and task-completion benchmarks, making HelpMeOut a testbed for error-aware assistive agents.

</details>

### 301. Pushback in a Haystack: Language Models Respond to Factual Errors Ambiguously and Ineffectively

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17703)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`pushback`、`sycophancy`、`cross-lingual`、`human study`

👤 **作者**：Li Lucy、…、Yulia Tsvetkov

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LM 谄媚（不反驳错误输入）被广泛研究——但若 LM 确实反驳了，谄媚就解决了吗
- 🔬 **研究方法**：短语级人工验证的响应标注框架（五语言）+n=300 人类实验测反驳模糊性对纠错的实际效果
- 📌 **结论**：反驳多间接措辞并混入肯定（印地/约鲁巴语更间接）；模糊反驳可能无法纠正用户的事实性误解——反驳方式而非有无决定纠错效果

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

The sycophancy of language models, or their failure to push back against faulty user input, is now widely studied. However, if LMs do push back, is sycophancy solved? We show that LMs vary in how they present pushback, and thus vary in how effectively they correct user beliefs. We introduce a phrase-level, human-validated response labeling framework to uncover fine-grained insights into how LMs push against and/or affirm false inputs. We develop and apply our framework on model responses in five languages: English, German, Chinese, Hindi, and Yoruba. We find that when models do push back against users' false assertions, they tend to use indirect phrasing and mix in affirmation, with more indirectness in Hindi and Yoruba than in other languages considered. In a large-scale human study (n = 300), we find that the pushback ambiguity we identified may fail to correct and prevent users' factual misconceptions.

</details>

### 302. From Token Correction to Claim Admission: Training-Free Hallucination Mitigation for Large Vision-Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17706)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`hallucination`、`claim admission`、`LVLM`、`training-free`

👤 **作者**：Hyeonggeun Han、Taehyun Cho、Sangwoo Hong

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LVLM 对象幻觉生成无图像支撑的主张——token 级分布纠正之外的声明级准入未被探索
- 🔬 **研究方法**：EGVCA：语义主张仅在获得足够图像依赖支撑（观测图 vs 视觉空输入下的主张似然比较）时准入；描述控语义扩张不改 token 生成，二值 VQA 结合原子视觉支撑与 Yes-No 边际变化
- 📌 **结论**：CHAIR/POPE/MME 三骨干上大幅降对象幻觉且九分之八设置最高 POPE F1——声明级准入的免训练幻觉缓解（证据采信的显式门控）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large vision-language models often exhibit object hallucination, generating claims that are not supported by the input image. To mitigate this issue, we propose Evidence-Guided Visual Claim Admission (EGVCA), a training-free framework that admits a semantic claim only when it receives sufficient image-dependent support. EGVCA measures this support by comparing the claim likelihood under the observed image and a visual-null input. For captioning, it controls semantic expansion without altering token generation. For binary visual question answering, it combines atomic visual support with the image-induced change in the model's native Yes--No margin. Experiments on CHAIR, POPE, and MME across three LVLM backbones show that EGVCA substantially reduces object hallucination while preserving caption quality. It achieves the highest POPE F1 in eight of nine backbone--sampling settings and improves the native MME total score on all three backbones. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of claim-level admission as a simple alternative to token-level distribution correction.

</details>

### 303. The Human--VLA Communication Gap: What Humans Leave Implicit and What Policies Cannot Recover

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17746)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`VLA`、`instruction ambiguity`、`communication gap`、`human factors`

👤 **作者**：Yiqing Xu、Jiajun Wu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：机器人会做任务却在人类自然提出时失败——人机沟通缺口的信息层（语义/接地/事件结构未解）与残余执行层未分离
- 🔬 **研究方法**：同一物理任务的受控变体+人们自然表达演示目标的方式刻画（π0.5 详细分析，π0-FAST/Cosmos3-Edge 一致趋势）
- 📌 **结论**：人类随任务复杂化压缩指令、更多依赖语境接地与隐式执行结构；策略容忍接地依赖但在语义/事件结构须重建时挣扎——人类中心策略学习与接口的具体靶点（指令权威的具身侧）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

A robot may know how to perform a task yet fail when a human asks for it naturally. We study this human--VLA communication gap by separating an \emph{information gap}, where task semantics, grounding, or event structure remain unresolved, from a residual \emph{execution gap}, where even a fully specified task depends on how it is organized and invoked. To do this, we characterize how people naturally express demonstrated objectives and evaluate controlled variants of the same physical tasks, with detailed analysis on $\pi_{0.5}$ and consistent trends on $\pi_0$-FAST and Cosmos3-Edge. Our study reveals that humans increasingly compress instructions as tasks grow, relying more on contextual grounding and implicit execution structure. Current policies are misaligned with this behavior: they tolerate grounding dependence relatively well, but struggle when task semantics or event structure must be reconstructed. Even after the information gap is resolved, execution remains sensitive to command granularity and invocation state. These findings identify concrete targets for human-centered policy learning and interfaces that better bridge natural human objectives to robot behavior. Project website: this https URL.

</details>

### 304. Connecting the Dots: Enduring Performance Gaps in ASR Systems Between Standard and Minority English Varieties

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17761)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`ASR disparity`、`minority varieties`、`longitudinal`、`equity`

👤 **作者**：Alexander Metzger、…、Farhan Samir

- 🎯 **研究动机**：ASR 的口音性能差异常被当作更大更通用的模型会自行修正的问题——该假设未被纵向检验
- 🔬 **研究方法**：2021-2025 十六个系统×八个口音数据集的纵向评测
- 📌 **结论**：主流美式英语可靠胜任而少数口音承受代际间大幅退化（最坏 +65% 绝对 WER）——标准基准隐含语言政策，差异不随时序收敛（低资源群体的系统性失效）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) system evaluations have consistently revealed performance disparities along lines of race, gender, socioeconomic status, and language variety. While these disparities are well-documented, they are often (implicitly or explicitly) treated as issues that subsequent, larger, and more universal models will correct. We challenge this assumption of steady progress in reducing accent-related performance disparities, conducting a longitudinal evaluation of 16 prominent systems released between 2021-2025 across 8 datasets representing diverse English accent varieties. We find that the performance disparity between Mainstream US English (MUSE) and minority accents is stubborn, not reliably declining as a function of time. Specifically, while systems are reliably performant on MUSE, minority accents are subject to risks of large performance degradations between generations of systems, sometimes worsening by 65% absolute Word Error Rate. We argue that standard ASR benchmarks enact an implicit language policy privileging Mainstream US English varieties. As such, these performance disparities reflect systemic failures of language technology development infrastructure that disproportionately affect underrepresented speaker populations.

</details>

### 305. Agentic4PDM: Host-Governed Evidence Admissibility for Industrial Fault Diagnosis

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17786)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`evidence admissibility`、`host governance`、`industrial diagnosis`、`agent`

👤 **作者**：Nam V. Nguyen、Hoang Van Truong、Hieu D. D.、Duy Khuong Nguyen、Quang Pham、Tung Kieu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM agent 选析工具诊断工业信号——固定规则或训练导引解不了每个信号的歧义，无约束解读又会夸大弱结果或冲突结果
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Agentic4PDM：自适应调查与证据使用权分离——确定性宿主控制器执行契约检查的门控裁决（完整工具批次先承诺后观察、按对标注参照的检查分配证据角色区分佐证与参考）
- 📌 **结论**：免训练诊断+协议合规检查与调查记录——宿主治理的证据准入（窄域但机制完整的 agent 治理样板）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

We study industrial fault diagnosis as a classification task that assigns sensor time-series windows to predefined health states or fault types. Scarce labels and changing operating conditions make repeated training of specialized classifiers costly. Yet structures such as periodicity, trends, and transients can remain diagnostically informative and be examined through reusable analytical tools. LLM agents can select and interpret these tools, but fixed rules or training-derived guidance may not resolve each signal's ambiguity, while unconstrained interpretation may overstate weak or conflicting results. We introduce Agentic4PDM, a framework that separates adaptive investigation from control over evidence use. LLM agents compare each signal with labeled references from every candidate class, identify unresolved distinctions, and propose follow-up measurements. A deterministic software controller, called the host, enforces contract-checked gated adjudication (CCGA): it checks requests, requires commitment to a complete tool batch before results are observed, and assigns evidence roles based on tool checks against labeled references. These roles distinguish corroborative support from advisory information. The Judge Agent proposes a diagnosis, while the controller checks protocol compliance and records the investigation. Agentic4PDM supports training-free diagnosis using pretrained representations and labeled references without task-specific diagnostic models, and a standard setting that additionally investigates trained-model predictions. Experiments on selected bearing and motor diagnosis challenges and an unfiltered industrial test set supplied by our customer show gains in aggregate macro F1 over the evaluated diagnostic and agentic baselines. Ablations further support the value of the governed workflow.

</details>

### 306. BRIDGE: Selective Boundary Repair with Dependency Graph Evidence in Multi-Agent Systems

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17805)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`multi-agent systems`、`handoff repair`、`dependency graph`、`runtime control`

👤 **作者**：Encheng Su、…、Aoran Wang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：协作工作流的系统性瓶颈是断裂交接（关键上下文被遗漏/错路/忽视）而每个 agent 各自输出局部合理
- 🔬 **研究方法**：BRIDGE：交接修复=显式依赖图上的选择性干预——反事实交接扰动提供经验监督的归因校准管线+交互轨迹编译出处图定位未解依赖+经验门权衡增益与破坏风险、形式局部后置条件满足才执行
- 📌 **结论**：SILO-Bench +12.3pp、TeamBench +11.1pp 且成本增加极小——可验证运行时控制修复 MAS 交接（协作接口完整性）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Multi-agent collaboration has emerged as a critical paradigm for tackling complex, long-horizon tasks; however, while coordination protocols continue to advance, systemic failures at the inter-agent collaboration interface remain a pervasive bottleneck. In collaborative workflows, systems frequently suffer from \emph{broken handoffs}---where critical task context is omitted, misrouted, or ignored despite each agent emitting locally plausible responses. To address this challenge, we present \method, a verifiable runtime control framework that treats handoff repair as selective intervention over explicit dependency graphs. Central to \method is an integrated fault-attribution and calibration pipeline, \dataset, which decouples execution dependencies and injects counterfactual handoff perturbations to provide empirical supervision for runtime intervention. Leveraging this diagnostic foundation, \method dynamically compiles observable interaction traces into a provenance graph to pinpoint unresolved dependencies, without accessing private agent states. It then evaluates candidate repairs through an empirical gate that weighs expected gains against disruption risks, and executes repairs only when formal local postconditions hold. Extensive evaluations across demanding benchmarks demonstrate that \method boosts task success by up to 12.3 absolute percentage points on SILO-BENCH and 11.1 on TeamBench. Across both backbones, \method maintains strict cost-effectiveness, adding as little as 2.0\% marginal token overhead. Furthermore, on 562 handoff-relevant traces from the MAST benchmark, \method achieves 69.8\% F1 in a single pass, establishing a reliable operational boundary for verifiable coordination repair.

</details>

### 307. Perturbation, Not Provenance: Why Activation Steering Does Not Make a Robust Text Watermark

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17810)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`watermark`、`activation steering`、`robustness`、`negative result`

👤 **作者**：Sergey Pletenev

- 🎯 **研究动机**：激活空间的线性方向因果且普适地影响行为——沿语义方向转向再探测重编码文本激活似乎可作鲁棒文本水印
- 🔬 **研究方法**：两模型族×四方向（正式度/情感/复杂度/随机对照）×两种改写攻击（句级/段级）的检测对照
- 📌 **结论**：匹配流畅度下 KGW 句级改写 AUC 0.81 vs 最佳转向方向 0.65；随机方向与有意义方向同样可检（信号非语义）——激活转向不构成鲁棒水印（转向应用边界的负结果）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Linear directions in the activation space of large language models causally and universally influence model behavior. This suggests that they might serve as a basis for a robust text watermark. The idea is to steer the generation along a specific semantic direction and then recover the mark by probing the activations of the re-encoded text. We test this hypothesis by conducting a series of experiments. We vary the strength of the steering and use four different directions (formality, sentiment, complexity, and a random control) across two different model families. We also test two paraphrasing attacks: a sentence-level one and a stronger paragraph-level one. The results show that activation-steering watermarks are outperformed by a standard logit-based watermark (KGW) in terms of detection accuracy. At a matched level of fluency (within 1.15 times the original text's perplexity), KGW achieved an AUC of 0.81 against a sentence-level paraphraser, while the best steering direction only reached 0.65. A stronger paragraph-level paraphraser lowered both (0.65 for KGW and 0.59 for steering), narrowing the gap but not reversing it. On Qwen, it left the two indistinguishable but at near-chance levels for both. We further showed that the detectability provided by steering was not semantic: a random direction was just as detectable as a meaningful one. The signal was independent of which layer was probed and whether a surface feature control was used. We conclude that, in this case, semantic activation does not encode a localized, recoverable semantic mark, but rather a global, content-independent shift that can only be strongly detected when the text is degraded.

</details>

### 308. Empowering Edge Agents: A Systematic Analysis of Edge-Cloud Collaboration in LLM Agent Execution

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17816)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`edge-cloud`、`agent execution`、`privacy exposure`、`benchmark`

👤 **作者**：Pengcheng Huang、…、Maosong Sun

- 🎯 **研究动机**：长时程 agent 的云参与贯穿执行而非单次决策——交错参与的轨迹级效应（性能/资源/隐私）未系统理解
- 🔬 **研究方法**：EC2-Bench：可执行数字工作区任务+统一评框架（任务性能/资源/隐私暴露）下多种边云协作形式的实证研究
- 📌 **结论**：协作在三者间取得有利平衡；边缘变强时性能趋同但隐私暴露仍强依赖策略——边云 agent 执行的隐私-能力权衡（架构层的隐私测量）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Edge-cloud collaboration combines the lower inference overhead and greater data locality of edge-side execution with the stronger capabilities of cloud-hosted models. However, as LLM agents increasingly operate in long-horizon scenarios, cloud participation becomes interleaved throughout execution rather than a one-shot decision for each query. Such interleaved participation introduces trajectory-level effects that remain insufficiently understood. To address this gap, we introduce EC2-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating edge-cloud collaboration in long-horizon agent execution. It provides executable digital-workspace agent tasks and a unified evaluation framework covering task performance, resource usage, and privacy exposure. Using this benchmark, we empirically study diverse forms of edge-cloud collaboration. Our study reveals three findings. First, collaboration achieves a favorable balance among task performance, resource usage, and privacy exposure compared with edge-only and cloud-only execution. Second, as edge models become stronger, collaborative execution approaches cloud-only performance, and performance differences across strategies shrink, while privacy exposure remains strongly strategy-dependent. Third, stronger edge capability shifts preferred collaboration from externally controlled cloud participation and direct cloud execution toward more edge-controlled collaboration. Further analyses identify cloud-invocation decision-making and instruction following as important edge-side abilities for effective collaboration. These findings provide a systematic understanding of edge-cloud collaboration in long-horizon agent execution and insights for designing future edge agents that effectively collaborate with cloud models. Code and resources are available at this https URL.

</details>

### 309. Benchmarking and Training Reward Models for Cross-Source Evidence Grounding in Search Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17827)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`reward model`、`evidence grounding`、`search agent`、`citation`

👤 **作者**：Haochen Zhao、…、Sujian Li

- 🎯 **研究动机**：搜索 agent 训练评测重最终答案——引用支撑不建立来源独立性，也不处理冲突证据的归因裁决
- 🔬 **研究方法**：CSEG：完整搜索轨迹上的六层决策树评据（含跨源冲突解决与细微归因）+CSEG-Bench 与可训奖励模型（九条规范路径 1,160 条验证轨迹，重写真实检索池答案缓解自然轨迹偏斜）
- 📌 **结论**：前沿 judge 独立复现金路径+人工验证——跨源证据接地的分层信号（搜索 agent 的证据完整性训练基础）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Existing approaches to training and evaluating search agents focus heavily on final-answer quality, largely neglecting how generated answers map back to retrieved evidence. Recent methods track citations to enforce grounding, but citation support alone does not establish source independence or whether an answer appropriately handles conflicting evidence. We argue that credibility in open-web retrieval depends on principled cross-source evidence grounding that accounts for source independence and systematically attributes and adjudicates conflicting evidence, while hierarchical judgments of these grounding decisions provide a unified signal for training and evaluation. To this end, we propose an evaluation framework, Cross-Source Evidence Grounding (CSEG), that employs a fine-grained, six-layer decision tree over the complete search trajectory. We introduce CSEG-Bench and trainable reward models to provide targeted supervision following this framework. Our approach helps eliminating the severe data skew of natural trajectories by providing targeted coverage over complex, deep-layer decision paths, such as cross-source conflict resolution and nuanced attribution, that standard rollouts fail to capture. Specifically, (1) we define the hierarchy at label, node, and path levels; (2) we construct 1,160 verified trajectories over nine canonical paths by rewriting answers on real retrieved document pools, with independent frontier judges reproducing the entire gold path and human experts deciding inclusion; (3) we train CSEG-RM, which reaches 83.9% exact accuracy on our benchmark while lying on the performance-token efficiency Pareto frontier. Experiments that use this reward alone to supervise a frozen-retrieval answer policy raise the independently judged mean label from 2.83 to 4.19, and show that learning the hierarchy generalizes across layers at a fraction of frontier-judge cost.

</details>

### 310. Prefilling-dLLM v2: Learned Chunk Routing for Long-Context Diffusion Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17868)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`dLLM`、`long context`、`chunk routing`、`sparse attention`

👤 **作者**：Qi Han、Jing Xiong、Ziyuan Zhang、Ngai Wong

- 🎯 **研究动机**：dLLM 每个去噪步对全上下文双向注意——长上下文使每步昂贵，且只在推理期稀疏化会让模型从未在其运行的稀疏模式下训练过
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Prefilling-dLLM v2：继续训练使 dLLM 用原生 mask token 路由——每块尾附 mask+学习角色偏移标记路由槽，1/4 层查询保留双向局部窗与 top-K 块，其余层双向滑窗
- 📌 **结论**：Dream-7B 16K→128K 三个 RULER 任务全领先（128K 均分超 NSA 7.4 点），解码比全稠密快 172×——dLLM 长上下文的原生路由（扩散模型基础设施）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Diffusion language models apply bidirectional attention over the whole context at every denoising step, so a longer context makes each step substantially more expensive. Sparsifying only at inference leaves the pretrained weights unchanged, so the model is never trained under the sparse pattern it runs with. We propose Prefilling-dLLM v2, which continues training a diffusion language model so that it routes with its native mask token. One mask is appended at the end of each chunk, and a learned role offset marks it as a routing slot. On every fourth layer, a query keeps a bidirectional local window and the top-K chunks scored from that mask; the other layers use a bidirectional sliding window. Starting from Dream-v0-Base-7B extended to 16K with YaRN, we compare Prefilling-dLLM v2 with bidirectional NSA, DSA, and SWA under the same layout, key budget, and dense initialization, using the same Fast-dLLM decoder. With the YaRN factor scaled to the evaluation length, Prefilling-dLLM v2 leads the other sparse methods on all three RULER tasks from 16K to 128K. Its mean accuracy at 128K is 7.4 points above NSA, the next sparse model, while DSA and SWA remain near zero. At the same length, decoding is 172x faster than with full dense attention.

</details>

### 311. Interaction-as-a-Judge

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17895)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`evaluation`、`computer-use agent`、`interactive judging`、`coding agent`

👤 **作者**：Wayne Chi、…、Cho-Jui Hsieh

- 🎯 **研究动机**：coding agent 产出交互应用——人类交互式评估而自动评测只做代码分析与单测，judge 会漏用户一眼可见的失败
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Interaction-as-a-Judge：CUA 直接与应用交互；6,858 对人类偏好应用+agent 轨迹与 222 个带人工验证需求标签的应用两数据集
- 📌 **结论**：偏好预测 65.6%→69.1%、需求验证 77.0%→81.6%，推理时修复 75.5% 破损需求（纯代码 55.4%）——交互式评估范式与交互质量缩放

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Coding agents are tasked with producing sophisticated interactive applications such as websites, desktop applications, and games. While humans assess these applications interactively, current automated LLM evaluations primarily focus on code analysis and unit tests. This mismatch can cause LLM judges to miss failures that are immediately apparent to human users. We study Interaction-as-a-Judge, an evaluation paradigm where computer-use agents (CUAs) directly interact with an application. We demonstrate that Interaction-as-a-Judge can improve both \emph{evaluation} and \emph{generation} of interactive applications. To this end, we collect and release two complementary datasets: $6,858$ pairs of applications with human preferences and corresponding agent traces, and $222$ applications with human-verified requirement labels. Relative to standard non-interactive approaches, we find that Interaction-as-a-Judge improves evaluation in terms of predicting human preferences ($65.6\% \to 69.1\%$) and requirement verification ($77.0\% \to 81.6\%$). We also observe scaling benefits: across both preference alignment and requirement verification, higher-quality interactions improve performance. Finally, through both inference- and training-time experiments, we demonstrate that Interaction-as-a-Judge can also improve the quality of generated artifacts. At inference time, Interaction-as-a-Judge repairs $75.5\%$ of broken requirements within five attempts, compared to $55.4\%$ for code-only self-feedback. At training time, using Interaction-as-a-Judge as a reward signal improves win-rate over the base model by $6.7$pp while reducing both generated code length by $35.7\%$ and build failure rates by $7.2$pp compared to a code-only reward signal.

</details>

### 312. Batch-Paced: Fixed-Budget Batch Decoding in Discrete Diffusion Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17917)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`dLLM`、`batch decoding`、`serving`、`compute allocation`

👤 **作者**：Shuai Zhang、Zhanchao Zhou、Huachuan Qiu、Anqi Li、Zhenzhong Lan

- 🎯 **研究动机**：dLLM 并行生成有助高效推理——但既有解码方法只优化单序列，多请求批次下解码算力分配未研究
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Batch-Paced 免训练：固定预算下跨请求联合分配解码算力（分析解码轨迹发现增益主要来自提升低置信请求的已提交 token 置信度）
- 📌 **结论**：两 dLLM 骨干×五基准及混合批次下匹配预算提升批次性能且解码时间有竞争力——dLLM 批量服务的算力调度（扩散模型服务栈）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

As generative language models become more capable and widely used, inference demand grows, making efficient serving increasingly important. Discrete diffusion language models (dLLMs) offer a promising path to more efficient inference through parallel token generation. Prior dLLM decoding methods, however, focus on improving inference efficiency for individual sequences. We formulate fixed-budget dLLM batch decoding and introduce Batch-Paced, a training-free method that jointly optimizes the allocation of decoding compute across requests to improve overall batch quality. Across two dLLM backbones, five benchmarks and their representative mixtures, Batch-Paced improves batch performance under matched budgets; gains generally grow with batch size and largely persist across decoding configurations. Analysis of requests' decoding trajectories suggests that these gains arise largely by raising the committed-token confidence of lower-confidence requests. We further show that Batch-Paced's batch decode time remains competitive and that adding a decoding-depth allowance during Batch-Paced decoding can shorten it further at a modest performance cost.

</details>

### 313. Provenance and Authorization in Autobiographical Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17935)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`provenance`、`authorization`、`autobiographical LM`、`noninterference`

👤 **作者**：Faruk Alpay、Taylan Alpay

- 🎯 **研究动机**：自传式语言模型调用含观察/引述/情感报告/早期输出的档案——密码学认证保档案完整却让来源语义混同：叙事内容不得静默变成同意或改变授权决策规则
- 🔬 **研究方法**：类型化记录+认证证明+独立策略通道+可采视图的形式化；标准完整性归约与 noninterference 论证+冻结公共模型 32 全合成案例 576 配对条件的可复现试点
- 📌 **结论**：明确精确决策保持摘要所需信息、被动历史与不动点收敛不必识别偏好动作——自传式记忆的计算归因与授权（agent 自我档案的治理形式化）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Autobiographical language models may draw on archives containing observations, quotations, emotional reports, and earlier model outputs. Cryptographic authentication can preserve an archive while leaving these sources semantically conflated. We formulate this gap as a computer-security problem: narrative content must not silently become consent or alter an authorized decision rule. We define typed records, authenticated attestations, a separate policy channel, and admissible views. Under explicit assumptions, we give a standard integrity reduction and a noninterference argument, characterize the information required by exact decision-preserving summaries, and show why passive history and fixed-point convergence need not identify a preferred action. Alpay Algebra supplies a comparative language for iterative memory without being a premise of the proofs. Sociology of religious affiliation, source-monitoring psychology, and self-determination theory motivate distinctions in a bounded Latter-day Saint vignette. A separate, reproducible pilot uses a frozen public language model on 32 wholly synthetic cases across 576 paired conditions. It compares flattened records, source labels, and an explicit admission gate under contradictory summary repetition. The study concerns computational attribution and authorization, not the motives or psychology of the people in the vignette.

</details>

### 314. HaloBlock: Faster and Better Decoding for Diffusion Large Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17948)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`dLLM`、`decoding`、`halo mechanism`、`efficiency`

👤 **作者**：Zhaohui Zheng、Chenhang He、Jinrui Zhang、Ming-Ming Cheng、Lei Zhang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：dLLM 推理两问题：解码难度由块右侧 token 主导、块级解码固有边界上下文不连续
- 🔬 **研究方法**：HaloBlock：双向累积熵刻画块解码难度，按难度自适应在块两侧分配双向 halo 区域缓解边界不连续
- 📌 **结论**：LLaDA-8B/Dream-7B/LLaDA-1.5 上数学与代码基准一致提精度且推理更高效——难度感知的 dLLM 解码（扩散解码质量-效率）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>
