Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) have attracted increasing research attention due to their bidirectional attention and arbitrary order decoding. However, there are two issues in dLLMs inference: the decoding difficulty is dominated by the tokens on the right side of the block, and the block-wise decoding inherently suffers from boundary contextual discontinuity. To tackle these limitations, we propose HaloBlock, a difficulty-aware adaptive halo mechanism for dLLM inference. We first leverage bidirectional cumulative entropy to characterize block decoding difficulty. Then, based on the bidirectional decoding difficulty, we adaptively allocate bidirectional halo regions on both sides of each block to mitigate boundary contextual discontinuity. The difficulty-aware design of HaloBlock smooths cross-block context propagation and lowers decoding difficulty for hard tokens, yielding better generation quality and faster convergence. Evaluated on LLaDA-8B-Instruct, Dream-7B-Instruct and LLaDA-1.5 across mathematical reasoning and code generation benchmarks, HaloBlock demonstrates consistent accuracy improvements and higher inference efficiency. Source codes are publicly available at this https URL.

</details>

### 315. COMPASS: Contextual Moment Prior Alignment for Generalizable AIGI Detection

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18034)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`AIGI detection`、`generalization`、`subspace adaptation`、`deepfake`

👤 **作者**：Zhan Yang、…、Ming-Hsuan Yang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：可泛化的 AI 生成图像与深伪检测受不对称现象制约——有限分布适配会退化视觉基础模型的可迁移表示
- 🔬 **研究方法**：COMPASS：小型无标注语义代理集估计目标激活统计，把激活协方差纳入预训练权重分解——目标相关表示作稳定语义参照、残差适配器学伪造相关变化
- 📌 **结论**：跨数据集/跨方法/联合/开放世界跨生成器评测 SOTA——数据感知子空间适配的泛化检测（生成内容真实性）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Generalizable AI-generated image (AIGI) and deepfake detection remain challenging due to the asymmetry phenomenon, where adaptation to limited training distributions can degrade the transferable representations of Vision Foundation Models (VFMs). Recent subspace adaptation methods mitigate this issue by freezing high-magnitude principal components while adapting the residual subspace. However, such magnitude-based decomposition is data-agnostic and does not explicitly account for target-relevant semantics, potentially leading to semantic misalignment. We propose COMPASS (COntextual Moment Prior Alignment for Subspace Separation), a data-aware subspace adaptation framework that uses a small unlabeled Semantic Proxy Set to estimate target activation statistics. COMPASS incorporates activation covariance into the decomposition of pre-trained weights, preserving target-relevant representations as a stable semantic reference while the residual adapter learns forgery-related variations. The proxy calibration requires no gradient-based optimization. Extensive experiments demonstrate strong generalization across cross-dataset, cross-method, joint cross-dataset-method, and open-world cross-generator evaluations, achieving state-of-the-art performance on both Deepfake and AIGI detection benchmarks.

</details>

### 316. Failure Recovery via Belief Updates and World Modeling for Long-Horizon Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18082)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`failure recovery`、`belief state`、`long-horizon agent`、`world model`

👤 **作者**：Xiyang Wu、…、Dinesh Manocha

- 🎯 **研究动机**：长时程 agent 常难以从根因在数步之前的失败中恢复——局部反思与回溯不够
- 🔬 **研究方法**：BELIEFWEAVER：跨尝试持久证据+修复方向前瞻推理的关系信念状态（边界/尝试/观察/检查点）+隐式世界模型评估修复假设并蒸馏进 9B 学生
- 📌 **结论**：三终端基准总分 +5.1pp、蒸馏学生 +9.8pp 且教师 API 成本降 54.8%——持久证据与前瞻推理的失败恢复（agent 可靠性基础设施）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Long-horizon agents often struggle to recover from failures whose causes lie several steps earlier, beyond what local reflection or backtracking can resolve. We introduce BELIEFWEAVER, a framework that combines persistent evidence across attempts with prospective reasoning over repair directions. It maintains a relational belief state over frontiers, attempts, observations, and checkpoints. Execution evidence updates related frontier priorities across attempts and rollback, while diagnostic evidence gates execution. An implicit world model (IWM) evaluates repair hypotheses, predicts progress, and identifies relations among frontiers to guide exploration. We further distill these recurring IWM reasoning components into a 9B student through supervised fine-tuning and on-policy distillation (OPD). Across three terminal-based benchmarks, BELIEFWEAVER improves aggregate final score by 5.1 percentage points over native Terminus-2 continuation, with positive trends in exploration efficiency. The distilled 9B student improves final score by 9.8 percentage points over the reported native baseline while reducing estimated teacher API cost by 54.8% relative to the matched teacher. These results show that persistent evidence and prospective reasoning can improve recovery in long-horizon agent tasks while substantially reducing reliance on teacher-model inference.

</details>

### 317. AReaL-RSI: Adaptive Scaling and Coordination of Recursive Self-Improvement

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18083)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`recursive self-improvement`、`scaling`、`research agents`、`coordination`

👤 **作者**：Wenhao Zhou、…、Binhang Yuan

- 🎯 **研究动机**：RSI 扩展需额外算力产出有用研究进展——更多 researcher agent 探索更多假设，但早期评分可误代表前景、共享发现丢失适用条件
- 🔬 **研究方法**：AReaL-RSI：骨干权重冻结的持久 agent 研究团队——leader 选任务适配方向、条件记忆共享区分未解问题与已支撑发现、逐战役证据式审查修订取向/知识/技能
- 📌 **结论**：Terminal Bench +2.2pp、KernelBench 加速 +32.2-36.8%，优于原生扩展——RSI 的自适应扩展与协调（自改进系统的组织化）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Scaling recursive self-improvement requires additional computation to produce useful research progress in reaching persistent objectives. Intuitively, more researcher agents can explore more hypotheses, but early evaluation scores might misrepresent their promise, shared findings could lose their applicability conditions, and useful procedures could remain confined to completed investigations. In this paper, we present AReaL-RSI, a framework for organizing a persistent research team of agents without updating backbone LLM weights. We formulate the scaling problem in terms of evaluated outcomes and whole-system resource expenditure. Concretely, a leader agent selects task-appropriate research orientations and guides ongoing investigations; conditional memory sharing distinguishes unresolved problems from supported findings and enables recipient adaptation. During recursive evolution, after each campaign, evidence-based review revises researcher orientation, knowledge, and skills for later campaigns. Experiments compare AReaL-RSI with Codex-Goal on downstream harness optimization, GPU kernel optimization, and AI research tasks using two backbones. Under the reported resource allocations, AReaL-RSI improves Terminal Bench performance by 2.2 percentage points for each backbone and increases mean KernelBench speedup by 32.2-36.8%; further scalability comparisons favor the design of AReaL-RSI over native scaling: combining experience sharing with specialized researcher roles yields 1.9-27.5% lower kernel latency than independent exploration and 1.3-33.9% lower latency than sharing alone across all seven comparable task-backbone pairs.

</details>

### 318. ResearchFlow-Eval: Tracing the Execution Gap in End-to-End Multi-Claim Autoresearch

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18088)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`autoresearch`、`evaluation`、`execution gap`、`multi-claim`

👤 **作者**：Peng Jiang、Dongyang Li、Quanying Liu、Xiaoxuan Jia

- 🎯 **研究动机**：autoresearch 系统承接整个研究项目——须在连续多阶段工作流中追求多个相互依赖的主张并随证据修订实验，而既有评测只打孤立任务或最终工件
- 🔬 **研究方法**：ResearchFlow-Eval：以已发表锚论文接地，阶段特定评分细则分解各研究阶段，主张级多声明完成与跨尝试修订细查
- 📌 **结论**：所有评测系统的实验执行持续落后于设计与规划、重复尝试很少带来可测改进——端到端 autoresearch 的执行缺口（自动化研究的过程级评估）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Autoresearch systems increasingly undertake entire research projects. In practice, scientific research requires systems to pursue multiple interdependent claims through a continuous, multi-stage workflow and revise experiments as evidence accumulates. Existing evaluations of autoresearch systems, however, typically score isolated tasks or final artifacts, leaving these process-level capabilities largely unmeasured. We therefore introduce ResearchFlow-Eval, a fine-grained framework for assessing continuous scientific execution and iteration at both the stage and claim levels. Each evaluation is grounded in a published anchor paper, from which we derive stage-specific rubrics that decompose each research stage into dimensions and separately scored rubric items. Using these rubrics, we score each research stage and examine experimental execution in detail, focusing on multi-claim completion and revisions across attempts. We apply the evaluation framework to general-purpose agent harnesses, skill-augmented harnesses, and systems with research-specific orchestration. Across all evaluated systems, experimental execution consistently lags behind design and planning. Within execution, systems struggle to produce high-quality results and meet claim requirements, and repeated attempts rarely yield measurable improvements. Moreover, neither skill augmentation nor research-specific orchestration reliably yields higher scores. ResearchFlow-Eval thus extends autoresearch evaluation beyond isolated, single-objective runs to continuous workflows in which systems pursue multiple claims and refine their work across iterations.

</details>

### 319. AM-Bench: Understanding Agents under Task Underspecification across Models and Harnesses

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18090)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`task underspecification`、`agent benchmark`、`model-harness`、`clarification`

👤 **作者**：Kaiyu Huang、Yiting Li、Angyang Gu、Pinjia He、Minchen Yu、Qingjiang Shi

- 🎯 **研究动机**：模糊或不完整的任务规范复杂化执行与澄清——agent 对信息缺口的反应跨模型与 harness 未被刻画
- 🔬 **研究方法**：AM-Bench：48 任务×七变体（目标/上下文/约束的缺失或模糊），5 模型×4 harness×20,160 次运行
- 📌 **结论**：目标缺口碍完成、上下文缺口增 token、约束缺口弱合规；缺失比模糊更伤成功率；完整信息下的优势不必持续——信息条件×模型×harness 的联合评测与澄清时机

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Ambiguous or incomplete task specifications complicate both execution and clarification for language-model agents. Yet agents' responses to these information gaps remain poorly characterized across models and execution harnesses. We present AM-Bench, a benchmark built from 48 tasks adapted from Terminal-Bench 2.0 and Harness-Bench. Each task includes a fully specified reference and six variants spanning ambiguous or missing goals, context, and constraints. We evaluate every pairing of five models and four harnesses over 20,160 runs. Our results reveal distinct execution deficits: goal gaps hinder completion, context gaps increase token use, and constraint gaps weaken compliance without necessarily preventing completion. Missingness lowers success more than ambiguity in all three domains. These effects also reshape system comparisons: advantages under complete information need not persist, and rankings depend on model $\times$ harness pairings and the evaluation criterion -- completion, success, or repeat consistency. Estimates based on observed clarification requests and matched fully specified runs further suggest that clarification could recover unfinished work while leaving most constraint violations unresolved. Together, these findings motivate joint model-harness evaluation across information conditions and targeted clarification of unresolved requirements before dependent actions. To support future research on agents under task underspecification, we provide the AM-Bench project repository at this https URL.

</details>

### 320. From Spectral Saliency to Finite Deletion: Evaluating Selective Language Model Edits

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18098)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`model editing`、`selective deletion`、`spectral saliency`、`interval screening`

👤 **作者**：Ibne Farabi Shihab、Joyanta Jyoti Mondal

- 🎯 **研究动机**：删除奇异分量是从语言模型移除能力的精确方式——一阶谱显著性只预测局部损失变化而删除是有限干预
- 🔬 **研究方法**：误差分解（跨项目分裂的导数转移+删除路径上的非线性响应）+外区间筛查保留所有可行候选；Mistral-7B 删 1024/7168 坐标以算术为目标保持代码与问答
- 📌 **结论**：校准区间覆盖 968/1024 评估掩码、三次选定编辑仅一次全过测试约束——选择性模型编辑的预测误差与筛查（能力移除的精度边界）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Deleting singular components offers a precise way to remove a capability from a language model, yet the first-order spectral saliency used to rank components predicts only a local loss change, whereas deletion is a finite intervention. In this paper, we study how prediction error affects selective editing, where selective editing requires degradation of a target task while preserving others. We decompose the error into derivative transfer across item splits and nonlinear response along the deletion path. We establish that outer interval screening retains every feasible candidate when every candidate's intervals contain its finite validation loss changes on every task. We evaluate these effects using one answer-only negative log-likelihood objective for search, validation, and test, with disjoint calibration and evaluation masks. The primary configuration targets arithmetic in Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.3 while preserving code and question answering; extensions cover Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct and reversed target roles. In the primary study, which deletes 1024 coordinates from a support of 7168, calibrated intervals cover 968 of 1024 evaluation masks. Outer screening reaches the direct-selected edit after 25 verification checks, whereas direct verification abstains at a 32-check cap, but it rejects 4 of 74 feasible masks. All three selected edits pass validation, yet only one meets every test constraint. Interval coverage, feasible-candidate retention, and independent test preservation are therefore distinct properties and require separate assessment.

</details>

### 321. Attacking Only Half the Data: Two-Sided Evaluation of AI-Text Detector Robustness

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18151)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`AI-text detection`、`two-sided attack`、`robustness`、`human text`

👤 **作者**：Apu Chandraw Shill、Rabindra Nath Nandi

- 🎯 **研究动机**：AI 文本检测器评测通常只攻击机器文本——人类文本也常被语法修正/格式化/不可见字符插入修改
- 🔬 **研究方法**：双侧协议：两类文本同时施加攻击，两语料×九检测器
- 📌 **结论**：双侧评测下报告的 AUROC 增益消失、被攻击人类文本误判率最高 100%；预处理可逆字符攻击但词级同义替换仍有效且不可逆——检测器鲁棒性的双侧协议

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

We investigate the robustness of widely used AI text detectors under attacks applied to both human and machine text. Prior evaluations typically attack only machine text, overlooking that human text is routinely modified through grammar correction, formatting, or invisible character insertion. We introduce a two-sided protocol that applies attacks to both classes. Across two corpora and nine detectors, reported AUROC gains vanish under two-sided evaluation, with attacked human text misclassified at rates up to 100%. While simple preprocessing reverses character-level attacks, word-level synonym substitution remains effective meaning-preserving, and irreversible.

</details>

### 322. Fan Out or Relay? Ownership-Aware Routing for LLM Agents Querying Siloed Tables

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18157)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`data ownership`、`query routing`、`siloed tables`、`information flow`

👤 **作者**：Zhexi Feng

- 🎯 **研究动机**：组织内一个问题的数据常分属多个所有者（各查己表不交出）——LLM 协调者扇出（并发全员）还是接力（逐个并复用行）在事前都不安全，对错由数据所有权决定
- 🔬 **研究方法**：十三设置×三模型族对照+OAR：先起草纯 schema SQL、数其连接的表属所有者数、读任何私有行之前路由
- 📌 **结论**：扇出在单所有者问题 12/13 更准但对双所有者连接最多答 4.3%；接力靠条件化返回行（藏行则 23.2%→5.1%）——所有权感知的查询前路由

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Inside organizations, a question's data often sit with several owners, who query their own tables but never hand them over. An LLM coordinator can fan out, sending the question to every owner at once, or relay, querying owners in turn and reusing earlier rows. We show that neither is safe in advance, because data ownership decides which one is right. Across thirteen settings on PeopleJoin and BIRD with three model families, fan-out is more accurate in twelve on questions one owner can answer, yet answers at most 4.3% of those joining two owners' tables. The flip follows ownership, not query shape: on BIRD two-table joins, fan-out answers 32-44% when one owner holds both and 1-4% otherwise. Relay wins by conditioning on returned rows, and hiding them from an otherwise identical planner cuts two-owner accuracy from 23.2% to 5.1%. Since ownership is public, we propose Ownership-Aware Routing (OAR), which drafts a schema-only SQL query, counts the owners whose tables it joins, and routes the question before any private row is read. In ten settings OAR is never significantly less accurate than the best fixed protocol, and beats every fixed protocol on PeopleJoin, by up to 9.3 points on held-out organizations.

</details>

### 323. A Few More Tokens Ahead: Reinforcing Parallelism in Diffusion LLMs

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18200)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`dLLM`、`parallel generation`、`RL`、`local potential`

👤 **作者**：Jiahao Kuang、…、Tao Ji

- 🎯 **研究动机**：并行生成被视为 dLLM 固有优势——固定配额评测显示每步 16 token 时数学与代码全面退化至 15% 以下
- 🔬 **研究方法**：RePara：强化短连续 token 块（组相对策略优化）并降低 rollout 与对数概率重算成本
- 📌 **结论**：LLaDA/SDAR 上更低去噪预算下仍提升 GSM8K/MATH-500 且训练步提速 1.96×——dLLM 并行性的强化修复（扩散解码能力基线）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Parallel generation is widely regarded as an inherent advantage of diffusion large language models. Yet our fixed-quota evaluation of four representative models reveals severe degradation across mathematics and code, with all scores below 15% at 16 tokens per step. Token-level probes identify local predictive potential near the observed prefix, especially in mathematical text. We propose RePara, which reinforces short consecutive token blocks through group-relative policy optimization and reduces rollout and log-probability recomputation costs. On LLaDA and SDAR, \ours improves GSM8K and MATH-500 accuracy over JustGRPO under reduced denoising budgets, with a 1.96x training-step speedup in our LLaDA/MATH setup.

</details>

### 324. Holding Agents Accountable with Verifiable Agent Insurance

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18230)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`agent accountability`、`insurance`、`escrow`、`trace verification`

👤 **作者**：Cheng Hua、Taro Tsuchiya、Nicolas Christin、Giulia Fanti

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agent 经常以破坏性方式失当（如删重要数据）——既有工作重预防，事发后如何追偿未被设计
- 🔬 **研究方法**：VAI：提供方对可编程策略承诺财务担保，承诺与保证金托管上不可篡改账本；用户发现违规可促 verifier 审查 harness 共享工具执行路径记录的轨迹，核实即赔付
- 📌 **结论**：三基准上检测 42.2% 含违规运行（受限因授权与安全信息埋在自然语言中），显式结构化输出后提升——agent 问责的经济承诺机制（账本为实现载体）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

While AI agents are useful for many tasks, they regularly misbehave in destructive ways, such as deleting important data. While prior work has focused on preventing such misbehavior, we consider how to recover after an agent misbehaves. We propose verifiable agent insurance (VAI), a framework for agent providers to commit financially to promises, a list of programmatically-specified policies that an agent is meant to follow. These promises are embedded in a smart contract on an immutable ledger (e.g., a blockchain) to prevent tampering, along with a monetary escrow. A user who detects a violation of committed promises can prompt a verifier to review agent execution traces, which are logged through the agent harness's shared tool-execution path, without instrumenting each tool separately. Upon verification of the violation, the agent provider's escrow funds are paid to the reporting user. We instantiate VAI as an end-to-end implementation on an Ethereum layer-2 network, and evaluate its coverage on three prominent agent benchmarks, two of which we re-annotated to obtain task-level policy-compliance labels. Currently, many agents do not emit user authorizations and security-relevant information as explicit, structured data objects -- rather, they embed the data needed to verify policy violations in natural language exchanges. Even so, VAI manages to detect violations in 42.2% of runs containing violations across three benchmarks. By augmenting agents to explicitly emit such objects, violation detection rises to 83.0% of traces. Our findings suggest that for improved auditability, agents should be co-designed with their security policies to emit explicit, structured records of user authorization and security-relevant actions.

</details>

### 325. JevGPT: Thinking Fast and Slow in Autonomous Research

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18272)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`autonomous research`、`dual-system`、`evidence processing`、`agent`

👤 **作者**：Yasheng Sun、…、Jürgen Schmidhuber

- 🎯 **研究动机**：自主研究 agent 须在有限预算内获取并推理证据——相关源可保持未被检视，检索覆盖与支撑研究的证据间存在缺口
- 🔬 **研究方法**：JevGPT 双系统：System Two 定证据目标并整合发现，System One 定位支撑/评估相关性/引导后续检视（独立源判断并行、选中原文进入下一步）
- 📌 **结论**：固定语料问答与文档排序领先且更快更省——高效证据处理是更强自主研究的路径（自动化研究系统的架构基线）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Autonomous research agents must acquire and reason over evidence within a limited budget. Yet relevant sources can remain uninspected or unused, leaving a gap between retrieval coverage and the evidence that informs research. The challenge is to align fast local judgments with changing evidence needs and feed their findings back into research decisions. We introduce JevGPT, a dual-system architecture for autonomous research that couples fast evidence processing with deliberative research planning. System Two sets evidence objectives and integrates findings, while System One locates support, assesses relevance, and guides further inspection. Independent source judgments run in parallel; selected original passages inform the next research step. Experiments on fixed-corpus question answering and document ranking show that JevGPT achieves leading task performance, with faster completion and lower cost than comparably effective research agents. Evidence analysis shows broader source inspection and greater coverage of supporting documents in the research loop, highlighting efficient evidence processing as a route to more capable autonomous research.

</details>

### 326. Output Discipline Sets the Floor: A Falsification-Guided, Model-Agnostic Agent Protocol for Auditable Hypothesis-Driven Decision Making

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18298)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`output discipline`、`falsification protocol`、`auditable decisions`、`agent`

👤 **作者**：Jing Liu、Yongxing Qi、Ping Yao、Ting Wu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：可审计假设驱动决策任务上绑定 LLM 的是什么——两年模型缩放零样本分只动 -0.03~+0.08，输出纪律失效（不终止/格式不符/大方差）在前沿模型持续
- 🔬 **研究方法**：模型无关五阶段闭环+候选×证据矩阵证伪协议（每候选对每证据判定、至少半数候选被证伪才许结论、幸存者单行机器可验证输出）
- 📌 **结论**：效应随零样本输出纪律符号条件化：救回失 disciplined 模型（+0.267）、把高方差模型 SD 0.218→0.007，但伤害已 disciplined 模型——输出纪律设定可审计决策的地板

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

What binds large language models on auditable, hypothesis-driven decision tasks, where every candidate can be checked against the evidence? On the public TCMEval-SDT benchmark for Traditional Chinese Medicine syndrome differentiation (200 cases), the binding constraint is output discipline and reasoning structure, not model capability alone: two years of model scaling move zero-shot scores by only -0.03 to +0.08, while output-discipline failures (non-terminating reasoning, format non-compliance, large run-to-run spread) persist even in frontier models. We test this with a model-agnostic agent framework formalizing decision making as a five-stage closed loop, enforced by a candidate x evidence matrix falsification protocol: every candidate is judged against every evidence item, at least half of the candidates must be falsified before a conclusion is licensed, and survivors are emitted in a machine-verifiable single-line format. Across five base models, the effect is sign-conditional on zero-shot output discipline: it rescues models whose discipline fails (DeepSeek-V4-Flash: +0.267/+0.271, entirely termination rescue), reduces a high-variance model's run-to-run standard deviation from 0.218 to 0.007 (Kimi K3), and significantly costs already-disciplined models (-0.087 to -0.120 on budget-matched Qwen3-4B/32B). Ablations attribute the cost to the candidate-bounded format layer and show the effect is independent of injected knowledge content (protocol-knowledge decoupling). The protocol also transfers to misinformation-robust mathematical reasoning, detecting injected false premises at 89.8% recall with a 6.8% false-positive rate (MCC 0.54; a flag-everything baseline scores 0) and restoring accuracy to the no-misinformation ceiling. Where zero-shot output discipline is weak, the reasoning protocol sets the floor; base-model capability always sets the ceiling.

</details>

### 327. Lightweight but Linguistically Fair? Quantisation Robustness across Bavarian, German, and English

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18343)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`quantisation`、`linguistic fairness`、`low-resource variety`、`robustness`

👤 **作者**：Jophin John、Michael Hoffmann、Piroska Lendvai、Florent Dufour

- 🎯 **研究动机**：训练后量化降内存——压缩是否同等可靠地保留地区语言变体（巴伐利亚语）与标准语的技能未知
- 🔬 **研究方法**：BAVGROUND 三语对齐多选基准×14 个 7B-10B 指令模型：BF16 vs 8-bit GPTQ vs 4-bit GPTQ/AWQ，含校准语言效应与自由生成评测
- 📌 **结论**：8-bit 基本保持三语相对行为；4-bit 改变 11.9-16.0% 决策且集中于本已不确定样本、受影响语言因模型方法而异——量化的跨语言稳健性（低资源变体的部署公平）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Post-training quantisation reduces the memory cost of large language models, but it remains unclear whether compression preserves language skills of regional varieties as reliably as their standard-language counterparts. We study this question for Bavarian, Standard German, and English using BAVGROUND, a multiple-choice benchmark, where 206 source questions are aligned across all three languages. Across 14 open-weight 7B--10B instruction models, we compare BF16 with 8-bit GPTQ and two 4-bit settings, GPTQ and AWQ. Eight-bit GPTQ remains close to BF16 and largely preserves the relative behaviour of the three languages, while 4-bit quantisation was found to be much less stable. The two 4-bit methods reduce average accuracy by about 1.4--1.5 percentage points and change 11.9--16.0% of BF16 decisions, with changes concentrated on examples where the BF16 model is already uncertain. This instability does not translate into a consistent Bavarian disadvantage. Although Bavarian, a low-resource dialect, has lower baseline accuracy than German and English, the language most affected at 4 bits varies across models and quantisation methods. Calibration further shapes these cross-language effects: the relative Bavarian--German effect changes with calibration language, while changing the calibration source has little average effect on GPTQ-4 but can affect AWQ-4 much more strongly. Free-form evaluation on five models further shows that 4-bit compression can degrade generated responses even in settings where multiple-choice accuracy changes only modestly, while 8-bit GPTQ remains comparatively stable. Overall, our results show that quantisation robustness for regional language varieties depends on the model, quantisation method, calibration data, and evaluation setting, and cannot be measured by aggregate multilingual accuracy alone.

</details>

### 328. PRISM: Calibrating Black-Box Judges with Counterfactual Probes

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18389)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`judge calibration`、`counterfactual probes`、`scoring bias`、`rubric-free`

👤 **作者**：Kakei Yamamoto、So Kuroki

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM judge 分数与人类判断系统性不一致——对显式施加的评估偏差的响应能否用于校正隐式评分偏差
- 🔬 **研究方法**：PRISM：固定被评内容、测属性强调/弱化时的分数变化（代理 Jacobian），岭回归学人类标签上的基线分校正——免评分细则只用总分
- 📌 **结论**：SummEval/HANNA 上显著提升与人类排名的一致性并与 rubric 法竞争；学得系数揭示跨 judge 的共享与相反校正方向——黑盒 judge 的反事实探针校准

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language models are increasingly used as judges to reduce reliance on costly human evaluation, yet their scores can systematically disagree with human judgments. We ask whether responses to an explicitly imposed evaluative bias can help correct a judge's implicit scoring biases. We introduce PRISM (Probe-based Residual Inference of Scoring Misalignment), which holds the evaluated content fixed and measures score changes when attributes are emphasized or de-emphasized. A local sensitivity model motivates representing these responses as a surrogate Jacobian. Ridge regression on human labels learns a correction to the baseline score. The resulting scoring protocol is rubric-free: it uses overall scores without requesting criterion-level ratings. In the main Jev and Sol comparisons on SummEval summarization and HANNA story evaluation, PRISM and its learned-baseline variant significantly improve agreement with human rankings over uncalibrated scores while remaining competitive with rubric-based methods. A controlled SummEval comparison yields similar agreement for rubric-derived quality probes and style probes, supporting calibration without reproducing rubric criteria. The learned coefficients reveal shared and opposing correction directions across judges.

</details>

### 329. Multi-Stream Residual Computation in Diffusion Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18407)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`dLLM`、`residual streams`、`routing`、`architecture`

👤 **作者**：Ekaterina Alimaskina、Suren Kazarian、Gleb Molodtsov、Aleksandr Beznosikov

- 🎯 **研究动机**：dLLM 的 Transformer 主干用单一残差流同时承载可见证据与演化的掩码预测——两角色是否该分离
- 🔬 **研究方法**：多残差流+深度持续与自适应路由：预训练任务上双流降重建困惑度 5.71%；因果干预显示可见位与掩码位学出不同读/写路由模式
- 📌 **结论**：额外流保留早期 token 表示供后期复用同时证据在掩码位累积，机制跨规模复现——dLLM 的架构-机制分析（扩散模型表示动力学）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Diffusion language models reconstruct masked tokens in parallel, offering an alternative to autoregressive generation. Yet their Transformer backbones typically carry visible evidence and evolving masked predictions through a single residual stream. We equip masked diffusion models with multiple residual streams that persist through depth and adaptive routing between them, then investigate how they support these complementary roles. On the pretraining task, two streams reduce reconstruction perplexity by 5.71% on validation data and 5.13% on average across external corpora compared with standard residual connections. We observe that, without predefined roles, visible and masked positions learn distinct routing patterns. Causal interventions show that this distinction organizes read and write routing, while token-specific write strength remains important within each role. Guided by these findings, a model trained with role-conditioned routing recovers half the validation gain from adding a second stream. State interventions explain the benefit of the additional stream: it preserves early token representations for later reuse while evidence accumulates at masked positions. This mechanism recurs at a larger model scale, showing how persistent streams support context preservation alongside prediction refinement.

</details>

### 330. Do Factuality Methods Make LLMs More Factual? Reproducibility and Fair Comparison

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18415)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`factuality`、`reproducibility`、`reward hacking`、`negative result`

👤 **作者**：Pratuat Amatya、Vinay Setty、V Venktesh、Avishek Anand

- 🎯 **研究动机**：事实性对齐方法各自在自家基准上声称改进——从未被独立复现且基座/数据/评测/judge/指标互不可比
- 🔬 **研究方法**：五种事实性对齐方法（PPO/GRPO/Dr.GRPO/DPO/KTO）的首次独立复现与共同基准公平比较
- 📌 **结论**：无一完整复现、共同基准上无一显著超基座；奖励实际优化格式/长度/简洁而非事实性；评测指标本身也是问题——事实性方法文献的可复现性危机

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large Language Models (LLMs) are aligned to human preferences, after pre-training and supervised fine-tuning steps. However, standard RL alignment steps such as RLHF do not focus on factuality of LLMs, hence they may generate non-factual information, which could result in unintended consequences. In response, a number of factuality alignment approaches have been proposed in the literature which claim to improve factuality of LLMs on their own benchmarks. However, these methods have not been reproduced independently and are also not comparable since each uses a different base model, training data, evaluation set, judge, and metric. In this paper, we present the first independent reproduction of five factuality alignment methods, which span on-policy RL (PPO, GRPO, and Dr.~GRPO) and offline preference optimization (DPO and KTO), and compare them on a common benchmark with common evaluation metrics. We found that none of these methods reproduces in full, and on the common benchmark none of the methods significantly outperforms the base model. On closer inspection, the rewards optimize for format, length, or brevity rather than factuality and relevance, whereas the choice between on-policy and offline training matters little. Furthermore, the evaluation metrics and benchmarks are also part of the problem since they do not measure relevance of text to the prompt. Since there is no ground truth associated with the prompts, the reliance on LLM judges and retrieval for verification also makes the evaluation fragile. We release our code, data, and checkpoints.

</details>

### 331. Receiver Decides Agent-to-Agent Negotiations

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18424)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`agent negotiation`、`resource holding`、`persuasion`、`fine-tuning effects`

👤 **作者**：Fardeen Sadab、Alif Hasnain、Afsana Shehrin、Md. Azam Hossain、Abu Raihan Mostofa Kamal

- 🎯 **研究动机**：一个 agent 向另一个请求资金时谁决定转手多少——1,049 场循环赛揭示持币方主导（47% vs 2% 方差）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：15 agent 循环赛+首轮拒绝率排序（Spearman -0.73）+说服语料微调与从不失手持币者蒸馏的方向性操纵实验
- 📌 **结论**：几乎无拒绝的说服微调使 Qwen3-8B 作为持有者交出 84% 资金；蒸馏从不失手者使 4B 损失 95%→1%——资源持有角色的 agent 脆弱性应被评测（说服攻击的资产侧）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

When one language-model agent asks another for money, which of the two decides how much changes hands? We ran a round-robin tournament of 1,049 negotiations among fifteen agents. In each game a requester tries to raise money from a holder that has been told to keep a $100 endowment. Knowing which agent holds the money explains 47% of the variance in the amount transferred; knowing which agent asks explains 2%. Dropping the most extreme agents narrows the split to 22% against 4%. Changing the requester's appeal from one named person to a group changed what requesters wrote, but the amounts transferred under the two framings are statistically equivalent. The holders that lose least are the ones that turn down the opening request, and their turn-1 refusal rate ranks them by total losses (Spearman correlation = -0.73). Training moves this behaviour in both directions. Fine-tuning Qwen3-8B on a persuasion corpus with almost no refusals raised what it gave away as a holder from 12% to 84% of the endowment. Distilling two never-losing holders into a 4B student cut its losses from 95% to 1%. Evaluations of agents that hold resources should test them in the holder role, including after persuasion fine-tuning.

</details>

### 332. The Singularity Has a Distribution: Exact Timing Laws and Detection Limits for a Stochastic Intelligence Explosion

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18445)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`intelligence explosion`、`stochastic model`、`timing distribution`、`detection limits`

👤 **作者**：Xiaoyu Li、Zheng Gao、Chiwun Yang、Zhizhou Sha

- 🎯 **研究动机**：自动化 AI 研究形成软件能力与研究努力的反馈环——随机化该环后爆炸时刻的分布与可检测极限未明
- 🔬 **研究方法**：独立正乘性改进按能力幂次速率到达的随机模型：等尺寸改进下爆炸时间为几何加权和（q 级数表示）；Lomax 尺寸下精确 Gamma；固定样本检验的最小样本量
- 📌 **结论**：随机性不改变确定性阈值但给出显式时间分布；参数已知时临近爆炸的相对不确定度不消失；增长指数近临界值的 1/t 尾致条件平均等待无限——智能爆炸时序的可检验化（检测极限的形式刻画）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Automating AI research creates a positive feedback loop between software capability and research effort. We study a stochastic realisation of this loop in which independent, positive, multiplicative improvements arrive at a rate proportional to a power of current capability. Within this model, randomness leaves the deterministic explosion threshold unchanged but replaces the blow-up date by an explicit distribution. For equal-sized improvements the explosion time is a geometrically weighted sum of independent exponentials; its $q$-series representation gives its transforms, moments and tail behaviour, including a two-term small-time asymptotic. For Lomax-distributed improvement sizes the explosion time is exactly Gamma distributed. Self-similarity implies that, with parameters known, the relative uncertainty about the remaining time does not vanish as explosion approaches. We formulate a fixed-sample test between constant and geometrically increasing idea-arrival rates. If the baseline rate and the alternative log-rate increment $\ell$ are known, the minimum sample size at error level $\alpha$ is asymptotic to $(12z_\alpha^2)^{1/3}\ell^{-2/3}$, where $z_\alpha$ is the upper-$\alpha$ standard normal quantile. Uncertainty about the growth exponent near its critical value produces a $1/t$ tail with an explicit constant, and hence an infinite conditional mean waiting time, although explosion is certain given a positive exponent. Separate extensions show that a fixed deployment delay prevents explosion in the specified delayed-feedback models, that power-law diversion of effort shifts the growth threshold, and that in a deterministic oversight race a fixed allocation share does not change the asymptotic regime, except at a critical equality of exponents. Numerical illustrations, which are not empirical forecasts, show how timing and detectability depend on the granularity of improvements.

</details>

### 333. Autonomous Science Bench: Evaluating AI Agents for Autonomous Scientific Discovery

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18449)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`autonomous science`、`benchmark`、`design-build-test-learn`、`evaluation`

👤 **作者**：Yibo Wen、Han Liu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：科学 agent 基准多给固定数据集或依赖仿真——有限预算下自主决定做哪些实验的能力未测
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Autonomous Science Bench：六个基于已发表实验的预算化设计-构建-测试-学习战役（生物/化学/材料），双校准门（发现门评所测设计+学习门评模型对 held-out 设计的排序）
- 📌 **结论**：八配置 144 试验最佳 67%、最难战役 24 试 3 成；两门揭示不同弱点（会排序找不到 vs 找得到不会预测）——自主科学发现的分解评测（自动化研究的过程评估）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

AI agents for autonomous scientific discovery must decide which experiments to run under a limited budget. Most benchmarks for science agents provide fixed datasets, while others that support active experimentation largely rely on simulation. We introduce Autonomous Science Bench, an initial release comprising six budgeted design-build-test-learn campaigns grounded in published experiments across biology, chemistry, and materials science. Agents select batches of designs, receive measurements from a lab service, and use the results to guide subsequent experiments before delivering a predictive model. Success requires passing two calibrated gates: a discovery gate, which evaluates the designs the agent measured, and a learning gate, which evaluates its model's ranking of held-out designs. In a pilot evaluation of eight agent-model configurations and 144 trials, the best configuration succeeds in 67% of trials, while the hardest campaign is solved in only three of 24 attempts. The two gates reveal different weaknesses: some agents build accurate rankings but find too few promising designs, while others find promising designs but fail to predict which unseen designs will perform well. These differences are obscured by overall success rates alone. We release version 0.1 of the campaigns, lab runtime, and public leaderboard at this https URL.

</details>

### 334. Physics-Aware Deepfake Detection via Distance-Speech Consistency

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18485)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`deepfake detection`、`physics cue`、`distance-speech consistency`、`talking head`

👤 **作者**：Kyeongrae Kim、Kim Sung-Bin、Oh Hyun-Bin、Tae-Hyun Oh

- 🎯 **研究动机**：说话头深伪检测多依赖唇-音同步且为静态正面视频设计——动态野外录制中唇线索退化或缺失
- 🔬 **研究方法**：物理感知检测器：真实录制中语音能量随说话者-相机距离可预测变化而深伪生成可破坏该耦合；视频距离估计+距离依赖声学度量结合
- 📌 **结论**：野外基准上物理线索在动态场景有效并与唇同步检测器互补、简单集成跨数据集一致增益——物理约束的深伪检测新通道

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Recent progress in generative models has made talking-head deepfakes highly realistic and easy to create, jeopardizing public trust. However, most audio-visual deepfake detectors rely on lip-speech synchronization and are designed for static, frontal videos, limiting their reliability in dynamic, in-the-wild recordings where the speaker moves and lip cues are degraded or missing. As an alternative, we propose a physics-aware detector for dynamic speaking videos that leverages an acoustic constraint: in real recordings, speech energy varies predictably with speaker-to-camera distance, whereas deepfake generation can break this coupling. Our method combines distance estimates from video with distance-dependent acoustic measures from speech to detect deepfakes. Experiments on in-the-wild benchmarks show that the proposed physical cue is effective in dynamic scenarios, and that it complements lip-sync-based detectors, with a simple ensemble achieving consistent gains across datasets. Codes are available at this https URL.

</details>

### 335. A Global View of How LLMs Value Human Time

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18515)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`value bias`、`country-level`、`agentic settings`、`audit`

👤 **作者**：Yifan He、Sofie Goethals、Manon Reusens、David Martens

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM 日益充当个人助理——其国家级偏好在全球尺度如何嵌入未知（以 60 分钟延误补偿衡量时间价值）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：仅变用户国家×193 国×5 LLM，与 GDP_PPP/平均收入/耐心指数对照并测提示稳健性
- 📌 **结论**：欧洲获最高补偿而非洲最低；按 GDP_PPP 低估亚洲与南美用户时间价值——地理模式嵌入 LLM 并可能在 agentic 设定转化为差别对待（定向群体偏差的全球审计）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language models (LLMs) increasingly act as personal assistants; however, little is known about how they capture national-level preferences on a global scale. Given that "time" is a universal and representative concept, we examine LLMs' time preferences by varying only users' country and measuring the compensation LLMs deem appropriate for a 60-minute appointment delay. We then compare LLMs' responses with GDP per capita on Purchasing Power Parity (GDP_PPP), Mean Income, and a literature-based index (Patience Score). Our results across 193 countries and 5 LLMs demonstrate that people's time across the world is evaluated in noticeably different ways among LLMs, as well as varying levels of robustness under prompt variations. LLMs consistently recommend the highest compensation for Europe, but mostly the lowest for Africa. Based on GDP_PPP, LLMs underestimate the value of time for users living in Asia and South America. These findings indicate that geographical patterns are embedded in LLMs and may translate in different treatment in agentic settings.

</details>

### 336. How Reliable Are System-One Decisions in Medicine? A Comparative Study of Jev, Jev-like Models, and LLMs

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18529)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`System One model`、`medical reliability`、`abstention`、`comparative study`

👤 **作者**：Yanrui Du、…、Bing Qin

- 🎯 **研究动机**：Jev 类 System One 决策模型快速泛化——医疗可靠性未被探索（能力边界/稳定性/不可靠决策识别）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：15 个医疗数据集 8,742 实例统一协议：准确率+决策稳定性+缺失正确答案识别与可选弃答
- 📌 **结论**：Jev 文本任务匹敌领先模型、数值任务弱；格式变化下决策反转率最低但弃答意识普遍受限；选项级二元分数归一化优于选择打分——医疗场景 System One 决策的早期指南

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Jev has attracted considerable attention for its rapid, generalizable decision-making, yet its medical reliability remains under explored. To address this gap, our study compares various types of system-one decision models across 15 medical datasets comprising 8,742 instances under a unified protocol. Beyond accuracy, we assess decision stability and the ability to recognize unreliable decisions. Our results show that Jev rivals leading models on primarily text-driven tasks but is less effective on numerically driven tasks, revealing a distinct capability boundary. Under question-format variations, Jev demonstrates strong robustness, achieving the lowest decision reversal rate and relatively small accuracy fluctuations. Among evaluated open-source alternatives, only StartLux exhibits comparable robustness. But these strengths do not extend to reliable abstention awareness: most evaluated models show limitations in both recognizing missing correct answers and using optional abstention. Moreover, we find that Jev's probability-based answer selection can be improved by changing how decisions are scored: normalizing option-wise binary scores yields higher retained accuracy than choice scoring. Taken together, our findings provide early guidance for improving and applying decision models in medicine.

</details>

### 337. Behavioural self-monitoring for autonomous earth observation agents: The attribute reliability monitor (ARM)

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18548)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`self-monitoring`、`earth observation`、`reliability`、`behavioral`

👤 **作者**：Alejandro D. Mousist

- 🎯 **研究动机**：语言/视觉语言模型支撑自主系统——变化条件下其语义输出可靠性可能受损，需要行为级自监控
- 🔬 **研究方法**：ARM 属性可靠性监视器：随时间积累怀疑证据评估单个语义属性，黑盒仅用可观察响应；受控输入退化下响应分歧作选择性停挂依据
- 📌 **结论**：外部行为监控可告知模型输出是否继续使用并区分运行怀疑与语义正确性——自主 agent 的免内部访问监控样板

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Language and vision language models can support autonomous systems, but changing operating conditions may compromise the reliability of their semantic outputs. This work introduces the Attribute Reliability Monitor (ARM), a framework that accumulates evidence of suspicion over time to assess individual semantic attributes. ARM accommodates different detectors and temporal monitoring methods, while leaving intervention policies to the consuming system. An initial implementation was evaluated using a specialized vision language model developed for satellite thermal infrared imagery. Monitoring followed a black box approach, using only observable responses without access to internal model states, confidence scores, or external reference answers. Under controlled input degradation, disagreement between responses provided evidence for selective suspension of attributes. The results illustrate how external behavioral monitoring can inform continued use of model outputs, while distinguishing operational suspicion from semantic correctness.

</details>

### 338. Detecting Unreliable Classifications from Quantized Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18575)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`quantization`、`reliability detection`、`label-free`、`clinical deployment`

👤 **作者**：Ananya Kotla、Jorge T. Gomez

- 🎯 **研究动机**：INT8 量化按精度保持验证——需要真值标签而低资源临床场景常缺
- 🔬 **研究方法**：免标签替代：FP32 与 INT8 输出概率向量的余弦相似度（单次前向），四架构固定百分位分割下分离低精度预测
- 📌 **结论**：一致超越仅置信基线——量化模型可与自身基线比较评估漂移并判断临床部署适配性（部署变换的可靠性检查）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Post-training INT8 quantization is typically validated by checking accuracy retention, which requires ground-truth labels often unavailable in low-resource clinical settings. We show a label-free alternative: comparing a model's own output before and after quantization. We compute cosine similarity between the FP32 and INT8 models' output probability vectors for each prediction, which requires only a single forward pass. This output-agreement similarity separates lower-accuracy predictions across four evaluated architectures (ResNet-50, VGG-16-BN, Wide ResNet-50, and ViT-B/16) under fixed-percentile splits, outperforming a confidence-only baseline for all four. These results show that quantized models can be compared to their baselines to assess their drift and determine whether they are well suited for clinical deployment.

</details>

### 339. Decomposing and Predicting Annotation Disagreement in Harmful Content Across Languages

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18588)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`annotation disagreement`、`harmful content`、`multilingual`、`moderation`

👤 **作者**：Irem Ecem Yelkanat、Faeze Ghorbanpour、Alexander Fraser

- 🎯 **研究动机**：标注者分歧是主观任务的有意义属性——预测哪些条目易被争议可分配标注努力并把难例路由人工
- 🔬 **研究方法**：LLM 回答危害特征/分歧线索（边界严重度/缺上下文）/语言敏感线索（本地 slur）的二元问题集，分类器从答案预测分歧；四数据集（英/德/土）×三开源 LLM
- 📌 **结论**：结构化问题特征超直接提示 10.5 ROC-AUC、最强文本基线 5.0；贬损/缺上下文/决策边界问题是最强预测子——危害内容分歧的可预测性（审核标注的路由信号）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Annotator disagreement is increasingly recognized as a meaningful property of subjective tasks, rather than merely noise to be removed. Predicting which items are likely to be contested could help allocate annotation effort and route difficult cases to human review. We propose predicting disagreement from interpretable semantic properties of the text. An LLM answers a set of binary questions covering harmful-content characteristics, disagreement-related cues such as borderline severity and missing context, and language-sensitive cues such as local slurs. A classifier predicts disagreement from these answers, and we analyze which questions contribute most to the predictions. Across four datasets in English, German, and Turkish and three open-weight LLMs, our structured question features outperform direct prompting by 10.5 ROC-AUC points and the strongest text-based baselines by 5.0 points. A compact subset of eight questions performs within 1.5 ROC-AUC points of the full twenty-question set. In leave-one-dataset-out evaluation, models retain roughly 90-95% of their in-domain ROC-AUC. Across models and datasets, questions about derogation, missing context, and decision boundaries serve as the strongest predictors, suggesting that what makes harmful content contested is partly observable in the text.

</details>

### 340. AgentStripes: An Evidence-Graded Maturity Model for AI Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18603)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`maturity model`、`agent governance`、`evidence grading`、`audit`

👤 **作者**：Nutan Kumar Naik、Vijay Prasad Poudel、Aditya Kumar Saroj、Saurav Samantray、Abhishek Patel

- 🎯 **研究动机**：基准报告 agent 在任务套件上的能力——不报告某版本是否被工程化为可靠/安全/可观测以及证据强度
- 🔬 **研究方法**：AgentStripes：0-7 条纹×12 过程域 40 能力×三门（基本/安全/证明）×证据等级 E0-E5；确定性聚合+LLM judge 在明确标准下解释证据；30 个公开仓库的探索性演示
- 📌 **结论**：静态证据封顶 2 条纹、重判改变 4/22 仓库终级——带验证协议的证据分级成熟度模型（agent 工程化治理的可审计框架）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Benchmarks report what an Artificial Intelligence (AI) agent can do on a task suite, but not whether one released version has been engineered with the controls that make it reliable, safe and observable, or how strongly that is shown. This paper proposes AgentStripes, an evidence-graded maturity model for AI agents. It defines levels (0 to 7 stripes), process areas (12 areas holding 40 capabilities), three gates (basic, safety, proof) that hold an agent at a level however high its score, and evidence grades E0 to E5. Each applicable capability receives a rating from 0 to 3 and a separate evidence level; these are combined into a credit, aggregated into a severity-weighted score and converted into stripes that the gates limit. Instruments gather evidence, language-model judges interpret it under explicit criteria, and the arithmetic is deterministic. The model is compared with standards at topic level without a claim of conformance, and an unexecuted validation protocol is specified. An empirical evaluation (exploratory, non-confirmatory) of 30 public Python agent repositories, with static evidence only, is a demonstration and not a validation: static evidence caps every final stripe at 2, 26 repositories end at one stripe and 4 at two, and this distinction is unreliable, since re-judging changed the final stripe of 4 of 22 repositories (engine, dossier and panel changed), whereas none of 8 panel-only repeats, all at stripe 1, changed. The parameters are expert conventions without outcome based calibration, and the stripes have not been shown to predict reliability or safety.

</details>

### 341. To Align or Not To Align: Check Your COMPASS Before You Train

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18638)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`model compatibility`、`merging`、`diagnostic`、`pre-alignment`

👤 **作者**：Fabian Gröger、Petar Damjanović、Maria Brbić

- 🎯 **研究动机**：组合预训练模型（视觉编码器对齐 LLM/checkpoint 合并）前需知兼容性——经验搜索昂贵且对齐/合并后才知
- 🔬 **研究方法**：COMPASS：k-NN 重叠的配对特定 ease+训练范式允许的最大重叠上限两轴，72 个跨模态对齐与 392 个任务算术合并上验证相关性
- 📌 **结论**：与下游成功平均 Pearson 0.91 相关并给出四类行动处方——训练前的兼容性诊断（合并/组合的安全与效用预测底座）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Combining pretrained models is a common strategy for building AI systems, whether by aligning a vision encoder with a language model or merging fine-tuned checkpoints. However, finding compatible pairs typically requires costly empirical search, as compatibility is known only after alignment or merging. We propose COMPASS (COMPatibility ASsessment Score), a theoretically motivated diagnostic that estimates whether a model pair will be compatible prior to alignment or merging. Building on the observation that representational convergence is most robust at the level of local neighborhoods, COMPASS decomposes model compatibility into two cheap-to-compute axes: a pair-specific ease, defined as the $k$-NN overlap between the two encoders, and a paradigm-level ceiling, capturing the maximum overlap their training paradigms allow. Together, these two axes provide a ranking of candidate model pairs and prescribe how to act on each pair: (i) prioritize pairs that already agree and whose paradigms are compatible, (ii) invest additional compute on pairs with compatible paradigms but low initial agreement, (iii) switch the pretraining paradigm when it cannot support sufficient agreement, or (iv) deprioritize pairs that score poorly on both axes. We apply COMPASS to $72$ cross-modal alignment experiments and $392$ task-arithmetic model merges and show that compatibility estimates by COMPASS strongly correlate with downstream success, achieving average Pearson correlation of $0.91$ on cross-modal alignment tasks and $0.70$ on model merging.

</details>

### 342. Known-Voice Cloning and the Limits of Personality Transfer for Character Design

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18669)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`voice cloning`、`personality transfer`、`character design`、`human study`

👤 **作者**：Johanna Magdalena Kuch、Maud van Lier、Daksitha Senel Withanage Don、Patrick Gebhard、Tanja Schneeberger、Elisabeth André

- 🎯 **研究动机**：零样本声音克隆可把熟悉声音赋予任意虚拟角色——熟悉声音是否系统性转移人格印象（操纵人们把角色当有能力的社会行动者）未被直接检验
- 🔬 **研究方法**：两研究：真实大学场景（讲师克隆声）+线上（社媒创作者与其社区成员的克隆声）
- 📌 **结论**：人格转移证据有限且不跨特质或情境泛化；可识别声音身份线索激活说话者期望但不必然重建人格——声音克隆操纵担忧的实证边界

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Recent advances in zero-shot voice cloning systems make it possible to reproduce a recognizable voice from a short recording and attach it to arbitrary virtual characters. Prior work suggests that familiar vocal identity can carry over associated personality impressions to new targets such as robots or virtual characters. However, these works have mainly examined familiarity and personalization effects rather than directly testing whether a cloned familiar voice systematically transfers personality impressions to an embodied virtual character. Such transference raises the concern that cloned voices could be used to manipulate people into engaging with virtual characters as capable social actors. Across two studies, we investigated whether personality impressions transfer when a virtual character speaks with the cloned voice of a familiar person. Study 1 examined this question in a real-world university setting using the cloned voice of a lecturer familiar to the participating students. Study 2 examined the same question online using voices of social media content creators and members of their communities. Drawing on the concepts of identity activation, trait transfer, and person reconstruction, we show that evidence of personality transfer was limited in both studies. Observed effects were selective and did not generalize across traits or study contexts. In the online study in particular, recognizable voice identity cues activated expectations about the speaker but did not reconstruct the broader person impression. Qualitative responses further indicated that judgments were frequently shaped by perceived artificiality and by whether voice and appearance fit together. Our findings moderate common concerns that cloned voices straightforwardly manipulate people into perceiving artificial characters as capable social actors.

</details>

### 343. AIRRI: Towards a Human-Readable, Machine-Resistant Reading Interface

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18724)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`machine-resistant`、`reading interface`、`OCR adversarial`、`assessment`

👤 **作者**：Emory Salaun、Syed Ayon、Kaleel Mahmood、Shaun Wallace

- 🎯 **研究动机**：图像转文系统与 LLM 使作业材料易被转录并最少人力解决——教育评估需要机器抗性阅读界面
- 🔬 **研究方法**：AIRRI：图像变换（字距/词距/行距/不透明度/条纹/噪声）+决策式对抗攻击（RayS/SurFree/ADBA）两族技术，对 Tesseract/EasyOCR/GOT-OCR2 评测并测人类可读性
- 📌 **结论**：EasyOCR CER 6.9%→55.9%、Tesseract→100%；ADBA 最强——机器抗性与人类可读性的平衡框架（内容防自动抽取的界面设计）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

The rapid advancement of image-to-text extraction systems and large language models (LLMs) poses new challenges for educational assessment by making assignment materials easy to transcribe and solve with minimal human effort. To address this problem, we present AIRRI (Artificial Intelligence Resilient Reading Interface), a framework for generating machine-resistant educational content, a dataset of higher-education assignments for evaluation, and an initial evaluation of the human readability of AIRRI machine-resistant text designs. AIRRI employs two categories of machine-resistance techniques. First, AIRRI applies image transformations including character spacing, word spacing, line spacing, character opacity variation, stripe overlays, and image noise. Second, AIRRI applies decision-based adversarial attacks: RayS, SurFree, and ADBA. We evaluate AIRRI against three image-to-text systems: Tesseract, EasyOCR, and the transformer-based GOT-OCR2 model. Our results show that AIRRI substantially degrades automated text extraction performance. AIRRI increased character error rates for EasyOCR (6.9% to 55.9%), Tesseract (2.3% to 100.0%), and GOT-OCR2 (30.7% to 48.1%). Word error rates increased for EasyOCR (24.9% to 96.7%) and Tesseract (10.7% to 100.0%). Among the evaluated decision-based attacks, ADBA consistently produced the largest degradation in image-to-text performance, while RayS and SurFree produced more moderate reductions. EasyOCR and Tesseract were generally more susceptible to both image-based and adversarial perturbations than the transformer-based GOT-OCR2 model. Our findings suggest that AIRRI can significantly reduce the reliability of image-to-text-to-LLM workflows, increasing the effort required for AI-assisted assignment completion.

</details>

### 344. SolBehave: Evaluating the Behavioural Correctness of LLM-Generated Smart Contracts

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18745)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`smart contract`、`behavioural correctness`、`LLM code generation`、`evaluation`

👤 **作者**：Giovanni Pio Delvecchio、…、Thomas Troels Hildebrandt

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM 生成的 Solidity 合约部署后永不可补丁——单一潜伏错误即永久可利用，而现行评测大多只查能否编译
- 🔬 **研究方法**：SolBehave：250 个真实以太坊合约上差分模糊+历史交易重放+DCR 图引导的合成交易重放（枚举合法调用序列含链上从未发生的顺序）
- 📌 **结论**：编译率 26.4-92.7% 但可编译函数体仅 31.4% 等价原版、合约级分歧是常态——不可变代码的行为正确性基准（LLM 代码的一次性部署风险）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language models increasingly generate Solidity smart contracts that, once deployed, can never be patched, making a single latent fault permanent and exploitable. Yet current evaluations mostly ask only whether generated code compiles. We introduce SolBehave, a benchmark for evaluating the correctness of model-generated contracts at scale, moving beyond ad hoc unit tests. Across 250 real, reproducible Ethereum contracts, we apply three complementary tests of behavioural fidelity: differential fuzzing, historical-transaction replay, and, as our core contribution, synthetic-transaction replay guided by a Dynamic Condition Response (DCR) graph that enumerates the space of legitimate function-call sequences. Differential fuzzing compares each generated function with the original on automatically generated inputs; historical replay compares the whole contract on the transactions it actually received on-chain; DCR-guided replay compares it on sequences of calls that follow its intended usage, including orderings that never occurred on-chain. We evaluate seven state-of-the-art open-weight models after one round of automated repair. Compilation success ranges from 26.4% to 92.7%, but under differential fuzzing only 31.4% of all generated function bodies that compile, pooled over models and samples, are equivalent to the original. Divergence at the contract level is the norm rather than the exception: for six of the seven models, between 13% and 31% of the generated contracts that compile diverge from the original under historical replay, and between 13% and 44% under DCR-guided replay; only Gemma-4-31B stays below 3% on both. Compilation is therefore a necessary condition for correctness and far from a sufficient one.

</details>

### 345. Looking Complete Is Not Being Grounded: Evaluating LLM Sitreps Against Human-Authored Situation Reports

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18762)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`situation report`、`groundedness`、`crisis`、`provenance`

👤 **作者**：Sachin Kumar

- 🎯 **研究动机**：自动危机报告的评测应测从业者所需而非便于比较的——专业 sitrep 的信息完整与出处接地未被测
- 🔬 **研究方法**：15 槽信息模式（专业人道主义报告的定性内容分析）+121 人写与 261 LLM 生成报告跨五灾害三灾种+judge 校准与出处审计
- 📌 **结论**：最佳机器臂名义超人类上限但统计不可区分且不跨 judge 复现；机器数字 85-91% 链接社交流而非官方、发布出处缺口在全部 12 格为正——看似完整不等于有据

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Evaluation for automatic crisis reporting should measure what practitioners need, not what is convenient to compare. Through LLM-assisted, author-approved content analysis, we derive a 15-slot information schema from professional humanitarian situation reports using qualitative content analysis, then evaluate 121 human and 261 LLM-generated reports across five disasters and three hazard types. Under google/gemini-3.7-flash on Cyclone Idai, the best machine arm is nominally above the empirical human ceiling (0.827 vs. 0.821), but the difference is statistically indistinguishable from zero (95\% bootstrap CI $[-.049,.059]$; permutation $p=.852$), and the ordering does not replicate under openai/gpt-5.6-luna. On a blind 100-item Idai sample, the author agrees with the Gemini judge at 0.910 accuracy and unweighted Cohen's $\kappa=.799$. All four judges clear the plan's raw-agreement criterion of .7; only Gemini reaches .7 on chance-corrected $\kappa$. Across the three primary events, machine reports reproduce 5.4-11.0% of same-day official figures. Across those same events, raw exact-number matching links 14.3-19.6% of official figures and 85.4-90.1% of machine figures to the social stream; manual audit confirms 45/50 declared absences, 4/25 official matches, and 24/25 machine matches semantically. The issuing-provenance gap is positive in all 12 observed event-judge cells: report date, organisation, series, and authority were not supplied at professional-report coverage in any cell. On the three primary events, ROUGE-L is uninformative, BERTScore is negatively associated with completeness after multiplicity correction, and a generic LLM judge changes sign.

</details>

### 346. Rubrics from the Task, Verdicts from Evidence: Pointwise Rubric Rewards with Tool-Augmented Judges

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18766)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`reward model`、`rubric`、`tool-augmented judge`、`policy optimization`

👤 **作者**：Tianci Liu、Haoyu Wang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：评分表 RM 从观测响应候选导出标准——响应诱导偏差限制泛化；judge 纯阅读难应付机械约束；成对 judge 只给相对偏好难以直接用于策略优化
- 🔬 **研究方法**：ReVeR：仅从指令推理出硬规则与分级原则+工具增强的独立逐点评判，两部件经标签盲蒸馏训练
- 📌 **结论**：超成对评分表 RM（指令跟随最明显）；生成器 RL 提升偏好准确率却降评分表保真、偏好准确≠下游更好——评分表保真/偏好准确/下游效用是三种性质（RM 的目标分离）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Rubric-based reward models (RMs) evaluate responses against explicit criteria. However, existing methods often derive criteria from observed response candidates, introducing response-induced bias that may limit rubric generalizability. Their judges also struggle with mechanical constraints when relying on reading alone, and commonly used pairwise judges provide only relative preferences, complicating their direct use for policy optimization. We propose ReVeR, a pointwise rubric RM that derives hard rules and graded principles by reasoning about the instruction alone and evaluates responses independently through tool-augmented judging. Both components are trained through label-blind distillation, in which preference labels only filter a teacher's outputs, yielding ReVeR-Traces, a large-scale dataset of rubric-reasoning and judging trajectories. Experiments demonstrate that ReVeR outperforms pairwise rubric RMs in average preference accuracy, most clearly on instruction following, as well as in test-time scaling and downstream policy optimization. We further find that generator RL improves preference accuracy while reducing rubric fidelity, and that higher preference accuracy does not necessarily translate into better downstream policies. These findings highlight that rubric fidelity, preference accuracy, and downstream utility are distinct properties, shedding light on future RM research.

</details>

### 347. Jeva: System One Models from First Principles

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18773)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`System One model`、`decision-native architecture`、`null-aware`、`permutation invariance`

👤 **作者**：Qi Li、Xinchao Wang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：System One 模型支持直接低延迟决策——但从序列化文本预测答案本身不尊重决策问题的结构（四项结构要求被忽视）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Jeva 决策原生架构：共享编码器独立表示请求与选项+紧凑决策栈只对请求状态精修（选项内存不可变）、对称聚合得置换等变概率、覆盖估计区分答案缺失与候选竞争
- 📌 **结论**：强类型决策性能+近完美 null 检测+任意置换零翻转、可扩展至数千选项并缓存复用——System One 决策模型的第一性原理重构（Jev 生态的架构基座）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

System one models enable direct, low-latency decision-making. Yet recent practice of predicting an answer from serialized text does not, by itself, respect the structure of a decision problem. We argue that current designs underemphasize four important structural requirements. (i) null-aware: Models should distinguish answer absence from uncertainty among candidates; (ii) permutation-invariant: Reordering alternatives should not change the decision; (iii) option-scalable: Growing candidate sets should not require redefining the output space; (iv) cacheable: Request-independent computation over recurring alternatives should be reusable across requests. We introduce Jeva, a decision-native architecture that addresses these requirements jointly. A shared encoder independently represents the request and each option, while a compact decision stack refines only the request state against immutable option memory. Symmetric aggregation yields permutation-equivariant probabilities; shared scoring accommodates variable-size candidate sets. A separate coverage estimate distinguishes answer absence from candidate competition, enabling explicit null outcomes. Request-independent option representations and layer projections support reuse across newly assembled sets. These mechanisms unify Choice, Noul, and Score outputs. Across evaluations, Jeva combines strong typed-decision performance with near-perfect null detection and no decision flips under any tested permutations. It scales to thousands of candidates and substantially reduces latency through cross-request reuse without changing decisions.

</details>

### 348. Bytes to Threats: Byte-Sequence Transformer For Malware Detection

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18787)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`malware detection`、`byte-level`、`transformer`、`AI-for-security`

👤 **作者**：Abdelrahman Yassin Eljamal、Abdeltawab Hendawi

- 🎯 **研究动机**：恶意软件检测依赖手工特征（需专长、专用工具、耗时）——原始字节含丰富语义结构但长程依赖难捕获
- 🔬 **研究方法**：MalwareFormer：字节级 transformer 直接作用于原始字节免特征提取
- 📌 **结论**：准确率 98.35%、FPR 1.94% 全面超越基线——字节级恶意软件检测的可扩展路线（AI 作为安全机制的原始信号面）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Existing malware detection approaches heavily rely on handcrafted features derived from static or dynamic analysis of the executable files. These methods require specialized domain expertise, dedicated analysis tools, and time-consuming analysis, which limits their scalability and robustness in the face of the increasing complexity and diversity of modern malware. Consequently, there is a growing interest in approaches that operate directly on raw bytes, which contain rich semantic and structural information. However, capturing long-range dependencies within executable files remains challenging for conventional static and CNN-based methods. This paper presents MalwareFormer, a transformer-based, byte-level malware detection framework that operates directly on raw bytes, without requiring domain knowledge or feature extraction. MalwareFormer achieves an accuracy of 98.35%, a balanced accuracy of 98.32%, a macro-F1 of 98.32%, and a false positive rate of 1.94%, outperforming both baselines across all threshold-dependent classification metrics and demonstrating that transformers provide a powerful and scalable approach for byte-level malware detection.

</details>

### 349. Trust Before Autonomy: Rethinking Agentic Remote Sensing for Crisis Response

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18789)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`trust calibration`、`autonomy ladder`、`remote sensing`、`meta-analysis`

👤 **作者**：Georgios Angelidis、…、Georgios Th. Papadopoulos

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agentic 遥感按能答多少题被评测——危机响应取决于答案可否信赖与多大决策可安全交给 agent：自治应挣得而非假设
- 🔬 **研究方法**：十二个 agentic RS/EO/GIS 基准的证据性元分析（重复运行/不可答任务/校准）+三个 EO 特异信任属性+危机任务自治阶梯+上线前重放历史应急激活的就绪协议
- 📌 **结论**：仅一个基准重复运行、无一致弃答与能力的关系——当前证据只支持辅助角色（自治授权与已证可靠性成比例的立场）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Agentic remote sensing is evaluated on how many questions it can answer, whereas crisis response depends on whether its answers can be relied on and how much of the decision can safely be handed to the agent. This paper argues that \emph{autonomy should be earned, not assumed}: An Earth-Observation (EO) agent should be granted autonomy in proportion to demonstrated reliability, epistemic honesty and verifiability. The position is supported by an evidential meta-analysis of twelve agentic RS/EO/GIS benchmark studies from peer-reviewed venues. Only one repeats runs, three contain unanswerable tasks and none measures calibration. Their own tables show that completed workflows are often not correct (task success exceeds tool-call correctness by up to 54pp on identical queries), that the gap to an expert trajectory widens overall with trajectory length (from 7 to 56pp), and that capability does not predict abstention (Spearman r from -0.25 to 0.50, all n.s.). The paper then contributes three EO-specific trust properties, an autonomy ladder for crisis EO tasks, and a readiness protocol that replays archived emergency activations before any live use. In this context, current evidence justifies assistive roles only.

</details>

### 350. SEAR: Self-Evolving Agentic Robotics

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18792)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`self-evolving robotics`、`benchmark`、`learning controls`、`transfer`

👤 **作者**：Xinyi Wang、…、Dejia Xu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：前沿语言 agent 已能自己改进机器人（写控制代码、测试、留作技能与记忆）——真在学习还是只是试得多？所学在新任务/新仿真/真机还有用吗
- 🔬 **研究方法**：SEAR：三模式（code-as-policy/agent-as-policy/auto-research）+分离保留经验与重复尝试/熟悉任务/特权状态/更大预算/运气的对照组；七前沿模型、330 仿真+5 物理任务、4 年 agent 运行时
- 📌 **结论**：学习曲线取决于总时间预算与任务相对难度；控制代码与共享例程跨任务跨仿真迁移而任务特定解不迁移；不同模型在不同模式下演化更有效——自演化机器人学的受控测量

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Frontier language-model agents can now improve robots on their own: they write control code, test it, and keep what works as skills and memories for the next task. We introduce SEAR (Self-Evolving Agentic Robotics), a framework and benchmark built around two questions. Is the agent really learning, or just trying more often? And does what it learns still help on new tasks, in a new simulator, and on a real robot? The framework runs an agent in three modes: it writes code that becomes the policy (code-as-policy), acts live in the control loop (agent-as-policy), or works as a researcher that fine-tunes a vision-language-action model (auto-research). The benchmark adds controls that separate retained experience from repeated attempts, familiar tasks, privileged simulator state, larger budgets, and luck. Across seven frontier language models, 330 simulated and five physical tasks, and over four years of agent runtime, we highlight three findings. First, the learning curve of a robotic agent depends both on the overall time budget and the task difficulty relative to model capability. Harder tasks make longer sessions worthwhile; on easier tasks, re-rolling short attempts does just as well. Second, control code and shared routines transfer across tasks and simulators, while task-specific solutions do not. On a real robot, we observe a performance gap between agent-designed practice and novel human-designed scenes. Third, different models evolve more effectively in different ways. Some do best acting live, others writing code first. And a text-only model keeps pace with a comparable multimodal one: perception helps with error assessment, but agentic reasoning and tool use can matter more. We release the benchmark and codebase to support future research.

</details>

### 351. Self-Evolving Agent Harness

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18813)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`harness self-evolution`、`theory`、`PAC learning`、`admission rule`

👤 **作者**：Chuyang Wei、…、Yifei Shen

- 🎯 **研究动机**：harness 自演化修订 agent 的软件脚手架（提示/记忆/工作流/可执行组件）且接受的修订跨运行持久——可靠性缺乏形式化
- 🔬 **研究方法**：反馈驱动过程形式化（更新目标与提议/准入机制分离）+进化优化刻画+持续 PAC 学习的泛化界/回归预算下的保持界/用新鲜独立验证数据的高概率准入规则
- 📌 **结论**：可靠部署要求随任务分布与评估器变化的持续验证，学习/运行时特权/后果动作需独立防护——Harness RSI 的 66 页理论-算法-评测-影响综合（自改进基础设施的安全理论）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Harness self-evolution revises an agent's software scaffolding, including prompts, memory, workflows, and executable components, while keeping model weights fixed. We formalize a feedback-driven process in which accepted revisions persist across runs. The framework separates update targets from proposal and admission mechanisms. It distinguishes changes to interpreted state from revisions to tools and code, repository-level control logic, and plugin composition. We characterize search through evolutionary optimization and examine its reliability through continual PAC-learning. Under explicit assumptions, the analysis yields an expected generalization bound, a retention bound conditional on historical regression budgets, and a high-probability admission rule using fresh, independent validation data. We examine how persistent harness revisions affect knowledge sharing and organizational workflows, with implications for scientific reliability, physical safety, and institutional accountability. Cases from prior work illustrate model--harness co-design, distributed execution, and plugin reuse. Reliable deployment requires continual validation as task distributions and evaluators change, with independent safeguards for learning, runtime privileges, and consequential actions. We provide a comprehensive 66-page discussion of related work, theory, algorithms, evaluation, applications, and the broader impact of Harness RSI, together with continuously updated code and supplementary materials, at this https URL.

</details>

### 352. WorldOracle: Benchmarking Action-Conditioned World Models against Real-World Executions

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18826)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`world model`、`benchmark`、`real-world execution`、`manipulation`

👤 **作者**：Haoyi Duan、…、Guanghui Ren

- 🎯 **研究动机**：动作条件世界模型声称预测动作后果——与真实执行配对对照的系统性评测缺失
- 🔬 **研究方法**：WorldOracle：同初始观测与低层动作序列下比较预测 rollout 与真实发生（物理后果与任务态迁移）；四维评测（物理状态正确性/交互空间对齐/关键交互区保真/多视角动作一致）+含成败执行的长多段评测集
- 📌 **结论**：七个代表性 ACWM 展现不同强项与失效模式——动作条件世界建模的系统测量底座（世界模型安全研究的对象基线）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

We introduce WorldOracle, a benchmark specifically designed to evaluate action-conditioned world models (ACWMs) against paired real-world executions. Given the same initial observation and low-level action sequence, WorldOracle asks whether a predicted rollout reproduces what happens in the real world, including physical consequences and task-state transitions. We evaluate models along four complementary dimensions: physical state correctness, interaction spatial alignment, key interaction area fidelity, and multi-view action coherence. To support this evaluation, we curate real-world training and evaluation suites that cover different interaction dynamics and embodiments. The evaluation suite contains long, multi-segment episodes with both successful and failed executions, enabling evaluation across varied action outcomes and behavior distributions. Experiments on seven representative ACWMs reveal distinct model strengths and failure modes. WorldOracle provides a systematic foundation for measuring and advancing action-conditioned world modeling for manipulation. We will release the datasets, evaluation code, and leaderboard.

</details>

### 353. AegisAsync: Symbolic Async-Event Simulation for Risks in Python Asynchronous Programs

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18831)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`async program`、`risk detection`、`symbolic simulation`、`static analysis`

👤 **作者**：Baihong Chen、Wen Li

- 🎯 **研究动机**：Python 异步程序的安全与鲁棒风险依赖事件顺序、对象身份、异常/取消后的执行——须跨函数调用连接相关操作并沿执行路径追踪
- 🔬 **研究方法**：AegisAsync：字节码级 Async Event Transition Graph（控制流+解析调用+异步事件+符号对象关系）+有界路径局部模拟与 await 挂起点取消探索+六检测器插件
- 📌 **结论**：AsyncBench 180 标注风险全检出零误报；2,500 个 PyPI 包版本 457 个独立 bug、166 个 PoC 复现、157 已报告开发者——异步风险的符号化检测（软件供应链完整性）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Security and robustness risks in Python async programs depend on event order, object identity, and execution after exceptions or cancellation. Detecting these risks requires connecting related operations across function calls and tracking their effects along execution paths. We present AegisAsync, a bytecode-based analysis of Python async risks for CPython 3.10-3.14. Its Async Event Transition Graph combines bytecode control flow, resolved calls, async events, and symbolic object relations. Bounded path-local simulation tracks event order and object states and explores cancellation at await suspensions. Six detector plugins evaluate the resulting traces and states to produce candidates with supporting witnesses. On AsyncBench, our benchmark of 180 labeled risks, AegisAsync detects all labeled risks with zero false positives on each of the five versions. Across five path budgets on 2,500 PyPI package versions, source review confirms 457 of 910 AegisAsync candidate records (50.2%), representing 457 unique bugs; across three flake8-async samples, 70 of 2,187 review judgments are confirmed (3.2%). By submission, 166 PoCs reproduce AegisAsync findings, and we have reported 157 bugs to developers, of which 16 have been confirmed and fixed.

</details>

### 354. Who Improves the Robot? Toward Physical Recursive Self-Improvement

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18842)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`physical RSI`、`L0-L5 framework`、`safety preservation`、`survey`

👤 **作者**：Hai Zhou、…、Shangding Gu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：机器人任务执行日益自主而改进仍由人驱动——物理经验引导对能力与改进过程本身的持久更新（Physical RSI）面临噪声观测/连续动态/昂贵试验/不可逆后果
- 🔬 **研究方法**：L0-L5 框架：区分能力级与元级更新、任务内与跨任务增益，L5 保留给独立验证的递归增益；考察 harness 适配与模型训练方法及安全控制机制
- 📌 **结论**：现有文献尚未在其定义下实现 Physical RSI；可靠反馈/因果归因/安全保持/基准化是研究优先级——物理自改进的安全框架

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Robots are increasingly autonomous in task execution, yet their improvement remains largely human-driven. This study investigates Physical Recursive Self-Improvement (Physical RSI), in which physical experience guides lasting updates to both a robot's capabilities and its improvement procedure, making subsequent improvement more effective across tasks. Physical RSI faces challenges from noisy observations, continuous dynamics, costly trials, and irreversible consequences. We present an L0-L5 framework that differentiates capability-level from meta-level updates and within-task from across-task gains, reserving L5 for independently verified recursive gain. We study existing methods around harness adaptation and model training, alongside safety and control mechanisms that govern execution and successive updates.
